Explanatory Remarks:

2. Editorial notes and additions are enclosed in () or [].

Text:

<1>
śrīḥ
āyurvedasūtram
sabhāṣyam
vaprakrīḍoddaladdantaghātaniryanmahāṃśubhiḥ |
merorātmā''vṛto bhāti taṃ vande sindhurānanam ||
paramakāraṇībhūtaparabrahma sakalajīvanopakārakamāyurvedaṃ prajāpatibrahmaṇe'nujagrāha.
oṅkāraścāthaśabdaśca dvāvetau brahmaṇaḥ purā |
kaṇṭhaṃ bhittvā viniryātau tasmānmāṅgalikāvubhau ||
ityaktarītyā athaśabdena paramamaṅgalamācaran prathamasūtramanugṛhṇāti-- atheti |
athāto dhātusthadoṣagatyavikārahetubhūtārthavārdhakadravyāṇyadyāt || 1 ||
asyārthaḥ--dhātuṣu tiṣṭhantīti dhāsusthāḥ doṣāṇāṃ gatiḥ, vikārābhāvakāryahetupūrvikā gatiḥ. tāsāṃ hetubhūtārthāni aprakopakārakāṇi vārdhakadravyāṇi bhojyayogyāni adyāt iti sūtrārthaḥ | dhātuṣu--
'rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedo'sthimajjāśuklāni dhātavaḥ' |
ityukteṣu saṃcārārthaṃ tiṣṭhantīti dhātusthāḥ | te ca te doṣāśceti samānādhikaraṇasamāsaḥ |
vāyuḥ pittaṃ kaphaśceti trayo doṣāssamāsataḥ |
dhātuvṛddhikārakadravyāṇāṃ tāvadvṛddhimātraphalamuddiśya pravṛttiścet alpaphalamiti āyurvedapravṛtteḥ bahupuruṣārthapradāyakatvādityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha-- cireti |
cirāyuricchāpravṛttirāyurvedārthapuruṣārthopapādikā ||
ciraśabdena kāryasiddhidravyādane jāte āyuṣkāmayamānasya pravṛttiḥ icchataḥ puruṣasya cirāyurme bhūyāditi kāmanāviṣayakapravṛttiḥ śāstrasampradāyakathitaviṣayakānubhavahastakauśalyādipāṇḍityaṃ yasyāsti tasya īdṛśapravṛttiḥ puruṣārtho'yamiti cirāyuricchāpravṛttiviṣayaḥ | āyurvedārthapuruṣārthopapādiketi prayojanam | atra sūtrasthānavacanam--
āyuṣkāmayamānena dharmārthasukhasādhanam |
āyurvedopadeśeṣu vidheyaḥ paramādaraḥ ||
nanvasmin śāstre prayojanaṃ āyurvedārthapuruṣārthopapādiketi prayojanamityuktaṃ tathā sati rogābhāvakāryahetutāyāḥ kāraṇatvaṃ na syādityasvarasādāha-- taddhetviti ||
taddhetubhūtārthaṃ rakṣet || 3 ||
taddhetubhūtārtho nāma arogakāryahetubhūtārthaḥ | tadeva prayojanam | tadrakṣaṇameva phalībhūtārthaṃ taddhetu karaṇīyamityarthaḥ | atra sūtravacanaṃ--
vicārayitvā sarvāṇi nityaṃ dehaṃ nirīkṣayan |
avikāraṃ nirīkṣyainaṃ pathyenaiva ca taṃ haret |
jīrṇājīrṇāvivekatvaṃ jihvāyāṃ vidyate nṛṇām ||
evamākāreṇa vikāraṃ jñātvā tannivartanaṃ pathyādanameva bhāvyam | taddhetubhūtārthaṃ tvidameva saṃrakṣaṇīyam | hīnātimātrānnādānaṃ vikārahetukam | tannivartakahetubhūtārthaṃ rakṣaṇīyamiti tātparyam |
indriyātilālanātipīḍanayoḥ vikārahetukatvaṃ na syādityasvarasādāha--neti |
nendriyātilālanātipīḍanau || 4 ||
indriyāṇi śrotratvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇāni | teṣāṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandhā viṣayāḥ | vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthāḥ karmendriyāṇi | hitamitapravṛttiviṣayakaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | nanu hīnabhojanātibhojanayoḥ rogahetukatvaṃ pratipāditam | tatra vacanaṃ--
bhojanaṃ hīnamātraṃ tu na balopacayaujase |
atimātraṃ punassarvānāśu doṣānprakopayet ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa anayoreva hetutvaṃ pratipāditaṃ, nendriyātilālanātipīḍanayoḥ | tasmādetatsūtraṃ vyarthaṃ syādityasvarasādāha--doṣeti |
doṣavṛddhikṣayopapādakau || 5 ||
doṣāṇāṃ vṛddhirapi doṣarogaheturbhavati | doṣāṇāṃ kṣayo'pi doṣaprakopaheturbhavati | doṣahetukatvaṃ nāma doṣaprakopahetukatvam | doṣavikārasya vṛddhikṣayayośca kāryakāraṇabhāva uddiṣṭaḥ nendriyātilālanātipīḍanayoriti | atra vacanaṃ--
na pīḍayedindriyāṇi na ca tānyatilālayet |
iti | ..........................doṣāṇāṃ vṛddhikṣayayorupapādakatvamanayoreva vṛddhipratipādakatvāt--
vṛddhissamānaissarveṣāṃ viparītairviparyayaḥ |
etayoreva kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ | paramparayā kāraṇatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata ityarthaḥ ||
nanu doṣavṛddhikṣayau svata eva prāptau, nendriyātilālanātipīḍanayoḥ | tayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvastu kālavaśādeva prāptaḥ | tatra sūtrasthānavacanaṃ--
cayaprakopapraśamā vāyorgrīṣmādiṣu triṣu |
varṣādiṣu tu pittasya śleṣmaṇaśśiśirādiṣu ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa anayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ kathaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata ityasvarasādāha-- lakṣaṇeti |
lakṣaṇānyapi tathā || 6 ||
aprāptakāle prāptalakṣaṇāni yatra bhāsante prāptakāle tallakṣaṇābhāvo yatra bhāsate tatra niyamaḥ | lakṣaṇapramāṇābhyāṃ hi vastusiddhiḥ | tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
kālārthakarmaṇāṃ yogā hīnamithyātimātrakāḥ |
samyagyogaśca vijñeyo rogārogyaikakāraṇam |
rogastu doṣavaiṣamya doṣasāmyamarogatā ||
asādhāraṇadharmavacanahetujñānajanyāmayanivartakayogyakāryaṃ vyapadiśati--yogyairiti |
yogyaistacca nivartayet || 7 ||
abhighātāmayajanyaruṅnivartakarecakopayogidravyarasaiḥ bhāvyaṃ yat tadyogyamityarthaḥ | yāvadabhighātāmayastacchabdārthaḥ | taṃ nivartakadravyeṇa recayediti | atra sūtravacanaṃ—-
āmadoṣaṃ mahāghoraṃ varjayedviṣasaṃjñitam |
viṣarūpāśukāritvādviruddhopakramatvataḥ ||
nanu indriyāṇāṃ rogahetukatvaṃ pratipādayituṃ indriyātilālanātipīḍanādbhavatīti yaduktaṃ tanna rocate | atimātrabhojanamāmahetukaṃ bhavati | āmasaṃgraha eva sarvarogakāraṇam | āmanivartakatvaṃ yuktamityasvarasādāha-- anāmeti |
anāmapālanaṃ kuryāt || 8 ||
yasmādāmasaṃgrahābhāvasyaiva rogābhāvahetukasāmagrītvaṃ tasmādanāmapālanaṃ pratyahaṃ vidhiriti | āmaṃ pālayatītyāmapālanaṃ, āmapālanaṃ na bhavatītyanāmapālanaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ | atra anāmapālanakarmakaraṇasya hetuvacanaṃ sutre pratibhātam |
mātrāśī sarvakālaṃ syānmātrā hyagneḥ pravartikā |
mātrāṃ dravyāṇyapekṣante gurūṇyapi laghūnyapi ||
nanu jvarādīnāmapravartakasāmagrī jaṭharāgnipravartakasāmagrī | tasmādanāmapālanamaprayojakaṃ syādityata āha-- āmamiti |
āmaṃ hi sarvarogāṇām || 9 ||
āmameva kāraṇamiti, hiśabdaḥ prasiddhivācakaḥ | atra sūtravacanam--
āmāśayastho hatvā'gniṃ sāmo mārgānvidhāya ca |
vidadhāti jvaraṃ doṣaḥ tasmātkurvīta laṅghanam |
jaṭharāgnipravartakasāmagrī yasyāsti tasya sarvabhoktṛtvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate | cirāyurapi labhyate |
nanu sarveṣāṃ rogāṇāmāmanivṛttireva tatra bheṣajamiti vaktavye sati jvarādāvāmanivṛttyakaraṇaṃ doṣahetukaṃ bhavati | āmanivṛttirapi laṅghanādeva bhāvyeti laṅghanakaraṇamapyāvaśyakamiti brahmaṇā pratipāditamityāha--ādīti |
ādibhūtamāha brahmā || 10 ||
ubhayamapi roganivartakapratipādanādāmaśoṣaṇārthaṃ laṅghanakaraṇamapi paramparāhetubhūtamiti brahmā āhetyarthaḥ | atra sūtravacanaṃ--
laṅghanaṃ kāryamāme tu viṣṭambhe svedanaṃ bhṛśam |
iti vacanāllaṅghanena āmanivṛttau satyāṃ tena phalībhūtaṃ kimāsīdityata āha--taditi |
tannivṛttiranāmayam || 11 ||
āmanivṛttereva puruṣārtho bhūtvā arogavān bhavati |
doṣāḥ kadācitkupyanti jitā laṅghanapācanaiḥ |
ye tu saṃśodhanaiśśuddhā na teṣāṃ punarudbhavaḥ ||
iti laṅghanapācanairjitadehasya vyādhayo na punarbhavantītyuktam | tathā sati sarvaviṣayātmakāni pañcendriyāṇi | manasa atmagocaratvaṃ kenopāyena hetunā bhāvyamityata āha--anāmayīti |
anāmayī atmānaṃ sammanyate || 12 ||
pūrvoktarītyā dehasya anāmayatvaṃ sampādya ātmamanananididhyāsanayogena paramasukhaṃ jīvo'nubhavati | tatra vacanaṃ--
krameṇopacitā doṣāḥ krameṇopacitā guṇāḥ |
gatā ye ca punarbhāvaṃ aprakampyā bhavanti ca ||
anāmayakāryakaraṇena arogo bhavan svargasukhamevānubhavatītyarthaḥ | evamuktarītyā dehasya anāmayatvaṃ sādhitam | tāvatā ātmamananasāmagrī na pratipāditā | kiṃ arogavānbhavatīti dṛḍhatarajaṭharānalavaśatvena sarvendriyāṇāṃ sarvaviṣayabhoktṛtvaṃ sādhitam ? anāmayatvamātmamananasāmagrīmātrajanyatvaṃ na bhavatītyasvarasādāha ahamiti |
ahampratyayaḥ karmakartā || 13 ||
ahaṃkārādhiṣṭhitamātraṃ rakto'haṃ śyāvo'haṃ iti pratyayaviṣayakamahamiti jñānam | tathā jñānavānahamiti anuvyavasāyena jñātuṃ śaktaḥ satkarmādhikārī bhavet | sukarmaṇā ātmamananaṃ kartuṃ śakyata iti |
karmaṇaiva hi saṃsiddhimāsthitā janakādayaḥ |
iti dharmaviṣayakajñānagocaratvena parabrahmaṇaḥ manoviṣayakajñānagocaratvaṃ sidhyati | adhiṣṭhānabhūto'yamātmā, yassidhyati sa eva jīvātmā | tāvubhau śarīrādhiṣṭhitāveva | tayoranyasya sarvabhoktṛtvaṃ vyapadeṣṭumāha--sthāneti |
sthānavānahaṃkārī || 14 ||
paramātmanāmadhiṣṭhānarūpatvaṃ nopapadyate | tajjīvātmanāṃ sambhavatītyāśayavānāhetyarthaḥ | yasya yāvatkālo yogaścarati tasya arogitvasya svatassiddhatvādityata āha--yāvaditi ||
yāvadarogī abhimanyate || 15 ||
śoṣakapoṣakadravyavijñānānantaraṃ arogakāryahetukasāmagrībhajanāt 'ahamarogavān' iti vyavasāyajñānena abhimantuṃ śakyata ityarthaḥ | yaḥ arogī ātmamanananididhyāsanayogayogyo bhavet tena tattadviṣayagocarāṇi pañcendriyāṇi śarīre saṃsṛjya atmānugocarāntarindriyeṇa manasā paramātmā mantavya ityuktam | bāhyendriyāṇāṃ bāhyaviṣayagocaratvamaprayojaka syādityata āha--sa iti |
sa eva bhuṅkte || 16 ||
ajo hyadhiṣṭhātā || 17 ||
sarvāviṣayānubhavo jīvassarvamaśnute | atra śrutiḥ--
dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā
samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte |
tayoranyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvatti
anaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti ||
iti śrutireva atra pramāṇam | icchati, jānāti, prayatate iti sarvakāryasya ātmanaḥ ātmamanassaṃyogasya kāraṇatvaṃ pratītam | atassarvaviṣayabhoktṛtvaṃ jīvātmana upalabdheḥ | tasmātparamātmanā'nubhūyata ityāha--bhuṅkte iti |
bhuṅkte viṣayabhogān || 18 || || |
svaprakāśarūpaḥ paramātmā saccidānaṃdānubhavaviṣayavāniti tātparyam | satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahmeti satyānantajñānaṃ svaviṣayānandātmanaḥ paramātmā |
nanu ekassakalaviṣayabhogānbhuṅkte | bhogāḥ karmādhīnāḥ | jīvasya karmabhoktṛtvaṃ vidhiriti vyaktavye sati upasthitaviṣayabhoktṛtvaṃ jīvasya, paramātmanassarvaviṣayabhoktṛtvaṃ bhāsate, sarvajñatvāt | ubhayoḥ karmabhoktṛtvaṃ prasaktamityasvarasādāha--anya iti |
anyaḥ karma bhuṅkte || 19 ||
anyo jīvātmā karmādhikārī san svakarmādhīnaviṣayabhogān bhuṅkta ityarthaḥ | sukarmaṇā sukhapradānaviṣayaphalaṃ bhuṅkte | duṣṭakarmaṇā duḥkhaviṣayakaphalaṃ bhuṅkte | bhogasya sukhaduḥkhātmakatvāt | sukhaduḥkhānubhavo bhogaḥ jīvasya lakṣaṇaṃ pratibhāsitam | jīvasya sakalaviṣayānubhavatvaṃ prasajyata ityarthaḥ | duṣṭakarmajaṃ śarīre sati satkarmaiva sadā kuryādityarthaḥ |
paramātmanaḥ satkarmaviṣayakakarmakaraṇatvaṃ vidhiḥ | īśvarasyāpi yatkiṃcinkarmajanakakarmādhikāritvaṃ prasaktamityasvarasādāha--ya iti |
yaḥ kartā karmabhogī || 20 ||
rājasatāmasaphalapradahetukadravyādanajātasukhaduḥkhaphalaṃ ahaṃkāraviśiṣṭakarmādhikāriṇaḥ prayacchata iti | sākṣātkarmaṇaḥ phalāvaśyaṃbhāvanigamādityata āha-- karteti |
kartā śarīrī || 21 ||
yāvatkarmakartā śarīrī atra kartā bhavatītyarthaḥ | yatkiṃcitphalamuddiśya yaḥ karma karoti sa śarīrītyuktam | śarīrābhāvādātmana anna danatvaṃ na yogyamityasvarasādāha-- aśarīrīti |
aśarīrī nānnamatti || 22 ||
karmaphalayoḥ kāryakāraṇatvaṃ niyamena annādanatvam | svakarmānukūlaphalaṃ labhyate | taccharīriṇaḥ sa phalaṃ prayacchatīti bhāvaḥ | aharaharmanuṣyā aśanamicchanti | pratyahamaśanārthaṃ loke tatphalopayogi karma cetkṛtaṃ tatra phalābhāvo na daśyate | tatrāpi śarīramasti | tasmāt karmaphalayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvo nāstītyāha neti |
na kevalaṃ śarīraṃ bhogayogyamātmano yogāt || 23 ||
prathamataḥ śarīrotpattikālaḥ tajjanmāntarasaṃskārādeva bālakādīnāṃ stanyapānādiphalaṃ prayacchatītyarthaḥ |
nanu bālakādīnāmapi śarīramasti, tatra karma na kṛtaṃ, teṣāmayogyatvāt | stanyapānādiphalaṃ mātrā dīyate | tasmātkarmaphalayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvo vaktuṃ na śakyata ityata āha-- bhogayogyamiti | tadānīṃ dṛṣṭakarmābhāve'pi janmāntarakarmakaraṇaṃ kalpyate, śarīravattvādātmanaḥ | śarīradhāraṇasyocitatvādeva bālakādīnāṃ stanyapānādikaṃ mātrā dīyate iti cenna | śarīradhāraṇādīnāṃ janmāntarakarmahetuphalatvaṃ kalpyate | ātmanaśśarīravattvāt ātmanaśśarīradhāraṇasyocitatvāt ātmasaṃyogakāraṇabhūtasya śarīriṇo bhogāyatanatvaṃ jīvasya pratītam | tasmāccharīrādhiṣṭhānayogyatvaṃ nānyasyetyata āha-- ajāviti |
ajau hyadhiṣṭhitau || 24 ||
na jāyata ityajaḥ| tau jīvātmaparamātmānau śarīrādhiṣṭhitāvityarthaḥ | ghaṭajñānavānahamiti yasya pratītistasyaiva viṣayānubhavatvaṃ anayoranyatarasya sambhavatītyarthaḥ | ata āha--anya iti |
anyo'nnamatti || 25 ||
śarīrāṅgāni indriyāṇi, aṅgī śarīrī | jīvātmana indriyagocaraviṣayānubhavārthaṃ tadaṅgabhūtendriyāṇi saṃsṛjya jānāti, icchati, prayatate, ityetatpratyayānyathānupapattyā jīvātmā annamaśnute | tadvyatiriktātmanā kiṃ tadanubhūyata ityāśaṅkāyāmāha-- anattīti |
anattyevamanyaḥ || 26 ||
anyasya paramātmanaḥ sarvaviṣayānubhavatvaṃ prasiddhamiti anyasya phalenāviśiṣṭatvāt etādṛśārthaphalaṃ anatti svayaṃ svaprakāśātmakatvāt svayamevānubhavatītyarthaḥ |
vikārakāryahetukasāmagrīviṣayakakāryānubhavaśarīramasyātmano bhogayogyaṃ syādityata āha-- satīti |
sati śarīre bhogāya || 27 ||
śarīre sati sarvaviṣayabhogārhatvamātmanā sampāditamityāhetyarthaḥ | śarīriṇaḥ kānyaṅgāni ? pañcendriyāṇāmapyaṅgatvaṃ pradipāditam | manasa agocaratvena manaso'ntarindriyatvena ṣaḍityupadiṣṭam | ṣaṇṇāmātmano bhogaviṣayaprāpakahetubhūtārthatvāt | tasmāccharīrasya bhogayogyatvamiti svata eva siddhatvāt | viṣayavijñānamindriyādhīnaṃ, indriyāṇi ca śarīrādhiṣṭhitāni | sarvaviṣayāḥ śarīrādhīnā ityarthaḥ |
nanu pravṛttirdvividhā iṣṭaprāptyarthamaniṣṭaparihārārthaṃ ceti | svādhiṣṭhānānubhūtaśarīraṃ saṃrakṣaṇīyamityekā pravṛttiḥ | sā dvividhā pravṛttirātmana upapadyate | tatkathaṃ śarīrāṅgabhūtāni pañcendriyāṇi ? taddvārā iṣṭaprāptirūpakalaṃ kathaṃ labhyate ? aniṣṭaparihāradvārā iṣṭaprāptirūpaphalamapi labhyate ? aniṣṭaparihāradvārā ubhayaphalaṃ śarīriṇo bhāvyamityata āha-- aṣṭeti |
aṣṭāṅgī śarīrī || 28 ||
aṣṭānāmaṅgānāṃ samāhāraḥ aṣṭāṅgam | śarīrama yāstīti śarīrī | atra sūtravacanam--
kāyabālagrahe rtvāṅgā śalyadaṃṣṭrājarāvṛṣān |
aṣṭāvaṅgāni tasyāhuścikitsā yeṣu saṃśritā ||
śarīropadravakāryakāraṇībhūtavyādhaya aupādhikabhūtāḥ vātapittakaphaprakupitahetukadhātavo'pi na pravardhanta iti aṣṭarogāṇāṃ aṅgatvaṃ pratipāditam | śarīrāṇāmetādṛśā vyādhayaḥ | teṣāṃ dhātūnāmaṅgatvaṃ pratipāditam | saptadhātumayaṃ śarīramiti aṅgopadravakārakavyādhīnā paramparayā aṅgatvaṃ pratipāditamityarthaḥ |
nanu dhātupravardhakadravyāṇāmaṅgatvaṃ vakta'vyaṃ cet tathā sukaramiti dhātuvināśakārakāṇāṃ śarīranāśakānāṃ aṅgatvaṃ kathaṃ syāditi cenna, śarīrābhāvakāryasya vyādhīnāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ |
śarīravināśahetubhūtānāmaṣṭarogāṇāṃ lakṣaṇapramāṇe vyapadiśati-- yāvaditi |
yāvatkṛtividheḥ kāyaḥ || 29 ||
yāvannidānajñānaviṣayakajñānajanyasādhakībhūtanivartakānāṃ kṛtiḥ kāryo nivartyaḥ | tatraitayoḥ yāvadvidhiḥ yāvacchāstroktakarmasahiṣṇutvaṃ yasya nivartakaṃ bhavati sa eva kāya iti upadeśaḥ | atra sūtravacanam--
śodhanaṃ śamanaṃ ceti samāsādauṣadhaṃ dvidhā |
śarīrajānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ krameṇa paramauṣadham ||
śarīratvāvacchedena kāyabālaśarīrasyāpyekatvena krameṇa paramauṣadhamiti kāyabālacikitsābhedena vivakṣitatvātkramaśabdārtho vivakṣitaḥ | nanu kāyabālāmayau āmahetujanyāmaroganivartakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ tajjanyaroganivṛtterdṛṣṭatvāt tajjātāmayau kāyabāleti pratipāditau | tadanyajātarogā grahā grahajātarogāḥ | teṣāṃ kā gātirityāśayavānāha-- āmeti |
āmajāmayakāryahetukacittakṣobhabhayanigrahavigraho grahaḥ || 30 ||
atra kecit-- āmajanyā vyādhayaḥ | teṣāṃ kiṃcitsāmagrīprayuktakarma tatra bheṣajam | grahābhibhūtāmayānāṃ āmanivṛttikarmakaraṇasyāyogyatvāt, atra tannivartakasāmagryā ananubhūtatvāt, śāstrataḥ adṛṣṭatvācca | tasmādetatsūtramayuktamiti vadantaṃ prati parihartukāmassūtraṃ vyācaṣṭe-- āmeti |
tadroganivartakatadgrahanigraharūpaṃ tatra bheṣajam | mantratantrabalipradānādayo nivartakāḥ | graheṣvanuguṇeṣvekadoṣamārgā grahajanyādirujo'pi balidānamantratantrādyaiḥ nivartanaiḥ nivartayituṃ sukhena śakyā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tatra sāmagrīgraharūpamantratantraiḥ bhayanigraho bhavati | āmahetukasāmagrījanyarogābhāvādityarthaḥ | ūrdhvāṅgajātā anekarogāḥ pūrvoktaprakāranivartakā nivartyāḥ te ūdhvāṅgajātarogā bahavo dṛṣṭāḥ | tannidānametadbhinnaviṣayakam, etannidānasyāpi tadbhinnaviṣayakatvāt | karṇanetraśironāsikājihvāsthitapavanapittaprakopakārakāḥ taddhetukāryāhetukāryahetubhūtāmayāḥ kāraṇajanyāḥ ityūrdhvāṅgarogānviśinaṣṭi-- āmeti |
āmajāmayakāryahetukānyahetukāmayanivartakā apūrvāṅgahetukāḥ || 31 ||
pūrvakāyahetukajātarogakāryahetukasāmagrījanyahetupratipādanāt uttarakāyādhiṣṭhitarogapratipādakāmajātarogakāryahetusthitāḥ nivartakaiḥ nivartayituṃ yogyā bhavantītyarthaḥ |
recakalaṅghanābhyāṃ nivartayitumayogyā ityāśaṅkya tadāmayānāṃ tannivartakāḥ prakāśyante-- doṣeti |
doṣasañcārābhāvajāsthikarṇanāsādisandhijarujaśśastreṇaike nivartakāḥ || 32 ||
doṣāḥ vātapittakaphāḥ prakupitāḥ | vikārahetubhūtasañcārajanyakarṇanāsādisandhijātaduṣṭaraktaśalyarūparujāṃ tannivartakāni śastrakṣārāgnikarmāṇi śiro'sthiraktaṃ vimocayantītyarthaḥ | tatra vacanaṃ--
śastrādisādhakaḥ kṛcchrassaṅkare ca tato gadaḥ |
ekaśabdena ekeṣāṃ mukhyatvaṃ pratipāditam ||
tannivartakā iti bahujātaruṅnivartakā na bhavantītyarthaḥ | doṣahetukāste ca rujo dhātuvināśakāḥ | tannivartakaprakārakāḥ auṣadhādayaḥ | atra vacanaṃ--
nāvanāñjanapāneṣu yojayedviṣaśāntaye |
viṣabhuktāya dadyācca śuddhayordhvamadhastathā ||
sūkṣmaṃ tāmrarajaḥkāle sakṣaudraṃ hṛdviśodhanam |
śuddhe hṛdi tataśśāṇaṃ hemacūrṇasya dāpayet |
na sajjate hemapāṅge padmapatre'mbuvadviṣam ||
iti vacanāt |
annapānaṃ viṣādrakṣedviśeṣeṇa mahīpateḥ |
yogakṣemau tadāyattau dharmādyā yannibandhanāḥ ||
iti vacanāntareṇāpi sūtrārtho jñāpyata iti vyādhihetukadravyādanājjātarogāṇāṃ tannivartakauṣadhayogavaśādvyādhinivṛttireva phalamiti aniṣṭaparihārārthamapi phalapravṛtterdṛṣṭatvāt saṃsthānābhivardhakasarvabhoktṛtvaṃ kathaṃ syādityata āha-- prakāśamāneti |
prakāśamānaprathamaprayāṇādhigatanissṛtāmṛtapratiprayāṇāhṛtayogavibhāgadhātupoṣakasurasādanādarogavānajaraḥ || 33 ||
agnijasvarūpakuṇḍalyudbhavaśvāsānilajātenoṣmaṇā prakāśamānassan prathamaprayāṇādutthitasahasrakamalādāgatāmṛtamāhṛtya yathāyogaṃ saptadhātūnsantarpya yo yogaṃ karoti sa cirāyurbhavati | śuddharasādanācca phalaṃ bhavatīti ajaratvaṃ suprasiddhamityarthaḥ | atra kavivākyaṃ--
ṣāḍguṇyamupayuñcati śaktyapekṣo rasāyanam |
bhavantyasyaivamaṅgāni sthāsnūni balavanti ca ||
itaḥ paraṃ śārīraviṣayakayogakramavidhiṃ pratipādayiṣyāma ityuktam | idānīmiṣṭaprāptirūpaphalaṃ vaktuṃ "āyuṣkāmayamāno yogaṃ kuryāt" iti yatpratipāditaṃ 'sarvajane duṣkṛtamiti' tattadduritabāhulyādduḥkhado'yamiti āśayavān sūtrāntaramāha-- kāyeti |
kāyakālādhikāle caramadhātupravardhakarasavaddravyairvājīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt || 34 ||
rasāyanaṃ ca seveta dhātupuṣṭīndriyapradam |
iti vacanāt |
yāvadvargottamadravyairyatpradhānapramātitāḥ |
tāvadrasā viruddhāpūḥ tadrasāyanamīritam ? ||
iti rasāyanalakṣaṇam | rasāyanavidhau karmavidhau kramaḥ ratnavargoktadravyeṣu vajrasya rasadravyatvāt sa śreṣṭho bhavati | maṇivargoktadravyeṣu kāntamaṇiḥ rasadravyatvāt śreṣṭho bhavati | pāṣāṇavargeṣu rasavaddravyatvādabhrakasya śreṣṭhatvam | satvavaddravyavargeṣu bhūnāgasatvasya rasavaddravyatvāt śreṣṭhatvaṃ bhavati | rasavaddravyopādhikabhedadravyeṣu rasendrasya rasavaddravyatvāt śreṣṭhatvaṃ bhavati | saṃskārādikarmasu bījakarmakaraṇasya rasavaddravyatvāt śreṣṭhatvaṃ bhavati | atra 'cirāyuricchāpravṛttiviṣaya āyurvedārthapuruṣārthopapādikā' iti prayojanaṃ tathā hyekaikasya kāryakāraṇabhāvajñānaṃ tattadviṣayakajñānagocarajñānaṃ prayojakamiti tatsarvaṃ saṅgruhītam | evaṃ puruṣārthopapādito veditavya ityāha-- ya iti |
ya evaṃ veda || 35 ||
evamuktaprakāreṇa jñātavya ityarthaḥ |
etāvatā nivartyanivartakajñānaṃ jātam | tāvatā uttamapuruṣārtharūpaphalaṃ kathaṃ labhyate ityata āha-- seti |
sa cirāyurbhavati || 36 ||
nanu nivartakā loke bahavassanti | ekadā sarvaviṣayakapadārthajñānaṃ sampādanīyaṃ sarvaśarīrāṇāṃ nivartakajñānaṃ paramapuruṣārtha ityarthaḥ | etatpadārthajñānānantaraṃ jñānaviṣayakakāryakāraṇacirakālajīvitasya puruṣārtha upalabhyate |
etajjñānaviṣayakajñānameva bheṣajaṃ bhavatītyāha-- taditi |
tadeva bheṣajam || 37 ||
bheṣajajñānānantaraṃ cikitsākarma vyācaṣṭe-- seti |
saiva cikitsā || 38 ||
narāśvagajādiśarīrāṇāṃ nānāvidharogāssambhavanti | tannivartakāni vṛkṣalatādiyogyadravyāṇi ṣaḍrasātmakapadārthopakārakāṇi | āyurvedapratipāditārthamālokanaṃ kuryādityāha-- tāvaditi |
tāvaddravyāvalokanaṃ kuryāt || 39 ||
doṣaprakopābhāvakāryahetubhūtadhātudūṣakayāvadviṣayagocarendriyābhivardhakadravyāvalokanaṃ kuryāt |
ṣaḍrasātmakadravyāṇi vyācaṣṭe-- rasā iti |
rasāḥ svādvamlalavaṇatiktoṣaṇakaṣāyakāḥ tatparitrāṇakāḥ || 40 || |
dehasya saptadhātumayatvāt te rasāssaptadhātūnyathākramaṃ paripālayanti | tena sapta dhātavaḥ pratyahaṃ trāyanta iti tatparitrāṇakāḥ | tatra vacanaṃ--
te rasā nu rasato rasabhedāḥ
tāratamyaparakalpanayā ca |
sambhavanti gaṇanāṃ samatītāḥ
doṣabheṣajavaśādupayojyāḥ ||
tatparitrāṇakāḥ śarīrāsthitadoṣānsamāsantaḥ śarīrasaṃrakṣakā rasāḥ | rasādīnāṃ prakoparūpakārthakāraṇatvaṃ vaktumayogyatvāt tadarthaṃ doṣā eva suprasiddhāḥ | atastrayāṇāṃ ṣaḍbhissākaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ prasiddha ityata āha-- syādviti ||
svādvamlakaṭukāḥ pāyā rasāḥ || 41 ||
yathā samadoṣā yathāgnipravartakāḥ || 42 ||
te rasāḥ pāke svādvamlakaṭukā bhavanti | rasāssamāśceddoṣā api samā eva | te doṣā rasānvayādinā prakoparahitāssantaḥ jaṭharāgnipravartakā ityarthaḥ | atra sūtravacanaṃ
tridhā vipāko dravyasya svādvamlakaṭukātmakaḥ |
ṣaḍrasānāṃ pāke tridhā vibhāvitvaṃ pratipāditam | nanu ṣaṇṇāṃ madhye tridhā vibhāvitvaṃ doṣatrayāṇāmeva ṣaḍbhissākaṃ kāryakāraṇatvaṃ bhāsate | tadanyatrayāṇāṃ kā gatirityata āha-- siddhā iti |
siddhāṣṣaṭpāke trayaḥ || 43 ||
ṣaḍrasāstridhā bhūtvā doṣāṇāmaprakopakārakā iti pratipāditam | tadeva viśinaṣṭi-- tikteti. |
tiktassvādu pāke || 44 ||
tiktarasadravyaṃ pācakapittena pāke kṛte sati svādutvaṃ bhajati |
kaṣāyaraso'pi tadrasavadamlībhūto bhavan kaphapittaprakopaṃ haratītyāha--kaṣāyeti |
kaṣāyo'mlarasaḥ || 45 ||
kaṣāya amlo bhūtvā pāke anyonyadravyasaṃyogaguṇān prayacchati | kaphapittaprakopasya kaṣāyayuktāmlarasena sākaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvo bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
ūṣaṇaraso'pi pāke lavaṇaraso bhūtvā kaphapavanaprakopakāryasya lavaṇoṣaṇarasau kāraṇībhūtau | tannivartakatvena kāryakāraṇabhāvo bhavedityāha-- ūṣaṇamiti |
ūṣaṇaṃ lavaṇaḥ pāke || 46 ||
ūṣaṇaraso lavaṇadravyaṃ bhūtvā pavanakaphahārītyarthaḥ |
nanu madhurarasādiṣaḍrasāḥ saptadhātupoṣakāḥ | kaṣāyaraso sasāsṛgdhātupravardhakaḥ | asṛgdhātoreva rasasṛgiti vyapadeśaḥ | kaṣāyarasapoṣya ekadhāturbhavati | tasmādekarasapravartakatvena poṣakatve karmaṇi ekadhāturiti vyapadeśaḥ | māṃsadhātūnāṃ ṣaḍrasā eva poṣakā iti vyavahriyamāṇatvāt, loke ṣaḍrasavaddravyavyatirekeṇa rasavaddravsya adṛṣṭatvāt ṣaḍrasavaddravyābhāvasya dṛṣṭānubhavatvācca |
atrarogotpādakapadārthānāmadṛṣṭatvena tu utpādakadravyāṇāmasadbhāvitatvena tadroganivṛttyarthaṃ śāstrapravṛttiḥ kathaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- vyutkrama iti |
vyutkramarasā vikārakārakāḥ || 47 ||
svādutiktayormitratvam | kaṣāyāmlarasayormitratvam | lavaṇoṣaṇarasayormitratvam | etadvyatiriktānāṃ etadvyatiriktapadārthairanyonyayoge vyutkramarasayossattvam | tasmādvyutkramarasāveva kārakāvityarthaḥ | tasmātkarmādhīnavaśātpāke sambhavati tatra adṛṣṭameva niyāmakam | īśvarassarvajñassa eva svakarmānukūlaphalaṃ prayacchati | pratyahaṃ sarvarasādanatvasya sarvaśarīrāṇāṃ sambhāvitatvāt | ataḥ paramāptaṃ paṇḍitaṃ svanikaṭe saṃsthāpya bhakṣyābhakṣyapadārthau vibhajya tatsarvaṃ paṇḍitāya vijñāpayan tena arogakāryakāraṇabhāvaṃ jānan pravarteta | tatra vacanaṃ–
rājā rājagṛhāsanne prāṇācāryaṃ niveśayet |
sarvadā sa bhavatyevaṃ sarvatra pratijāgṛviḥ ||
vyutkramarasavaddravyādanameva viṣamānnam | tasmādarakṣaṇīyaṃ na kiṃcit | | jaṭharāgninā pacyate |
nanu aharaharbhakṣitānnaṃ jaṭharāgninā pacyate | tatkṛtapacanena bhakṣitānnaṃ bhasmībhavati | sarvaṃ sārakiṭṭatayā vibhajya tena dhātupoṣaṇaṃ kathaṃ kriyata ityata āha-- pācaketi |
pācakapittaṃ pacatyāhāram || 48 ||
jaṭharāgninā bhakṣitānnaṃ na pacyate | tat pācakapittena bhāvyam | tena sārakiṭṭatayā vibhajya kiṭṭakarma kartuṃ yogyamityarthaḥ | tatra vacanaṃ-
pittaṃ pañcātmakaṃ tatra pakvāmāśayamadhyagam ||
pañcabhūtātmakatve'pi yattaijasaguṇodayāt ||
nanu pācakapittaṃ na sārakiṭṭatayā pṛthagvibhajya annamātraṃ pacati | doṣā duṣṭānsantaḥ dhātūndūṣayanti | tenāvaśyaṃ doṣapacanamapi kartavyam | tatra vacanaṃ--
āhāraṃ pacati śikhī doṣānāharavarjitānpacati |
doṣakṣaye ca dhātūnpacati hi dhātukṣaye'pi ca prāṇān ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa pācakopakārakatvena jaṭharānala eva pratibhāti na tatpittamityasvarasādāha— doṣeti |
doṣadhātumaloṣmajaṃ tathā || 49 ||
dhātudoṣānmaloṣmajān pittatadvikāraṃ vibhajya pacatītyarthaḥ | nanu doṣapacanaṃ roganivṛttikāraṇam | pācakapittena āmāntalaṅghanena saṃśoṣya doṣasthitavikārānpacati | tatra anilānalātmakakuṇḍalitābhyāṃ jātoṣmaiva pācakapittamiti vyapadeśaḥ | anyathā śuddhānalasyaiveti nāśaṅkanīyam | taddvaṃdvaṃ doṣe hṛte tadviparītaguṇānprayacchatītyāśayavānāha-- grahaṇīti |
grahaṇīkalā''yustejāṃsi dadhāti || 50 ||
bāhyasthitapadārthānāṃ guṇānsaṅgṛhya doṣadhātusthitadurguṇānnigṛhya nigrahaṃ kṛtvā svārjitaguṇānugrahaṃ kartuṃ samarthaṃ sat pācakapittam | nigrahānugrahayoḥ pācakapittasyaiva samarthatvāt | pittakalāyāḥ grahaṇīpadaṃ viśeṣitam | sā kalā āyustejāṃsi dadhāti | atra śārīravacanaṃ--
annabhautikadhātvagnikarmeti paribhāṣitam |
annasya paktā sarveṣāṃ paktṝṇāmadhiko mataḥ ||
tanmūlāste hi tadvṛddhikṣayavṛddhikṣayātmakāḥ |
tasmāttaṃ vidhivadyuktairannapānaudanairhitaiḥ |
pālayetprayatastasya sthitau hyāyurbalasthitiḥ ||
ityādivacanāni bahūni santi | kuṇḍalyutthitaśvāsānilānalacakrajātapittodbhavatvādeva kalājvalane śikhipadaṃ prasiddham | kuṇḍalinī analānilātmikā | tasyāḥ pittamastīti sakalamatasiddham | sā kalā jvalanakalā | sā dehaṃ dahatīti yattadayuktam | yāvadbhuktānnaṃ sārakiṭṭatayā vibhajya dandahyate | annapacanakāryamātraparvatvena kuṇḍalinyudbhavaśvāsānilasyaiva dahanarūpakarmapratipādakatvam | tatsarvaśarīrāntassthitaṃ sat jīvanopakāraṃ karotītyarthaḥ |
nanu grahaṇīkalā āyustejo dadhātīti yaduktaṃ tanna yuktam | hitāmitānnādanameva śarīradārḍhyarūpadhāraṇaṃ ca karoti | tadyathā pathyāpathyavidhiṃ vijñāya annapānādane sati tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiśca bhavati | tadabhāve kārśyaṃ pratīyate | tasmādetayoreva anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ | annādanasya nigrāhake roge sati sa eva taditi cet tailādinā dehalepanadvārā dhātupuṣṭimindriyapradāṃ kuryādityasvarasādāha–- bhaktamiti. |
bhaktādanenāmāśayātirodhanāt kalā'balā bhavati || 51 ||
bhaktamannam | annādanenāmāśaye'tipūrṇe sati kuṇḍalinīsthitapittakalā abalā bhavantī satī annādanājjātapākakṛtajanyarasena dhātuṃ vyāpakaṃ kartuṃ asamarthā bhavatīti kṛtvā tasmātkalā abalā bhavatītyarthaḥ | tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
miśraṃ pathyamapathyaṃ ca bhuktaṃ samaśanaṃ matam |
vidyādadhyaśanaṃ bhūyo bhuktasyopari bhojanam ||
akāle bahu cālpaṃ vā bhuktaṃ tu viṣamāśanam|
trīṇyapyetāni mṛtyuṃ vā ghorānvyādhīnsṛjanti vā ||
evamādikāraṇena kalā abalā bhavatītyarthaḥ | tadeva viruddhāhāra ityucyate | kalā abalā satī annaṃ na pacati | sa rogo'ṣṭaprakāro bhavati | nānāvidhakāryotpādakasāmagrībhedena ajīrṇapradhānāṅgatvena jvaropadravāḥ tadbhedā bhavantītyāha-- āmeti |
āmāśayātipūrṇāt kalā'balādāmāśayasthitapavanagatitirodhānādanilaprakopo hi bhavati ||
kalā daurbalyāt "ajīrṇena vinā na jvaraḥ" iti jvarāḥ āvirbhavantītyarthaḥ | atra nidānavacanaṃ--
sa jāyate'ṣṭadhā doṣaiḥ pṛthaṅmiśraissamāgataiḥ |
āgantuśca malāstatra svaissvairduṣṭāḥ pradūṣaṇaiḥ ||
āmāśayaṃ praviśyāmamanugamya pidhāya ca |
srotrāṃsi paṅktisthānācca nirasya jvalanaṃ bahiḥ ||
saha tenābhisarpantastapantassakalaṃ vapuḥ |
kurvanto gātramatyuṣṇaṃ jvaraṃ nirvartayanti te||
sa jvara eko'pi jvaropadravakārakahetavo nānārūpā bhavanti | tasmāt narāśvapaśugajādiṣu bahurūpā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tattajjātividhivihitānnādanādajīrṇājjātajvarāḥ tadupadravabhedena aṣṭadhā bhavantītyarthaḥ | ajīrṇājjātajvarastu aṣṭadhā upahūyata iti yattadayuktam, bhedajanakasāmagryabhāvādityasvarasādāha-- yāddaśā iti |
yādṛśā doṣāstādṛśānilaprakopajātānalāstādṛśā jvarāḥ || 53 ||
ye doṣaprakopahetukāstadviruddharasajātāstajjanyadoṣāḥ pācakapittādipañcaprakārāḥ te svasthānacyutā bahuvidharūpā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tatra vacanaṃ--
vividhairnāmabhiḥ krūro nānāyoniṣu vartate ||
tadviruddhalakṣaṇajñāpakahetubhedapradarśanena satyavacchedake jñāte sati eka eva jvaraḥ kāryabhedena nānārūpo bhavatīti vaktuṃ śakyate | tallakṣaṇaṃ jñātaṃ sat tattadbhedo'pi jñātuṃ śakyata ityasvarasādāha-- akṣīti |
akṣikarṇanāsāśirorugvidāhajihvāsyaśoṣakukṣyo jvalayan jvalati || 54 ||
akṣikarṇānāsādiṣu, tathā śirovyathā, jihvāyā vidāhaḥ, āsyaśoṣaḥ | kukṣya iti antastāpaḥ | āpādamaskaṃ jvalanāt jvalati sa jvara ityarthaḥ | tatra nidānavacanaṃ--
tasya prāgrūpamālasyamaratirgātragauravam |
āsyavairasyamarucirjṛmbhā sāsrākulākṣatā ||
aṅgāmardo'vipāko'lpaprāṇatā bahunidratā |
romaharṣo vinamanaṃ piṇḍikodveṣṭanaṃ klamaḥ ||
hitopadeśeṣvakṣāntiḥ prītiramlaṣaṭūṣaṇe |
dveṣassvāduṣu bhakṣyeṣu tathā bāleṣu tṛḍbhṛśam |
śabdāgniśītavātāmbucchāyoṣṇeṣvanimittataḥ |
icchā dveṣaśca tadanu jvarasya vyaktatā bhavet ||
viruddharasādanājjātajvarasyaitādṛśalakṣaṇalakṣitve jñātavya ityarthaḥ |
ekahetukṛtakāryasyaikatve'pi tattaddoṣaprakopakārakarasavirasajanyāmavṛddhibhedajanyaupādhikajvaraṃ vyapadiśati-- aṅgeti |
aṅgapīḍanamalamārgātirodhanātipīḍanadhātumārgavigamanānilādanalasthalacalanādinā'nilajvaraḥ ||
sarvāṅgānāmatipīḍanaṃ karoti | sarvamalamārgānatirudhya pīḍanaṃ karoti | dhātumārgagamananirodhanābhyāṃ cāratijāḍyādirvikāraṃ karotītyarthaḥ | atra nidānavacanaṃ--
āgamāpagamakṣobhamṛdutāvedanoṣmaṇām |
vaiṣamyaṃ tatra tatrāṅge tāstāssyurvedanāścalāḥ ||
pādayossuptatā stambhaḥ pīṇḍikodveṣṭanaṃ śramaḥ |
viśeṣa iva sandhīnāṃ sāda ūrvoḥ kaṭograhaḥ ||
pṛṣṭhaṃ kṣodamivāpnoti niṣpīḍyata ivodaram |
chindyanta iva cāsthīni pārśvāṅgāni viśeṣataḥ ||
hṛdayasya grahastodaḥ prājanenaiva vakṣasaḥ |
skandhayormathanaṃ bāhvoḥ bhedaḥ pīḍanamaṃsayoḥ ||
aśaktirbhakṣaṇe hanvoḥ jṛmbhaṇaṃ kaṇayossvanaḥ |
nistodaśśaṅkhayormūrdhni vedanā virasāsyatā ||
kaṣāyāsyatvamathavā malānāmapravartanam |
rūkṣāruṇatvagāsyākṣinakhamūtrapurīṣatā ||
prasekārocakāśraddhā vipākāḥ svedajāgarāḥ |
kaṇṭhoṣṭhaśoṣastṛṭśuṣkau chardikāsau viṣāditā ||
harṣo romāṅgadanteṣu vepathuḥ kṣavathoḥ grahaḥ |
bhramaḥ pralāpo gharmecchā vināmaścānilajvare ||
pavanapitte aṅgapīḍanaṃ sarvāṅgeṣu prapīḍanam | malamārgātirodhanaṃ sarvamaladvārāṇāṃ vāyupūritatvānmalamūtrādikāryakāraṇaṃ duśśakaṃ ca bhavatītyarthaḥ | dhātucaramārgavigamanaṃ viruddhagamanaṃ rogahetukam | anilādanilasthalacalanaṃ jaṭharānalasthalacalanaṃ bhavati | lagnasthalacalanatvāt āhārapacanaṃ kartumaśakyatvādityarthaḥ | tatra doṣaprakopahetukarasavirasādanādajīrṇe jāte doṣabhedena jvarāstrividhā bhavantīti vaktuṃ śakyate | sarvasāmānyajvara ekaḥ tadantaradoṣabhedena tridhā bhūtvā jvaraścaturvidho jātaḥ | itaḥ paramaṣṭajvarasaṅkhyāpūraṇārthaṃ pañcamo vātapittajvara iti vaktuṃ na śakyate | etatpratipāditajvaravyatirekeṇa tadutpādakasāmagryabhāvādeva tatkāryaṃ nopapadyate, kāraṇena vinā kāryābhāvasyoktatvādityasvarasādāha-- pavaneti |
pavanapittahetukarasavirasajātājīrṇajanyāmarasāsṛgdhātucarādyadoṣa ubhayalakṣaṇayuktarogaḥ pavanapittaprakopajvaraḥ || 56 ||
nanu pavanapittajvaro'stīti sūtre astītyasya vyākhyānaṃ gamyate | sūtravyākhyānamanyathā kāryaṃ nidānaśāstre'dṛṣṭatvāditi sūtrārthe'rucirna pratipāditavyā | pavanapittajvaraḥ, pavanaśleṣmajvaraḥ, pittaśleṣmajvara iti dvandvajāni trīṇi bhāsante | tathā sati āgantukābhighātajvara iti jvarasyāṣṭarūpatvaṃ caivamākareṇa subodhitamityarthaḥ | nidāne vātapittapradhānadvandvajvarasya tallakṣaṇapratīteḥ abhāvāt | kintu pavanakaphajvara ekaḥ, śleṣmapittajvaraḥ, āgantukābhighātajvara ityaṣṭadhā jvarabhedaḥ pratipāditaḥ | āyurvede pavanapittajvaro dvandva iti pratipāditaḥ | parabrahmaṇā pratipāditārthassāmānyajvaraḥ, tridoṣotthitā jvarāstrayaḥ, tridoṣadvandvajāstrayaḥ, ityaṣṭarūpo bhavati jvara ityāyurvedavacanatātparyam | pavanabhūtadravyasya itaradhātubhyāṃ sākaṃ yogagrahaṇasya aupayogikatvāt |
................pavanastaditarayoḥ prabhuḥ |
vibhutvādāśukāritvādbalitvādanyakopanāt |
"svātantryapāratantryābhyāṃ vyādheḥ prādhānyamādiśet ||"
tasmāt dvandvajāni trirūpāṇi bhavantītyarthaḥ | tatrāpi pavanasya dvābhyāṃ sākaṃ dvirūpadānaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | kaphapittayoḥ laghutvāttādṛśabalaṃ nāstīsyarthaḥ | tatra vacanaṃ--
sarvākāraṃ rasādīnāṃ śuddhyā'śuddhyā'pi vā kramāt |
vātapittakaphaissapta daśa dvādaśa vāsarān |
prāyā'nuyāti maryādāṃ mokṣāya ca vadhāya ca ||
iti vacanānusāreṇa sāmānyato dvandvajāstrayaśca, āgantukena sākamaṣṭaprakāro bhavatīti tātparyam | sāmānyajvarasya yāvadajīrṇābhāvakālasya tattadvimocanahetukatvāt | ekaikadhātuprādhānyaṃ sapta dinānantaraṃ vimucyate | pavanapittapavanakapadvandvajānāṃ daśa dvādaśa dināni avadhiḥ | kaphapittajvarasya dinamavadhiḥ | pavanapittapavanakaphayoḥ daśadinānyavadhiḥ | tāvanmātreṇa jvaro nivartate | rasādīnāṃ dhātvākāraṃ cet nivṛttirbhavati | rasādayo virasāssantaḥ dhātucarāścet pūrvoktakālānanatikramya śarīraṃ vimuñcantītyarthaḥ |
evaṃ--
pavanakaphavikārahetukarasavirasayājātājīrṇajanyāmayamāṃsamedodhātucara ubhayalakṣaṇasahitaḥ pavanakaphavikārajātajvaraḥ || 57 ||
etasyāpi pūrvoktarītyā vyākhyānaṃ kartavyamiti | dvandvodoṣāntaramāha-- kaphapitteti |
kaphapittavikārahetukarasavirasajātājīrṇajanyāmayāsthimajjādhātucarādyadoṣaścobhayalakṣaṇayuktorogaḥ kaphapittavikārajātajvaraḥ || 58 ||
tattatkālahetukavikārā bhavantītyarthaḥ |
nanu aṣṭavidhajvarāṇāṃ saṅkhyāpūraṇaṃ, doṣatrayotpāditajvarāstrayaḥ, tattaddvandvadoṣajātajvarāstrayaḥ | kevalājīrṇajātajvara ekaḥ | sarve'pi jvarā rasavirasajātājīrṇajanyā eva | ajīṇābhāvajvarābhāvayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvo vaktuṃ śakyate | ajīrṇabhāvasya ca jvarasya ca vyāpterdṛṣṭatvāt | "nājīrṇena vinā jvaraḥ" iti niyāmakaśāstrasya vidyamānatvāt | tadvyatirekeṇa āgantukajvarā bhāvatvena tena sākaṃ aṣṭavidhaprakāratvaṃ nopapadyata ityasvarasādāha-- mana iti |
manaḥkāmabhayabhūtābhighātakālahetūtpannajvarasyāntarvidāhaśvāsakhāsamadabhramamūrchācchardyatisāraśokakrodhabhayaguṇāstannivartakanivartakā āgantukajvarāḥ || 59 ||
mano'bhighātajvaro mānasikajvaraḥ | manodārḍhyasampādanameva tatra bheṣajam | kāmaḥ-- yāvatprītiviṣayakamanobhāvahetūtpannajvaraḥ | yāvadarthānabhibhūtatadanyānubhavanityajñānaviṣayaviparītajñānajanyajvaro bhayajātajvaraḥ | evaṃ bhūtajvarasya abhighātajvaratvam | te kālasvabhāvajātajvarāḥ eva | tādṛśajvarā bahavassanti | tasmādāgantukajvarasyāpi sannidhānapūrvakatvādaṣṭasaṅkhyāpūraṇaṃ sūtreṇa kṛtamityarthaḥ | tatra nidānavacanaṃ--
varṣāśaradvasanteṣu vātādyaiḥ prākṛtaḥ kramāt |
vaikṛto'nyassa dussādhyaḥ prāyaśca prākṛto'nilāt ||
varṣāsu māruto duṣṭaḥ pittaśleṣmānvito jvaram |
kuryātpittaṃ ca śaradi tasya cānuvalaṃ kaphaḥ ||
tatprakṛtyā visargācca tatra nānaśanādbhayam |
kapho vasante tamapi vātapittaṃ bhavedanu ||
balavatsvalpadoṣeṣu jvarassādhyo'nupadravaḥ |
sarvathā vikṛtijñāne prāgasādhya udāhṛtaḥ ||
ityaṣṭavidhajvaralakṣaṇāni vyapadiṣṭānītyarthaḥ | śleṣmapittādidvandvajvararūpadoṣāṇāṃ etaddhetubhūtavirasajanyarasā rogā asthimajjādhātuparyantaṃ uktadoṣaiḥ sākaṃ dṛḍhatarasañcāreṇa vadhotpādakalakṣaṇasāmagrīsahitaiḥ doṣāḥ prāṇaharaṇaṃ kartuṃ yogyā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tatra grahaṇī yadā abalā bhavati tadā rasāḥ pāke virasā bhūtvā tattaddhetukatattadrogān kuryuḥ | kalābalābhāvakāryahetvanimittakāryakāraṇameva tatra bheṣajam | ādoṣapacanaparyantaṃ laṅghanaṃ kāryamiti āmahetukakāryābhāvahetujanyāmayānāṃ āmābhāvaparipālanaṃ nivartakamiti nopadyate āmahetukakāryabhāvāt | atra ko'yaṃ niyāmaka ityasvarasādāha-- pavaneti |
pavanādyaprakopādagnibalaṃ poṣayankriyākramaḥ || 60 ||
nirvartakairauṣadhādyaiḥ vātapittakaphā aprakupitāḥ bhavanti | tairagnibalaṃ poṣayan yāvatkarma vidhīyate, sa eva kriyā kramaḥ |
laṅghanaiḥ kṣapite doṣe dīpte'gnau lāghave sati |
svāsthyaṃ kṣuttṛḍruciḥ paktirbalamojaśca jāyate |
iti vacanam | atra doṣaprakopakāryābhāvakarmakaraṇaṃ ca agniprajvalanakāryaparipālanaṃ ca kriyākramo bhavati |
yaduktaṃ tadeva pratipādayati-- pathyeti |
pathyāddoṣe kṣīṇe'gnibale jāte kṣuttyaḍruciśśaktiḥ balakālavibhāgajhovamanavirekācchvāsakhāsahṛdrogaviṣamajvarātisārebhyo'bhayātsukhībhavet || 61 ||
hitamitapathyādanāddoṣe kṣīṇe doṣaprakopakāryābhāvakaraṇe tasmādagnerbalaṃ kartuṃ dṛḍhatarasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ kṣuttṛḍruciśśaktiḥ | dhātukṣamo yadi bhavettadanantaraṃ vamanavirekakarmakaraṇena śvāsakhāsahṛdrogaviṣamajvarebhyaḥ bhayarahitassan sukhī bhavet | tasya cirāyuṣṭvaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu idamapyanupapannam | pathyānnādanātpūrṇe sati dhātavaḥ puṣṇāntīti yattadayuktam | annādanasya ca tajjātājīrṇasya ca kāryakāraṇabhāvo vaktuṃ śakyaḥ | tasmātsārakiṭṭatayā pāke kṛtakāryakaraṇena analena āmāśayayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvo vaktuṃ śakyatvādityata āha-- āmeti |
āmāśayasthitaṃ sarvadhātubalakārakam || 62 ||
annapānādanājjātāhāro dhātūnyathāyathaṃ kurvan santarpayan sāraṃ visṛjya tenāmāśayamapūrayati | tena pūrṇe sa dhātūnpuṣṇātītyarthaḥ | atra sūtravacanam--
prasṛṣṭe viṇmūtre hṛdi suvimale doṣe svapathage |
viśuddhe codgāre kṣudupagamane vāte'nusarati ||
tathāgnāvudrikte viśadakaraṇe dehe ca sulaghau |
prayuñjītāhāraṃ vidhiniyamitaḥ kālassa hi mataḥ ||
āmāśayātipūraṇāyāhāraṃ vidhiniyamitaṃ kālahitaṃ prayuñjīta |
nanu ṣaḍrasadravyādanātpāke tadrasān dhātuṣu saṃsthāpya dhātavastuṣṭāssantaḥ tena virasadraveṇāmāśayaḥ pūryatām | tāvatā pācakapittakalāyāḥ abalatvena kāryakaratvāsambhavāt tayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ kathaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- samāneti |
samānānilena tatsthān kalā jvalayan pacati || 63 ||
samānavāyunā'nale prajvalite sati sā kalā sarvaṃ bhuktaṃ pacati | dhātudehaśoṣakakāryasya ca āmāśayasya ca kāryakāraṇabhāvasya ca vaktuṃ sukaratvāditi bhāvaḥ | atra sūtravacanam--
samāno'gnisamīpasthakoṣṭhe carati sarvataḥ |
anugṛhṇāti pacati vivecayati muñcati ||
rase koṣṭhe carati sati evamākāreṇa kalāyāḥ abalatvaṃ sa vimocayedityarthaḥ |
nanu abalāgninā āmāśayasthitamannaṃ paktuṃ asāmarthye sati atassamānānilena sanbukṣayantīti yaduktaṃ tadasaṅgataṃ, atyaśanājjātājīrṇasya tannivartakena vinā śuddhasamānānilena āmāśayasthitānnaṃ, analaprajvalanībhūtadravyādanaṃ vinā grahaṇīkalāyāḥ abalatvamapahartuṃ śakyate | ata āha-- duṣṭeti |
duṣṭagrahaṇī rogasya kārikā || 64 ||
pratyuta grahaṇīkalā duṣṭā satī rogakārikā bhavatītyarthaḥ |
āmenānnena duṣṭena tadevāviśya kurvate |
viṣṭambhayanto'lasakaṃ cyāvayanto viṣūcikām ||
iti sūtravacanam | viruddhānnādanena grahaṇīkalā duṣṭā satī bhuktānnaṃ saṅgrāhyadhātupoṣaṇaṃ kartumasamarthā | grahaṇīkalāyā bhāvyakāryasya ajīrṇajātāmayasya ca hetumattayā kāryakāraṇabhāvatvena duṣṭagrahaṇyā rogakārakatvaṃ siddhamityarthaḥ |
nanu mandānalaprajvalanaṃ jaṭharānalena bhavitavyam | atra bahiranalasyāyogyatvāt samānānilena bhavitavyamabhivyañjanamiti yattatkevalaṃ nivartakadravyādanaṃ vinā na samartho'yamiti matvā nivartakadravyamāha-- draveti |
dravadravyairvibhajankālādannaṃ prāṇānilena koṣṭhaṃ gataṃ samānānilena tatsthā kalā jvalayantī pacati || 65 ||
vastutastu dravadravyaissākaṃ yo'nnamatti sa vyañjānāni dravībhūtāni kṛtvā bhunakti | tadbhuktānnaṃ agnisamīpasthitasamānānilena paktuṃ prajvalitena sārakiṭṭatayā vibhajatānnaṃ hṛdgataṃ sat prāṇānilena āhārakoṣṭhaṃ gataṃ karoti, tadanantaraṃ nābhipradeśagataṃ kārayati | tasmātsā kalā atiprajvalitā satī dhātupoṣakaguṇaṃ na dhatte | pratyuta dhātuśoṣaṇaṃ ca karoti | grahaṇīkalā atiprajvalitā satī dhātudūṣaṇaṃ karoti | tasmātsā kalā dāhāvaśeṣaṃ pacati | dhātukṣayakārikā'pi bhavati | evamāśayāṃśca pacet | tadapyajīrṇamiti vyavaharati | tena dhātupoṣakatvasya patitatvāt tatpacanaṃ anagnikṛtīmiti yattanna rocata ityarthaḥ | atra vacanaṃ--
cayādīn yānti sadyo'pi doṣāḥ kāye'pi vā na vā |
vyāpnoti sahasā dehamāpādatalamastakam ||
nivartate tu kupito malo'lpālpaṃ jalaughavat |
nānārūpairasaṃkhyeyaiḥ vikārāḥ kupitā malāḥ |
nāpayanti tanuṃ tasmāttaddhetvākṛti sādhanam ||
nanu dravadravyādanena yāvaddhātutṛpti bhojanaṃ kṛtaṃ cet pañcavidhapācakapittamadhye atyagninā pacati cet tatpācitānnaṃ dhātupradaṃ na bhavati | tatsāvaśeṣapacanamapi na kriyate | tasmādāśayā riktībhūtāssantaḥ tadvadajīrṇavadannaprakopaṃ kurvantītyasvarasādāha-- rikta iti |
rikte vāyuḥ prakupyate || 66 ||
tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
balavatyabalātvannamāmameva vimuñcati |
grahaṇyā balamagnirhi sa cā'pi grahaṇībalaḥ ||
dūṣite'gnāvato duṣṭā grahaṇī rogakāraṇā |
yadannaṃ doṣadhātvojobalavarṇādvipoṣaṇam ||
iti śārīravacanam | atyagninā dravībhūtatvena kālādannamapi sāvaśeṣābhāvapacanāt āśayā riktībhūtāḥ | teṣvāśayeṣu vāyuḥ pravardhate | anilasya rūkṣaguṇavaddravyatvāt | āśaye rikte sati vāyuḥ prakupyata ityarthaḥ | tatra rukṣo laghussitaḥ kharassūkṣmaścalo'nilaḥ | agniprajvalanahetubhūtendhanasaṃyoge sati jvalanaṃ bhavet | āśayānāṃ riktībhūtatvena analasya jvalanahetukadravadravyābhāvena grahaṇīkalāyāḥ svaprajvalanaguṇavattvena sthātumaśakyatvādityasvarasādāha-- analeti |
analo'lpo bhavati || 67 ||
vastutastu idamapyanupapannaṃ, āmāśaye rikte sati pavanaprakopakāryaṃ prati hetoradṛṣṭatvāt | anāmayakāryakāraṇatvaṃ anā mapālanatvam | tasyārogahetukatvāt | 'rikte vāyuḥ prakupyate' ityetatsūtraṃ vyarthaṃ syāt | grahaṇīkalāyāḥ samānānilasamīpasthitatvena jvalanahetukasāmagrīsatvāt | 'analo'lpo bhavati' iti sūtramapi vyarthaṃ syāditi cet, pañcaprāṇopakārakānnagrahaṇāddoṣopakārakāḥ | malamūtrādhikamapi viśeṣataḥ | āśaye rikte sati pavanaprakopahetutvaṃ yatra dṛśyate tatrāyaṃ niyamaḥ-- malamūtranirodhanājjātāmaye dṛṣṭe sati tannivartakadravyeṇa recite tena riktīkṛtāśaye yatra pavanaprakopahetutvaṃ tatra taddṛśyata iti tātparyam, tasya dṛṣṭatvāt | āmāśayasya riktatayā rūkṣatā vātasya | rūkṣaguṇatvaṃ prathamata upāttaṃ viruddharasādanājjātājīrṇaguṇahitāmayena sārakiṭṭatayā avibhajya pacanāt malamūtrādikaṃ dravībhūtamaprakṛtamiti pratibhāti | tatra siddhakalāyāḥ ajvalitatvājjātānalapacanāt jātāmaye dṛṣṭe sati tadviruddhacalakṛtāśayasya riktatayā doṣaprakopahetukatvaṃ syāt | tasmāt 'rikte vāyuḥ prakupyate' iti sūtraṃ samyakpratipāditamityarthaḥ | ajīrṇajātāmaye āmāśayariktatvājjātānalaprakopasya ca sandhukṣaṇāvaśyakatvena ādau pavanaprakoparahitakāryasya kāraṇībhūtarasadravyaṃ vyācaṣṭe-- madhurīti |
madhurībhūtābhyavahṛtānnaṃ pavanaprakopahārakam || 68 ||
tatsvabhāvasiddhamadhurarasena tattaddravyakarmopādhinā anilaprakopābhāvakāryasya yannivartakaṃ madhurībhūtaṃ kṛtvā tāvadbhuktānnaṃ pavanaprakopaṃ haratīti hārakam | āmāśayariktatākārye tatsthānaprakope sati yāvadbhuktānnaṃ paktuṃ agneranavakāśāt tannivartakasvādurasavaddravyamadyātprathamataḥ aśnīyādityarthaḥ | tadvadantyamadhurībhūtadravyamanilaprakopahārakamityarthaḥ | tatra
tridhā vipāko dravyasya svādvamlakaṭukātmakaḥ |
etadvacanānusāreṇa pāke madhurībhūtadravyaṃ doṣanivartakam, uta svatassthitasvādurasavaddravyaṃ vā | nādyaḥ, sakaladravyāṇi pāke tridhā vikārīkaroti | tannivartakadravyamastīti niyāmakābhāvāt | nāntyaḥ, svādurasavaddravyaṃ sakaladoṣānharatīti | tatra vacanaṃ--
takrādyā mārutaṃ ghnanti trayastiktādayaḥ kapham |
kaṣāyatiktamadhurāḥ pittamanye tu kurvate ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa ekasyeva niyāmakatvaṃ bhavatīti avacchedakatvābhāvāt | tasmādetadaikyamiti tanna | madhurībhūtavyañjanaissākaṃ prathamato'nnamaśnuyāt | tat hṛdgatamamlībhūtaṃ bhavet | tadā hṛdayaṃ praviśya tataḥ pakvāśayagataṃ bhavet | tataḥ kaṭuraso bhūtvā pacyate | tasmānmadhurībhūtadravyaṃ........ bhavati, tadvatpavanaprakopamapaharatīti matvā tasmādetatsūtraṃ sārthakamityarthaḥ | āmāśayariktatvameva pavanaprakopahetukaṃ, pittaprakopakāryasya ca hetoradṛṣṭatvāt | aharaharbhuktānnaṃ pacyamānaṃ sat doṣatrayanivartakatvena dhātunā poṣayatīti vaktavyam | tatra pittaprakopakāryasya ca hetoradṛṣṭatvāt aharaharbhuktānnaṃ pacyamānaṃ sat doṣatrayanivartanaṃ karotīti vaktavyam | tatra pittaprakopakāryasya ca taddhetoradṛṣṭatvāt | kāryajñānasya kāraṇajñānena vinā'prasakterityasvarasādāha-- hṛdgateti |
hṛdgatakaphaplutānnamamlībhūtaṃ pittaprakopanāśakam || 69 ||
asyārthaḥ-- āmāśayasya pavanaprakopahetutvaṃ pratipāditam | pittaprakopasyājīrṇotpattikāraṇatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyatvāt, tatkāryasya ca hetoradṛṣṭatvāt ityasvarasaṃ manasi nidhāya kaiścidvyākhyānaṃ kriyata ityasvarasāntaramāha-- hṛditi |
asyārthaḥ-- madhurībhūtārthān bhuktavataḥ udānānilena kaṇṭhaṃ praviśattat hṛdgataṃ sat kaphaplutaṃ saṃsargadoṣavaśāt amlībhūtaṃ bhavati | sa rasaḥ pittaprakopaṃ karoti | atra sūtraṃ--
āmlo'gnidīptikṛtsnigdhaḥ hṛdyaḥ pācanarocanaḥ |
uṣṇavīryo himasparśaḥ prīṇano bhedano laghuḥ |
karoti kaphapittāsraṃ mūḍhavātānulomanam ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa pittaprakopahetoradarśanāt pittanivartakaḥ svādurasaḥ pittaprakopaṃ haratītyarthaḥ | tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
"takrādyā mārutaṃ ghnanti" iti pratipāditam |
etadvacanānusāreṇa svādurasasya pittaprakoparāhityajanakatvaṃ pratipāditam | etatsūtravacanadvayasyārthassubodha ityarthaḥ |
nanu doṣaprakopanivartanadvārā dhātūnpuṣṇantau svādvamlarasau pavanaprakopaṃ kurutaḥ | tasmāttannivartako na bhavedityasvarasāddāha-- madhureti |
madhurāmlarasau kaphakarau || 70 ||
madhurarasaścāmlarasaścobhayamapi kaphaprakopaṃ datte | madhurarasaḥ pavanapittanivartakarasaḥ tattaddravyaṃ bhūtvā'ntaḥ praviśati | tasya tannivartanameva puruṣārthaḥ | tatkaphahetukamityucyate | tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasāḥ kaphanivartakāḥ | rasādiṣaṭkasya doṣanivartakatvam | rasādiṣu svādvamlarasau vātapittaprakopahetū bhavataḥ | tayoreva hi rṣeṣyat kaphakārakā iti | sa ca svādurasa aupādhikaraso'mlarasa ubhāvapi pacanapittaprakopakārakau | pratyuta pavanaprakopasyāpi hetū bhavataḥ | kathaṃ kaphanivartakau bhavataḥ ityasvarasādāha-- hṛditi |
hṛccyutāhāramanalaśoṣitamāmāśayasthaṃ kaphaṃ harati || 71 ||
prāśitāhāramanalaśoṣitamāmāśasthaṃ kaphaṃ harati | tadevānnaṃ prāśitaṃ yathākramamāśayādiṣu sthitvā sārakiṭṭādivibhajanaṃ kurvat yadannaṃ pacati āmāśayasthaṃ sat tadeva kaṭuraso bhavati | tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
tridhā vipāko dravyasya svādvamlakaṭukātmakaḥ |
etadvacanānusāreṇa svabhāvatassvāduraso'pi aupādhikāmlaraso bhūtvā pittamapi janayati | tadanantaraṃ amlarasasya kāryakāritvaṃ vaktavyam | āmāśayasthitassan kaṭuraso bhūtvā kaphanivartako bhavatītyarthaḥ | kaṭurasasya ca kaphanivartakatvaṃ suprasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ |
udvejayati jihvāgraṃ kurvaṃścimicimāṃ kaṭuḥ |
srāvayatyākṣināsāsyaṃ kapolau dahatīva ca ||
nanu ekameva dravyaṃ tridhā vibhajanaṃ karotīti sūtre pratipāditamityuktam, taccintyam | ādau svādurasasyaiva kāryakā ritvaṃ ca vaktavyam | tadanantaramamlarasasya kāryakāritvaṃ vaktavyam | kaṭurasasyāpi tathā kāryakāritvabhajanāt | tasmātsvādvamlakaṭurasānāmeva doṣasāmyaṃ pratipāditam, naiva rogamātranivartakasāmagrī prātipāditā iti | tatra sūtravacanam |
rogastu doṣavaiṣamyaṃ doṣasāmyamarogatā
etadvacanamatra pramāṇamiti trayāṇāṃ doṣāṇāṃ samatā yā sāmagrī dṛśyate sā sāmagrī tribhireva kāryā | tathā sati taditararasānāṃ kā gatirityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya taccintyamityuktam | tatra śārīravacanaṃ--
ādau ṣaḍrasamutpannaṃ madhurībhūtamīrayet |
phenībhūtaṃ kaphaṃ yātaṃ vidāhādamlatāṃ tataḥ ||
pittamāmāśayāt kuryāccyavamānaṃ cyutaṃ punaḥ |
agninā śoṣitaṃ pakvaṃ piṇḍitaṃ kaṭumārutam ||
ekarasavaddravyameva pāke tridhā vibhajanaṃ karotītyarthaḥ | sarvadhātupacanaṃ haratāṃ vātapittakaphadoṣāṇāṃ avikārakagatiṃ prāpayituṃ tatra bhojyadravyādanaṃ, teṣāṃ bahurasātmakatvāt | te rasāḥ tattadvidhicoditaphalāni prayacchantīti yān rasān yo bhoktamupalabhate tena upalabhyamānarasaṃ tadanubhavavaśāt phalaṃ pradīyate iti rasān vidyāt | tasmādbahurasadravyādanādeva tattadvidhicoditaphalaṃ prāpyata ityasvarasādāha-- svādviti |
svādvamlakaṭukāḥ pākyā rasā yathā samadoṣā yathāyathaṃ yogaphaladāyakāḥ || 72 ||
sarve rasāḥ pākena svādvamlakaṭukā bhūtvā yathārthasthitā rasā eva tattatphaladāyakā iti | samādhatte-- ya iti |
ye rasāstaddhetubhūtāstadudbhūtajātānalāstadudbhūtadhātupoṣakāḥ || 73 ||
asyārthaḥ-- taddhetukāryahetubhūtāstaddhetubhūtāḥ iti | tattadudbhūtarasādanakāryajātānalatvaṃ pācakapittameva | analapācitarasāḥ tattadbhūtahitadhātupoṣaṇaṃ kurvantītyetatsūtrārthaḥ | tatra vacanaṃ--
bhaumāpyāgneyavāyavyāḥ pañcoṣmāṇassanābhasāḥ |
pañcāhāraguṇānsvānsvānpārthivādīnpacantyanu ||
yathāsvaṃ te ca puṣṇanti pakvā bhūtaguṇānpṛthak |
pārthivāḥ pārthivāneva śeṣāśśeṣāṃśca dehagān ||
sarvaśarīravaddravyaṃ pañcabhūtātmakamiti tatpārthivabhūtādhikaṃ vyañjanānnaṃ bhoktumicchataśśarīrasya pārthivavāyavaṃ pupoṣati | abbhatādhikavyañjanānnaṃ bhoktumicchataśśarīrasya abbhūtādhikavṛddhiṃ karoti | evaṃ vahnibhūtādhikavyañjanānnaṃ bhoktumicchataśśarīrasya vahnibhūtāvayavavṛddhiṃ karoti | evamanilasya ca | evaṃ nābhaso'pyabhivṛddhiṃ karotītyarthaḥ | yadyaddravyānvito rasastattaddravyaguṇapradaḥ | pañcabhūtādhikadravyebhyaḥ tattaddravyābhivyañjanāvayavā abhivardhante | tasmāt rasādīnāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ kimāsīdityata āha-- raseti |
rasādraktam || 74 ||
raktadhātujanakarasāsṛgabhi vardhakakāryasya kaṣāyaraso heturbhavatītyarthaḥ | "raso hyasṛk," raso vai saḥ" | tasmādrasāsṛjoraikyena ṣaḍrasāssaptadhātupoṣakā iti | tatra śārīravacanaṃ--
rasādraktaṃ tato māṃsaṃ māṃsānmedastato'sthi ca |
asthno majjā tataśśukraṃ śukrādgarbhaḥ prajāyate ||
evaṃ saptadhātūnāṃ rasādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ ca kāryakāraṇabhāvo'stīti jñāpyate | rasavaddravyaṃ raktadhātujanakaṃ bhavatu, kaṣāyaraso raktadhātujanaka ityatra ko'yaṃ niyamaḥ ? doṣādīnāmarogakāryakāraṇatvaṃ pratipāditam | teṣāṃ paraṃparayā dhātupoṣaṇadvārā dhātujanakatvaṃ ca vivakṣitam | doṣadhātuvikārābhāvasya rasādīnāṃ ca kāryakāraṇabhāve jñāte sati dehārogyakāryasya rasānāmeva sattve suprasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | kaṣāyarasānnādanāt rasāsṛgdhāturedhate | ūṣaṇaraso māṃsadhātupravardhakaraktadhātūnsampoṣya heturbhavatītyarthaḥ | rasādraktaṃ, tasmānmāsaṃ māṃsānmedaḥ, medasosthi, asthno majjā, majjātaśśuklaṃ śuklādgarbho bhavati | abbhūtodbhavasvādurasaśśukladhātupoṣakaḥ | tadvirasastannāśako bhavati | pṛthivyudbhavo raso majjādhātupoṣako bhavati | tadvirasastannāśako bhavati | tejobhūtodbhavoṣaṇarasaḥ asthidhātupoṣakaḥ | tadvirasastannāśako bhavati | lavaṇamūṣaṇarasabhedaḥ | pavanabhūtodbhavastiktarasaḥ māṃsamedobhivardhakaḥ, medaso'pi māṃsarūpatvāt abhedo'tra vivakṣitaḥ | gaganabhūtodbhavakaṣāyarasaḥ rasāsṛgdhātupradaḥ rasāsṛjorekadhāturūpatvāt | tasmāccharīraṃ pāñcabhautikamiti vyapadiśati-- pañceti |
pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīram || 75 ||
pṛthagvyavasthitāḥ pañcabhūtāḥ asmin śarīre nopapadyante dehasya doṣadhātumalāśayātmakatvāt | ekaikasya mahādravyatvāt saṃsṛṣṭerabhāvāt | ekaikasya ca nāṃśatassaṃsṛṣṭiḥ | teṣāṃ vibhajanaṃ kartumaśakyatvāt | ekaikaśarīre tattallakṣaṇasahitadravyopalabdherabhāvāt | tasmātpañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti vacanaṃ na kṣodakṣamamityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya ādau gaganabhūtasya tatsaṃsṛṣṭilakṣaṇaṃ tatkāryaṃ ca vyapadiśati-- śrotreti |
śrotrāntassthitaśabdaguṇakabodhakasirābhyāṃ śrūyate || 76 ||
asyārthaḥ-- antastiṣṭhata ityantasthitau | tayorantassthitayoḥ śabdo guṇo yayostau śabdaguṇakau | bahuvrīhau kapratyayaḥ | upasthitayogyaviṣayasya bodhikābhyāṃ sirābhyāṃ śrūyate | tatra śārīravacanam--
ṣaḍaṅgamaṅgaṃ pratyaṅgaṃ tasyākṣihṛdayādikam |
śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaḥ kramādguṇāḥ |
khānilāgnyabbhuvāmekaguṇavṛddhyanvayaḥ pare ||
ekādaśaguṇavaddravyaṃ śarīre'nveti | gandhavadguṇabhūto nāsikāyāmupalabhyate | abbhūtaguṇalakṣaṇasya jihvāyāmupalabdhiḥ | taijasaguṇavaddravyaṃ akṣṇoḥ pratibhāti | tatra rūpopalaṃbhakajñānaṃ pramāṇam | pavanadravyaṃ sparśaguṇam | śabdaguṇavadākāśadravyam | karṇaśaṣkulyavacchinnākāśe śabdopalambhakatvāt | tasmātpañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti vākyaṃ suprasiddham | śabdaguṇavānākāśa ityākāśasya lakṣaṇaṃ prasiddhamityarthaḥ | śrotrāntassthitasirābhyāṃ śrūyate śabdaḥ | tāvata ākāśasya śarīrātmakatvaṃ kathaṃ saṅgacchate? ‘yatsatve yatsatvam; yadabhāve yadabhāvaḥ' iti nyāyena śabdagrāhakapratītaśabdagrahaṇaṃ anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ dehasyākāśātmakatvaṃ na sādhayati | tasmātpañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti vacanasya yaddūṣaṇaṃ dattaṃ tadaparihāryaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- śabda iti |
śabdaguṇaka ākāśaḥ || 77 ||
śabdo guṇo yasya sa śabdaguṇadravyamākāśaḥ | bahuvrīhau kapratyayaḥ | ākāśasya śabdaguṇakatvāt | dravyāntarasparśe sati tadviṣayakajñānaṃ jāyate | tadanyathānupapattyā tadvyāpakatvaṃ vāyoḥ pratītam | (ākāśavannirguṇadravyapratītatvāt) sparśaikavedyatvaṃ vāyoḥ pratīyate, sparśaguṇavānvāyuḥ ityupalambhakatvāt | sparśo guṇo yasya sa sparśaguṇavān | dravyāntarasaṃsarge sati tadviṣayakajñānaṃ jāyate | tadvyatiriktendriyāṇāṃ aviṣayatve sati tadviṣayakatvāt | tadadhīnajñānaviṣayakajñānaṃ tvagindriyajñānaviṣayakajñānapavanasya ekadeśaśarīravyāpakatvaṃ suprasiddham | rasāsṛgdhātujanyāṃ tvak | tadanyadhātvanādhārabhūtatvacaḥ śvāsocchvāsarūpapavanadhārakatvaṃ pratipādyate-- raseti |
rasāsṛgdhātujanyā tvak śvāsocchvāsavahā ||
saptadhātuttvagāvṛttānilo viyajjātaḥ || 79 ||
ubhayorapi dhātutvagvyāpyakāryaṃ prati ekaśarīrasya kāryakāraṇatvaṃ bhavati | etāvatā tvaṅmātrasya rasāsṛgdhātujanyatvaṃ sarvajātiśarīratvacāṃ vyāpyatvaṃ pratyapādītyarthaḥ | sa vāyurākāśajanyaḥ, "ākāśādvāyuḥ" iti pramāsiddhatvāt | nanu dehasya aniladhāraṇatvaṃ dāhapratibandhake'sati analadhāraṇatvaṃ ca vaktavyam | anyathā vahati nabhonilavat | anila dhāraṇāyogyatvāt | jaṭharāgnerannapacanakāryasya dāhapratibandhakābhāvakāryatvenādṛṣṭatvāt iti na vaktavyam, kuṇḍalīgatapittasya analavatkāryakarmakaraṇopayogikatvāt | tatpācakakarma grahaṇīkalā pacanarūpakāryaṃ karoti | tasmādanalātmakaṃ śarīramiti vaktumayogyatvādityasvarasādāha-- ṣaḍiti |
ṣaṭpañcāśatsirāvṛtākṣigatānalaḥ pañcarūpopapannamāhāraṃ pacan anilajātānalassvagatārthaṃ paśyati || 80 ||
asyārthaḥ--
ṣaṭpañcāśatsirāvṛte akṣiṇī yayostayorgatānalaḥ taijasarūpāgniḥ jvaladdravyasirāvṛtanetragolamadhyasthitatvāt jvālāvṛtadravyāvayavānāṃ dahanapratibandhakatvaṃ sampādya so'nalaḥ bhuktānnapacanārthaṃ grahaṇīkalā bhūtvā śarīraṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhati | atidūrasthamapi tatsajātīyadravyaṃ viṣayīkaroti | tasmānnetragolamadhyasthitassan yaḥ paśyati sa evānalassarvaśarīravyāpakaḥ | anilajātānalaḥ svagatārthaṃ anilajātānaladravyaṃ dūrasthamapi viṣayīkaroti | atidūrastho'pyanilaścakṣuṣā viṣayo gṛhyate | kuṇḍalīgatapittaṃ tu sarvabhuktānnapacanārtham | tasyāḥ kuṇḍalyā devatārūpatvāt | tatpittamapi taijasavadbhavati | cakṣurgocarārthaṃ cakṣurgatataijasaṃ dūrasthitamapyarthaṃ viṣayīkaroti | sā kalā sarvaṃ paktuṃ yogyā bhavatītyarthaḥ |
rasādibhedavibhajanārthaṃ rasanendriyaprakalpanam | tadudbhūtadravyaṃ tadvibhajanārtham | tadudbhūtavyatiriktasya tena grahītumaśakyatvāt, ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya rasanendriyasvarūpaṃ vivṛṇoti-- lāleti |
lālārūpo rasādibhedānvibhajan jihvayā rasaṃ gṛhṇāti || 81 ||
lālārūparasaḥ jihvāgravartī padārthāngṛhṇāti | bhuktānnavibhajanaṃ ca karotītyarthaḥ |
nanu rasādivibhajanaṃ grahaṇīkalā aduṣṭā satī karotīti sarvaṃ vidhivatpratīyate | sā aduṣṭā satyapi tadvibhajanajñānaṃ nānubhūyate, tadvibhajanajñānasya jaṭharāgneśca kāryakāraṇabhāvo gṛhyate cet jihvāgrasthitalālājalasya kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ kathaṃ gṛhyate? tadbhedavibhajanasya anyathā siddhatvāt | tasmādananyodbhūtātmakatvaṃ nopalabhyate ityasvarasādāha-- jihveti |
jihvāsaṃsthitadvisirādhārasarvarasābhijñā || 82 ||
analajātāblālārūparasanendriyasya rasasparśamātreṇa tattadvyañjanaṃ kṛtvā rasādayo'nubhūyante | anvayavyatirekapramāṇasiddhatvāt | dvisirādhārakatvaṃ sarvarasābhijñatvaṃ analajanyatvaṃ caitasyaiva pratīyate nānyasyetyarthaḥ | pṛthivīlakṣaṇaṃ gandhavattvam | gandhavatī pṛthivī sarvaśarīragandhopalaṃbhikā bhavati | śarīraṃ pārthivaṃ gandhaguṇasyopalabhyamānatvāt, yannaivaṃ, tannaivaṃ yathā vāyavyaśarīram | yatheti vāyulokanivāsināṃ dṛṣṭāntaḥ | asya tu śarīrastha pārthivāvayavatvena sarvagandha upalabhyate | tasmānna pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramityasvarasādāha-- caturiti | caturviṃśatisirā nāsikāgragatodbhavā pṛthivī caturviṃśatitatvabodhikā || 83 ||
sarvāṇi tatvāni nāsāgre pratibhānti | tasmātpārthivāvayavi śarīraṃ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- yatsireti |
yatsirāvṛtaṃ śarīram | annādbhūtāni jāyante, jātanyannena vardhante, adyate'tti ca bhūtāni, tasmādannaṃ taducyate || 84 ||
sarvāṇi śarīrāṇi pārthivāni | taditarāḥ āpo hyagnyanilākāśāḥ tatkāryopalambhakamātracaritārthatvāt | ato'nyāvayavaṃ śarīraṃ syādityāśaṅkate-- nāsiketi |
nāsikādhikyavikārabhūtāḥ nāsāgre pratīyante ||
tattanniṣṭhaguṇāni tāni caturviṃśatitatvarūpaguṇāni caturviṃśatisirādhārakāni | sarve'vayavā gandhavadguṇabhūyiṣṭhāḥ | tasmāt śarīraṃ pārthivamiti sarvajanasiddhamiti etatsirāvṛtaṃ śarīramiti vaktuṃ śakyate | doṣadhātumalāśayātmakaṃ tatsarvaṃ gandhopalabdhijñānapūrvakaṃ pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti sūtraṃ na kṣamaṃ, taddehātiriktadeśasthitaṃ śarīraṃ samavetya tiṣṭhatīti nopayuktam | kiṃ ca "annādbhūtāni jāyante" iti vacanamapi nopapadyate, sarveṣāṃ sarvakāle'pi prajotpattireva syāt iti cenna | annādanasāmagryāsarvadoṣasthitatvāttādṛśaṃ kāryaṃ nopapadyate iti | kintu adṛṣṭadvārā strīpuṃsayoge sati prajāḥ prajāyante | tasmāccharīrāntassthitasaptadhātūnāṃ mātṛjāstrayaḥ pitṛjāśca trayaḥ | ubhayamelanaṃ yoniḥ | śuklaśoṇitasannipāto yonirityatra pitṛśuklasānnipātyam | etacchuklamahimnā anekeṣāṃ samāhāre sati sannipātaḥ | pitṛjaśśuklasannipātaḥ | ekarasaścaramadhātuḥ | bahuvacanaṃ nopapadyata iti pitṛjaṃ asthimajjāśukladhātusānnipātyaṃ yonerlakṣaṇam | evaṃ mātṛjāni raktamāṃsamedāṃsi | eṣāṃ sānnipātyaṃ yoniḥ | tasmāt strīpuruṣayossaṃyoge sati saptadhātūtpādakasāmagrīsannipāta eva yoniriti vyapadeśaḥ | raktamityatra rasāsṛjoraikyaṃ, tadekadhāturiti pūrvameva pratipāditam | iti mātṛjāścatvāro dhātavaḥ pitṛjāśca trayaḥ | ubhayordhātvormelanaṃ yoniriti samādhatte-- mātṛjamiti ||
mātṛjaṃ raktamāṃsamedaḥ, pitṛjaṃ majjāsthiretaḥ, satvarajastamoguṇātmakaṃ śarīram || 86 ||
strīṇāṃ catvāro dhātavaḥ | puṃsāṃ trayo dhātavaḥ | nyūnādhika mātrasaṃyogavaśāt strīpūrvakameva prathamamuktatvāt | tasmāt strīpuṃsayormelanam | tatra vadanti-- 'mātāpitarau pitarau'
jagataḥ pitarau vande pārvatīparameśvarau,
strīpuṃsāvātmabhāgau tebhinnamūrtessisṛkṣayā |
prasūtikāle sarvasya tāveva pitarau smṛtau ||
mātāpitṛbhyāmanyatarasya bahuvacanena pratipādanam | tasmādubhayordhātumelanarūpaṃ yonirityarthaḥ | jagadutpattau yonireva kāraṇamiti jñāpanārthaṃ ca strīdhātavaścatvāra iti | piturapi māturapi bahukāraṇībhūtaḥ jagadutpattihetuḥ prakṛtireva | tasyāḥ "prakṛteḥ mahat, mahato'haṃkāraḥ, ahaṃkārātpañca tanmātrāṇi" iti śrutervidyamānatvāt sarvaśreṣṭhā mātā prathamata upāttā | tatra śārīravacanaṃ-
bījātmakairmahābhūtaissūkṣmaissatvānugaiśca saḥ |
mātuścāhārarasajaiḥ kramātkukṣau vivardhate ||
nanu mātṛdhātavaścatvāraḥ | pitṛjāśca trayaḥ | tāvanmātreṇa śarīrotpattiḥ pratipāditā | itaḥ paraṃ satvarajastamoguṇānāṃ kasya dravyasya guṇitvam? | tathāsati guṇaguṇinoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvo vaktuṃ śakyate | teṣāṃ guṇirūpadravyādarśanāt na guṇinamantareṇa guṇāssanti | tasmāt satvarajastamoguṇātmakaṃ śarīramiti sūtrasyaikadeśaṃ vyarthaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- āyuriti |
āyurārogyatejobalaśuddhādi vapussatvaguṇotpādakam || 87 ||
satvaguṇasyaitādṛśalakṣaṇāni santīti pratipāditam | nanu guṇāśrayo dravyamiti guṇāśrayatvaṃ dravyasya | pañcabhūtānāṃ guṇāḥ | iti te tattadravyaniṣṭhāssanti | ataste pṛthagvyavasthitāḥ | ata eteṣāṃ satvarajastamoguṇānāmete etasya dravyasyeti vyapadiśet | teṣāṃ gandhādīnāṃ gandharasarūpasparśaśabdāḥ pañcabhūtānāṃ ekaikaśo'vacchedakāḥ | guṇatrayasya teṣāṃ nāvacchedakatvāt, guṇo guṇinamantareṇa sthātuṃ nopapadyata ityasvarasāntarādāha-- āyuriti |
āyuśca ārogyaṃ ca tejaśca balaṃ caitāni svarūpāṇi vapuṣa āvirbhavantīti kāraṇānyathānupapattyā tattatkāryakalpanasya vidheyatvāt | atra śārīravacanaṃ--
tejo yathārkaraśmīnāṃ sphaṭikena tiraskṛtam |
naidhanaṃ dṛśyate gacchat satvo garbhāśayaṃ tathā |
sātvikarājasatāmasaguṇāḥ tattadutpādakahetutvakāḥ kāryamanuyāntīti hetumadbhāvasya niyāmakatvāt | tattaddhetubhūtarasādanājjātasātvikarājasatāmasaguṇāḥ jīvātmani santi | ata eva tasya guṇitvaṃ pratipāditam | yathā sphaṭikasthitaṃ tejaḥ | arkatīkṣṇakiraṇāvirbhāve sati tatrānalo dṛśyate | tatra sphaṭikārkakiraṇasaṃyogaḥ kāraṇam | kāryotpādakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ avilambena kāryakāraṇabhāvasāmagrīsānnidhyāt kāryaṃ yatra dṛśyate tatrāyaṃ niyamo jñātavyaḥ | tatra jīvasyāvayave sātvikādayaḥ adṛṣṭādisakalasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ mātāpitrantarupasthitahitāhitānnādanādantaḥ praviṣṭe sati grahaṇīkalāśrayo dhātūnsampoṣya rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedasāṃ samāhāro raktadhāturiti vyapadiśyate | paitṛkaṃ tu śuklam | asthimajjāretasāṃ samāhāraśśuklamiti vyapadiśyate | anyathā raktaśuklāveva kāraṇe saptadhātumayaśarīrotpattirūpakāryasyeti vaktuṃ na śakyate | tasmātsaptadhātumayaṃ śarīramiti jñāpanārtham ||
mṛdvatra mātṛjaṃ raktamāṃsamajjāsṛgādikam |
paitṛkaṃ tu sthiraṃ śukraṃ dhamanyasthikacādikam ||
iti vacanānusāreṇa |
atra kāvyavacanaṃ--
strīpuṃsāvātmabhāgau te bhinnamūrtessisṛkṣayā |
prasūtikāle sarvasya tāveva pitarau smṛtau |
tasmācchuklaśoṇitasannipāto yoniriti | sānnipātyaśabdamahimnā bījātmakadhātumelanaṃ sānnipātyam | tatra śārīravacanaṃ--
cetanyaḥ cittavṛkṣāṇāṃ nānāyoniṣu janmasu |
nānāyoniṣu teṣu cetanyaścittaṃ āvirbhavati tādṛśamityarthaḥ |
nanu paitṛkāstrayaḥ mātṛjāścatvāra evaṃ saptadhātumayaṃ śarīramiti śarīrakāryasya kāraṇatvaṃ pratipāditam | evaṃ sati śrutivirodhassyāt | "ātmana ākāśassambhūtaḥ| akāśādvāyuḥ" | iti vidyamānatvāt | antassthitadhātupoṣaṇaṃ kurvanti te | tasmādeva vyākhyānamayuktamityasvarasādāha-- yatreti |
yatrasthā rasāstattadbhūtajātāste dhātupoṣakāḥ ||
yatra sthitāḥ tattadavacchedakaguṇādhikyaṃ yatra bhāsate tadavacchedakāvacchinnā rasāstadavacchedakāvacchinnā bhavantaḥ tattaddhātujanakā iti te rasā dhātupoṣakāḥ ||
athāta ādibhūtaṃ yaḥ kartā yāvatkṛtestathā tadeva bheṣajaṃ manaḥkāmahṛdgatakaphaśuklādibhirāvṛtaṃ śarīraṃ daśa ||
ityāyurvedasya prathamapraśnasya bhāṣyaṃ yogānandanāthakṛtaṃ
suprasiddhaṃ mahājanasaṃmataṃ pratisūtravyākhyānaṃ
lokopakārakam
prathamapraśnaḥ samāptaḥ
<2>
āyurvede dvitīyapraśnaḥ.
guhāntaranivāsinīṃ gurukṛpākaṭākṣādamī
sahāyamanucintakāssarasijeṣu ṣaṭsu kramāt |
guhāmayabhiṣaktayā satatamaṣṭavāgvādinīṃ
mahātripurasundarīmiha muhurnamaskurmahe ||
rogatvaṃ pratipāditam | roganivṛttidvārā dhātupoṣaṇaṃ vaktumucitaṃ bhavati | tathā sati doṣaprakopanivartakaṃ rasadi vinā loke nivartakadravyābhāvaḥ suprasiddha ityasvarasādāha-- dviraseti|
dvirasārtharasahīnārthahīnarasavānanilanivārakaḥ ||
svādurasau rasapacanaprakopanivartakaḥ | pittaprakopo'pi svādurasena bhāvyaḥ | ekasya dravyasyobhayadoṣanivartakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate | dvirasārthahīnādhikarasadravyaṃ pavanapittaprakopanivartakam | adhikasvādurasadravyaṃ pittaprakopanivartakam | tasmādadhikarasadravyaṃ pavanaṃ haratīti sūtre pratipāditam |
kaṣāyatiktamadhurāḥ pittamanyaistu kupyate | iti ||
nanu śarīraṃ saptadhātumayaṃ, tajjanyamapi saptadhātumayaṃ bhavati śarīre sirāḥ kati santi ? āśayāśca marmasthānasandhayaḥ ? tatsarvaṃ vihāya paitṛkāstrayaḥ mātṛjāścatvāro dhātava evābhihitā ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya tatrasthitasirāmarmāsthisandhyāśayānalādīni vyācaṣṭe-- ṣaḍiti || ṣaṭsahasratriṃśatsirāstriṃśacchalyāśśatadvayadaśottarasandhayo navāśītisnāyupeśidharā bhavanti || 2 ||
śarīramātraṃ sarvatra sirāvṛtam | tathā'sthīni sandhayaścāśayāśca sarvairebhirāvṛtaṃ śarīramiti | tatsarvaṃ bhūtamayam | māturevotpadyanta ityarthaḥ | doṣāssirāvṛtā marmagatā āśayasthāśca | ete svasthadoṣamārgāḥ anyasvasthadhātūnpraviśanti | svasthāsvasthadoṣavijñānārthaṃ sarvaśarīraṃ vibhajya tattajjñānaṃ vijñāya doṣaprakopānapahartuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ | śarīrasthitasirādīnvibhajati |
nanu saptadhātudharāssaptatvaṃ ca pavanasaptatvaṃ cāśritam | antaḥpraviṣṭassvāduraso bhūtvā'nilaprakopaṃ haratīti yattadayuktaṃ, dhātupoṣakakāryopakārakatvenānyathāsiddhatvāt | tvagantasstharasāsṛgādayo yathāvidhi mārge sañcārīkṛtāḥ | pavanaprakopasya nivārakatvaṃ svādurasasya kathaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- sapteti |
saptadhātudharāssaptāśayāstanmadhyagagrahaṇīkalājātaḥ svāduraso'nilaprakopaṃ harati || 3 ||
asyārthaḥ–- saptatvacaḥ saptadhātūndadhati | rasaraktādyapi anilasañcārādyathāmārgamanatikramya sthitasthāpakatvamanila eva harati | tasya tāvanmātropakārakaguṇatvāt svādurasaḥ anilaprakopaṃ haratīti yattadyuktamitārthaḥ | gatāgatakalopakārakatvaṃ anila eva karoti | tasmādanilaprakopanivṛttyarthaṃ svādurasadravyādanaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
prāṇādibhedātpañcātmā vāyuḥ prāṇo'tra mūrdhagaḥ ||
uraḥkaṇṭhacaro buddhihṛdayendriyacittadhṛk |
ṣṭhīvanakṣavadhūdgāraniśśvāsānnapraveśakṛt |
urassthānamudānasya nāsānābhigalāṃścaret |
vākpravṛttiprayatnaujobalavarṇapratikriyaḥ ||
iti | etadvacanānusāreṇa sarvaśarīravyāpakatvamanilasya | tasmātsvādurasaḥ anilakopaṃ haratītyarthaḥ ||
nanu etādṛśāśayānāṃ sarvaśarīrāntassthitatvasya sārvajanīnatvena tattatkāryopalambhakatvaṃ yathāvidhi coditamiti saptāśayāssaptadhātumantaḥ sarvasādhāraṇatvāt | tathā sati eṣvāśayeṣu prajājananadeśaṃ nirṇītumaśakyatvāt, kiṃ ca saptatvaksirādhātvāśayābhivardhanaṃ pañcapācakapittānilajātasvāduraso'nilaprakopamapaharatu | tasya rogārogaikakāraṇatvaṃ sarvaśarīrāṇāṃ suprasiddhamiti tātparyaṃ manasi nidhāya prajājananahetupradeśaṃ vivṛṇoti-- aṣṭeti |
aṣṭamo garbhāśayaḥ strīṇām || 4 ||
prajājananahetukāśayaḥ strīṇāmeva sambhāvya ityarthaḥ | tatra śārīravacanaṃ--
garbhāśayo'ṣṭamaḥ strīṇāṃ pittapakvāśayāntare ||
saptāśayātiriktaṃ pittapakvāśayāntaraṃ netareṣu sambhāvitamiti garbhādhānahetukatvaṃ strīṇāmeva sambhāvitatvāt | ata eva prathamataḥ strīṇāṃ nāmoccāraṇānantaraṃ puṃnāmoccāraṇamiti sakalaśāstreṣu śreṣṭhakramo'stīti vyapadeśitam | ādau strīpuruṣayordhātureva yonyāṃ praviśya janayati | tathā sati dhātumelanameva piṇḍaṃ bhavet | yāvatkāraṇaṃ tāvatkāryamiti nyāyāt | idānīmaṅgajananakā ryadarśanasya hetvabhāvo dṛśyate | kāraṇena vinā kathaṃ kāryotpattirityasvarasādāha-- svamiti |
svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmaṃ caujaḥ | svaṃ svaṃ svasya svayameva heturbhavati || 5 ||
svāṅgāni svasminneva prajāḥ prajāyante, anyathā tadanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ sukhaduḥkhe kathamupapadyete | anyajananasya tadanvayavyatireke sati sukhaduḥkhānubhavo'stītyata itarajanane svaśarīrameva kāraṇamiti | tatra kāvyavacanaṃ--
ātmānamātmanā vetsi sṛjasyātmānamātmanā |
ātmanā kṛtinā ca tvamātmanaiva pralīyase ||
śrutivacanaṃ--
aṅgādaṅgātsambhavasi hṛdayādadhijāyase |
ātmā vai putranāmāsi sa jīva śaradāṃ śatam ||
garbhāśayagatoṣmaṇā mātuḥ pittasāraḥ stravati | tatsārajanyassāraḥ śirautpādako bhavati | tadrasātmakaṃ caujo bhavati | garbhāśayagatoṣmaṇā mātuḥ pāṇiḥ sravati | tatsārajanyo rasaḥ pāṇyutpādako bhavati | garbhāśayagatoṣmaṇā mātuḥ pādasārassravati | tatsārajanyasāraḥ pādotpādako bhavati | evaṃ pārśvau ca | pṛṣṭhodarajaṅghaśiśnopasthapāyvaṅgādīni garbhāśayagatoṣmaṇā māturaṅgāni eṣu sravanti | tatsārajanyasārāṇi tadutpādakāni bhavanti | māturāhārarasāhārāstadvyatiriktāṅgābhivardhakāḥ | tānyaṅgāni raktabhūtāni santi | tattadaṅgeṣu vāyuḥ prakupyate | tatpavanakopanivṛttyarthaṃ svādurasavaddravyam, tannivartanadvārā bheṣajaṃ svādurasamaśnīyāt ||
atha jñāpanārthaṃ tadutpādakānyaṅgānyāha--śira iti ||
śiraḥpāṇipādapārśvapṛṣṭhorūdarajaṅghaśiśnopasthapāyvaṅgāni bhavanti || 6 ||
etāni māturgarbhāśaye sravanti | tattadaṅgānyāvirbhavantītyarthaḥ | te rasā garbhāśayamukhaṃ puṣṇantītyarthaḥ | garbhāśayagatoṣmaṇā mātuḥ pādapārśvapṛṣṭhodarajaṅghaśiśnopasthebhyassārāsravanti | tatsārajanyasārāḥ pārśvādyaṅgotpādakāḥ | tadrasātmakaṃ caujo bhavati | antarvatnyāḥ śiromāṃsāśayānāṃ sārasya garbhe srāvitatvāt | garbhāśaye pavanaprakopasya yuktatvāt, tadarthaṃ svādu rasavaddravyamadyāt | aṅgāvirbhāvānantaraṃ śrotratvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇānāmatikṣatvāt tattatsthitāni tattatsthānaṃ vihāya garbhāśayaṃ praveṣṭumayogyatvāt śrotrādīnāmāvirbhāvaḥ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- śrotreti ||
śrotratvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇāstathā || 7 ||
sarvendriyāṇi sūkṣmaśarīrāṇyapi mātussvaśarīrasthitapañcabhūtānāṃ garbhāśaye upalabhyamānatvāt | indriyādīnāṃ tattadguṇatvādeva guṇinamantareṇa guṇāvasthānasya vaktumayogyatvāt tattadbhūtapraveśanāttattadindriyasahitā eva garbhāśayaṃ praviśantītyarthaḥ | dhātvādīnāṃ sāradravyatvena svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravattattadākṛtikāryahetukamātṛdhātusrāvitāvayavo garbhāśaye kuṇapāvayavābhivṛddhihetukakāryaṃ rasāvakāśavadbhavatīti tasmāttayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvo vaktuṃ śakyate | atra tu nirākṛti dravyaṃ sarandhrakaṃ kathamanyatra praveṣṭuṃ śakyate ? śiraḥpāṇyādivatsāvayavadravyatvābhāvāt sāvakāśapraveśānavakāśaḥ | idaṃ tu niravayavadravyaṃ svamātṛsthāvayavasāraṃ garbhāśaye sravatīti vaktumaśakyamityasvarasādāha-- sarandhreti |
sarandhrakābhyantaradharāstrayastrayassirāḥ | pādayoścatustriṃśatsarandhrakābhyantaradharāḥ || 8 ||
asyārthaḥ--
kāścidabhyantaradharāḥ kāścitsarandhrakāḥ ekaikaśastrisirāvṛtāḥ | sāvakāśadravyasya vyāpyākāśatvena śarīrasya paścabhūtātmakatvena ākāśadravyasyāpi tatpraviṣṭatvāt tasya tritrisirāvṛtadravyatvāt garbhāśaye srāvayituṃ yuktamityarthaḥ | atra tritrisiravṛtāḥ sarandhrakābhyantaradharāḥ saptaśatasaṅkhyākāḥ pādamastakādhiṣṭhitā ityarthaḥ | mātṛgarbhāśaye tadaṅgāni tatraiva sravantītyuktam | tadeva vivṛṇoti-- pādeti | pādayugmasthitapadmaṃ garbhāśaye srāvitatvāt tatpādayugamajāyata | avarṇadvayamapi tatraivājāyata | tadanantaraṃ mātṛjānusthitatrisirāvṛtaṃ ca mātṛgarbhāśaye kuṇapapiṇḍarūpasyāvirbhāve kathaṃ nu māturaṅgasāra eva tatrāvirbhavatītyata āha-- pādeti |
pādasthitapadmamavarṇajanakaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam || 9 ||
māturaṅgabhūtapādaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakasarandhrakābhyantaraṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ tatpādasthitāvarṇaṃ vāmapāde svaritaplutasahitāvarṇātmakaṃ pādayugamajāyatetyarthaḥ | tadūrdhvāṅgaṃ jānuyugaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakamajāyatetyāha--jānviti |
jānupadmagatamivarṇajanakaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam ||
sarandhrakābhyantaradharacatustriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ vāmetarajānuyugaṃ svaritaplutasahitevarṇadvayaṃ garbhāśaye srāvitaṃ tajjānuyugamajāyatetyarthaḥ |
gatāgatakarmopakārakavarṇaprayuktavātapittakaphadoṣāśrayadravyādanena avikṛtāssantaḥ gatāgatakarmopakārakajaṅghādeśagatapadmaṃ sarandhrakābhyantaraviṃśatisirāvṛtaṃ jānupadmamajāyatetyāha-- uvarṇeti |
uvarṇātmakamūruṃpradeśapadmaṃ viṃśatisirāvṛtam ||
svaritaplutasahitovarṇajaṅghāpadmaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravedrasātmakaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
śroṇipradeśasthitasarandhrakābhyantaradharāśritarvarṇajñāpakamūrupradeśapadmaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakaṃ dṛśyata ityāha-- ṛvarṇeti |
ṛvarṇajanakaṃ śroṇipradeśapadmaṃ viṃśatisirāvṛtam || 12 ||
ṛvarṇahetukamūrupradeśapadmaṃ viṃśatisirāvṛtaṃ svaritaplutasahitarvarṇādhārabhūtaṃ pācakapittoṣmabhirviṃśatisirāvṛtaṃ vāmetarorupadmaṃ gatāgatakarmopakārakabahubhārajaṃ māturaṅgādeva garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
lṛvarṇajñāpakaṃ śroṇipradeśapadmaṃ tathaivājāyatetyāha-- lṛvarṇeti |
lṛvarṇajanakaṃ śroṇipradeśapadmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam || 13 ||
sarandhrakābhyantarasirāvṛtamūrdhvāṅgabhāravahaṃ śroṇipradeśapadmaṃ āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
evarṇādhārabhūtamevarṇasvaritaplutavarṇādhārabhūtakaṭibījavaṅkṣaṇatatpārśvalagnaṃ ādhārabhūtamāturaṅgānyeva tatrāvirbhavantītyāha-- evarṇamiti |
evarṇajanakaṃ kaṭipradeśagatapadmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam || 14 ||
aivarṇajanakaṃ bījagatapārśvagatapadmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam || 15 ||
ovarṇajanakaṃ vaṅkṣaṇapradeśagataṃ padmaṃ dvādaśasirāvṛtam || 16 ||
auvarṇajanakaṃ bījapradeśagataṃ padmaṃ dvisirāvṛtam || 17 ||
mithunakarmopayogikā e ai o au ityādyacaḥ kaṭipradeśapadmaṃṃ vaṅkṣaṇapradeśapadmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ māturaṅgāni svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavantītyarthaḥ ||
dīrghasvaritaplutasvararūpabhūtānāṃ e ai o au varṇānāmādhārabhūtāni | anubandhakahetuvargasya madhye kakhavarṇadvayasya evarṇādhārapadmapārśvasthitapadmasya prajājananahetubhūtasya mithunakarmahetupadmaṃ vivṛṇoti kakheti |
kakhavarṇajanakaṃ tatpārśvapadmaṃ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtam ||
avarṇakavarṇahavarṇajñāpakasirāvṛtapavanatālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpāra auvarṇajñāpakasāmagrī sarandhrakābhyantaracatustriṃśatsirāgatapavanaṃ dīrghasvaritaplutasahitamajjaṃ tatpārśvasthitaṃ sat śrotrākāśamudbhāvayatītyarthaḥ |
mithunakarmopakārakameḍhrapadmaṃ sarandhrakābhyantaradharatriṣaṣṭisirāvṛta gaghavarṇadevatādhārabhūtaṃ saptadhāturasajanyaretaādhārabhūtaṃ sakaladhātusirāmayaṃ manmathagehapadmaṃ vivṛṇoti-- gagheti |
gaghavarṇajanakameḍhrapradeśastriṣaṣṭisirāvṛtaḥ || 19 ||
garbhāśaye piṇḍaṃ yathā bhavati tathā māturāhārarasajātaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakaṃ puṃvatpiṇḍaṃ yathā dṛśyate tanmeḍhrapadmājjāyata iti vaktavyam, tasya māturaṅgābhāvatvena meḍhrapadmasyāyogyatvāt | etadāvirbhāvakathanamayogyamiti na vācyam | reto'dhikatvāt putro bhavatīti nyāyāt | śuklaśoṇitasannipāto yoniriti lakṣaṇavacanācca | etayoraikyaṃ yoniḥ | tadvacanaṃ tattadaṅgāvirbhāvakathanaṃ māturāhārapariṇāmavaśāt puṃniṣṭharetasa ādhikyaṃ yatra bhavati tatra meḍhrapadmasya hetutvāt śukraśoṇitasānnipātyāvirbhāvakathanaṃ tattadabhivardhanaṃ ca | svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇāsravadrasātmakaṃ punniṣṭhāṅgāvirbhāvaṃ tatsānnipātyābhivardhanaṃ tasyāpi yoniliṅgasyāpi meḍhrapadmatvāt tajjātatvakathanaṃ tadaṅgābhivardhanaṃ ca yuktamityarthaḥ | punniṣṭhameḍhrapadmaṃ strīniṣṭhayonipadmaṃ cetyubhayasyāpi manmathagehatvāt | retaādhikyajātopādhikavaśācca | dīrghameḍhrahetukapadmasya punniṣṭhatvena tadaṅgābhivardhanaṃ yuktamiti tātparyam |
strīniṣṭhasirāvṛtameḍhrapadmādhārabhūtapṛṣṭhadeśapadmaṃ vivicya darśayati-- ṅeti |
ṅoccāraṇahetukaṃ pṛṣṭhadeśagataṃ padmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam || 20 ||
sarandhrakābhyāntarādhāracatustriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ ṅavarṇadevatādhārabhūtapṛṣṭhadeśapadmaṃ ūrdhvāṅgasarvadehabhāraṃ bhajat pādajānujaṅghorukaṭiśroṇibhyāṃ gatāgatarūpacalanātmakakarma kurvat mithunakarmopakārakaphalamātrameva bhajat apānapavanādhārakaṃ jaṭharānalapradeśādhā rabhūtaṃ tajjātamalanissāraṇahetukaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | nanu strīpuruṣasādhāraṇaliṅgameḍhrapadme tattajjātijanyasvarā eva tattadvarṇaśarīrajñāpakāśśarīrabhedamapi tatra jātā varṇā eva jñāpayantītyuktam | tattaddeśasthitagudavivarasyāpi sarandhrakatvāt tadgudapradeśavivaraṃ vihāya yoniliṅgameḍhrapadmasthitarandhrāṇāṃ hīnādhikatvāt tattajjātijanyasvarajñāpakagudasthitarandhramārgaṃ vihāya liṅgameḍhrarandhrādhārasirārandhramārgagatapavananissaraṇagaghavarṇabhedajñānajñāpakaṃ bhavatītyāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya gudadvārādhārabhūtavarṇadevatāṃ vivṛṇoti-- ṅeti |
ṅoccāraṇahetukaṃ pṛṣṭhadeśapadmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam || 21 ||
ṅakārānubandhadevatādhārabhūtapṛṣṭhadeśapadmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam | pṛṣṭhadeśapadmavivarasya viṭsaṃsaraṇamātra eva caritārthatvāt savarṇasvarābhedajñāpakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
sarvāśayānāmādhārabhūtagudāvṛtavalayapadmayoniliṅgameḍhrapadmāvṛtasirāvarṇādhārabhūtagudāvṛtacakrapadmaṃ cachavarṇadevatātmakaṃ vivṛṇoti cacheti |
cachavarṇotpādakaṃ tatpārśvapadmaṃ caturdaśasirāvṛtam || 22 ||
gudapadmasya vakrākāreṇāvṛtapadme tatpārśvapadmaṃ pavananissaraṇahetubhūtacachavarṇadevatātmakaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
jaṭharānalarūpapācakapittasya ādhārabhūtajaṭharānilapadmaṃ āvirbhavatītyāha-- jajheti |
jajhavarṇoccāraṇahetukaṃ jaṭharānilapadmaṃ caturdaśasirāvṛtam || 23 ||
kuṇḍalyādhārabhūgatapavanadevatārūpaṣaṭkamalānāmādhārabhūtatvāt śvāsocchvāsarūpapavanādhārabhūtaṃ nābhipadmaṃ māturāhārarasajātarasāhṛtakamalakāṇḍālavālastharūpaṃ vivṛṇoti-- ñeti |
ñavarṇotpādakaṃ nābhipradeśapadma viṃśatisirāvṛtam || 24 ||
kuṇḍalīgatatatpārśvagapavanasya ādhārabhūtaṃ sarandhrakābhyantaradharaṃ viṃśatisirāvṛtam | nābhipradeśapadmaṃ ñavarṇadevatātmakaṃ pācakapittāgnijanakaṃ agnicakrajātaśvāsāniloṣmaṇā sahasradalāvṛtaśiraḥkamalasthāmṛtajanakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
nābhirandhrasirāvṛtakuṇḍalīrūpapavanacakraṃ sarandhrakābhyantaradharatriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ nābhipadmāvṛtapavanacakraṃ vyācaṣṭe--ṭaṭheti |
ṭaṭhavarṇotpādakaṃ nābhipradeśapadmaṃ triṃśatsirāvṛtam || 25 ||
kuṇḍalyādhārabhūtānilātmanābhirandhrasirāvṛtapavanacakraṃ māturāhārarasādgarbhāśayaṃ poṣayet ṭaṭhavarṇadevatātmakaṃ triṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ nābhyāvṛtapavanacakramāvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
nābhipārśvasthitaromarājikāryajanakābhyāvṛtālavālacakraṃ dvāviṃśatisirāvṛtaḍaḍhavarṇadevatātmakaṃ māturāhārarasādanājjātarasasrāvitaṃ garbhāśayaromarājiśākhi jāyata ityāha-- ḍaḍheti |
ḍaḍhavarṇahetukaṃ romarājipārśvagataṃ dvāviṃśatisirāvṛtam || 26 ||
sarandhrakābhyantaradharadvāviṃśatisirāvṛtapavanena ḍaḍhavarṇadevatā śrotravivaraprāpaṇaromarājihetukapārśvadvayapadmaṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ |
tadromarājiphalībhūtahṛtkamalaṃ vivṛṇoti-- ṇavarṇeti |
ṇavarṇādhārabhūtaṃ hṛtkamalaṃ triṃśatsirāvṛtam ||
triṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ hṛtkamalaṃ prādurbhavatītyarthaḥ |
pañcāśatsirāvṛtastanadvayapadmaṃ sakalasvarābhivyañjakamāha-- tatheti |
tathavarṇotpādakaṃ stanadvayapadmaṃ pañcāśatsirāvṛtam ||
adhikastanadvayapadmaṃ susvarābhivyañjakahetukaṃ stanadvayapadmavat puṃsāmadhikaghoṣadhātujñāpakaṃ tanmāṃsagranthirūpaṃ dīrghasvaritaplutasahitācāmādhārabhūtaṃ tādṛśastanayugmaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakaṃ māturāhārapariṇāmājjātaṃ garbhāśaye stanayugaṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ ||
sarandhrakābhyantaradharakaṇṭhadeśapadmaṃ dadhavarṇotpattikāryahetukaṃ kaṇṭhadeśapadmaṃ jāyata iyāha-- dadheti |
dadhavarṇotpādakaṃ kaṇṭhadeśapadmaṃ pañcāśatsirāvṛtam |
sarvavarṇānāmādhārabhūtaṃ sarvasirābhivyañjakaṃ garbhāśaye māturāhārarasapariṇāmahetukaṃ kaṇṭhadeśapadmaṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ ||
sarandhrakābhyantaradharasirāvṛtaṃ anusvāranavarṇadevatātmakaṃ jāyata ityāha-- neti |
navarṇotpādakaṃ grīvāpadmaṃ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtam || 30 ||
navarṇotpādakaṃ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtaṃ garbhāśaye grīvāpadmaṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ | śirassthānādhiṣṭhitanavarṇarūpānusvāradevatātmakaṃ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtaṃ grīvāṅgādhārabhūtapadmaṃ māturgarbhāśayaṃ praviśya grīvāṅgaṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ ||
paphavarṇasthānabāhupradeśaṃ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtapadmaṃ māturaṅgaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakaṃ paphavarṇadevatātmakaṃ bāhupradeśamityāha-- papheti |
paphavarṇotpādakaṃ bāhupadmaṃ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtam ||
paphavarṇotpādakaṃ tathā bāhvoḥ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtaṃ sarandhrakābhyantaradharabāhupradeśapadmaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ ||
jihvāgrasthitalālārūparasādibandhanahetukaṃ babhavarṇadevatātmakaṃ dvisirāvṛtaṃ jihvantaḥpradeśagatapadmaṃ māturaṅgātsravat tattatsāragarbhāśayaṃ praviśya rasabandhanapadmaṃ jāyata ityāha-- babheti |
babhavarṇajanakaṃ prakoṣṭhapradeśapadmaṃ pañcāśatsirāvṛtam || 32 ||
babhavarṇajñāpakaṃ rasabandhanapadmaṃ sarandhrakābhyantaradharaṃ pañcāśatsirāvṛtaprakoṣṭhagatapadmaṃ babhavarṇadevatātmakaṃ rasavaddravyādanasārajātaṃ māturaṅgātsravībhūtaṃ prakoṣṭhapadmaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
anusvārasahitabindurūpamavarṇadevatātmakaṃ manoviṣayakayāvacchabdoccāraṇajātārthabodhakahastavinyāsajñāpakaṃ hastapadmaṃ jāyata ityāha-- meti |
mavarṇoccāraṇahetukaṃ sakalaśabdārthajñāpakaṃ hastagatapadmaṃ pañcāśatsirāvṛtam || 33 ||
tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārādhīnaśabdārthaviṣayajñāpakayāvatsirāsajātīyavyāpakaṃ hastapadmamāvirbhavatītyarthaḥ ||
jihvāgrasthitalālārūparasādijñāpakaṃ yavarṇadevatātmakaṃ dvisi rāvṛtajihvāgragatapadmaṃ māturaṅgātsravībhūtarasabandhanapadmaṃ garbhāśaye jāyata ityāha-- yavarṇeti |
yavarṇajñāpakaṃ rasabandhanapadmaṃ dvisirāvṛtam || 34 ||
sarvavarṇajñāpakahetubhūtaṃ lālārūpajalātmakāvirbhūtaṃ yavarṇadevatātmakaṃ garbhāśaye jihvāgragatapadmaṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ |
jihvāgrasthitapadmapārśvavartisarandhrakābhyantararephavarṇadevatātmakaṃ māturāhārarasapariṇāmavaśāt garbhāśaye jihvāpārśvāgrapadmaṃ jāyata ityāha-- repheti |
rephavarṇotpādakamoṣṭhapadmaṃ ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtam ||
lavarṇajanakaṃ vācaspatipradeśasthaṃ dvisirāvṛtam ||
sakalavarṇotpādakavācaspatipradeśagatamoṣṭhapadmaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakaṃ garbhāśaye dṛśyata ityarthaḥ ||
caturviṃśatisirātmakalavarṇajñāpakaṃ tadvarṇādhiṣṭhitadevatātmakaṃ svaṃ svaṃ svoṣmaṇā sravadrasātmakaṃ nāsikāgragatapadmaṃ jāyata ityāha-- veti |
vavarṇotpādakaṃ nāsikāgragatapadmaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtam || 37 ||
gandhavatī pṛthivī sarvadravyaniṣṭhagandhaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtā jighrantī garbhāśayaṃ praviśya prādurbhavatītyarthaḥ ||
ekaikasirāvṛtaṃ tāludvayagatapadmaṃ śavarṇoccāraṇahetubhūtaṃ āsyapradeśaṃ samprāpya śrotrapradeśapadmaṃ bhavatītyāha-- śeti |
śavarṇajñāpakaṃ gandhavahapadmaṃ dvisirāvṛtam || 38 ||
dvisirāvṛtaśavarṇadevatātmakaṃ tāludvayapadmaṃ rasanendriyagatalālārūpajalajanakaṃ vyācaṣṭe-- ṣeti |
ṣavarṇotpādakatāludvayapadmamekaikasirāvṛtam ||
tālupradeśādhārakadvisirāgatapavanena ṣavarṇaṃ śrotrendriyaṃ prāpya tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasāmagrīsānnidhye bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
pañcāśatsirāvṛtamakṣipadmaṃ savarṇādhārabhūtaṃ jāyata ityāha-- seti |
savarṇajñāpakamakṣipradeśagatapadmaṃ catuḥpañcāśatsirāvṛtam || 40 ||
tattajjanākṣipadmaṃ savarṇajñāpakaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
sarandhrakābhyantaradvisirāvṛtapakṣapradeśapadmaṃ āvirbhavatītyāha-- heti |
havarṇotpādakaṃ pakṣapradeśapadmaṃ dvisirāvṛtam ||
māturāhārādanājjātaṃ havarṇadevatātmakaṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ |
sarandhrakābhyantaradharadvisirāvṛtalavarṇadevatātmakaṃ apāṅgadeśapadmamāvirbhavatītyāha-- leti|
lavarṇotpādakamapāṅgadeśapadmaṃ dvisirāvṛtam ||
māturāhārarasājjātaṃ māturapāṅgapadmaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
ekaikasirāvṛtaṃ dvātriṃśaddantapaṅktipadmaṃ kṣavarṇadevatātmakaṃ jāyata ityāha-- kṣeti | kṣavarṇajñāpakaṃ dantapaṅktipradeśapadmaṃ triṃśatsirāvṛtam || 43 ||
māturāhārarasājjātaṃ dantapaṅktipadmaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
pañcadaśasirāvṛtaṃ kapolapadmaṃ ravarṇadevatātmakaṃ garbhāśaye prādurbhavatītyāha-- repheti |
rephotpādakaṃ kapolapadmaṃ ravarṇadevatātmakam ||
rephadevatātmakaṃ kapolapadmaṃ garbhāśaye āvirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
ādikkṣāntavarṇādhārakāṅgapadmaṃ pādādyaṅgādhiṣṭhitaṃ varṇoccāraṇaprayatnajātavarṇādyanubhavajñānaviṣayakatālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārajātapavanaprayatnapreritāṅgajñāpakavarṇajñānaviṣayakajñānavattvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | svaritaśabdaviṣayakajñānaṃ tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārādhīnasaṃskārajātavarṇānubhavaviṣayakaṃ jñānecchāprayatnajātavarṇānubhavajñānaviṣayakatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivam | iyaṃ śabdaviṣayapravṛttiḥ icchājñānaviṣayikā, samarthapravṛttijanakatvāt, yathā ghaṭaḥ | etadanumānacatuṣṭayaṃ sarvavarṇānāṃ tattadaṅgādhiṣṭhitaviṣayakaṃ bhavati |
