sarandhrakābhyantaradharāssaptaśatasirā āsyaṃ ca tatpradeśamāvṛtya tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārabhedakarmaṇāṃ varṇānāṃ utpattikāraṇaṃ, tadjñānādikarmavyatirekeṇa varṇāni notpadyante | anyathā vīṇānādavattantrīṇāṃ yāvadduṣṭatāḍanātpavanena nakhajātakarmaṇā tattadvarṇajanakahetubhūtatvaṃ bhavatīti vīṇāyantraśarīre tattadvarṇotpādakaṃ tattatpradeśasthitatattadvarṇajanakanibandhanaṃ kṛtvā tāvadvarṇajanakāṅguṣṭha puṭaghaṭṭanena etādṛśavarṇanimittametat iti tajjanakahetubhūtakāraṇakarmaṇāṃ tajjanyaśabdānāṃ vyāptiṃ gṛhītvā tattadvarṇajanakakarma karotīti tattatkāraṇānuguṇakāryameva utpadyate | sarandhrakābhyantaradharāstriṃśatsirāvṛtāssantaḥ hastāṅguṣṭhatāḍanena preritā raṇatsaptasvarādibhedāḥ pṛthakpṛthagvibhinnāssantaḥ śrutimaṇḍalāḥ svaravyaktottarottaratāratamyaṃ bhūtvā hīnamadhyamottamāḥ karmakaraṇāttattadanusvaritasvarāśśrūyante | tadvadeva śārīrayantraṃ vijñāya tattadvarṇānāṃ vyāptiṃ gṛhītvā tattadvarṇātmakāḥ śabdāvacchedakaprayatnavyāpārakarmakaraṇāttattatsvarādhiṣṭhitavarṇātmakāśśabdāḥ tattaddhetubhūtasirābhiḥ śrūyanta ityāha-- sireti |
sirāsyadoṣagatijātaśabdaḥ karṇayoḥ prapadyate ||
nanu śabdaguṇavadākāśamiti ākāśasyaiva śabdajanakatvam | karṇaśaṣkulyavacchinnākāśa eva śabdo bhāsate, nānyatra | tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārādikaṃ nimittakāraṇam | na tāvatsirāṇāṃ samavāyatvaṃ tāsāṃ śarīrāvayavākārajanakatvam | tā na śabdabodhakāḥ | pāvakasya duṣṭatvena dhātupoṣakatve bhramaḥ | tasmātpūrvoktarītyā sutravyākhyānaṃ na saṅgatamiti | atrocyate-- ākāśasyaikatvena anekavarṇabhedajanakakāryasya sāmagryabhāvāt | śarīrāntassthitadhātusañcāritavāyuḥ atra na vivakṣitaḥ, kintu tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārāṅgāṅgasaṅaghaṭṭanājjātaśabdakāryahetukapavanaśśabdajanaka iti vaktuṃ śakyate | tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārāṅgāṅgasaṃsargājjātapavanāt yogyaṃ bhavatīti | yāvatkālaviṣayakaupādhikapavanajātaśabdāntarārambhakaḥ kadambamukulanyāyena vā vīcītaraṅganyāyena vā śabdāntaraṃ jāyate | tasmāddravyasaṃyogajātapavanasya samavāyakāraṇatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate |
tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpāro yatra dṛśyate tatraiva vātaśabdanimittakaśabdo nigamena śrūyate | tasmāttayossaṃyogaḥ samavāyakāraṇamiti dyotyate | tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārahetukakāryasyāsyasaṃyogasya sirādīnāṃ varṇabhedasya nimittakāraṇatvaṃ jñāpyate | varṇabhedāt śabdaviṣayakajñānabhedakāryaṃ vicitrahetupratipādakasāmagrījanyaṃ kāraṇasya kriyāviśeṣamātravailakṣaṇyatayā vicitravarṇajñāpakatvamanubhūyate | so'nubhavaḥ sāmagrībhedādbhavatīti deśataḥ kālataḥ ghaṭanārūpakāryaviśeṣataḥ sirādibāhulyasaṃyogāt hīnālpataroccaritasvarādivarṇabhedajñānaṃ tatsāmagrībhedāt bhavatīti yatra yassaṃbandhavarṇaḥ sa eva vausiṣṭaka ? ityubhyupagamāt śrotrākāśasya vyavadhānasāmīpyadeśabhedajātamarimnā uccoccataroccatamoccāraṇabhedāttattadvarṇātmakaśabdabhedo jñāyate | śabdabhedabodhakavākyaṃ sirābhedodbhavavarṇabhedabodhakakāryaṃ ca pakṣadvayaṃ gṛhītam | sādhyaṃ tu taduccāraṇahetubhūtavarṇabhedaviṣayakajñānapūrvaṃ sirādibhedajanyavarṇaviṣakajñānātmakatvāt bheryādiśabdavat |
atra śārīravacanaṃ--
doṣadhātumalādīnāṃ ūṣmetyātreyaśāsanam |
tadadhiṣṭhānamannasya grahaṇādgrahaṇī matā ||
saiva dhanvantarimate kalā pittadharāhvayā |
āyurārogyavīryaujo bhūtadhātvagnipuṣṭaye ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa tattatpradeśodbhavavarṇajñāpikā grahaṇīkalā | tattadrasādanājjātavarṇādibhedajñānaṃ tattadrasadravyapākajñātapoṣakaikakāryaviṣayakaṃ rasavirasādanajātadoṣādoṣajñāpakaṃ uccoccataraśabdabhedaviṣayakajñānagocarajanakatvāt | tattadrasāhārādrasajātaphala kāryaṃ varṇoccāraṇahetupūrvakaṃ tattadvarṇagrahaṇīkalāṣoḍaśakaikaphalamātrakāryatvāt śrotrāntassthitasirāsaṃbandhakaraṇena grahaṇīkalāyāḥ śabdabodhakakāryahetukatvaṃ vyapadiśyata ityarthaḥ | pādapadmamārabhya karṇaśaṣkulīkṛtapadmaparyantasthitavarṇānāṃ kṣetrāntassthitasirāsaṃbandhakāryakāraṇaṃ saptaśatasaṅkhyāpūrvakānāṃ sirāṇāṃ āvedhyatvaṃ vidhiriti jñāpyate | kiṃ ca dehadhārakāṣṭāśītisirā na vedhyāḥ sirāsaṅkīrṇagranthikṛtakṣudravakragranthiṣu sthitāśca raktavimocanakaraṇe na yogyā ityarthaḥ | grahaṇīkalāyāḥ rogābhāvakāryahetukatvaṃ aviruddharasādanājjātakarmānusāreṇa pācakapittākhyā grahaṇīkalā vātapittakaphairjuṣṭānubodhikānusvārān savarṇān subalān akārādikṣakārāntavarṇān svasya cāparasya ca bodhayituṃ samarthā bhavantītyarthaḥ ||
yadi viruddharasādanājjātājīrṇāt anilo'jīrṇaḥ tathā sati doṣagatayo viruddhā vikārakāḥ satyaḥ śarīraṃ pīḍayanti | yattadudbodhakaṃ rasādanānusāreṇa viruddhabodhakatvaṃ vivarṇajñāpakatvaṃ abalatvaṃ svarahīnatvaṃ tattadvarṇeṣūpalabhyate | tattadvarṇādhiṣṭhitasthalaṃ vikārabhūtamiti vijñāya tattannivartakakāryakaraṇaṃ vidhiriti |
yenārogyaṃ bhavedyasya bhavedyattadbalaṃ tathā |
tadeva bheṣajaṃ saukhyaṃ bhavettenaiva tattadā ||
iti uktatvāt | yena kena prakāreṇa tannivartanaṃ kārayedidityarthaḥ | śrotropalabdhavarṇātmakaśabdaḥ sirānyadoṣagatikriyājanyahetuvat anyāhetukatve sati taddhetujanyatvāt ghaṭavat | etadanumānena sarvavarṇātmakaśabdānāṃ sirānyadoṣagatikriyājanyatvaṃ bhāsate | trayāṇāṃ kāraṇatvaṃ saṅghe saṅghe śaktiḥ na tvekaikasyeti pratipāditam | nanu sirāṇāṃ śarīrayantradhāraṇaprayojakakāryahetukatvaṃ brūmaḥ | saśabdotpādakakāryahetubhūtatvasya anyathāsiddhatvāt | doṣagatiprakopena ca rogasambhavāt | tathā hi viruddharasādanādajīrṇe sati yāvatkāraṇādhikyajātāḥ tattadrogakāryahetukāḥ saśabdopalambhakānyahetukāḥ | tattadroganivṛtyarthaṃ tattatpradeśasirāgatāsṛgvimocanena tattaddhetubhūtaroganivartanasya dṛṣṭatvāt | sirānyagatarogasya tanniṣṭhāsṛgvimocananivartakakāryasya kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ sūcyate | karaṇasya kriyāviśeṣavailakṣaṇyamātrapratipādakatvāt | tatra vacanam--
sirāvidhiśśalyavidhiśśastrakṣārāgnikarma ca |
iti | sūtravacanasya sirāsṛgvimocanarūpakāryapratipādakatvācca na te śabdajanyakāryahetukā iti | atrocyate-- viruddharasādanājjātaroganidānabhūtalakṣaṇāttattatsirāprabodhakavarṇaniṣṭhavikāraviruddhasvarāghoṣavarṇātmakāni liṅgāni vijñāya tattadroganivṛttyarthaṃ tattatsirāgataduṣṭaraktavimocanamātra eva caritārthatvāt | tasmānnānyathāsiddhirityarthaḥ |
nanu varṇaprabodhakasirāḥ saptaśatasaṅkhyākāḥ | tā eva marmasthānabhūtāḥ āvedhyā ityuktam | yatra yatra vikāre jāte tattatpradeśasthitasirāsṛgvimocanaṃ kartavyamityuktam |
śironetravikāreṣu lalāṭyāṃ mocayetsirām |
ityādivacanāni bahūni santi | tattadvacanānusāreṇa tattatpradeśasthitasirā vedhyāḥ | vedhārhasirāṇāmavedhakatvapratipādanaparaṃ sirāsyasūtraṃ vyarthaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- rikteti |
riktātiriktātipūrṇāssirā na vedhyāḥ || 46 ||
riktāssirā hrasvavarṇoccāraṇahetubhūtāḥ | atiriktāssirā (dīrgha) varṇoccāraṇahetubhūtāḥ | atipūrṇāssvarāssvaritaplutavarṇoccāraṇahetubhūtāḥ |
nanu tatra saptaśatasirāṇāṃ madhye aṣṭāśītisirā varṇotpādakahetubhūtāḥ hrasvasaṃyogoccāraṇahetubhūtatattadvedhavijñānaviṣayakajñānaviṣayānubhavapramāpadavṛttinimittakāḥ susvarāḥ aṣṭāśītisirājanyapadaviṣayakapravṛttihetubhūtatvāt | tā na vedhyā iti brumaḥ | hrasvavarṇānāmadhomukhāssirāḥ tajjanakahetubhūtāḥ sirā riktāḥ | atiriktāssirāḥ saṃsargavarṇotpādakavakramārgagāḥ | atipūrṇāḥ sirā gatāgatodbodhakapravartakapavanagatakāryānukūlasusvarāḥ plutāśca | tāsāṃ karaṇatvaṃ pratipāditam | karaṇasya kriyāviśeṣavailakṣaṇyadyotakakāryahetubhūtatvāt | saptaśatasirāṇāṃ madhye aṣṭānavatisirāḥ sarandhrakābhyantaradharāḥ | tā eva riktātiriktātipūrṇāssirā na vedhyāḥ | kasya ciddehabhedena aṣṭānavatisirāḥ | kāścitsarandhrakābhyantaradharāḥ | sarveṣāmekaprakārakavarṇotpādakavarṇasamūhapadajanyavākyānāṃ tattaccharīrāvacchedadarśanāt | saptaśatasirāṇāṃ madhye tādṛśā eva notpādakā iti vyavacchettuṃ vaktumaśakyatvāt | tattaccharīrarūpavicitrasvaraśabdajñānaviṣayakaḥ ātmā pratiśarīraṃ bhinnaḥ sukhaduḥkhādivaicitryādibhedenopalabhyamānatvāt, śabdasvaravicitravarṇāṅgabhedadravyaguṇādhārakatvāt | tasmātparodbodhakatvasarvavarṇapradātmakānāṃ te vaicitryopalambhakatvāt | saptaśatasirāṇāṃ avedhakatvaṃ vaktuṃ vidheyāḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ bhedodbodhakāḥ | tasmātsaptaśatasirāṇāmadhomukhatvaṃ pratipāditamityarthaḥ |
"dhanvantaristu trīṇyāha sandhīnāṃ ca śatadvayam"
etadvacanamārabhya na hi dhanvantarimate |
"tadadhiṣṭhānamannasya grahaṇāt grahaṇī matā |
saiva dhanvantarimate kalā pittadharāhvayā ||"
etāvatpratipāditārthaṃ sadvarṇātmakapadārthapravṛttiviṣayakajñānagocarajñānakāryodbodhakasirāḥ saptaśatasaṅkhyākāḥ tā na vedhyā iti jñāpanārthaṃ "saiva dhanvantarimate" iti śārīre pratipāditam | tadevāyurvedapratipāditārthamidamiti vivicya darśanāt 'saiva' ityuktam āmapittaṃ paktvā pācakakalā suprasannā satī tadadhiṣṭhānavarṇānāṃ jñāpakatvāt grahaṇītyabhidhīyate | aviruddharasadravyādanājjātakalā prabalā satī suvarṇasusvarasupadasuvākyabodhikā | karṇaśaṣkulyavacchinnākāśasya etena āyurārogyatejodhātupratipādikā bhavatītyarthaḥ | atra sūtravacanaṃ--
unmārgagā yantranipīḍanena
svasthānamāyānti punarna yāvat |
doṣāḥ praduṣṭā rudhiraṃ prapannā
tāvaddhitāhāravihārabhāksyāt ||
nātyuṣṇaśītaṃ laghudīpanīyaṃ rakte'panīte hitamannapānam |
tadā śarīraṃ hyanavasthitāsramagnirviśeṣāditi rakṣaṇīyaḥ ||
savyādhinidānahetubhūtasya śabdoccāraṇādavagamya tadvyādhividhiriti jñānamupalabhya cikitsāṃ kārayediti | yadyantassthitavikāraṃ śabdoccāraṇādeva jñātvā sādhyavidhiṃ vijñāya kārayediti tātparyam | svarāsvaraṃ jīrṇahetukasirābhibodhakakāryaṃ duṣṭāsṛgvimocanadvārā sirāyā hetutvaṃ pratipāditamityarthaḥ | riktātiriktā tipūrṇasirāṇāmavedhyatvajñāpakaṃ yādṛśānāṃ svarūpajñāne sati vaktuṃ śakyate tatprayojanamapi tathā | tasmānmarmāśayamajñātvā tanniṣeddhumaśakyamityasvarasādāha-- tā iti |
tāssirā marmāśayagā riktāḥ || 47 ||
randhrasañcāre pavanasya yogādhārakatvāt sarandhrakāyasirāvacchedanena doṣaprakopo bhavati | tā na vedhyāḥ | yā sirā atiriktābhyantaradharadoṣasañcārārthaṃ avakāśābhāvadravyatvāt sarandhrā satī antarindriyaṃ bhūtvā vartate sā sirā na vedhyā marmasthānatadguṇahetubhūtatvāt | tatprayojanaṃ yāvadvarṇamātroccāraṇaṃ tadanvayādhīnam | tāssirā na vedhyāḥ | atipūrṇāssarvakāleṣu pavanapūritarandhreṣu doṣasaṃcārahetubhūtāḥ śrotrākāśasya svabhedaprāpyopakārakāḥ tāśca marmāśayagā ityarthaḥ |
nanu aṣṭanavatisirāṇāṃ madhye yatra yatra sthāne yatsirāśritavarṇadoṣaḥ pradṛśyate, kiṃ ca virasādanājjātagrahaṇīkalādaurbalyena tatrasthitavarṇadoṣaḥ gamanāgamanamārganirodhātpavanādiṣu vikāraśca pradṛśyate, tatra tattadvarṇotpādakasirāśrayasthaleṣu tattaddhetubhūtarasavirasajātarogāḥ prāpnuvanti | śiro'sthivikāreṣu satsu śirorogā iti vyavahriyante | sirāvikāreṣu netradeśeṣu satsu, tatrāyaṃ netrarogavāniti vyavahriyate | karṇadeśasthitasirāvikāreṣu satsu karṇarogavāniti vyavahriyate | tattatsirādhiṣṭhānasthitadoṣādayaḥ virasādanajātagrahaṇīkalādaurbalye tattadadhiṣṭhānasthitasirāmārgagamanābhāvajātarogāḥ gamanāgamanakṣobhavaśāt tattatsthānasthitasirāvikārān prāpnuvanti | tattadvikārān tāvatsirā eva bodhayanti | tattatpradeśādhiṣṭhitatvāt, tattaddeśopādhinā śirā roga-netraroga-karṇaroga-mukharoga-nāsikāroga-jihvāroga-kaṇṭharoga-bāhuroga-hṛtkukṣiroga-ajīrṇāgnimāndyādiroga-gulmaroga-meharoga-grahaṇyatisārārśoroga-śvāsaroga-romarājipradeśodbhavaśobhapāṇḍuroga-aṇḍajātaroga-jaṃghājānuroga-śroṇikaṭipradeśotthitarogabodhakasirā eva tattatpradeśeṣu vyādhīnsaṃsṛjya tattatpadeśasthitasirā eva vyādhīn jñāpayituṃ pavanagatyā tāvatsirā eva tattadvyādhilakṣaṇāni vijñāpayanti | tattadrogahetubhūtavirasādanāt kalādaurbalyāt āmaprakope sati tattadrasābhivardhakarasā eva tattadroganirvartakā ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya "tāssirā marmāśayagāḥ" ityāha | nanu aṣṭanavatisirāssaptaśatasirā riktātiriktātipūrṇāssirā na vedhyā iti pratipāditam | idānīṃ sarvarogeṣu tattadvidhicoditasirāgatāsṛgvimocanameva roganivartakasāmagrīti tatkāryāpekṣitatvāt vedhārhasirāṇāṃ pratipādanaṃ yuktam | tāssirāḥ kīdṛśāḥ ? tadjñānānantaraṃ tatpratipādakaṃ tadeva jñātumāvaśyakamityasvarasādāha--sireti |
sirāsṛgvibhāgavidhiṃ jñātvā vimocayet || 48
sirāgatāsṛgvimocanamātravidhiḥ | kāścidavedhyāḥ | tasmāt vedhyāvedhyatvapratipādanakarmaṇaḥ prastutatvāt tattatsirāgatāsṛggatakāryasya tatsirāgatāsṛggatarogasya nivṛttyarthaṃ sirāsṛgvimocanaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | na ca tattadroganivṛttyarthaṃ taddhetukasirābhedanaṃ avasaratvāt marmasthānādhiṣṭhitasirāvyatirekeṇa taddhetukasirābhedavijñānaviṣayakajñānaṃ grahītumaśakyamiti vācyam | yatra viruddharasādanāt jātājīrṇajanyāmayā eva doṣavikāragatayo bhavantīti tasmāddoṣapacanaparyantaṃ laṅghanaṃ kāryamiti | yatra viruddharasādanā jjātājīrṇajanyāmayavikāro viruddhadhātuprabhavo bhavati tatra tenoccāritaśabdabhedātsirā vijñāya tatsthānagatavarṇaviruddhamidaṃ varṇamiti tadvarṇajanakasirāsthitiṃ vijñāya doṣadhātugatyā'sṛgvikāro yatra dṛśyate tatra duṣṭāśayaṃ vijñāya tattaddhetukasirājālaṃ ca vijñāya taddhetukṛtāmayādhiṣṭhānasthalaṃ vijñāya sirāvedhanakarmaṇāṃ taddhetujanyāmayo nivartya iti "sirāsṛgvibhāgavidhiṃ jñātvā vimocayet" iti sūtramīritamityarthaḥ | tatsarvamāyurvedadvitīyapraśne uktamityalamatiprasaṅgena ||
nanu prajājananahetubhūtaṃ vārdhikadravyameva bhavati | itthameva prathamasūtre pratipāditam | yāvadvikāro yatra dṛśyate tadānīmeva tannivartakena sa nivartito bhavatīti pratipāditam | yāvadvikāre'pi laṅghanenaiva nivartata iti kathaṃ pratipāditam ? kathametatsirāsṛgvimocanena kāryamityasvarasādāha-- nīrujeti |
nīrujāvayavādīdṛśau pitarau || 49 ||
tayośca sarvāvayavāḥ puṣṭāssantaḥ prajotpādakā bhavantīti teṣāmavayavānāṃ raktadhātuvikāre sati pratibandhakaṃ bhavati | tadavikārakaraṇārthamayaṃ vidhiriti, tathā mātāpitroravayavāḥ avikāraphalaṃ prayacchantīti sūtramīritam |
prasannavarṇendriyamindriyārthān icchanta ityāhataraktavegam |
sukhānvitaṃ puṣṭibalopavarṇaṃ viśuddharaktaṃ puruṣaṃ vadanti ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa puruṣapadaṃ mithunopayogipadapratipādakatvāt striyamapi viṣayīkaroti, prajājanane ubhayorapi saṃyogasya hetutvāt | nanu pūrvoktarītyā arogakāryajanyaphalapradānameva sirāsṛgvimocanakarmaṇaḥ prayojanamityuktam | etena tayoḥ kāryakaraṇa bhāvaḥ pratipāditaḥ | mātāpitrośca śarīraviṣayakaśāstravijñānajanyakarmaṇaḥ phalaṃ śarīrāvayavadārḍhyakaraṇasāmagrīsampādanānubhavāvayavāvayavitvaṃ sampadyate | tayoḥ strīpuruṣayośśuklaṃ cārtavaṃ ca tau tathābhūtau garbhāśaye piṇḍatvena pratiṣṭhitau | tābhyāmeva bījātmakābhyāmavikārabhūtābhyāṃ prajājananahetubhūtābhyāṃ satvamāvirbhavati | tatra śārīravacanaṃ--
śuddhe śukrārtave sattvaḥ svakarmakleśacoditaḥ |
garbhassampadyate yuktivaśādagnirivāraṇau ||
nanu āmayakārakasirāgatāsṛgvimocanamātreṇa śuklārtavau | pariśuddhāvityuktam | na tāvanmātreṇa saṅgacchate'duṣṭāśayaḥ | aviruddhadoṣaśuddhendriyaśuddhānalā ete vārdhikadravyādanādbhāvyā etatsūtragatasirāgataduṣṭaraktavimocanamātrādeva | idānīṃ śukrārtavau bījotpādakahetubhūtāviti pārthivadravyādanaṃ vinā kathaṃ bhavetāmityasvarasādāha-- śuddhoti |
śuddhāśayaśuddhadoṣaśuddhānalaśuddhendriyaguṇahetukapārthivadravyaiḥ prajāḥ prajāyante || 50 ||
vārdhikadravyādanādeva sarvāśayāḥ pariśuddhā bhavanti | indriyāṇi cāvikāradoṣāṇi | tathā pañcānilāśca pañca pācapittāni cāduṣṭāssantaḥ laghuguṇakārakavārdhikadravyādanādbhavanti | kiṃ ca sātvikadravyādanaṃ sāttvikaguṇahetukam | rājasadravyādanaṃ rājasaguṇakārakam | tāmasadravyādanaṃ ca tāmasaguṇakārakam | vārdhikadravyādanājjātāśayādikaṃ vinā bījotpādanaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ |
idānīṃ śarīrajananakārye tatpratipādakasāmagrī sampādanīyā | sā āvaśyakī bhavati | āśayāśca doṣāśca analāśca indriyāṇi ca vārdhikadravyādanātte tu pariśuddhāssantaḥ śarīraṃ puṣṇanti na te śarīrotpādakā iti ādau śarīrotpādanakārye tadutpādakasāmagrīsampādanaṃ vinā kathaṃ śarīrotpādanasya āśayādīnāṃ ca sambandho ghaṭate ? ityasvarasādāha-- śukreti |
śukraśoṇitasannipāto yoniḥ || 51 ||
puruṣajanyaṃ śukram | strījanyaṃ śoṇitam | tayorhetubhūtaṃ yoniḥ | sānipātyaṃ saṃsargahetubhūtātmakaṃ tayossānnipātyamabhidhīyate | śukrasya sarvadā bījotpādakatvaṃ vaktumaśakyam | kintu āśayāḥ pariśuddhāssantaḥ tathā prajājananahetubhūtā bhavanti | strījanyaśoṇitasyāpi tathaiva | yogyakālastrīpuruṣasaṃyogavyāpārahetubhūtadivyayonidṛḍhatarasaṃparkāt bījādhārapradeśo garbhāśaya iti tajjanakatvasya ca suprasiddhatvāt | yonāveva satvaḥ garbhassan tadanantaraṃ garbhāśayaṃ praviśya vivardhata ityarthaḥ | nanu asminkārye āśayāḥ pariśuddhā bhavantu | taddvārā śukraśoṇite api pariśuddhe ca tayoreva bījātmakatvāt | kathaṃ tadedhate ? kathaṃ vivardhate ? kathaṃ ceṣṭāśrayaṃ calanāmakaṃ ceti vaktuṃ śakyate ? ityasvarasādāha-- yonyāmiti |
yonyāmāvirabhūdajo vidhicoditaḥ || 52 ||
ajo jīvātmā yoniṃ praviśya garbhāśaye āvirabhūt adṛśyata |
"retomātraṃ vijahati | yoniṃ praviśadindriyaṃ garbho jarāyuvṛtaḥ | ulbaṃ jahāti janmanā | ṛtena satyamindriyam |" iti śrutervidyamānatvāt |
nanu śuklaśoṇitasannipāte piṇḍe satvaṃ praviśya garbhāśaye yatsthitaṃ tadeva śarīram | lakṣaṇaṃ tu "pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīram" iti | tatpiṇḍānāṃ āvirbhāvaḥ kena kāraṇena bhāvyaḥ ? evaṃ gaganaṃ nityadravyam | pṛthivyādicatuṣṭayamanityam | tatra rūpadravyagrāhakaṃ tejaḥ | tattejaścakṣurgolake sthitvā sarvārthān paśyati | tadviṣayakajñāne cakṣuḥ kāraṇam | tajjanakībhūtaṃ ca lavaṇarasadravyaṃ kāraṇam | tadviṣayajñānakāryasya hetuhetumadbhāvapratipādanaṃ vinā pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti lakṣaṇaṃ na saṅgacchata ityata āha--yāvaditi |
yāvadāhārānuguṇarūpavān bhavati || 53 ||
yāvaddravyādanayāvatkāryānuguṇānalādidravyaṃ yāvadrasavaddravyādanajanyasvādurasodbhūtadravyaguṇakam | rasanendriyaviṣayakaṃ tacca jihvāgravarti sarvarasādibhedaṃ vivicya gṛhṇāti | svādurasavaddravyādanajanyālpadravyābhivardhakakāryānuguṇakasvādurasavaddravye svādurasavadviṣayakajñānavānayamiti | yāvaddravyādanajanyasvādurasābhivardhakanāsāgravarti ghrāṇendriyaṃ gandharasavadgrāhakaṃ sarvagandhābhivyañjakapārthivadravyaviśiṣṭagandhavadviṣayakajñānavānayamiti | yāvallavaṇarasadravyādanajanyānalābhivardhakacakṣurindriyaṃ netrāntargolavartirūpādibhedaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānavānayamiti | tiktarasadravyādanajanyapavanabhūtābhivardhakakāryānuguṇapavanaṃ bhūtadravyasparśavadviṣayakajñānagocarajñānavānayamiti | evamuktarītyā pārthivādicatuṣṭayābhivardhakakāryānuguṇavaddravyaṃ tattajjihvendriyaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānakāryahetukaṃ yāvadrasavaddravyādanajanyaṃ yattattadindriyaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānahetukamiti pradarśitam | vyatirekavyāpteḥ dṛṣṭatvāt | tasmādanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvo gṛhīta ityarthaḥ | tadarthaṃ sarvamanumānapramāṇena vivicya jñāpyate |
pṛthivītvaviśiṣṭadravyaṃ pṛthivyasaṃyogajanyasvādurasavadjñāna viṣayakaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇānadhikaraṇakarasanendriyagrāhyatve sati tadviṣayakajñānagocarapratyayahetukatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ, yathā kālādiḥ |
analatvavaddravyaviśiṣṭapṛthivīdravyaṃ āmlarasavadviṣayakajñānaviṣayakaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇānadhikaraṇakarasanendriyagrāhyatve sati āmlarasavaddravyaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānakāryahetukatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ, yathā kālādiḥ |
abdravyaviśiṣṭānalatvavaddravyaṃ lavaṇarasajñānaviṣayakaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇānadhikaraṇakarasanendriyagrāhyatve sati lavaṇarasavadviṣayakajñānagocarajñānakāryahetukatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā kālādiḥ |
gaganaviśiṣṭānalatvavaddravyaṃ tiktarasavadjñānaviṣayakaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇānadhikaraṇakarasanendriyagrāhyatve sati tiktarasavaddravyaviṣayakajñānagocarapratyayakāryakāraṇatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ, yathā kālādiḥ |
analatvaviśiṣṭānaladravyaṃ ūṣaṇarasavaddravyaviṣayakaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇānadhikaraṇakarasanendriyagrāhyatve sati ūṣaṇarasavaddravyaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānakāryahetukatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā kālādiḥ |
pṛthivītvaviśiṣṭānaladravyaṃ kaṣāyarasavajjñānaviṣayakaṃ, svasamānādhikaraṇānadhikaraṇakarasanendriyagrāhyatve sati kaṣāyarasavadarthaviṣayakajñānagocarakāryadahetukatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ, yathā kālādiḥ
atra sūtravacanam--
kṣmāmbho'gnikṣmāmbutejaḥkhavāyvagnyanilago'nilaiḥ |
dvayolbaṇaiḥ kramādbhūtaiḥ madhurādirasodbhavaḥ ||
nanvasyānumānasyānumitigrahe sati vyāptigrahapūrvakatvaṃ vaktavyam | atrānumitisvarūpaṃpṛ--thivītvavatsaṃsargadravyolbaṇajanyasvādurasavaddravyaviṣayakasvasamānādhikaraṇānadhikaraṇakarasanendriyagrāhyatvavaddravyaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānatvaṃ anumitiriti | tatsaṃsargajanyasvādurasasya rasanendriyaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānakāryatvaṃ, tayorvyāptigrahe sati anumityudayāt |
nanu dhūmāgnyoriva kāryakāraṇabhāve gṛhīte svādurasasya pākadoṣanivartakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate | tatra dhūmatvāvacchedena kāryakāraṇabhāvagrahe svādurasamātrasya vyādhinivartakatve ucyamāne atiprasaṅgāt ityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadārogyadā rasā nivartakāḥ || 54 ||
svādvamlalavaṇatiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasāḥ yāvacchabdārthabodhakāḥ te rasā eva nivartakāḥ ārogyadā ityarthaḥ |
nanu sarvaśarīriṇāṃ yadā vyādhayassambhavanti tadānīmeva teṣāṃ nivartakatvaṃ vaktavyam | karaṇasya tu kriyāviśeṣamātravailakṣaṇyatayā tasya phalāyāvyavacchinnatvaniyamāt |
vṛthā'pathyaṃ na kurvīta vṛthā bheṣajasevanam |
iti vacanānusāreṇa vyāptigrahaṃ vaktumaśakyatvādityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadabhyavahṛtarasebhyo mātṛjaṃ pālayet || 55 ||
yāvadabhyavahṛtarasebhyaḥ yāvadbhuktarasebhyaḥ mātṛjaṃ mātṛpiṇḍaṃ yāvatsaptadhātūn pālayet rakṣayet | rasebhyo jātadhātūnāṃ saṃrakṣaṇaṃ kārayedityarthaḥ |
nanu svādvādiṣaḍrasebhyaḥ saptadhātūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ kārayedityuktam | tadeva jñātumāvaśyakam | aviruddhakaṣāyarasādane vaktavye sati kaṣāyarasavaddravyādanasya rasāsṛgdhātupravardhakapuruṣārthasya ca hetumadbhāvasya sattvāt tayoreva kāryakāraṇabhāvo nānyeṣām | saptadhātupradatvaṃ tattajjanakarasādīnāṃ vaktuṃ śakyata ityata āha-- reta iti |
reto'dhikātpituḥ putro bhavati || 56 ||
yadā puruṣasthitaṃ tejaḥ garbhe āvirbhavati tatkālamārabhya puruṣavikārakāryaikaphalaṃ sāmarthyaṃ rasādayaḥ prayacchanti | mātā svādurasavaddravyādanakalā svādurasahetukaśukladhātupravardhanaṃ kurvantī adyāt | tasmātpitṛsadṛśaḥ putro labhyate | "ātmā vai putranāmāsi" iti śrutervidyamānatvādityarthaḥ |
mātussvādurasavaddravyādanajātaśoṇitasyāpi piṇḍaṃ śarīrābhivardhakam | tena svādurasajanyakāryaphalapradānasya tasya svabhāvatvāt yāvadantarvatnī patnī bhavati tāvatkālaṃ rasādayaḥ puruṣāṇāmiva strīṇāmapi dhātupradā bhavantīsyāha-- rakteti |
raktādhikānmātuḥ putrikā bhavati || 57 ||
tacchoṇitasyāpi svādurasajanyatvena piṇḍaṃ śarīrābhivardhakakāryahetubhūtaṃ bhavatīti tātparyārthaḥ | raktādhikapraveśanāt mātṛsadṛśaḥ atiraktapraveśanāt jātapiṇḍe dṛśyate |
mṛdvatra mātṛjaṃ raktamāṃsamajjāsṛgādikam |
paitṛkaṃ tu sthiraṃ śukraṃ dhamanyasthikacādikam ||
sarvamapi paitṛkaṃ nāma mātusvādurasavaddravyādanajātaśukramāṃsamajjāretaādikaṃ mātṛrasāhārajātadhātavassantaḥ tatraiva edhante tathaiva pitussvādurasadravyādanajātā api | pitṛsambandhidhamanyasthikacādikaṃ tanmithunakāle na jāyate | kintu tathotpattihetubhūtamātureva śuklaśoṇitasannipātamātraṃ tadānīmupasthitam | tadanantaramevābhivardhakaṃ mātuḥ rasāhārādikaṃ na kimapyanyataram | yāvaccharīrotpattiparyantaṃ ādimadhyāntajātarogārogaikakāraṇamiti | tasmāttadārogyaikahetukaṃ dravyādanaṃ pratimāsaṃ pratyekaṃ nivartakaṃ vidhiriti atra sūcyate | putrotpādanadravyādanamapi putrikotpādanadravyādanamapi dvayormithunakāle śukraśoṇite api same syātāṃ cettatra kā gatirityāśaṅkāyāmāha-- dvisama iti |
dvisamo yatra ṣaṇḍo bhavati || 58 ||
ajo nānā jāyate || 59 ||
pūrvoktarītyā mātṛrasādanaṃ tatra heturityarthaḥ | na jāyata ityajaḥ nānārūpavān bahurūpassan sa jīvaḥ jāyate iti sūtrārthaḥ |
nanu idamapyanupapannam | etadutpannapiṇḍaḥ aja iti pratipādyate | rogārogyaikakāraṇapratipādakasāmagrīkāryaṃ vyarthaṃ syāt | tasya ajanyatvamavināśitvaṃ cobhayamapi svata eva siddham | ārogyakāryahetubhūtakāraṇapratipādakaśāstramapi vyarthaṃ syāt ityasvarasādāha-- māsīti |
māsi māsi rajaḥ strīṇām || 60 ||
māse māse sriyaḥ rajasvalā bhavanti | taccatuṣṭayadivasaparyantaṃ sparśayogyā na bhavantītyarthaḥ | piṇḍotpādakasakalasā magryāṃ satyāṃ ajo vidhicoditassan rajasvalāśarīraṃ viśati | aje āvirbhūte sati tāvatkālopasthitasāmagrī saphalā bhavati | tatsamparkavaśāt jīvasya aupādhikānityatvaṃ aupādhikarūpatvaṃ ajñānitvaṃ ābhāsajñānavattvaṃ jīvātmani bhāsate | tathaiva vyavahriyate-- vṛddho'yaṃ śyāmo'yaṃ brāhmaṇo'yamiti | idānīṃ ahaṃ sukhī ahaṃ duḥkhīti etādṛśapratyayānupapattyā rajasvalāsaṃsargadoṣavaśāt jīvātmanaḥ kathaṃ karma phalādeśaṃ prayacchati, aupādhikasukhaduḥkhānubhavaṃ prāpnoti na tu svatassiddhamiti, atmatvāt nityatvācca aupādhikasukhaduḥkhādikaṃ labhate na svatassiddhamityarthaḥ | tasya rajasvalāsamparkadoṣa iti vyapadeśaḥ | tasmātsā cāṇḍālīva na tāṃ spṛśet nābhibhāṣeta | tatra śrutiḥ--
"tasmānmalavadvāsasā na saṃvadeta na sahāsīta nāsyā annamadyāt brahmahatyāyai hyeṣā varṇaṃ pratimucyāste'tho khalvāhuḥ abhyañjanaṃ vāva striyā annamabhyañjanameva na pratigṛhyaṃ kāmamanyat"
ityārabhya
"tasyai kharvastisro rātrīrvrataṃ caret añjalinā vā pibet akharveṇa vā pātreṇa prajāyai gopīthāya "
etatpratipāditārthaḥ rajasvalāyāssaṃsargadoṣamahimnā abhūt | atra śārīravacanaṃ--
kṣaireyaṃ yāvakaṃ stokaṃ koṣṭhaśodhanakarṣaṇam |
parṇe śarāve haste vā bhuñjīta brahmacāriṇī ||
caturthe'hni tatassnātvā śuklamālyāmbarā śuciḥ |
icchantī bhartṛsadṛśaṃ putraṃ paśyetpuraḥ patim ||
iti śāstrānusāreṇa "ajo nānārūpaḥ pururūpa īyate" iti sūtraṃ sārthakamityarthaḥ, bahuśrutipratipādakatvāt |
cirātprajotpādakasakalasāmagrīsāmarthye sati sa jīvaḥ ṛtumatīṃ striyaṃ praviśya sukhaduḥkhādhikaṃ pāpavaśādanubhavati | tatparihārārthaṃ snānādi, tena tatparihāro bhaviṣyatītyāha-- caturtha iti |
caturthe'hni snāyāt || 61 ||
caturthe divase tatpāpaparihārārthaṃ snānaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | tadanantaraṃ iṣṭadevatābhajanaṃ gurudevatābhajanaṃ devabrāhmaṇapūjanaṃ prajānugrahārthaṃ devatāprārthanaṃ ca kuryādityarthaḥ | ahaṃ putrakāminī tatsaṃpadavāptyarthaṃ patiṃ vrajāmīti pariśuddhā satī patisaṃsargaṃ kuryādityāha-- śuddheti |
śuddhā patiṃ vrajet || 62 ||
satputrāvāptirbhūyādityarthaḥ ||
nanu yathā strī rajasvalā ṛtumatī jātā satī tata ārabhya brahmacaryādivratācaraṇena garbhotpattirme bhūyāditi saṅkalpya caturthe snānaṃ kṛtvā pativrajanaṃ vidhirityupalabhyate | taddinavyatiriktakāle mithune jāte sati garbhotpatteradṛṣṭatvāt | tatra pūrvadinakartavyatākāryamaṅgatvenāpratipāditamiti na kopyanavasaraḥ ||
nanu caturthe'hni snāyāditi vidhiḥ | tatpūrvadinatrayaṃ vratācaraṇasya vidheyatvāt adṛṣṭādisakalasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ avilambena kāryotpatterdṛṣṭatvāt caturthadinaparyantaṃ nirīkṣaṇasya ca prayojakatvāt tāvatkālābhāvasya utpattirūpakāryābhāvasya hetumadbhāvasya dṛṣṭatvāt caturthe'hni snānaṃ kṛtvā patyā sākaṃ saṃsargakāryasya vidheyasya balatvāt ityasvarasādāha-- prajeti ||
prajākāmāstāvatprayatnaṃ mithunaṃ tāvaddhetukam ||
prajāsampādanaphalamavaśyaṃ me bhūyāditi kāmanayā pravṛttasya puṃsaḥ caturdaśadinaparyaṃntaṃ nirīkṣaṇasya ko heturityākāṅkṣāṃyāṃ tatra hetuṃ brūmaḥ-- rajasvalādinādārabhya sapta dhātavaḥ raktarūpeṇa sravanti | tadā strīṇāṃ saptamadhātuprasrāvaṇaṃ yato labhyate punniṣṭhacaramadhātuprasrāvaṇaṃ ekakāle yonyāṃ sannipāto yadi bhavet tadānīmavilambena kāryotpattirbhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ | puṃsastāvatkālanirīkṣaṇaṃ yuktamiti caturdaśadinaparyantaṃ sapta dhātavo raktarūpā bhavanti tāvatkālayogyamiti | tathāhi caturthe dine caramadhātuprasrāvaṇasya yogyatā | ahaśca rātriśca ekakālaṃ ekaikadhātuprasrāvaṇakālaḥ yatropalabhyate tāvatkālo yogyakāla iti | caturtharātrau caramadhātuḥ striyaḥ raktarūpeṇa sravati | tadānīmeva garbhotpattirbhavati yāvatkālaṃ puruṣadhātuprasrāvaṇaniyamo yatropalabhyate tatra tāvatkālamuddiśya trayodaśadinaparyantaṃ yogyaṃ pakṣamātraṃ garbhāśayaṃ prāpayituṃ yonipadmasya vikasitākāreṇa sthitatvāt | tatrobhayossānnipātyaparyantaṃ vikasitaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | sānnipātye mukulībhavatītyarthaḥ | pakṣamātramunmīlanībhavati | pakṣamātraṃ nimīlanībhavati | pakṣadvayaṃ māsa iti "māsimāsi rajaḥ strīṇāṃ" | iti atra strīṇāṃ pakṣamātraṃ raktasrāve sati sa doṣāya bhavatītyarthaḥ | atra śārīrasūtravacanaṃ-
ṛtustu dvādaśa niśāḥ pūrvāstisraśca ninditāḥ |
ekādaśī ca yugmāsu syātputro'nyāsu kanyakā ||
upādhyāyo'tha putrīyaṃ kurvīta vidhivadvidhim |
iti śārīrasūtravacanānusāreṇa yugmadineṣu putrotpādakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ putro bhavati | tadanyadineṣu putrikājanakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ putrikā bhavatītyartha upalabhyate | tasyāścaramadhātureva raktaṃ bhavati | tattacchuklasānnipātyaṃ yatkāle bhavati sa kālaḥ putraṃ putrikāṃ ca prayacchati |
ghṛtamākṣikatailābhaṃ sadgarbhāyārtavaṃ punaḥ |
lākṣārasaśaśāsrābhaṃ dhautaṃ yacca virajyate |
śuddhaśukrārtavaṃ svacchaṃ sarakaṃ mithunaṃ mithaḥ |
garbhotpādaśca raktaviśeṣe vaktavyaḥ | tadviśeṣastu śuklavikāra eva, saptadhātvātmakaṃ śarīramiti sarvasirāṇāṃ saptadhātvāvṛtatvāt | strīṇāṃ śuddhadhāturetādṛśaraktarūpo bhavatītyarthaḥ | puruṣajananasya hetuḥ kāla eva | kiṃ strījananasyāpi tadevetyata āha-- dvīti |
dvicatuḥṣaṣṭhāṣṭadaśadvādaśe'hani jātāḥ putrāḥ taditare'hani jātāḥ putrikāḥ || 64 ||
nanu utpannapiṇḍasya poṣakasvaṃ vaktavyam | tatpoṣakadravyairbhāvyam | tatrāpi kālaviśeṣo vaktavyaḥ | sa kālaḥ poṣako bhavatu | nanu śuklaśoṇitasannipāte jāte garbho'pi bhavati | tatraiva māse ṛtumatyā ca pradarśitāni dṛṣṭāni garbhacihnāni | tadrakṣaṇārthaṃ prathamartau poṣakadravyaṃ pāyayedityata āha-- prathameti |
prathamartau poṣakaṃ nivartakam || 65 ||
yadā garbhalakṣaṇamapi jātaṃ saiva ca ṛtumatīti dṛṣṭā, cikitsā tatra poṣakadravyādanaṃ tatra vidhiḥ | dvitīyamāse poṣakadravyādanaṃ tatra pratīkāro bhavati | evaṃ ca daśamāsaparyantaṃ poṣakaśoṣakacikitsā vihitā | tayā garbhaṃ saṃrakṣya sukhena putraṃ sūte ityāha-- taditi |
tadūrdhve ṛtau śoṣakaṃ tadanantaramapi śoṣakarasāḥ garbhābhivardhakā yāvatkālopayogyāḥ || 66 ||
yathāyathopayogayogyakaraṇasevārhāṇi aninditāni ārogyakāryahetubhūtāni āgāmirogābhāvakāryakāraṇāni yāvacchaktidoṣākārakāṇi vidhivadvivicya jñāpyate-- prathameti |
prathamadvitrimāseṣu madhurarasāḥ pavanaharā nivartakāḥ || 67 ||
amlalavaṇarasopalambhakadravyābhyāṃ viśiṣṭaṃ svādurasavaddravyaṃ antarvatnyāḥ prathamadvitrimāseṣu praśastaṃ bhavati | etadanyonyasaṃsargajātarasavaddravyaṃ viruddharasajātapavanaprakoparogāṅkurahetukadravyādanaṃ pavanaharaṃ sakalaśarīropayogyaṃ sakalajīvamātrasādhāraṇam | tattajjātivihitapadārthāssevyā ityarthaḥ | tāvatkālamātraṃ tāvadrasādanena nivartakena nivartyāḥ nivartayantītyuktam | taditarakāle'pi viruddharasādanakāryasya sambhāvitatvāt tatrāpi garbhābhivardhakadravyādanasyāvaśyakatvāt yāvatkālopayogyaṃ yathāyathaṃ vidheyakāryahetubhūtānyaninditāni āgāmirogābhāvakāryakārakāṇi tāvaddravyāṇi vivicya prakāśyante-- caturiti |
catuḥpañcaṣaṭsu māseṣu amlarasāḥ pittaharā nivartakāḥ || 68 ||
svādulavaṇarasopalambhakadravyābhāvaviśiṣṭāmlarasavaddravyaṃ garbhiṇyāḥ catuḥpañcaṣaṭsu māseṣu pāyayitavyam | etadanyonyābhāvapratiyogyabhāvadravyaṃ amlarasavaddravyaṃ viruddharasāhārajanyapitta prakopajātakāryahetukadravyābhāvāt anyadravyādanaṃ sakalapitaprakopaśamanaṃ sakalaśarīrārogyakārakaṃ sakalajanopakārakahetubhūtaṃ tattajjātivihitāmlarasavaddravyaṃ garbhiṇīṃ pāyayedityarthaḥ | tenaiva garbho vardhate tattadaṅgaprakopakāraṇaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | antarvartnyāḥ ārogyaviṣaye yāvatsāmlarasavaddravyāṇāṃ nivartakatvaṃ pratipāditam | taditararasavaddravyāṇāṃ prayojyābhāvena teṣāmaprayojyatvaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- ṣaṣṭheti |
ṣaṣṭhāṣṭanavamāseṣūṣṇarasāḥ kaphaśoṣakāḥ pravartakāḥ || 69 ||
ṣaṣṭhāṣṭanavamāseṣu svādvamlarasopalambhakadravyābhāvaviśiṣṭoṣṇarasavaddravyaṃ antarvatnyāmayaviṣaye tattaddravyādanaṃ vidheyam | tattadanyonyābhāvapratiyogyabhāvadravyaṃ uṣṇarasadravyamiti | tadviruddhā rasāhārajātakaphakopakāryaṃ dravyābhāvadravyādanaṃ sakalakaphadoṣajātarukprakopaśamanakāryaṃ sakalaśarīrārogyakārakaṃ sakalajīvanopakārakahetubhūtaṃ tattajjātividhivihitoṣṇarasavaddravyaṃ garbhiṇyāḥ pāyayedityalam ||
nanu vātapittakaphadoṣanivartakatvaṃ ṣaḍrasānāmeva pratipāditam | uktarītyā svādvamlalavaṇarasavaddravyāṇāṃ doṣatrayasthitavikāraṃ nivartayitumaśakyatvāt "tatrādyā mārutaṃ ghnanti" iti vacanānusāreṇa upapāditavikāranivartakatvaṃ svādvamlalavaṇarasavaddravyāṇāṃ pratipāditaṃ na tiktoṣaṇarasavaddravyāṇāmiti cet na; rasapāke tatha trayāṇāmeva prayojakatvapratipādanāt tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasānāṃ tatraivāntarbhāvāt, kiṃca animitte nivartakadravyasyāprayojakatvāt |
na ca tatprāgabhāvaparipālanaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavediti vaktuṃ śakyate, sarvatrāpi tathātve atiprasaṅgādityasvarasādāha-- nivartya iti |
nivartyo vikāraḥ || 70 ||
nivartayituṃ yogyo nivartyaḥ, sa vikāraḥ | nivartakasya vikāraharaṇaṃ vicāraṇīyam | sarvavyādhiprāgabhāvasya sarvadā vidyamānatvāt | tasyedamidamiti pratyekāvacchedena tattannivartakatvasya nirṇetumaśakyatvāt | tasmāttadā vikāre jāte sati tattannivartakatvasya nirṇetumaśakyatvāt nivartyo vikāra iti sūtre pratipādita ityarthaḥ ||
nanu vikāre sati svādvamlalavaṇarasāḥ vikārapratikārakā iti pūrvameva pratipāditam | taccintyaṃ, "nivartyo vikāraḥ" ityatra rasādyā nivartakā na bhavantītyasvarasādāha-- āmeti |
āmavṛdvivikārassarvarogahetubhūtaḥ || 71 ||
ata eva yaḥ pācakapittena tūtpādyate so'nalaḥ pakvāśayagatārthameva pacati | tadviruddharasajanmāmayasya malāśayasthitatvāt | pārthivaddravyāvayavopādhikarasopalambhakasugandhaguṇabhūyiṣṭhapākaupādhikayogyasvādurasadravyaṃ poṣakadravyaṃ, analāvayavādhikalavaṇoṣṇarasavaddravyalaghutūlikāvayavajātakṣāraguṇakataditararasavaddravyātiriktadravyaṃ adravyādhikāvayavasnehavaddravyaṃ, svāduguṇabhūyiṣṭhapāke jaṭharānalapradaṃ tiktajñānaviṣayakaṃ yat tatpārthivadravyam | tatpatrabhedadravyeṣu trividhopakārakatvaṃ, puṣpaphalabhedadravyeṣu trividhadoṣopakārakatvaṃ, phalabhedadravyeṣu trividhopakārakatvam | tattajjātividhivihitadravyāṇi tattadvikārapratikārakāṇīti tattallakṣaṇeṣu śarīreṣu yogyānīti viśeṣitavyam | na tatra sthānāṃtaratattajjātīya vikārāṇāṃ nivartakabhāvaḥ | anvayavyatirekasahacaragamyatvaṃ yatropalabhyate tatrāyaṃ niyamaḥ | tadarthaṃ tattaddravyamityuktam | yāvatkāleṣu tattatkālocitarasavaddravyāṇāṃ nivartakatvaṃ niyamena yatropalabhyate tatrāyaṃ niyamaḥ | tadarthamuktaṃ tattatkāleti | yāvanto rogāḥ yāvannivartakakāryaviṣayāḥ iti niyamena kāryakāraṇabhāvā yatra dṛśyante, ‘yatsatve yatsatvaṃ yadabhāve yadabhāvaḥ' iti niyamena vyāptigraho yatra tatrāyaṃ niyamaḥ | tadarthamuktaṃ tadviṣaya iti | tattajjātivihitavikāreṣu nivartakā iti sūtrārthaḥ | nānājātistharogānivartyā ityuktam | sarvanivartakajñāne sati tathā vaktuṃ śakyate | sarvapadārthasvarūpasya jñātumaśakyatvādityata āha--nivartya iti | nivartyo vikāraḥ | śoṣakapoṣakavārdhikaviṣayakadravyatvāvacchedakajñānagocarajñānopāya iti vijñāya sarvapadārthaviṣayakatvāt puṣpaphalasārādidravyaṃ teṣu aprayojanam | kaṣāyacūrṇatailaghṛtalehyarasāyanayogeṣu tattadvidhivihitadravyeṣu tathoktatvādidaṃ pathyāpathyavidhivicārajñānaviṣayakadravyādanayogyabhojyakramaṃ agre pratipādayiṣyāma ityarthaḥ |
nanu patrapuṣpaphalādīnāṃ phalavatpravṛttiviṣayakaṃ kaṣāyatailacūrṇaghṛtalehyarasāyanādiṣu bhinnaprakārakaṃ pānabhojyadravyeṣu tadbhinnaprakārakam | sarvadravyāṇāṃ dvividhopakārakatvaṃ taccintyam | sarvajātiṣu sarvakāleṣu sarvadravyeṣu sarvavyādhiṣu anusyūtaprakārakatāvacchedakaṃ ekaikasyaiveti vaktavyam | anvayavyatirekapramāṇābhyāṃ yasya kasya kāryakāraṇabhāvo gṛhītaḥ tatraivocitamityasvarasādāha-- tattaditi |
tattaddravyaṃ tattatkāle tattadviṣaye tattannivartyeṣu nivartakam || 72 ||
yāvatpadārthā yāvatkāleṣu yāvadrogaviṣayeṣu nivartakatvena kāryakāraṇatvena yāvajjātiṣu santi teṣu tattajjātivihitapadārthā ityarthaḥ |
tadeva pratipādayati-- nivartakamiti |
nivartakaṃ bheṣajam || 73 ||
nanu nivartyaguṇaviśeṣyatvasya vikārasya nivartakatvaṃ ūrdhvādhovirekakaraṇamityuktam | tadanantaraṃ yaddoṣahetukaṃ bhavati tajjanyadoṣaprakope sati tadvirecanakāryamayogyaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- doṣeti |
doṣatrayahetukaḥ || 74 ||
doṣatrayakāryasya virecanakāryasya yuktatvāt tattannivartakatvena pratipādayitumaśakyamityarthaḥ |
yatpratipādanena āmavṛddherdoṣajanyatvaṃ pratipāditaṃ bhavet ityasvarasādāha-- sarveti |
sarvajantūnāmanāmapālanaṃ nivartakam || 75 ||
sarvabhūtānāṃ anāmapālanaṃ puruṣārthaḥ | doṣe prathamamāmavirecanaṃ karaṇīyam | tathāhi-- āmaprakope sati tannivṛttikaraṇaṃ jantūnāmanāmapālanaṃ puruṣārthakaṃ bhavati |
nanu anāmapālanaṃ pārthivadravyahetukaṃ na bhavati | tat alpāhārādanaṃ dhātupoṣakaṃ doṣopakārakaṃ ca bhavedityasvarasādāha-- vahnīti |
vahnipravardhakadravyaṃ yāvaccharīropabṛṃhaṇajaṭharānalapravardhakadravyam || 76 ||
dhātupoṣakaṃ vahnipravardhakatvāt | yenaiva anāmapālanamapi abhivardhate tenaiva tadeva poṣakaṃ tadeva śoṣakaṃ ca samaśoṣakakaraṇapoṣakakaraṇam | dravyabhede sati tadvaktuṃ śakyate | vahnipravardhakadravyādanamātreṇa caritārthatvābhāvāt | kiṃ tu snehavaddravyamiti vaktavyam, taddravyasya śoṣakāryahetukatvādityāha-- tattaditi |
tattadarthāssnehayogyāḥ pravardhakāḥ || 77 ||
snehavaddravyaṃ vahnipravardhakapadārthatāvacchedakatvena ekaprakārakatvepi snehavaddravyādane padārthabhedasaṃbaṃdhādityarthaḥ ||
nivartakapadārthā bahavassanti | tatsarveṣāṃ prayojakatvaṃ vaktavyaṃ, tādṛśasarvadravyādanasya yogyatvāt | śuddhadravyaṃ snehena sākaṃ vipācya tatpācakasnehaṃ śoṣakapārthivakāryaṃ karotītyāha-- viśuddheti |
viśuddhasnehamayaṃ nivartakam || 78 ||
abhojyadravyāṇi rogapratipādakakārakāṇi bahūni santi | bhojyadravyāṇi roganivartakāni bahūni santi | kāni ciddravyāṇi pāke rasāntaraṃ bhūtvā tattadrogopaśamanaṃ kurvanti | dravyāṇi dravyāntarasaṃyoge sati tattatsaṃyogajanyaphalaṃ prayacchanti | bahudravyasaṃyoge bahuphalaṃ prayacchanti | viruddhadravyasaṃyoge sati viruddhadravyaphalaṃ prayacchanti | tatsarvamālocya tattajjātīyabahudravyayogakaraṇaṃ śuddhaṃ bhavati | yathā yavakṣāraharitadravyasaṃyogena svasvarūpaṃ vihāya raktarūpaṃ bhāti | dravyaṃ dravyāṃtarasaṃyoge sati tattaddravyavihitādhikaguṇaṃ vidhatte | yāvaddravyasaṃyogajanyaṃ vidhatte | yaddravyasaṃyogajanyarasavaddravyaṃ śuddhamityuktaṃ | tādṛśaśuddhadravyaissākaṃ pācitaṃ snehapānābhyāṃ supācitaṃ snehaguṇaṃ prayacchati | svādvamlalavaṇaviśiṣṭayāvaddravyasaṃyogahetukaṃ pavana duṣkṛtakārakaśaktiviśiṣṭaguṇapradhānapratibhūtakarakarmakārakaṃ tāvatpākakarmakaraṇācchuddhaṃ snehaṃ tāvaddravyaguṇadāyakam | atipācitaṃ supakvaparyantaṃ pācitam | atipācitasnehaṃ nivartakamityuktam | pavanaprakopahetukayāvadrogaśoṣakapratīkārahetukaṃ nivartakam | tattu śuddhasneham | apatarpaṇaṃ yatra nivartakaṃ bhavati tatredaṃ vacanaṃ--
jīrṇāśane tu bhaiṣajyaṃ yuñjyāt stabdhagurūdare |
doṣaśeṣasya pākārthamagneḥ sandhukṣaṇāya ca |
śāntirāmavikārāṇāṃ bhavati tvapatarpaṇāt ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa śoṣakadravyahetukaṃ kāryam | etena śoṣakadravyāṇāṃ prayojakatvaṃ pratipāditam | idānīṃ śoṣakaṃ kathyate | tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasaviśiṣṭayāvaddravyasaṃyogahetukaṃ kapharogapratikārakaśaktiviśiṣṭaguṇapradhānapratibhūtakarmakārakaṃ yattatkarmaṇāṃ yāvadguṇadāyakaṃ śuddhasnehaṃ bhavati | atipācitamatyantapācitaṃ supākaparyantam , tattu snehadravyaṃ kaphapradhānarogajālanivartakam | pavanajanyarogeṣu kvacinnivartakāḥ śoṣakāḥ, kvacinnivartakāḥ poṣakā ityarthaḥ |
tasya snehasya prayojanamāha-- aṅgeti |
aṅgāṅgasaṅgo'ṅgavilepanādaṅgāṅgavibhajanaṃ vibhāti || 79 ||
aṅgalagnarogāṇāṃ tattatsthalasaṃsargāt rogo vinaśyati | atyantavedanābhāvādaṅgeṣu lepanamātrādeva tannivartate | doṣasañcārābhāvajāsthikarṇanāsādisandhijīvaneṣu vimardanapādaghaṭṭanaṃ samyakkāryam | tena saṅkucitāṅgātsamyagvikāro nivartate | garbhāśayasthapiṇḍasthasarvāṅgajanakakāryaṃ māturaṅgahetukaṃ, tadanyāhetukatve sati taddhetukatvāt | piṇḍāṅgaṃ māturaṅgajanyamiti pratipāditam | piṇḍasthitajaṭharānalakāryasya hetordṛṣṭatvāt, tasyāpi māturāhārarasajanyatvāt | pārthivādhikyadravyādanātpārthivāṅgaṃ bhavati | abdravyāṅgakāryasya abdravyādhikyadravyādanaṃ hetuḥ | piṇḍasthitajaṭharānalakāryaṃ taijasadravyādhikyajanyam | tasmādantarvatnyā ghṛtaṃ pāyayedityāha-- antarvatnyeti |
antarvatnījanyajātavedā antarvatnīghṛtādanajanyaḥ || 80 ||
antarvatnījanyajātavedā eva jaṭharānalo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
pratatriṇāmaṅgāṅgahetukatvaṃ aṇḍānāmaṅgajanakatvaṃ jaṭharānalasyāpi aṇḍasthitaghṛtameva hetuḥ | tattajjātīyadravyasyāpi garbhāṇḍajānāṃ īdṛśakāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ pratipādyata ityāha-- khecarāṇāmiti |
khecarāṇāṃ janayatassaha sārpiṣā pakṣiśarīre ||
taijasadravyādhikyāśritāḥ ūrdhvamārgagatyāśritatvāt, agneḥ prajvalanavat | taijasādhikyaśarīrādeva te jīvā aṇḍasthā evābhivardhante | aṇḍotpattihetubhūtau pavanānalādhikadravyaśarīrātmakau tāveva pitarau | tajjātīyapiṇḍāni svayamevābhivardhanta ityarthaḥ |
khagānāṃ gaganavartmārohaṇaṃ kena hetunā prāptam ? pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti lakṣaṇasya sarvasādhāraṇatvāt | gamanāgamanacalanātmakaṃ karma śarīrasāmānyamityata āha--ārohaṇamiti |
ārohaṇaṃ gaganavartmasu vrajatām || 82 ||
khagānāmārohaṇakarma analānilātmakadravyaguṇādhikaśarīratvāt | khe gamanaṃ sūkṣmārthagocaratvaṃ śītoṣṇasukhasahiṣṇutvaṃ ca tena labhyata iyarthaḥ ||
bhūcaraśarīrāṇāṃ tu pārthivāvayavādhikyaśarīratvāt bhūmisañcārasahiṣṇutvaṃ snehadravyādanajātāyustejāretodhāraṇaṃ bhavatītyāha-- ghṛteti |
ghṛtaplutānnādanādvāyustejoretāṃsi dadhāti ||
khecarāṇāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ svatassiddhadhātudārḍhyavattvam | bhūcaraśarīrāṇāṃ tanna rocate dhātudārḍhyaṃ | tattadrasavaddavyādanajātaupādhikadhātudārḍhyaṃ na svabhāvataḥ prāptatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu ghṛtaplutānnādanaṃ āyurādyabhivardhakamityuktam | taccintyaṃ, adhikaghṛtaplutānnādanasya ajīrṇakāryahetukatvāt, āyustejoretasāṃ kṣīṇatvakāraṇaṃ ca, gurutvakāryasya ca pratītatvāt | yāni dravyāṇyajīrṇakārakāṇi tāni tu kṣaṇatvakārakāṇi | evamanvayavyatirekapramāṇābhyāṃ siddhatvādityata āha-- yogyeti |
yogyadravyopayogairabhivardhate || 84 ||
vyādhinivartakatvaṃ yogyadravyāṇāṃ yogaḥ, tannivartakarasavaddravyatvaṃ rogābhāvakāryasya nivartakatvaṃ, yaddravyopayoganivartakatvaṃ nāma rogābhāvakāryasya hetutvameva | abhivardhantāmavayavā ityarthaḥ |
nanu idamapyanupapannaṃ, yogyāyogyadravyopayogakarmakaraṇaṃ yāvadrogābhāvakāryahetukāpratiyogikāryaphalaprāpakadravyatvāt yāvadro gābhāvakāryasya kāraṇatvapratipādanamātre paryavasitam | tāvadroganivartakaṃ prati hetutvaṃ pratipāditam | na tadgarbhasaṃrakṣaṇamātrasya hetutvapratipādakāvastheyam | atredaṃ dyotyate-- piṇḍābhivardhakakarmamātraṃ pratipāditamityasvarasādāha-- edhanta iti |
edhante'smā ṛtavaḥ || 85 ||
atra ṛtava ityukte ṣaḍṛtūnāṃ vārdhikatvaṃ pratipāditam | idaṃ tu ṛtupratipādakaprakaraṇaṃ na bhavati, kiṃ tu garbhāśayasthapiṇḍābhivardhakakāryasya hetutvapratipādakāvastheyam | atredaṃ na dyotyate, piṇḍābhivardhakakāryamātrapratipādakatvāt | tathāhi-- ṛtukarmoddeśya ṛtvabhāvakāryahetutvābhivardhakakarmādiviśiṣṭakālajñāpakatvaṃ ṛturiti | tādṛśaviśiṣṭakālo garbhāśayasthapiṇḍāvayavābhivardhakadravyādanādṛtvabhāvaviśiṣṭakāryā edhanta ityarthaḥ ||
nanu ṛtvabhāvaviśiṣṭakālābhivardhanena kathaṃ piṇḍāvayavābhivṛddhiḥ ? tadyogyābhāvakālasya adṛṣṭatvādityasvarasādāha-- anneti |
annādbhūtāni jāyante || 86 ||
piṇḍāṅkurāvirbhāvahetukayāvaddravyādanaṃ ṛtvabhāvakāryahetukaṃ bhavatīti sortho'tra vivakṣita ityarthaḥ | sarvarasadravyādanasya sarveṣāmapi sambhāvitatvāt, sarvatrāpi kāryasyādṛṣṭatvādityasvarasādāha-- jātānīti |
jātānyannena vardhante || 87 ||
jātapiṇḍāśayaḥ pūrvoktarasadravyādanaheturiti pratipāditamityarthaḥ ||
annādanaṃ śarīradārḍhyakārakamiti anvayavyatirekapramāṇasiddhatvāt | 'yatsatve yatsatvaṃ yadabhāve yadabhāvaḥ' iti nyāyena annādane sati śarīradārḍhyaṃ tadabhāve tadabhāva nyāyanayasiddhatvāt | anyathāsiddheśca-- divā abhuñjānasya devadattasya satvaṃ rātribhojanamantareṇa na saṃbhavatīti, tasyāpi rātribhojanamantareṇa satvaṃ nopapadyate, svāvayavavyatiriktapiṇḍāvayavābhivardhakaṃ na bhavati, anyasādhanasya anyāvayavāhetukatvāt ityasvarasādāha-- adyata iti |
adyate'tti ca bhūtāni || 88 ||
trividhabhūtāni | sātvikarājasatāmasadravyādanāttattadguṇabhūyiṣṭhaśarīrābhivṛddhikāraṇatvāttriguṇātmakaṃ śarīram | bhūtāni tattadabhivardhakadravyādutpadyanta ityarthaḥ |
uktārthamupasaṃharati-- tasmāditi |
tasmādannaṃ taducyata iti || 89 ||
annaṃ dhātupradaṃ puṣṭidaṃ avayavābhivardhanakaraṃ etādṛśaguṇaṃ prayacchati | itiśabdena heturucyate | rasavadannamavayavābhivṛddhiṃ karoti | tasmādannarasamayaṃ upasaṃhṛtārthaṃ śuddharasavadannaṃ prāṇamayaṃ piṇḍāvayavābhivardhakaṃ taducyata ityarthaḥ ||
annaṃ tu svaniṣṭha saguṇaṃ prayacchati, avayavābhivṛddhikaraṇe sāmarthyābhāvāt | śuṣkānnādanaṃ viruddhaphalaṃ prayacchati | kathamannaṃ jīvāturiti vaktuṃ śakyamityata āha-- ghṛteti |
dhṛtaplutānnamantarvatyāḥ pradāpayet || 90 || śuṣkānnaṃ viruddhaphalaṃ dadāti | ghṛtaplutānnameva kāryakārakam | tasmādantarvatyāḥ ghṛtaplutānnaṃ dāpayedityarthaḥ |
punarapyuktamarthamupasaṃharati-- tasmāditi |
tasmātsvāvayavavibhavaṃ dhātuvardhanaṃ kurvatyaḥ prajāḥ prajāyante || 91 ||
ghṛtaplutānnādanaṃ avayavābhivardhakamityuktam | yatkiṃcidvikāre sati tannivartakena nivartanīyam | taddhṛtaplutānnameva nivartakamiti vaktuṃ na śakyate, gurudravyatvāt | tasmādasaṅgatamityasvarasādāha-- taditi |
tacchoṣakapoṣakadravyaṃ tatra bheṣajam || 92 ||
vikāre sati śoṣakapoṣakadravye kāryakārake bhavataḥ | jātaśiśośca prathamartau prathamaṃ śoṣakadravyajātarujaḥ śoṣakadravyaṃ bheṣajam, poṣakadravyajātarujaḥ poṣakadravyaṃ nivartakam | evaṃ daśamamāsaparyantaṃ anyonyaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavati | garbhasthaśiśoryāvaddravyamabhivardhakaṃ bhavati tāvaddravyamātraṃ bheṣajamityarthaḥ ||
nanu garbhasthaśiśuvajjātaśiśurapi bheṣajadravyaiḥ poṣyate | prathamotpannaśiśoḥ dravyādanajñānaṃ naidhate | māturāhārarasāhārajanyapratikārakarasavaddravyādanabhaiṣajakarmaṇaḥ poṣakadravyatvāt | tadvadatrāpi poṣakadravyāṇyeva heturiti vaktumaśakyatvādityasvarasādāha-- evamiti |
evamuttarottarābhivṛddhirmāse rmāse || 93 ||
yāvadyogyarasādanaṃ pakṣīkṛtya kiṃcitkiṃcidedhitavyam |
avayavāśca kālaśca deśaśceti etānuddiśya tattadyogyadravyaṃ edhitaṃ hitaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | śoṣakapoṣakadravyasya iyaṃ vyavasthā kṛtā | evaṃ pratimāsaṃ cikitsākaraṇaṃ kartumayogyam | vardhiṣṇoḥ śarīrasya vṛddhikaraṇadravyādanameva yogyamityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadvārdhikāstatra bheṣajāḥ || 94 ||
vārdhikadravyamātraṃ praśastaṃ bhavati | tathā cedatiprasaṅgassyāt | yāvacchabdārthamahimnā yāvadyogyarasavaddravyādanaṃ vivakṣitamityarthāt | na kevalaṃ yāvadvārdhikadravyaṃ vṛddhikārakaṃ bhavati, kālavaśāt śoṣakakāryaguṇasvabhāvatvāt ityata āha-sarpiṣeti |
sarpiṣā poṣyapoṣakāḥ || 95 ||
yāvadvārdhikadravyaṃ sarpissaṃyogānalapākajanyaṃ saṃskāraviśiṣṭapākarasavaddravyaṃ kāryakārakaṃ bhavati, snehavaddravyasaṃyogajanyavārdhikaguṇaprāpakadravyatvādityarthaḥ ||
laṅghanādinā doṣe kṣīṇe sati dhātuśoṣe sati tatra poṣakadravyaṃ kāryakārakaṃ "asau poṣaṇakaro bhavatīti" | tatra śoṣakadravyeṇa nivartakakaraṇaṃ ayogyamityata āha-- neti |
na poṣakakāle śoṣakāḥ || 96 ||
poṣakakāryakaraṇayogyakāle śoṣakadravyakālocitakaraṇaṃ ayogyamityarthaḥ | nanu poṣakāśca śoṣakāśca loke vyavahārayogyāḥ ṣaḍrasā eva | ekaikarasavaddravyasya ekaikadhātupoṣakatvaprayojakatvamāgatam | tāvatpoṣakatvasya svata eva siddhatvāt | rasavaddravyātiriktadravyābhāvādityasvarasādāha--ṣaḍrasā iti |
ṣaḍrasāssaptadhātupoṣakāḥ || 97 ||
rasāsṛjorekaikavatkaṣāyarasavatpoṣakatayā janayitā ca ? tasmādekarasapradhānatvādityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya ṣaḍrasānāṃ saptadhātupoṣakatvaṃ vaktuṃ sukaramityarthaḥ || tathāhi-- saptadhātuguṇakaṃ tasyopakārakaṃ, teṣāṃ ṣaḍrasapoṣakatvasya svata eva siddhatvāt | tasmādidamasaṅgatamityasvarasādāha-- rasa iti |
raso hyasṛk || 98 ||
raso vai saḥ || 99 ||
rasadhāturasṛgbhavati ekarasajanyatvāt | antassthitassan raktamityabhidhīyate | sa eva bahirgataścet svedarūpeṇa sravati | tadrasanendriyaṃ ūṣmaṇā bahirgataṃ bhavati | raso lālārūpaḥ sa eva raśmiḥ | tajjñānavānātmā | tadviṣayakajñānamānandahetukam | rasanendriyajñānagrāhakajñānagocarajñānaṃ yasyāsti tasyaivānando bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
dvirasārtha jaṅghayoḥ nāsikāyāḥ śuddhāśayaṃ māsi māsi prathamartau vṛddhissarvajantūnāṃ yogyayogo nava ||
ityāyurvedasya dvitīyapraśnasya bhāṣyaṃ yogānandanāthakṛtaṃ
suprasiddhaṃ mahājanasammataṃ pratisūtravyākhyānapūrvakaṃ
ayurvedabhāṣyaṃ lokopakārakaṃ saṃpūrṇam.
<3>
āyurvede tṛtīyapraśnaḥ.
nanu rasajñānaṃ labdhvā''nandī bhavatīti sūtravyākhyānaṃ kṛtam | taccintyaṃ jīvātmanāṃ ānandānubhavavattvaṃ, bāhyendriyāṇāṃ bāhyārthaviṣayakagocarajñānaṃ ānandahetukaṃ na bhavati, tadviṣayakajñānānubhavasya indriyārthasaṃyogakālamātraṃ ānandahetukaṃ, tadvināśe sati tannāśāt | bāhyendriyaviṣayānubhavasya tatsaṃsarganāśe sati tannāśāt ityasvarasādāha-- atheti |
atha yogānuśāsanam || 1 ||
athaśabdenānantaryaṃ sūcyate | ṣaḍrasavaddravyādanasya śarīradārḍhyakāryahetutvaṃ sampratipāditam | tasmāddṛḍhataradhātava ātmamanassaṃyogasampādanārthaṃ hetubhūtāssaṃtaḥ tadviṣayakajñānānubhavānandaṃ antarindriyātmasaṃyogajanyaṃ bhavatīti manasi nidhāyāha jīvātmanaḥ paramātmānusandhānamānandahetukaṃ bhavatīti |
nanu yogānuśāsanamapi ajīrṇābhāvasampādanārthaṃ kāraṇaṃ bhavatu | tathā sati āmanivṛttereva phalatvāt aniṣṭaparihāramātraṃ phalaṃ labhyata ityata āha-- tasmāditi |
tasmādyogaḥ phaladaḥ || 2 ||
viṣayānubhavo viṣayānandahetukaḥ | ātmānubhavo'pyātmānandahetukaḥ, ānandahetukakāryasya ubhayorapi samānatvāt | yogānuśāsanena kimadhikaphalaṃ labhyata ityāśaṅkya anityaviṣayakajñā nagocarajñānajanyakāryamanityaṃ bhavati | ātmano nityadravyatvāt nityadravyaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānameva ānando bhavati | antarindriyasya manasaḥ ātmagocarasvabhāvatvena sarvakāle ānandānubhavo labhyata iti ātmamanassaṃyogasyaiva yogatvāt yogaḥ śāśvataṃ phalaṃ prayacchatītyarthaḥ |
nanu paramātmano nityadravyatvādvibhutvācca sarvadā manasaḥ ātmanā samavetya sthitatvāt sarvadā ānandānubhava eva bhavet | indriyasyārthena samavetya prāpyaprakāśatvaniyamāt | tajūjñānagocarajñānaṃ ātmano viṣayīkṛtaṃ bhavatītyata āha-- prakṛtīti |
prakṛtipuruṣāntarmukhajñānagocarapratyayaprakṛtipariṇāmo yogaḥ || 3 ||
prakṛtiviśiṣṭapuruṣaṃ viṣayīkṛtya antarindriyeṇa manasā guṇaviśiṣṭaparamātmani viṣayīkṛte sati bāhyendriyārthaviṣayajñānavirāmeṇa tādṛśaguṇaviśiṣṭaparamātmānubhavasya paramānandahetukatvāt tadviṣayakajñānayogaḥ pariṇāmayoga ityarthaḥ | prakṛtipuruṣaviśiṣṭadravyaṃ yadā viṣayo bhavati tadā ānandānubhava ityarthaḥ | tathā sati viṣayānandamuddiśya prakṛtiviśiṣṭātmaviṣayakajñānaṃ paramānandahetukamiti sarvadā ayamānandānubhavaṃ viṣayīkarotu, kiṃ bāhyendriyaviṣayagocarajñānena, alpānandānubhavahetukatvādityata āha-- raja iti |
rajaudrekādasthiraṃ bahirmukhātsukhaduḥkhahetu || 4 ||
paramātmā kaṃcitkālaṃ rājasaguṇaviśiṣṭo bhavati | kaṃcitkālaṃ sātvikaguṇaviśiṣṭo bhavati | kaṃcitkālaṃ tāmasaguṇaviśiṣṭo bhavati | yadā jīvātmani rājasādiguṇāḥ ātmabhāvā bhavanti tadguṇaviśiṣṭa ātmā manasā saṃyujyate, mana indriyeṇa, indriyamarthena, indriyāṇāṃ prāpyaprakāśatvaniyama iti nyāyasya dṛṣṭatvāt | rājasaguṇasaṃspṛṣṭadoṣavaśāt ātmānubhavaṃ pidhāya bāhyaviṣayakasukhamanubhūyata ityarthaḥ | yadā satvo jīvaḥ tāmasaguṇaviśiṣṭo bhavati tadā tāmasaguṇaviśiṣṭassan duḥkhānuṣaṅgaṃ sukhamanubhavatītyāha-- tama iti |
tamaudrekātkṛtyākṛtyājñānātkrodhādibhirniyamito mūḍhaḥ || 5 ||
tāmasaguṇādhikyāt kāryavivekajñānarāhityena mūḍhamanāḥ krodhadveṣādibhiḥ tadviṣayakajñānamanubhavati | īṣaṇatrayaviśiṣṭatadviṣayakasukhamanubhūyata ityarthaḥ | yadā satvasya sātvikaguṇāvirbhāvo bhavati, tadā dharmapratipādakavedāntaśāstreṣvāsaktaḥ svadharmaśāstrapratipādyakarmācaraṇena sukarmajanyasukhamanubhūyata ityāha-- sātviketi |
sātvikodayātsukham || 6 ||
duḥkhābhāvasādhakaśabdādipravartakaṃ sātvikam | jīvaḥ sātvikaguṇāvirbhāve sati taddharmapratipādakakāryakaraṇena svargaviṣayakasukhaṃ bhuṅkta ityarthaḥ |
nanu jīvātmanaḥ kadācidrājasaguṇānubhavaḥ, kadācittāmasaguṇānubhavaḥ, kadācitsātvikaguṇānubhavo dṛśyata iti pratipāditam | niyamena triguṇānubhavasya ko heturityākāṅkṣāyāmāha-- madhureti
madhurarasānnādanādanusātvikaguṇahetukam || 7 || |
madhurarasaviśiṣṭamannaṃ yo bhuṅkte sa sātvikaguṇavānbhavati | sa jīva aupādhikaguṇaṃ bhuṅkte |
triguṇā madhurāmlakaṭurasādanahetukā ityāha-- amlīti |
amlībhūtānnādanaṃ rājasaguṇakārakam || 8 ||
kaṭukāstamoguṇahetukāḥ || 9 ||
yasya madhurarase prītirāste sa mumukṣurbhavati | sa itararasānnādanaṃ vihāya madhurarasavaddravyaṃ bhuṅkta ityāha mumukṣoriti |
mumukṣormādhuryam || 10 ||
madhurarasavaddravyaṃ mokṣahetuḥ rājasatāmasajanakarasābhāvatve sati sātvikaguṇahetukarasavaddravyatvāt | yo rasaḥ sātvikaguṇaprado na bhavati sa mokṣado'pi na bhavati, kaṭurasavat | sarvadā mumukṣubhiḥ mādhuryarasaḥ pānayogyo bhavati sātvikaguṇahetukamokṣaphalaprado bhūtvā sātvikaguṇakārakatvāt | sātvikaguṇasya mokṣahetukatvācca |
nanu rājasaguṇena sukhānubhave sati sukhahetubhūtārthatatvajñāne sati sukhaviṣayakatāratamyānubhavo bhavati | nityānandānubhavaṃ mokṣahetukasukhaṃ madhurarasajanyam | āmlarasajanyaṃ rājasaguṇaviṣayakasukhamanubhūyate | tasmāddheyopādeyavivekajñānaṃ rājasaguṇajanyasukhānubhavavyatirekeṇa mokṣahetukajñānajanyasukhaṃ jñātumaśakyamityasvarasādāha-- āmleti |
āmlarasādanātsukhamekamanubhūyate || 11 ||
amlarasajanyasukhaṃ dṛṣṭānubhavadeśadehakālāntarabhāvyamokṣaviṣayakasukhaṃ pratyakṣānubhavamityarthaḥ | tasmādidameva mukhyamiti ekaśabdena jñāpyate |
sukhaduḥkhābhāvaprayojakatvaṃ tadduḥkhānubhavaṃ vinā jñātumaśakyamityata āha-- uṣṇeti |
uṣṇaraso duḥkhānuṣaṅgātsukhaṃ janayati || 12 ||
sukhānubhavo duḥkhānubhavābhāvasya sukhatvādityarthaḥ | nanu duḥkhakāryakārakakaraṇajātaduḥkhaṃ anubhavaikanāśyam | sukhajñānaṃ tu duḥkhānubhavapūrvakam | sukhānubhavasya duḥkhābhāvakāryapratiyogikābhāvānubhavajñānapūrvakatvāt | duḥkhānuṣaṅgavatsukhamapi duḥkhahetukamapi bhavati | sukhaduḥkhahetukāryahetukasāmagrīparipālanameva puruṣārtha eva phalaṃ bhavedityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha-- tadeti |
}tadā draṣṭussvarūpāvasthānam || 13 ||
draṣṭurīśvarasya svarūpāvasthānaṃ jagatsvarūpaṃ yasyāsti, sa īśvarastu jagatsvarūpo bhūtvā tiṣṭhati | tasya sukhānubhavasya duḥkhābhāvakāryapratiyogikābhāvānubhavasyaiva puruṣārthatvāt | draṣṭuḥ svarūpāvasthānaṃ sukhaduḥkhānurūpaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tasya jagatsvarūpaśarīraṃ bhogāyatanam sukhaduḥkhānubhavo bhoga ityarthaḥ |
nanu paramātmana īśvarasya nirlepanatvāt nirguṇatvāt nityānandānubhavavattvāt tasya sukhaduḥkhādivaicitryaṃ kathaṃ saṃbhavati, śarīrādhiṣṭhānatvasya jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ samānatvādityata āha-- sukheti |
sukhaduḥkhamohātmikādyāstrayaḥ prādurbhavanti || 14
ahaṃ sukhī ahaṃ duḥkhī karmādhīnakarmaupādhikarūpaḥ prakṛtyātmakatvāt | yadā jīvātmani āvṛtte sati ātmanaḥ māyāśabalitatvāt tāvanto dharmā upalabhyanta ityarthaḥ | ata eva karmādhikāritvaṃ jīvātmana upalabhyate | ātmana eva mokṣādhikārisvādurasannādanaṃ tasyaiva labhyate | tajjanyasātvikaguṇo mokṣopakārako bhavati | yadā āmlarasādanaṃ upalabhyate tadā rājasaguṇāvirbhāvo bhavati | tena kevalaṃ sukhaṃ bhuṅkte | kaṭurasadravyādanaṃ yadā bhavati tadā duḥkhānuṣaktasukhamanubhūyata ityata āha-- taditi |
tadrajastaddhetuguṇabhūtajātam || 15 ||
tadraja iti tacchabdena satvarajastamoguṇā vivakṣitāḥ | teṣāṃ madhye prathamaguṇarajoguṇakāryaṃ hi āmlarasavaddravyam | rajoguṇabhūtasya āmlarasavaddravyaṃ kāraṇaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tattu satvaguṇakāryasya svādurasalavaṇarasajanyatvaṃ tamaṃssatvaguṇayoḥ hetubhūtādityarthaḥ | sūtre hetava iti bahuvacanayogasya vidyamānatvāt tamassatvayorapi grahaṇam |
nanu uktarītyā svādvamlalavaṇarasānāṃ yojakaṃ satvarajastamoguṇajanakamātraṃ pratipāditam | vātapittakaphajanyarogāṇāṃ nivartakarasadravyasya anyathāsiddhatvāt tannivartakadravyāddhīnena janyarogāḥ kathaṃ nivartanta ityata āha--yatheti |
yathāroganivartakāḥ śoṣakapoṣakāḥ || 16 ||
svādurasavaddravyaṃ satvaguṇahetukam | āmlarasavaddravyaṃ rajoguṇahetukam | lavaṇoṣṇarasavaddravyaṃ tamoguṇahetukam | na te roganivartakāḥ | kintu śoṣakapoṣakadravyādanādeva anekadravyayogatvena bahurasavaddravyaṃ roganivartakaṃ bhavediti vaktavyam | rogastu yadā āvirbhūto bhavati tadā saḥ anekāmayānanujāyate | tatra tadroganivartakena anekarasavaddravyabāhulyena bhāvyamityarthaḥ |
nanu atra satvarasavaddravyaṃ vā roganivartakam ? yatkiṃcidrasavaddravyaṃ vā ? nādyaḥ atiprasaṅgāt | yatkiṃciddravyādanamiti cet tadā ekarasavaddravyādanenāpi bhāvyam | tathā sati anyathā siddhamiti asvarasādāha-- yogyeti |
yogyayogo yogaḥ || 17 ||
yāvadrogānusārinidānajñānaṃ tattaddrasavaddravyanidānajñānapūrvakaṃ anekarogāmagatarogasya bahurasavaddravyayoganivṛttisāmagrīkatvāt iti |
ṛṣireva hi jānāti dravyasaṃyogajaṃ phalam |
bheṣajakalpavacanaṃ tu--
deśakālānuguṇyena saṅgṛhītaṃ yathāvidhi ||
sthāpitaṃ bheṣajaṃ taistaiḥ kalpanairamṛtāyate ||
yathārogānusāritattadvidhicoditarasabāhulyayogatvaṃ roganivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
nanu "ādhikaṃ naiva dūṣyate" iti nyāyena anekasvādurasavaddravyaṃ vāyunivartakam | anekāmlarasavaddravyamelanaṃ pittaroganiva rtakam | anekalavaṇoṣṇarasavaddravyaṃ kaphanivartakam | etādṛśayogakalpanameva yogyayogo bhavedityasvarasādāha-- citteti |
cittavibhramo viparyayaḥ || 18 ||
yāvadrogapittarasavaddravyamelanavyatirekeṇa yogakaraṇaṃ viruddhayogo bhavati | tattu cittavibhramo bhavati | cittaṃ manaḥ | tayoraikyena rajatatvābhāvavati śuktau rajataṃ yatra bhāsate tatra cittavibhramo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu rajatatvaprakārakajñānagrāhakasāmagrīviśiṣṭapurovartinaṃ yadā viṣayīkaroti rajatatvaprakārakajñānagrāhakasāmagryā tadviśiṣṭameva bhāsate haṭṭapaṭṭaṇādau | tathā dṛṣṭatvāt | yāvajjñānagocarasāmagryā abhāvāt cittavibhramaḥ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- ataditi |
atasmiṃstaditi pratyayaḥ || 19 ||
yatra rajatatvābhāvavati śuktau rajatatvaṃ bhāsate tattat | atasmin anyasmin taditi bhramo jāyate | tatra vikalpaḥ, tadviṣayakajñānagrāhakasāmagrī tadviśiṣṭārthameva gocarīkaroti, purovartigrāhakasāmagryā satvādityata āha-- pitteti |
pittādhikaikajātātpītaśśaṅkhaḥ || 20 ||
pittaprakoparasādanena pitarogodbhavo bhavati | tatra kācakāmālādirogavaśāt śvaityaguṇaviśiṣṭapurovartini pītaguṇaviśiṣṭo'yamiti jñānaṃ jāyate, tattaddoṣavaśāt | tacca taddoṣaviruddhādanādbhavati | tadā pītaśśaṅkha iti jñānaṃ jāyate |
nanu sarve vyādhayaḥ doṣatrayajanyāḥ | te sarve'pi tattaddrasādanajanyāḥ | ataḥ sūtre pittaviruddhadravyādanāccittavibhramo bhavatīti yattaccintyam | śvaityaguṇaviśiṣṭe śaṅkhe pīto'yamiti jñānaṃ bhāsate | tadayuktamityasvarasādāha-- seti |
sā pratītiḥ pittaviruddhāhārajanyā || 21 ||
bhramaviṣayakajñānaṃ ca pittajanyam | pītaśśaṅkha iti jñānaṃ ca śuddhapittaviruddhāhārajanyam | pavanapittarogajanakaviruddharasajanyatve sati tadviruddhāhārajanyatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭādiḥ |
nanu śuktau idaṃ rajatamiti bhramaviṣayakajñānaṃ jāyate | pītaśśaṅkha ityatrāpi tathā pratīyate | tadubhayaṃ yāvaddravyarasavaddravyādanajanyanivartyabhāvakāryahetubhūtadravyaṃ tadviruddharasavaddravyādanakāryahetupūrvakaṃ rasavirasajanyarogābhāvakārakadravyatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ |
nanu yatkiṃciddravyaṃ viṣayīkṛtya atyantanirīkṣaṇāt pūrvaṃ pramāṇatvenānubhūtamapi tatraiva saṃśayo jāyate | tatra rasavaddravyādanaṃ heturna bhavati ityata āha-- atīti |
atinirīkṣaṇe doṣapramāyāṃ saṃśayo bhavati || 22
indriyārthasannikarṣe sati tadarthaviṣayakajñānaṃ jāyate | tadviṣaye tatraiva tatkāle atinirīkṣaṇadoṣavaśādbhramo bhavati | tatrāpi pūrvajātarajatapramāviṣayakajñānaṃ atinirīkṣaṇadoṣajanyaṃ na saṃśayajñānasya nivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu yadviruddharasavaddravyādanaṃ yāvadrogahetukaṃ tadviruddhadravyādanaṃ tattadroganivartakamiti vaktavyam | tathā satyamadhurarasajanyarogāṇāṃ nivartako madhurarasa eva bhavati | yathā kadalīphalavirasajanyarogasya ajīrṇasya kadalīphalarasadravyādanaṃ nivartakamiti vaktumaśakyamityarthaḥ | kiṃtu āmlarasādyā nivartakā bhaveyuriti tātparyam | yāvaddravyādanajñānaṃ yatkāle yathopalabdhaṃ tatkāla eva pūrvadinānnāpekṣayā laghutarānnādanaṃ pathyaṃ bhavatīti tatkālayogyadravyeṇa prathamadinajanyarogasya tatkālocitarogābhāvakāryasya prāgabhāvaparipālanaṃ puruṣārtha ityarthaḥ |
nanu rasavirasānnādanājjātapramāpratītasthale yathā ghaṭajñānagocarajñānānantaraṃ tatraivātinirīkṣaṇena cakṣurdoṣavaśāt tatpratītiviṣayatadviṣayatātkālikasaṃśayaḥ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- atyanteti |
atyantānimiṣadṛṣṭyā cakṣurindriyadoṣassambhavati || 23 ||
taddoṣavaśāt tatpramā atatpratītiviṣayasthale doṣagrastendriyagocarajñānajanyapramāyāḥ saṃśayajñānasya apratibandhakatvāt tatra saṃśayo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
na pīḍayedindriyāṇi na ca tānyatilālayet |
etadvacanānusāreṇa indriyātilālanameva animiṣadṛṣṭiriti na saṃśayaḥ | viruddharasādanajanyaṃ na bhavatīti viśeṣadarśanasāmagrīsaṃśayapratibandhakāt tannivartakarasādanasāmagrī ? tasya janakatvābhā vāt | yadā yasya bhramo bhavati tasya rājasatāmasaguṇapratipādakasāmagryā bhinnatvādityasvarasādāha raja iti |
rajastamoguṇahetukadravyapariṇāmakāt pittādvibhramo bhavati || 24 ||
rājasaguṇajanakāmlarasavirasadravyādanaṃ tadguṇapradhānajanakabhramahetukam | tāmasaguṇajanakalavaṇakaṭurasavirasavaddravyādanaṃ tadguṇapradhānajanakabhramahetukam | tasmādbhramastu rasavirasajanyaḥ | saṃśayastu na tathābhūta ityarthaḥ |
nanu yatsaṃśayavyatirekaniścayau yatpravṛttipratibandhakau tanniścayastaddheturanumitau vyāptiriveti nyāyaśāstrānusāreṇa animiṣadṛṣṭidoṣavaśādeva bhramajñānaṃ sambhavatītyata āha-- taditi |
tadviṣaye cākṣuṣāṇyanubhūtārtheṣu || 25 ||
anyeṣāṃ pramātvenānubhūtārthe sa eva viṣayo yasya tasmin tadviṣaye bhramo yasya bhavatīti sa bhramo nivartakena nivartyaḥ |
nanu indriyārthasannikarṣānantaraṃ ayaṃ ghaṭa iti jñānānubhūtārthe jāte sati mamotpannaṃ jñānamapramāṇamiti yo mānasikabhramo jāyate tadyāvadrasavaddravyādanajanyarogagrastendriyajñānagocaratvābhāvādityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadgarbhāśayasthaṃ piṇḍaṃ tāvaddravyaparipālanam ||
yāvadgarbhāśayasthaṃ mana ityarthaḥ | asminmanasi cakṣurindriyajñānagocaraghaṭaviṣayakamanasi anyotpannajñānaṃ atadviṣayakaṃ tadarthapravṛttau na samarthaṃ pravṛttijanakatvābhāvāt bhramaviṣayakapramāvat |
yāvadvirasadravyādanajanyarogagrastendriyagocarabhramajñānaṃ manoviṣayakaṃ mānasikabhramānubhavajñānagocarasāmagrīviśiṣṭatvāt | tajjñānaviṣayakaṃ piṇḍaṃ tadbhramanivartakarasavaddravyādanaparipālanam | bhramaviṣayakaṃ jñānaṃ tannivartakarasadravyebhyo rakṣaṇīyamityarthaḥ ||
nanu bhramotpādakavirasadravyādanābhāvakāryahetubhūtārthādane sati adoṣāssantaḥ tattadindriyārthasannikarṣe jāte sati tadvati tatprakārakatvānubhavāt tatra bhramo na jāyate, saṃśayajñānajanakasāmagryabhāvāt | bhramapratyayaviṣayagocarārthaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānānubhavena tasya bhramādyabhāvaviṣayakatvāt | tajjñānaviṣayakapiṇḍasya bhramasaṃśayajñānaviṣayakatvena yatkiṃciddravyādanakāryāsaṃbhava ityāha-- adoṣeti |
adoṣajātapiṇḍaṃ kvacinnivartayet || 27 ||
āśayā duṣṭāssantaḥ indriyārthasannikarṣe sati bhramaviṣayakameva gocarīkurvanti | tatsādhāraṇajñānaviṣayakasya adoṣagrastendriyaviṣayakajñānagocaratvādityarthaḥ ||
nimitte hi pathyaṃ bheṣajam || 28 ||
nanu nimitte sati rogo jāyate | tatraiva pathyaṃ kāryam | tatra laghvannādanaṃ pathyam | tathāca yadā āśayā doṣaduṣṭā bhavanti tatra hīnādane āśayānāṃ riktatvamastīti vaktavyam | 'rikte vāyuḥ prakupyate' iti sūtrārthānusāreṇa tatra vāyoḥ prabalatvāt samyaggatyāśayābhāvatvācca indriyāṇi savikārāṇi bhavānta | tatra pathyaṃ bheṣajamiti sūtraṃ na saṅgatamityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadāhārapariṇāmajātāḥ kliṣṭāḥ || 29 ||
yāvaddinabhojanamapi āśayānāṃ kleśakārakam | hīnabhojanamapi āśayānāmatikleśakārakam | tadubhayamapi pathyaṃ na bhavatīti kṛtvā sūtrameva ākṣipatītyarthaḥ ||
kliṣṭāḥ kleśāḥ, akliṣṭā akleśāḥ || 30 ||
kliṣṭāḥ kleśā bhavanti | akliṣṭā akleśā bhavantītyarthaḥ ||
nanu hīnabhojanājjātarogasya atibhojanamapi pathyaṃ na bhavati | atibhojanājjātarogasya hīnabhojanamapi pathyaṃ na bhavati | ubhayorapi nivartakaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyata ityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvatprastutamaharaharbheṣajam || 33 ||
aharaharbhuktānāmajīrṇābhāvakaraṇena āśayānāṃ bhukttatvaṃ yatra bhāti | āśayā nirduṣṭāssantaḥ yatrendriyāṇāmarogatvapratipādanaṃ tāvadgocarajñānameva tadvati tatprakārakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata ityarthaḥ ||
nanu āśayeṣu rikteṣu vātaprakopo bhavatīti pūrvaṃ pratipāditam | idānīmapi yāvadrogāṇāṃ prastutatvaṃ nāma āmāśayasya bhuktakaraṇatvaṃ vaktavyam | tathā sati pratyahaṃ riktakaraṇatvaṃ na yogyamityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadudbhūtaśoṣakaguṇajātā rujaḥ tattadudbhūtapoṣakadravyaṃ tatra bheṣajam || 32 ||
yāvatkāryāṇāṃ kāraṇībhūtarasavaddravyāvirbhūtaśoṣakadravyajātarujāṃ nivartakībhūtaniṣṭhapoṣakadravyaṃ tāvadrogāṇāṃ bheṣajaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
pūrvasūtrasyāsvārasyaṃ nissārayan āha-- āmeti |
āmanissaraṇakāryaṃ kartavyam || 33 ||
pratyahaṃ āmanissaraṇakāryaṃ kartavyamiti boddhavyam | "mama āmavṛddhiḥ jātā" iti jñānānantaraṃ āmanissaraṇakāryaṃ vidhiriti jñāpanārthaṃ āmanivṛttikaraṇameva "pratyahaṃ mama āmavṛddhirjāyate" iti yasya jñānamasti tādṛśaṃ prati idaṃ bheṣajaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu śoṣakadravyādanajātarogāṇāṃ poṣakadravyaṃ tatra bheṣajamiti vaktuṃ na śakyate | śoṣakadravyādanaṃ nāma śuṣkadravyādanam | tena ajīrṇe jāte sati tannivṛtyarthaṃ laṅghanameva kartavyam | tatra nivartakībhūtapoṣakadravyaṃ bheṣajaṃ bhavatīti rukpradarśanānantaraṃ laṅghanena doṣaprakopanivṛttiṃ kṛtvā dhātvabhivṛddhyarthaṃ poṣakadravyaṃ tatra bheṣajaṃ bhavatu, ādāveva rukpradarśane jāte prathamata eva poṣakadravyapradānena doṣāḥ prakupitā bhaveyurityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvaddhātvaṅkurābhivardhakadravyābhāvajātarogāḥ tatpoṣakasaṃskārayuktasnehādayo nivartakāḥ || 34 ||
kiṃcidviruddharasadravyādanajñānena rogāvirbhāvātpūrvameva tannivartakena nivartayituṃ sukarameva | taddravyānnādanādeva nivartayituṃ śakyate | anyathā rogāṅkure jāte sati tannidānahetubhūtāṅgāvirbhāvaparyantaṃ nivartakena te nivartyā bhaveyuḥ | dhātuṣu vyādhyaṅkurāvirbhāvakāryābhāvakāraṇājjātarujaḥ tatra poṣakadravyayuktasnehādayo nivartakāḥ | anyathā tatpoṣakadravyayuktasnehādyāḥ taddravyaissahitāḥ tatpānābhyaṅgāvalepanāt vyādhīnnivartayantītyarthaḥ |
nanu sarveṣāṃ sarvapadārthādanasya prastutatvāt sarvadravyādanaṃ rogahetukaṃ bhavati | yāvadrogāṇāṃ yāvaddravyāṇāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāve jñāte sati idaṃ vyādhihetukaṃ dravyaṃ idaṃ vyādhinivartakaṃ dravyaṃ iti tayorvyāptigrahe sati tathā vaktuṃ śakyate | sarvatrāpi tathāpyananubhavena tayorvyāpterabhāvādityasvarasādāha-- māturiti |
māturāhārarasāhārajanitāvayavasthitarasāḥ pravartakāḥ || 35 ||
tatra śiśūnāṃ stanyapānādanena yāvaddhātuṣu vyādhyaṅkurāvirbhāvakāryābhāvakaraṇājjātarogāṇāṃ tannivartakībhūtāḥ śoṣakapoṣakādayo mātuḥ rasāhārapariṇāmena sarvarogaprāgabhāvaparipālanam | stanyapānena arogāssantaḥ "śatāyuḥ puruṣaśśatendriyaḥ" iti rogakāryapratibandhakasya vidyamānatvāt nivartakanivartyānumitau vyāptirgṛhītuṃ śakyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu māturāhārarasāhārajanyāvayavasthitarasā vā āmayanivartakā iti pratipādyante | virasādanena māturvikāre jāte sati tatra payassthitarasāḥ anivartakā bhavanti pravṛttyādihitā ityasvarasādāha-- avikāramiti |
avikāraṃ nirīkṣyaināṃ mātṛjaṃ pāyayet || 36 ||
mātusstanyapānaṃ śiśorāmayanivartakaṃ na pravartakaṃ yadā bhavati tadā enāṃ mātaraṃ roganivartakadravyebhyaḥ avikāro yathā bhavati tathā kurvan tajjanyaśiśūn mātṛjaṃ stanyaṃ pānayogyaṃ pāyayedityarthaḥ |
śiśoḥ poṣakaśoṣakadravyamapi pravartakaṃ bhavati | kevalaṃ stanyapānamātraṃ na pravartakamityata āha-- yogyeti |
yogyadravyeṇa ca tānpoṣayet || 37 ||
yogyadravyeṇaiva ca tān poṣayet | mātṛstanyapānādinā ajīrṇe jāte na snehadravyaṃ tatra prayojakaṃ bhavati | tattadrogopayogayogyayogayuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ ca tailaṃ ca nivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tadeva yogyadravyamiti tātparyam | tān śiśūn pratyahaṃ poṣayedityarthaḥ |
nanu mātṛstanyapayaḥ tajjanyaśarīrāṇāṃ rogagrastānāṃ tatpayo nivartakaṃ bhavatītyuktam | itaḥ pūrvaṃ ṣaḍrasavaddravyāṇāṃ nivartakatvaṃ pratipāditam | idānīṃ mātṛstanyameva sarvavyādhīnāṃ nivartakaṃ bhavatītyuktam | tathā cet ṣaḍrasavaddravyāṇāṃ nivartakatvaṃ na syādityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvatstanyapāḥ tāvadbālakāḥ stanyapānam ||
stanyapānaṃ śiśūnāṃ dhātupoṣakaṃ annādanāhetukatve sati rasavaddravyapoṣakahetukatvāt | annādanaṃ tu na dhātupoṣakaṃ annādanāyogyadhātupoṣakatvāt poṣakadravyajanyatvācca ityanumānavidhayā mātṛjanyapayasāṃ śiśordhātupoṣakatvaṃ anvayavyatirekapramāṇasiddham | yāvatstanyapānopajīvyaṃ śiśośśarīramiti | sarvadā mātṛputratvamātraṃ śiśutvajñāpakaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ | māturajīrṇejāte sati tacchiśostadajīrṇavaśāt tatsadṛśā āmayāssambhavanti | tatpratīkārastu kena kāraṇena bhāvyaḥ | teṣāṃ śiśūnāṃ cūrṇaghṛtatailādīnāmasaṃbhāvitvāt, ayogyatvācca | tattadāmajanyāmayānāṃ tattadvidhivihitena nivartayitumaśakyatvāt ityasvarasādāha-- bāleti |
bālayogyanivartakaṃ kuryāt || 39 ||
stanyapānāhāropajīvyakāryaviśiṣṭabālāmayānāṃ nivartakaṃ brūmaḥ | tannivartakaṃ recakaprasādhyam | māturāhārajātastanyapānena amitatvena jātaruja ubhayaśarīrajanyatvāt | prāyaśa āmapravṛddhyā tattadvirecakaṃ sukumāradravyayogyakarmakaraṇāt tattadvyādhīnāṃ nivartakamityarthaḥ |
nanu cākṣuṣapratītijanyapramāpratibandhakalavaṇoṣṇarasavaddravyasaṃśayavibhramau cakṣurindriyadoṣajanyau | tannivartakaistu kathaṃ nivartata ityata āha-- śrotreti |
śrotrendriyācchabdajñānam || 40 ||
śrotrendriyajanyaśābdapramāpratibandhakatiktarasavaddravyādanaṃ tatsaṃśayavyatirekānvayau yajjñānapramāpratibandhakau |kaṣāyatiktamadhurarasāḥ pariśuddhāssantaḥ nivartakā bhaveyuriti vaktavyatvāt | tathā sati rasanendriyaviṣayakarasajñānajanyapratītipratibandhakasaṃśayavibhramau kena kāraṇena nivartanīyāvityata āha-- rasaneti |
rasanendriyajanyabhramādāmavirasadoṣādikaikarasātsaṃśayavibhramau bhavataḥ || 41 ||
rasanendriyajanyaśuddharasajñānapratibandhakau svādurasavaddravyādanavibhramādihetukau yatsaṃśayavyatirekaniścayau yāvadindriyajñānagocarapramāpratītipratibandhakau tadviruddhadravyādanaṃ taddheturityarthaḥ |
ghrāṇendriyaviṣayakagandhajñānajanyapramāpratibandhakasaṃśayavibhramaukena hetunā nivartyete ityata āha-- ghrāṇeti |
ghrāṇendriyādgandhapratītistathā || 42 ||
ghrāṇendriyajanyaśuddhagandhajñānapramāpratibandhakāmlarasavaddravyādanaṃ bhramasaṃśayahetukam | tanniścayastaddheturityarthaḥ ||
śrotrendriyaviṣayakaśabdajñānajanyaśābdapramāpratibandhakasaṃśayabhramau kathaṃ nivartyete ityata āha-- śrotreti |
śrotrendriyācchabdajñānaṃ tathā || 43 ||
śrotrendriyajanyaśābdapramāpratītipratibandhakakaṣāyavirasadravyādanaṃ śābdikasaṃśayabhramahetukaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
tvagindriyaviṣayakasparśajñānajanyapramāpratītipratibandhakatiktarasavaddravyādanaṃ tatsaṃśayabhramajanakam | yatsaṃśayavyatirekaniścayau pratibandhakau tanniścayastaddheturiti nyāyenaiva niścaya ityarthaḥ |
tvagindriyaviṣayakajñānapramāpratītipratibandhakadoṣanivṛttyarthaṃ poṣakādhikyaśoṣakadravyayogyakāraṇaṃ tannivartakaṃ bhavatu | poṣakadravyāṇāṃ nivartakatvaṃ svādudravyayuktayogakaraṇaṃ yasya bheṣajaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavati tadeva tvagindriyamityata āha-- śoṣaketi |
śoṣakadravyādhikyajātāsṛkpratibandhakapavanānalagatijātadoṣaviṣayakaviparyayajñānaṃ pramāpratibandhakahetukam || 44 ||
nanu pañcendriyaviṣayakajñānaṃ pratibandhakaruṅnivartakaṃ bhavatu | poṣakadravyayogārthaśoṣakadravyayogakaraṇaṃ kathaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- sārdramiti |
sārdradravyaṃ tatra bheṣajam || 45 ||
sārdradravyaṃ nāma appayaśca mūtraṃ ca madhu ca tailaṃ ca ghṛtaṃ ca, idaṃ sārdradravyaṃ bhavati | etadyogakaraṇaṃ viśiṣya jñāpyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu poṣakadravyayuktālpaśoṣakakalpakadravyayogaṃ bheṣajaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavediti pratīyate | idānīṃ kevalaṃ sārdradravyayogakaraṇena kiṃ phalaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha--sasnehamiti |
sasnehamārdraṃ kāryam || 46 ||
snehayuktadravyaṃ nivartakamiti yathocyate tathā poṣakadravyādhikyajātapārthivadravyaṃ cūrṇīkṛtyāvapet | tatpākakaraṇena nirdravaṃ yathā bhavati tathā agnau vipācya tatpānalepanābhyaṅgāvagāhanāttattajjanyapañcendriyaviṣayakapramāpratibandhakaṃ nivartayituṃ tadeva bheṣajaṃ bhavatīti na vaktavyam | tatra pratyakṣapramāṇena saṃśayabhramayornivāraṇasya śakyatvāt, ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya pratyakṣapramāṇasvarūpaṃ vivicyate-- cākṣuṣeti |
cākṣuṣapratītītarajanyabhinnaviṣayakajñānapramā pramāṇam || 47 ||
cakṣurindriyaviṣayakajñānānubhave sati tadabhāvavati tatprakārakatvaṃ tajjñānasya yathā viṣayo bhavati tathā jākṣuṣapratītijanyetaratvaṃ tadbhinnaviṣayakajñānaṃ pramāṇamiti tāddaśapramāṇena bhramanivṛttyāṃ satyāṃ tannivartakabheṣajaṃ vinā tatpramāṇasya jñātuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu kāmilārogagrastacakṣuṣaḥ asmin śaṅkhe pītaviṣayakajñāne jāte śaṅkhaḥ śvetaḥ śaṃkhatvāt ityanumānapramāṇena na pītatva bhramaḥ, nivartake purovartini pītatvānubhavasya balavattvāditi tattaddoṣarāhityameva bhramasaṃśayapratibandhakaṃ bhavedityata āha-- nirdoṣeti |
nirdoṣacakṣustatra janakam || 48 ||
tadvati tatprakārakatvaṃ doṣarahitacakṣuṣā gṛhyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu bhūyodarśanena dhūmāgnyorvyāptigrahe sati dhūlīpaṭalaṃ dṛṣṭvā yatra vahnibhramo jāyate tatra pūrvānubhavavyāptigrahasya adoṣaviṣayakatvāt bāhyajñānaviṣayakajñānagocarasāmagrīvirahādeva bhramo jāyate | tadbhramasya doṣajanyatvaṃ na vaktavyamityasvarasādāha-- cakṣuriti |
cakṣurgṛhītalaiṅgikajñānaṃ pramāṇam || 49 ||
nirdoṣacakṣuṣā bhūyo bhūyaḥ dhūmaṃ dṛṣṭvā vahniṃ paśyati, yatra dhūmastatrāgniriti | tatra doṣajanakarasavaddravyādanaṃ na kāraṇam | tadabhāve'pi bhramadarśanāt | bāhyendriyaviṣayajñānagocarasāmagrīvaikalyādeva bhramo jāyate | tatra rogotpādakasāmagryā adṛṣṭatvāt | bhramasaṃśayau rogotpādakasāmagrījanyau bhramajanakasāmagrījanyatvāt | tatra virasajanyarogotpādakasāmagryā niyamena dṛṣṭatvāt | āgamapramāṇenāpi bhramanivṛtteḥ bhramo nivartya ityāha-- bhrameti |
bhramābhāvajanakamāptavacanamāgamaḥ || 50 ||
āptavacanajanyā pramā svadoṣajanyapramāpratibandhikā na bhavati | āptavacanasya bhramābhāvapratītijanakatvāt | tasmādrasavirasajanyadoṣahetunā indriyagocarabhramaviṣayakajñānaṃ pratyakṣeṇa vā anumānena pramāṇena vā āgamapramāṇena vā doṣajanyabhramajñānaṃ nivartate | tatra doṣāṇāmeva kāraṇatvapratipādanādityarthaḥ ||
nanu dhūlīpaṭalaṃ dṛṣṭvā dhūmabhramo yatra bhavati tatra doṣa eva hi kāraṇam, na tatpramājanakasāmagrī | tathā sati doṣotpannakāle doṣasāmagrīsatvāt sarvatrāpi bhrama eva syādityata āha-- doṣeti |
doṣajanyajñānaviṣayako viparyayaḥ || 51 ||
viparyayajñānamātrasya doṣajanyatvāt pītaśśaṅkha iti bhramasthale śvaityānumānaṃ bhramanivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | nanu śuktīndriyaviṣayakasaṃsargānantaraṃ śuktiviṣayakajñāne jāte sati ahaṃ śuktiviṣayakajñānavāniti pratītirbhavati | tadanantaraṃ tatraiva anyasya bhramo jāyate | anyapuṃsaḥ atasmin taditi jñānamutpannamiti anyena vākye prayukte sati śaktijñānānubhavāddevadattasya ca śabdajñānānantaraṃ mamotpannaṃ śuktijñānaṃ pramāṇaṃ vā na veti pūrvānubhūtaviṣayikāyāṃ devadattasya ca śuktijñānaviṣayakapratītau vikalpo jāyate | tadbhramajñānaṃ rasavirasajanyarogahetujaṃ na bhavatītyasvarasādāha-- śabdeti |
śabdaprayogajanitajñānapratītiviṣayo vikalpaḥ ||
madindriyārthagocarayogyapurovartidravyaṃ śuktitvajñānaviṣayakajñānaṃ bhavitumarhati samarthapravarttakatvāt | asminviṣaye tadutpannaṃ jñānaṃ rajataviṣayakaṃ saṃśayajñānagrāhakasāmagrīvyatiriktasāmagrījanyatvāt, etadrajataviṣayakajñānaṃ apramāṇaṃ asamarthapravṛttijanakatvāt ityanumānavidhayā saṃśayanivṛtteḥ kartuṃ śakyatvāt | atra saṃśayabhramajanakarasavirasadravyādanasya adṛṣṭatvāt paraprayuktavākyajanyajñānaṃ pramāviṣayakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu idaṃ purovartīndriyaviṣayakaṃ pramāpramāgocarobhayajñānagrāhakasāmagrīviṣayakaṃ śuktirajatobhayaviṣayakajñānagrāhakasāmagrījanyatvādityasvarasādāha-- saditi |
sadasatsambandhavivekavidhirahitajñānaviṣayakam ||
asminviṣaye devadattasya jñānaṃ śuktiviṣayakam | yajñadattasya jñānaṃ rajataviṣayakam | etasminviṣaye sadasajjñānagocarajñānamidamiti nirṇetumaśakyatvāt idaṃ jñānaṃ na rogajanyaṃ, kiṃ tu anyaprayuktavākyajanyaṃ, tadanyajñānasya rogajanyatvāt | mamotpannajñānasya arogajanyatvādeva sadasadvidhivirahitajñānaviṣayakaḥ sa eva saṃśayo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
itaḥ paraṃ kaiścidvyākhyātaṃ vyācaṣṭe-- sacchabdasya purovartiśuktikāśakalaṃ indriyaviṣayakamartho bhavati | tatpadārthamuddiśya haṭṭapaṭṭaṇasthitarajatasadṛśacākacakyādisāmagrījātapurovartijñānaviṣayakaśuktau anyasya idaṃ rajataṃ iti jñānaṃ tadajñānaviṣayakaṃ asadviṣayakaṃ vā sādhyaṃ, tatsambandhavivekajñānamityucyate | tadvirahitajñānasya yaḥ padārtho viṣayo bhavati sa evārtho jñānaviṣayaka ityarthaḥ |
nanu itarapadārthajñānarahitajñānaviṣayakaṃ pramātvamiti vaktavyam | tathā sati asmin jñānaviṣayakajñānakāle sarvapadārthasāmagryabhāvāt kathaṃ purovartijñānaṃ pramātvena jñātuṃ śakyata ityasvarasādāha-- abhāveti |
abhāvapratyayāvalambanā vṛttiḥ nidrā || 54 ||
nitarāṃ purovartiviṣayakajñānagocarajñānagrāhakasāmagrīkāle taditarapadārthajñāne sati itarābhāvavati pramāṇatvaṃ jñātuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ |
sā pratītiḥ tiktakaṭurasavaddravyādanādbhavati ||
nanu sarvapadārthendriyasannikarṣasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ tattatsāmagryā tattadartho grahītuṃ śakyate | tasmāt sarvapadārthaviṣayakajñānagrāhakasāmagrī yāvajjñānasāmagrī śuktiviṣayakajñānagrāhakasāmagrī tāvatsāmagryāḥ tadānīmasaṃbhāvitatvāt kācitsāmagrī vaktavyā | sā samagrī tiktakaṭurasadravyādanasāmagrī yasyāsti sā sāmagrī sarvapadārthagrāhikā bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu sarvapadārthetaratvaṃ śuktau kathaṃ sambhavati sarvapadārthānāṃ sānnidhyābhāvāt | uktarītyā purovartinaṃ grahītuṃ na śakyata evetyasvarasādāha-- pramāṇeti |
pramāṇenānubhūtārthāsaṃpramoṣaḥ smṛtiḥ || 56 ||
pramātvena pūrvānubhūtārthaṃ purovartiviṣayakajñānagrāhakakāle padārthatvāvacchedena pūrvānubhūtapadārthaṃ upasthitā bhavantītyatra na śaṅkāviṣayā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tāvadarthān manasi smṛtvā pramoṣarāhityaṃ kartuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ | sātvikaguṇodayasāmagrī madhurarasavaddravyādanasāmagrī | tena hetunā manasā padārthatvāvacchedena sarvapadārthānsaṅgṛhya smartuṃ śakyata evetyāha-- madhureti |
madhurarasapariṇāmājjātasātvikodayāt so'yamiti vyapadeśaḥ || 57 ||
madhurarasavaddravyādanaṃ yasyāsti tatparimāṇavaśāt sātvikaguṇodayo bhavati | tatsātvikaguṇamahimnā sarvapadārthopasthitiṃ manasā saṅgrahītuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ | tasmātso'yamiti vyapadeśo bhavatīti pratīyate |
nanu śuddharasapariṇāmena sātvikaguṇodayāt indriyārthasaṃyoge sati tadvati tatprakārakatayā tanniṣṭhaprāmāṇyaṃ grahītuṃ śakyate | virasānnādanena rogī bhavan atadvati tatprāmāṇyaṃ gṛhṇāti, tatra doṣasāmagryā balavattvāt iti rasavirasadravyādanaṃ prāmāṇyāpramāṇyagrāhakasāmagrī ityukte idānīṃ yogābhyāsavaśātprāmāṇyaṃ grahītuṃ śakyata evetyasvarasādāha-- itareti |
itarendriyayogavirāmo'ntarniyamito'bhyāsaḥ |
svasya indriyārthasannikarṣe sati tajjñānaviṣayakajñānaniṣṭhāprāmāṇyajñānapratibandhakāḥ itarapadārthāropaṇasāmagrīsaṃpādanaviṣayārthāḥ itaraśabdena pratipāditāḥ | svendriyaviṣayajñānagrāhakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ tadviṣayakajñānaṃ tadviṣayakajñānābhāvaśca | evamitarendriyayogavirāmaḥ | tadvirāmaḥ antarindriyeṇa manasā antarātmani abhyāsavaśāt niyamitaḥ, tasya pratyayena niyamena purovartini prāmāṇyaṃ gṛhyata evetyarthaḥ ||
idaṃtvena jñātaṃ vastu itarabhinnaṃ bhāsate | mamotpannaṃ jñānaṃ saṃśayabhramānyaviṣayakaṃ samarthapravṛttijanakatvāt, ityanumānavidhayā prāmāṇye gṛhīte virasadravyādanasya antassthitadoṣajanyarogasya balavattvāt purovartini saṃśayanivṛttiḥ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- viṣayeti |
viṣayavirāgataḥ pravāhotsāhāt punaḥpunarabhiniveśanaṃ vairāgyam || 59 ||
asyārthaḥ-svacakṣurindriyagocaraviṣayakajñānasyāviṣayo rajatam | asmin bhrāntirāhityaṃ virāgatvaṃ, antaścittapravāhe śuktiriyamiti dhārāvāhikajñānam | taddhyānāhitajñāne utsāhaḥ prītiḥ, punaḥ punarabhiniveśanaṃ paunaḥpunyaṃ bhṛśārtho vā | iyaṃ śuktiḥ nedaṃ rajatamiti (saṃśaya) kriyādirāhityaṃ virāgasambandhi vairāgyam | purovartiviṣayaḥ śuktirbhavitumarhati rajataviṣayakajñānāviṣayakatve sati saviṣayakatvāt | manasi utpannaśuktiviṣayakajñānasya aprāmāṇyābhāvatvaṃ sidhyatītyarthaḥ |
nanu dhārāvāhikajñānānāṃ gṛhītagrāhiṇāmapi purovartinīdantvena rajatatvena apramājanakavākyajñānena śuktau saṃśayabhramajñānaviṣayakaṃ svata eva tadbhavatītyasvarasādāha-- dṛṣṭeti |
dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayatṛṣṇāvaśīkaraṇasaṃjñāstathā ||
pūrovartinīdantvena jñānotpattiṃ vinā idaṃ śuktiviṣayakaṃ śuktiviṣayakajñānagrāhakasāmagrījanyatvāt ityanumānena purovartiśuktikāśakale vigatatṛṣṇā yasyāssāpi vigatatṛṣṇā, tasyā vaśīkaraṇasaṃjñakaṃ viṣayo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu purovartini śuktau indriyārthasakalasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ tadvati tatprakārakānubhave sati so'nubhavo na bhramasaṃśayajñāna pratibandhakaḥ bhramajanakabhramagocarasāmagrīsatvāt | tathā hi-- bhavadutpannaṃ jñānaṃ na śuktiviṣayakaṃ, kintu tadrajataviṣayakaṃ, haṭṭapaṭṭaṇasthitarajataniṣṭhacākacakyādirūpaguṇavattvasya samānadharmatvāt, tasmādityanumānapramāṇena mamotpannaṃ jñānaṃ bhramaviṣayakaṃ ityanumānapramāṇenāpi tadvati tatprakārakatvasya jñātumaśakyatvāt | pratyakṣajanyapramāṇajñānasya anumitijanyapramāpratibandhakatvāt | tasmādubhayatrāpi prāmāṇyāgrahādityasvarasādāha-- tābhyāmiti |
tābhyāṃ cittavṛttinirodhaḥ || 61 ||
citte manasi vartata iti cittavṛttiḥ | tābhyāṃ pramāṇābhyāṃ tadvati tatprakārakatvāgrahaḥ rasavirasajanyarogahetukatvādityarthaḥ ||
nanu ayaṃ śuddharasavaddravyādanavān bhavati | so'prāmāṇyābhāvajñānavān bhavati | evamākāreṇa ubhayorvyāptigrahe sati apramājñānaṃ vaktumaśakyam | tathā hi-- pramāpratīkārakasāmagrīśuddharasavaddravyādanasāmagrīti vaktuṃ na śakyate | purovartini tadvati tatprakārakajñānānantaraṃ tasminviṣaye anyasya doṣavaśādbhramo jāyate | tena hetunā vākyaprayogo jātaḥ | etadutpannajñānaṃ apramāṇaviṣayakamiti vākyaśravaṇānantaraṃ etasyāpi prāmāṇyaviṣayajñāne sati saṃśayo bhavati | virasādanamapi aprāmāṇyapratipādakasāmagrī bhavati, ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya bhramotpādakasāmagryantaraṃ pratipādayati-- yāvaditi |
yāvaddhātusirādisañcaratprāṇāpānavyānodānasamānānilanirodhanaṃ bhramahetukam || 62 ||
prāṇādipañcakānāṃ nirodhanaṃ yatra dṛśyate tatra rogotpattirbhavati | tatpavananirodhanena āśayā duṣṭā bhavanti | dhātavo'pi duṣṭā bhavanti | tajjanyakācakāmilādivaśāt saṃśayavibhramau bhavata ityarthaḥ |
nanu sarvatrāpi saṃśayabhramajanakasāmagryāṃ prāmāṇyajñānocchedassyāt ityasvarasādāha-- āsanneti |
āsannaviṣayakajñānahetukāntarātmā'ntaḥkaraṇayogābhyāsavaśāt pavananirodhanādarogī sa cirāyurbhavati || 63 ||
