nanu ghaṭendriyasannikarṣānantaraṃ tajjñānagrāhakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ bhramajñānānubhavo bhavet | tanniṣṭhaprāmāṇyamapi gṛhyatāṃ nāma, itarendriyaviṣayeṣu tajjñānaviṣayakaprāmāṇyaṃ kathaṃ gṛhyate ? pañcendriyaviṣayajñāneṣu virāgo nāma viśeṣeṇa rāgo virāga ityarthaḥ | tasmātpañcendriyaviṣayajñāneṣu viśeṣeṇa rāgo bhavatītyarthaḥ | indriyārthasannikarṣānantaraṃ ātmamanassaṃyogasakalasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ yāvadviṣayakajñānamutpadyate, taddhetukaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ aprāmāṇyagrāhakasāmagrīvyatiriktasāmagrījanyatvāt, madutpannaghaṭajñānaviṣayakaprāmāṇyavat | ātmā jīvaḥ | antaḥkaraṇaṃ cittam | tasya jīvātmanā yogaḥ | tadvaśāt tatkāraṇavaśāt tadviṣayakajñānagocarajñānaniṣṭhaprāmāṇyapratibandhakaṃ bhramajanakasāmagrī pavananirodhanaṃ arogakāraṇaṃ, aprāmāṇyābhāvasāmagrījanyatvāt ityarthaḥ |
nanu viṣayapañcakeṣu virāgo nāma viśeṣeṇa rāgo virāgaḥ | śāstrasya viṣayānubhavamātraphalaṃ labhyate | tena mokṣavirodho bhavati | pratyuta sa vyādhikārako bhavati | etena cirāyuṣṭvaṃ kathaṃ prāpyate ? ityasvarasādāha-- tasmāditi |
tasmātprāṇādipañcakaphalībhūtaṃ kāryam || 64 ||
asyārthaḥ-- śarīraṃ doṣadhātumalāśayātmakam, prāṇādipañcakopajīvyaprakṛtyādhārakatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ, yathā ghaṭaḥ | ceṣṭāśrayaṃ śarīramiti ceṣṭāśrayatvaṃ śarīrāvacchedakam | ceṣṭā tu sā yaddhitāhitaprāptiparihārārthaṃ spandanarūpā | tasmāt śarīraṃ prāṇādipañcakaṃ phalībhūtaṃ bhavati | sā ceṣṭāśrayā prakṛtiriti | idaṃ sarvaṃ śrutyā pratipāditam | sā śrutiḥ-- "prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānasaprāṇā śvetavarṇātmikā sā tripadā vyāhṛtitrayātmikā" | tatra śrutivacanaṃ pramāṇam-- "bhūriti vai prāṇaḥ | bhuva ityapānaḥ | suvariti vyānaḥ | etairmahacchiro bhavati | maha ityannam | annena vāva sarve prāṇā mahīyante |" iti tasmāt prāṇādipañcakaṃ saṃrakṣaṇīyaṃ rogābhāvakāryahetukaṃ mokṣahetuḥ kathaṃ bhavet | yasya prāṇādipañcakaṃ svavaśaṃ bhavati, tasya cirāyuṣṭvamapi labhyate | tasmātprāṇādipañcakaṃ phalībhūtaṃ kāryamityuktam | tasmācchuddharasavaddravyādanaṃ śarīrasaṃrakṣaṇaṃ karoti, virasavaddravyādanaṃ prāṇaghātaṃ karotīti paryavasitārthaḥ |
nanu prāṇādipañcakasya phalībhūtaṃ nāma-- prāṇavāyuḥ dehacalanakarma vidhatte | sa pavanaḥ avikṛtassan pādābhyāṃ gatāgatatvaṃ ca karma nimeṣonmeṣādikaṃ ca āpādamastakaṃ karma kurvan calanātmakaṃ karma kurute | apānavāyośca prayojanamasti-- meḍhragudapradeśeṣu sthitassan svayamavikṛtassan tathā vikriyate | vyānānilasya kāryaṃ ca–- pācakapittena svayamapi pañcadhā bhūtvā pācakapittamapi pañcadhā kurvan annaṃ gṛhṇāti, pacati, viveśayati | sārakiṭṭatayā bhuktānnaṃ vibhajya muñcati | āśayapraveśaṃ karoti | sa sarvaśarīraṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhati | udānavāyośca prayojanaṃ--nāsādiparyantaṃ sañcaran vākpravṛttiṃ karoti | balavarṇayośca vṛddhiṃ vidhatte | samānavāyośca prayojanaṃ-- jaṭharānilasya samīpe sthitassan bhuktānnarasaṃ pakvānnaṃ kāyaṃ praveśayan pratimuñcati | evaṃ pañcavāyūnāṃ phalam | teṣāṃ mokṣaphalapradānasya anāvaśyakatvādityasvarasādāha-- adṛṣṭeti |
adṛṣṭenendriyeṇātmā manasā saṃyujyate || 65 ||
adṛṣṭaḥ īśvaraḥ, tena preritassan arthasaṃsargagocarendriyeṇa tadviṣayakagocaraphalaṃ vijñāya aupādhikaheyopādeyavidhiphalaṃ vijñāya mana itarapadārthaviṣayakavijñānaṃ visṛjya ātmajñānagocarajñānaphalaṃ āmuṣmikaphalaṃ ca jīvātmano vidhatte | evaṃ manaḥ ātmasaṃyogajanyādhikaphalaṃ jīvasya prayacchatītyarthaḥ |
nanu ātmā manasā saṃyujyate, mana indriyeṇa, indriyamarthena, indriyāṇāṃ prāpyakāritvaniyamāt | tata ātmā indriyaviṣayakajñānagocaraphalaṃ bhuṅkte | na tatphalamāmuṣmikaṃ viṣayasaṃyogajanyajñānaphalasya balavatvāt, ityasvarasādāha-- sarveti |
sarvadhātavaḥ satvavaśaṃ gatāḥ || 66 ||
manonirodhanena śvāsocchvāsanirodhanena ca mukhamārgeṇa bahiḥ pavananirodhanena ca svodarapūraṇakarmakaraṇena ca pṛṣṭhamārgeṇāpi bahiḥ pavanāpahāraṇam | tatpavanaṃ nābhipradeśe'pi kṛtakarmakaraṇāntaram pavanena kukṣisthitasirāmārgasyānubandhanaṃ niśvāsocchvāsānāṃ kaṇṭhapradeśanirodhanaṃ āpādamastakaṃ sañcaratītyarthaḥ |
nanu bāhyendriyaviṣayajñānagocarajñāne vairāgyaṃ manoviṣayakaṃ ātmaviṣayajñānahetukaṃ bhavatīti tatra bāhyendriyaviṣayajñānaniṣṭhabhramābhāvānubhavagrāhakasāmagryeva bhramotpādakasāmagrīvyatiriktasāmagrījanyeti vaktavyam | tathāhi rogābhāvakāryaṃ pañcendriyanirodhanajanyayogābhyāsapavanavaśāt bhavati | tathā sati dhātavo dṛḍhatarāssantaḥ ? rogābhāvakāryakāraṇavaśāt śrutipratipāditaphalaṃ labhyata ityasvarasādāha-- yatkāleti |
yatkāle yadyoge yathāvidhi phalapradāḥ prajāḥ prajāyante || 67 ||
tatsaṃyogajātadhātavaḥ pravartakāḥ || 68 ||
yāṃ kāmayeta duhitaraṃ priyā syāditi |
tāṃ niṣṭhāya dadyāt | priyaiva bhavati |
sa na tu punarāgacchati |
atra tu arogatvasampādanavaśāt śatāyuḥ puruṣaḥ śatendriyatvaṃ ca labhate |
nanu mātāpitroḥ dauhitrajananaṃ mokṣakaram | tayordhātudārḍhyakaraṇasya āvaśyakatvena rogābhāvakāryakaraṇadvārā mokṣaprāptirbhavatīti duhitṛśiśussākṣātsāmagrī strīprayuktaraktādhikyasāmagryā bhavatītyasvarasādāha-- strīpuṃsāviti |
strīpuṃsāvātmabhāgau bhinnamūrtessisṛkṣayā || 69 ||
strīpuṃsormadhye putrotpādakahetubhūtaretasa ādhikyaṃ putrajananahetukam | putrikotpādanahetubhūtaṃ raktādhikyaṃ putrikājananahetukam | svasmādbhinnamūrtereva putrādiriti smṛtau | mokṣārthinaḥ pravṛttiḥ putrikājananasāmagrīsaṃpādanapūrvikā dānayogyakanyājananasāmagryā eva hetutvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | putrikā jananasāmagrī sampannaputrikājananasāmagrī svasukhahetukamithunānubhavakāryaupakāryakatvāt | prajājananasāmagrī laikikasāmagrīsaṃpādanaṃ vinā kathaṃ labhyata ityasvarasādāha-- prakṛtīti |
prakṛtipuruṣau pitarāviti smṛtau || 70 ||
tathā sati guṇaviśiṣṭapuruṣatvāt māyāśabalita īśvaraḥ āmuṣmikaphalaṃ datta ityasvarasādāha-- vṛttīti |
vṛttivyavahitasvarūpaniṣṭhamanautsāhātpunaḥpunarabhiniveśanaṃ yogābhyāsaḥ || 71 ||
vṛttivyavahitapuruṣaḥ aupādhikasaṃsargajātaḥ prajājanakasaṃsargahetubhūtatvāt | svabhāvataḥ tasya guṇaprotadravyatvādeva vṛtivyavahitatvaṃ bhavati | tadeva tatsvarūpam | tasmin nihitaṃ manaḥ | tasya manasa utsāhaḥ prītiḥ | prītereva hṛdaye'bhiniveśanam | itarapadārtheṣu virāgassan ya ātmānaṃ dhyāyati sa taṃ dhyātāraṃ saṃsārasāgaranimagnaṃ tārayati | tadevottamaphalam | tadarthamevāyurvedapravṛtteḥ prayojanam | idameva bahupuruṣārthapradānahetukaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | yasya manasa utsāho jāyate tasya manasaḥ punarabhiniveśanaṃ tṛptikārakam | sarvadā abhyāsapāṭavavaśāt sarvasminviṣaye adhyātmaniṣṭhā manaḥ prasādya mokṣapradā bhavati |
ādarātiśayodbhūtabhūyobhūyonirīkṣāyogādiṣu bhūmiḥ || 72 ||
tatra bhūyo nirīkṣaṇāt svarūpaṃ ca pratipadyate | paramānandahetukatvāt iha loke paramasukhapradānatvāt bhūyonirīkṣaṇayogādiṣu bhūmiḥ aparimitānandasya bhūrityarthaḥ |
nanu ātmaviṣaye yāvadviṣayanāśe sati tannāśādityasvarasādāha-- tāmiti |
tāṃ niṣṭhāya dadyāt || 73 ||
sālaṅkārarūpādiviśiṣṭāṃ kanyāṃ yo dadyāt satkulaprasūtāya śrotriyāya, kanyādāne yaḥ pravartate tasya sukhaṃ mokṣaphalaṃ labhyate, aihikasukhamapi bhuṅkta ityarthaḥ |
kanyādānaphalamāha priyeti |
priyaiva bhavati || 74 ||
kanyādānaphalapradānasya pratibhūḥ īśvaro bhavatītyarthaḥ | īśvaraprītyarthaṃ yaḥ kanyādānaṃ karoti tasya aihikaphalamāmuṣmikaphalaṃ ca dīyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu kanyājananameva kāmayan svakarmakleśacoditassan īśvaraprītyarthaṃ dānaṃ karoti | tena īśvarassuprīto bhūtvā svayameva pratibhūssan phalaṃ dadātītyuktam | tatra kanyādānakarmaṇaḥ īśvaraprītireva phalaṃ bhavati | tenāpi tasya kiṃ phalamāsīdityasvarasādāha-- neheti
neha punarāgachati || 75 ||
yattasya ihalokasukhaduḥkhādikaṃ tadīśvaro nādadīta, īśvarakaivalyaṃ punarādadīta | ihalokaṃ prati sa punarnāgacchati garbhaduḥkhādikaṃ na bhuṅkte | tasmādyogābhyāsavaśāt saptadhātumayaṃ śarīramiti śarīrasya aihikaphalaṃ mokṣaphalaṃ ca | tayorvairāgyameva ubhayaphalaṃ vidhatta ityāha-- aihiketi |
aihikāmuṣmikaviṣayānubhāvyabhogaikanāśyamiti jñānaṃ vairāgyam || 76 ||
aviṣayānandātmakanairantaryajñānānandahetukaṃ vairāgyam || 77 ||
aihikāmuṣmikaphalajanyetarapadārthaviṣayakaphalānyuddiśya ātmā anubhavaviṣayakamanojanyatvāt tadvairāgyanairantaryajñānānandajanyaṃ viṣayānandānubhavaṃ vihāya ātmajñānānubhavaḥ paramapuruṣārtha iti itaraviṣayakajñānānubhavaṃ nirasyati | aihikāmuṣmikaphale nāśye iti jñānaṃ vairāgyam | aihikāmuṣmikaphalaṃ viṣayānubhāvyaṃ tattucchaṃ karma prayāsādanubhave sati tannāśāt | tasmādadhikaphaladaṃ vairāgyameva hi mokṣaphalahetukaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu paramātmano'tīndriyatvena indriyārthasannikarṣābhāvāt ātmaviṣayakajñānābhāvāt tajjñānaviṣayakānubhavānandassutarāmaprasiddha eva, kintu indriyārthasannikarṣādyādṛśārthopalabdhiḥ saiva sukhaviṣayakajñānānubhavopalabdhiścet tajjñānameva ānandānubhavahetukam | tadeva kāryaṃ yāvaddhātvindriyadārḍhyāpādakaśuddharasānnādanajanyajñānaṃ ānandahetukamiti pramāṇatrayopalabdhirityata āha-- tasmāditi |
tasmāddhātavastadrogabhūmyāvaṣṭambhāḥ || 78 ||
indriyārthasannikarṣajanyajñānaṃ ānandahetukamiti uktam | tasmāditi-– tatkāraṇavaśāt dhātavaḥ pañcendriyāṇi tadrogakāryakārakāṇītyarthaḥ |
nanu bhogāyatanaṃ śarīramiti prasiddham | tathā sati viṣayamātraphaloddeśyakāryakāraṇaviṣayanāśyaikanāśyaṃ iti tanniṣeddhumaśakyam | yāvadviṣayānubhavakāla eva bhogakāla ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha-- bhogeti |
bhogāyatanaṃ śarīram || 79 ||
sarvārthaviṣayakabhogyayogyānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ āyatanam | sukhaduḥkhānubhavo bhoga iti duḥkhasyāpi bhogayogyaviṣayakāmanātmakadravyopalambhakatvāt | tasmādbhogāyatanaṃ śarīramiti | śarīratvasya bhogāyatanatvāvacchedakatvādīti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt |
nanu sukhaviṣayakapravṛttikamanoviṣayakasukhānubhavajñānaviṣayakapravṛttimuddiśya pravṛttestadviṣayānubhavajānandaḥ matkāmanāviṣayakaḥ duḥkhaviṣayakajñānasya matkāmanāviṣayakatvābhāvāt | tadarthameva bhogopakāryajanakasaṃpādanasāmagryaiva phalitārthatvāt |
nanu sukhasyāpi duḥkhānuṣaṅgatvāt sukhamapi duḥkhameva | viṣayaduḥkhānubhavastu duḥkhajanakakarmavyatiriktakarmaṇā sādhyaḥ | tasmāduktarītyā ānandahetukaṃ bāhyendriyaviṣayopalabdhijñānānubhavakāryaṃ na bhavedityasvarasādāha-- taditi |
tatparamapuruṣakhyāterguṇavaitṛṣṇyam || 80 ||
tadeva paramapuruṣārthaḥ, yannityadravyajanyajñānaviṣayakajñāna ānandahetukaṃ bhavati, sā khyātiḥ saccidānandātmikā | ānandaviṣayakajñānaṃ yasyāsti sa eva jīvanmukta ityabhidhīyate | sa sarvadā''nandānubhavavān bhavati | matpravṛttistu duḥkhavyatiriktā, kāmanāprayuktatvāt, sukhātmikā kāmanā paramapuruṣārthikā | gu ṇavaitṛṣṇyaṃ nāma madhurarasādanaprayuktapuruṣaviṣayakajñānajanyajñānaṃ ānandahetukamiti vivakṣitamityarthaḥ | puruṣakhyātiḥ paramapuruṣajñānaviṣayakakhyātiḥ | viṣayajñānānandahetukavairāgyaṃ bāhyendriyaviṣayakajñānaṃ paramapuruṣagocaraṃ na bhavati, indriyārthasannikarṣābhāvāt, kiṃ tu antarindriyagocarajñānaviṣayakaṃ yāvadviṣayakajñānaṃ yāvadanubhavahetukaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadindriyaviṣayajñānānāṃ yadārthollekhibhāvanā kriyate sa vitarkaḥ || 81 ||
tarko vicāra ityarthaḥ | yāvadindriyaviṣayajñānānāṃ yāvavadviṣayānubhava ānandahetuka iti vicāraḥ, tarkasahakṛtajñānakāryakarmetyarthaḥ | yadārthollekhibhāvanākramastarkajñānena bhāvyaḥ tadbhāvanāyāḥ yathāyathaṃ yāvadindriyaviṣayakajñānānāṃ yāvadviṣayānubhava ānandahetuko bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu yāvadviṣayānubhavajñānaṃ yāvadānandahetukam | paramātmānubhavajñānaṃ paramānandahetukaṃ, śarīrasya ubhayorapyāyatanatvāt | tatra viṣayānubhavasya viṣayaikanāśyatvena paramātmānubhavasya paramātmaviṣayakatvena ayaṃ nityānandānubhava ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha--pūrveti |
pūrvāparānusandhānaśabdārthollekhabhāvanā nirvikalpaḥ || 82 ||
bāhyendriyajñānānubhavavān savitarkaḥ | savitarko nāma savikalpaḥ | savikalpajñānānusandhānameva paramātmānusandhānam | tadanirvācyajñānaviṣayakam | śabdārthollekhabhāvanāśūnyatvam | tadeva nirvikalpakarma | devadatto jīvanmukto bhavitumarhati paramātma viṣayakajñānānubhavāt | nityanaimittikakarmadvārā paramātmānubhavajñānamupalakṣyate | tasyāpi śarīradārḍhyakaraṇahetutvena tadeva ārogyakāryakaraṇamāvaśyakaṃ bhavati | ata eva bhogāyatanaṃ śarīramiti yathārthaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tatra smṛtivacanaṃ--
nityanaimittikaṃ karma duritakṣayakārakam |
jñānaṃ ca vimalīkurvan abhyāsena tu vedavit |
abhyāsapakvavijñānaḥ kaivalyaṃ labhate naraḥ |
tatra sūtrasthānavacanaṃ—
annapānaṃ viṣādrakṣet viśeṣeṇa mahīpateḥ |
yogakṣemau tadāyattau dharmādyā yannibandhanāḥ ||
iti | tasmāccharīradārḍhyakaraṇahetukaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
nanu paramātmā manoviṣayako na bhavati, nirguṇatvāt niravayavadravyatvāt niṣkāyakatvāt kālavat, manasa ātmagocaratvasya vaktumaśakyatvādityasvarasādāha-- antariti |
antaḥkaraṇadharmāvacchinnaviṣayāvalambitadeśakālabhāvanāyogaḥ savicāraḥ || 83 ||
paramātmā vicāraṇīyaḥ ata eva savitarka tarkasahakṛtapramāṇena jñātavyaḥ | tathāhi-- aṅkurādikaṃ sakartṛkaṃ kāryatvāt ghaṭavat, iti tarkasahakṛtānumānena jñātavyaḥ | antaḥkaraṇadharmāvacchinnaviṣayāvalambitatvaṃ manoviṣayakadharmatvaṃ, sarvadā ātmānubhavatvāt | tasya avacchedakaṃ śarīraṃ sat viṣayānubhavārhaṃ bhavati | tasyāpi śarīradārḍhyakaraṇamāvaśyakamityarthaḥ | manaḥātmānaṃ viṣayīkaroti | tasya svarūpamā nandasvarūpam | evaṃ guṇaviśiṣṭaparamātmānaṃ viṣayīkṛtya avalambate | evaṃ vicārarūpatarkasya rogābhāvakāryajanakaprayojanaprayatanaṃ phalaṃ bhavati | tasmāddeśakālabhāvanā yogasya vicāra ityuktam | deśastu dravyopādhinā tridhā bhavati | kvaciddeśaviśeṣajanyadravyādanena taddeśīyānāṃ vātādhikyaṃ bhavati | kvaciddeśaviśeṣajātadravyādanātpittaprakopo bhavati | kvaciddeśaviśeṣajanyadravyādanena kaphaprakopo bhavati | tattaddeśīyānāṃ tattadroganivartakadravyāṇāṃ tattaddeśasthitānāṃ śarīradārḍhyakaraṇaṃ vicārayedityarthaḥ | tattatkāladravyādanamapi pittaroganivartakamiti tattatprayojanāni vicārahetukāni saṃpādya kālaṃ prāpayedityarthaḥ ||
nanu manasa ātmaprakāśārthaṃ ātmavyatiriktasarvapadārthajñānasyāvaśyakatvāt tarkasahakṛtajñānamāvaśyakaṃ bhavati | sa tarka ātmājñānārthaṃ paraṃparayā heturbhavati | savicāra ityuktam | tarhi nirvicāro'pi mokṣaprapādako bhavatītyucyamāne sati vicārābhāvo nirvicāraḥ kathaṃ bhavatītyasvarasādāha-- asminniti |
asmindeśakāladharmāvacchinnadharmamātrāvabhāsanaṃ nirvicāraḥ || 84 ||
asmin brahmaṇi deśataḥ kālataḥ vastutaśca paricchedarāhityaṃ tasminnāropitadharmamātraṃ idaṃ bhāsate | tatra itarapadārthaviṣayakajñānarāhityasya upasthitatvāt, tatretarapadārthavicārābhāvo nirvicāra ityarthaḥ ||
nanu sarvaviṣayajñāne jāte sati ānando bhavati | etadabhāvo nirvicāraḥ, tarhyānandānubhavopalabdhiḥ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- sukheti |
sukhaprakāśodrekāccicchaktiviṣayakassa ānandaḥ ||
sukhānandānubhavārtho'pi yatsupte sati prakāśyate sa evānando bhavati | nirvicārabhāvyamanodrekasya heturbhavati | tasmāccicchaktiviṣayakaḥ citsvarūpajñānaśaktiviṣayakaḥ ānando bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu parabrahmaṇaḥ nirguṇatve'pi sa eva guṇavānbhavati | nirguṇatvaṃ nāma niravayavatvasya avacchedakatvam | ātmano'pi nirguṇatvāt manasastadgocaratvaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate | nirguṇatvasya niravayavadravyatvāt, manaso'pi nirguṇatvāt, indriyatvena ātmaviṣayakajñānagrāhakatvaṃ vaktumaśakyatvāt, iti ātmanaḥ agocaratvādeva indriyāṇāmapi tatprāpyatvāsaṃbhavena manaso'pyaprayojakatvāpattirityata āha--asminniti |
asmingrāhyasatyasattāmātraviṣayakatvena samādhiḥ sā smṛtā || 86 ||
asminbrahmaṇi grahītuṃ yogyo jīvaḥ tasya paramātmaviṣayakatvena brahmasattā svayaṃ jagastvarūpakāraṇatvena prakāśyata iti satyaṃ brahma manaso'pyaviṣayo bhavati | guṇarahitabrahmaṇaḥ manogrāhyatvasya vaktumaśakyatvāt | satteti sattāmātraviṣayakaṃ jñānamupalabhyate | ātmanā saha manassamādhīyata iti samādhiḥ sā smṛtā |
nanu nirguṇabrahmaṇaḥ jagatkārakatvaṃ vaktumayogyamiti cet, na, nirguṇabrahmaṇaḥ karacaraṇādyavayavābhāvāt na jagatsvarūpatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate | tathā śrutau tastvarūpaṃ pratipādyate--
etaṃ manomayamātmānamupasaṅkrāmati |
etaṃ vijñānamayamātmānamupasaṅkrāmati |
etacchrutyanusāreṇa jagastvarūpatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratipādyate, parabrahmaṇaḥ nirguṇatvaṃ pratipādyate | śuddhanirguṇapakṣe yogināmapyagocaratvena samādhireva na syāt | kiṃ ca manasaḥ ātmagocaratvānupapattiśca | jagato'nityatvena tasyāpyanityatvaprasaṅgāt, iti nāśaṅkanīyam, jīvātmano māyāśabalitatvena tathā pratīteḥ | atastu māyānivṛttiparyantaṃ vastubhedo'sti | sarvathā yasya samādhinā māyā na nivartate tāvatparyantaṃ vastubhedo nāsti, tatsamādhinā māyānivṛtteḥ | yadā manasa ātmasākṣātkāro'sti tadā ātmano manoviṣakatvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ | yadā jīvātmani māyānivṛttirupalabhyate tasya parabrahma jagadākāreṇa pariṇamati | ayaṃ ghaṭa ityatra ghaṭajñānaviṣayakatvamiva nirguṇabrahma manoviṣayakaṃ bhavati | saiva pramā bhavati | ata evoktaṃ "sā smṛtā" iti | prakṛtireva smṛtetyarthaḥ | sarveṣāṃ mokṣahetussamādhiḥ | tasya sarveṣāmasambhāvitatvāt tatpramāphalakasāmagryā abhāvācca | teṣāṃ madhurarasavaddravyādanasya sātvikaguṇapradāyakatvāt sātvikaguṇassamādhihetuḥ, rājasatāmasaguṇajanakāmloṣaṇarasavyatiriktasātvikaguṇahetumadhurarasajanyatvāt | tasmānmadhurarasa eva samādhiheturityata āha-- madhureti |
madhuraraso rasāsṛggatapavanāvikṛtamadhupravohātsāhodayaḥ pūrayan aṅguṣṭhaṃ ca samāśritaḥ || 87 ||
madhurarasastu rasāsṛgdhātucarapavanasya avikāraṃ kurvan kuṇḍalīsthitaḥ śvāsocchvāsābhyāṃ śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtamāhṛtya āpādamastakaṃ tarpayan iḍāmārgagamanena pavanena kumbhakaṃ kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhaparyantaṃ sañcārya tattadaṅgaṃ prasiñcedityarthaḥ |
nanu kalā prabalā satī madhurasajanyapavanasya śvāsocchvāsānilābhyāṃ śirassthitāmṛtamāhṛtya śirassantarpaṇaṃ vidhatta iti vaktavyam | kalā abalā satī tena āpādamastakasantarpaṇena dhātupoṣaṇaṃ kathaṃ vidhatta ityasvarasādāha-- tatreti |
tatra sthitakalā daśadalakamalaṃ vikāsayantī pracalati || 88 ||
kuṇḍalyudbhavapittakalādhāradaśadalapadmaṃ vikāsayantī amṛtaṃ visṛjatītyarthaḥ |
nanu pavanastu śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtamāhṛtya dhātusantarpaṇaṃ karotītyuktam | idaṃ tu samādhisāmagrīsampādanaprakaraṇam | samādhiphalaṃ tu ātmasākṣātkārānubhavarūpam | tatsāmagrī śarīravikārābhāvakāryaṃ sampādayati | tena ātmasākṣātkāro labhyata ityasvarasaṃ jñāpayan avatārāntaramāha--aṅguṣṭheti |
aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo'ṅguṣṭhaṃ ca samāśritaḥ || 89 || ||
īśvarassarvasya jagataśśarīraṃ vahati || 90 ||
amṛtenāpādamastakaṃ santarpaṇaṃ rogābhāvakāryasampādakasāmagrīpratipāditaśarīradārḍhyajanyaṃ samādhikāryahetukatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ, ityanumānavidhayā śarīradārḍhyaprayuktayogasampādanena mokṣaprāptirbhavati | "aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣaḥ" ityatra liṅgaśarīraṃ pādamātram | tatra śrutiḥ--
"pādo'sya viśvā bhūtāni tripāda-
syāmṛtaṃ divi | tripādūrdhva udaitpuruṣaḥ"
iti | eṣā śrutiḥ aṅguṣṭhamātraṃ pratipādya tiṣṭhati | sa eveśvaro bhavati | so'pīśvaraḥ tripādena divi sthitāmṛtamāhṛtya svaśarīraṃ santarpya jagaccharīramapi vahati | virāḍrūpeśvaragocarajñānasādhaka antarindriyātmagocarajñānasāmagrīsampādanāpekṣayā jagaccharīrātmaviṣayakajñānagocarajñānagrāhakasāmagryā ātmasākṣātkāro bhavedityarthaḥ ||
nanu yogināmapi mūlādhāramādibhūtaṃ sakaladhātupoṣakaṃ śvāsocchvāsābhyāṃ śirassthitamamṛtamāhṛtya śarīrasantarpaṇaṃ vidhatte tasya sarvadhāraṇatvāt | yogaṃ vināpi tatkarma kartuṃ śakyata evetya, svarasāntaramāha-- mūleti |
mūlādhāramādibhūtaṃ sakaladhātupoṣakam || 91 ||
mūlādhārasya kuṇḍalinīrūpasya anilānalacakrātmakasya tanniṣṭhaśvāsocchvāsābhyāṃ dhātūnāmṛtādanam | yogābhyāsajanyapavanena mūlādhāramādibhūtaṃ yāvatkālaṃ dhātudārḍhyakaraṇaṃ jaṭharānilapradaṃ cirāyuḥprāpakaṃ yāvadindriyāṇāṃ arthaviṣayakajñānānubhavasārthakaraṇaṃ ca labhyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu śarīrapādapaḥ amṛtajalapoṣyopakārakaḥ | amṛtātiviriktavyānapavanasya śarīravyāpakatāvacchedakatvāt | nanu mūlādhārapadmasya sakaladhātupoṣakasakalaśarīravyāpyapavanasaṃyoge sati tatpoṣakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate, tasya kuṇḍalyādhāramātropayogikatvāt, sarvaśarīravyāpakatvābhāvena tatpoṣakatvamapi na rocata ityasvarasādāha-- sireti |
sirāpathagatapavanaśśiraḥkamalasthāmṛtamāharānsiñcati || 92 ||
sirāmārgeṣu sarvadā sañcārabhūḥ pavano vyānapavanasya mūlādhārasthitatvena tatra sthitāmṛtamāharan śarīraśākhibījotpādakapādapadmamūle pūrvarasāsṛgdhātuṣu caran pavanaḥ prabhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu sakalāmṛtātmakānalānilacakrātmakakuṇḍalyudbhavaprakāśamānaprathamaprayāṇādhigataśvāsāniloṣmaṇo jātāmṛtamāhṛtya ucchvāsānilena tatsarvaṃ saṅgrāhya dhātusantarpaṇaṃ karotīti yattadeva śarīradārḍhyopakārakaṃ bhavatīti vyānavāyunā pādapadmapūrakena kiṃ phalaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- śarīreti |
śarīrāṅkuraśākhānāmādibhūtaṃ tathā || 93 ||
pañcaśākhaṃ śarīramiti śarīrameva aṅkuraṃ pañcaśākhānvitaṃ mūlādhiṣṭhānapadmādhārabhūtam | tasya śākhā eva avayavā ityarthaḥ |
nanu mūlādhārapadmameva śarīrāṅkurasya ālavālamityuktam | tat tadaṅgasthitavarṇānāṃ tattadadhiṣṭhānadaivatānāṃ amṛtapoṣakatvabhāvanacalanātmakakarmopakārakaṃ nopapadyata ityata āha-- tattaditi |
tattadadhiṣṭhānavarṇadevatānāṃ sirāmārgagatāmṛtaṃ poṣakam || 94 ||
tattaddevatātmakaṃ akārādikṣakārāntaṃ tattadaṅgādhiṣṭhānādhiṣṭhitam | tasya tattatsirāmārgagatāmṛtaṃ poṣakaṃ bhavati | tasya āpādamastakaṃ sañcāritavyānapavano'pi poṣako bhavati | avarṇotpādakapādapadmaṃ śākhāvelārūpam | tāvatparyantaṃ sahasrārapadmagatāmṛtaṃ rakṣakaṃ bhavatītyāha-- taditi |
tatpādapadmādhāre saritpravahati || 95 ||
pādapadmaparyantaṃ sirāsandhānamanusṛtya amṛtapravāho bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu kuṇḍalinīgataśvāsocchvāsamahimnā āgatāmṛtasya ādhārapadmālavālaparyantaṃ pravāhakaraṇasyocitatvāt kathaṃ hyādhārapadmaparyantaṃ pravāhakaraṇaṃ nopapadyata ityata āha-- māṃseti |
māṃsānusṛtasirāmārgagatapavanena kalāḥ pūrayan amṛtaṃ secayet || 96 ||
tanmārgamanusṛtya iḍāpavanena pādapadmaparyantaṃ secayedityarthaḥ | tena tadadhiṣṭhānasthitavarṇadevatāḥ santoṣya pālayedityarthaḥ | nanu rasāsṛgdhātupavanena amṛtapoṣakatvaṃ taddhātūnāmeveti vaktavyam | taditararaktadhātoramṛtapoṣakatvābhāvena jīvanaṃ nopapadyata ityasvarasādāha-- māṃseti |
māṃsānusṛtasirābhāvāssanti || 97 ||
iḍāmārgagataśvāsapavanaḥ sahasrārakamalasthāmṛtamāharan māṃsadhātuṃ santarpya śarīrapoṣako bhavatītyarthaḥ | rasāsṛgdhātupavanasya śarīrāṅgavibhāgakāryakārakaniyamasthāpakatvāt māṃsadhātugatasirāṇāṃ aprayojakatvena raktadhātupoṣakatvaṃ pavanasya kathaṃ saṅgacchate | pavanasirāsṛgdhātumātrapradānaṃ caran tatpoṣakatvamātrasyaivocitatvāt na raktadhātupoṣako bhavatītyasvarasādāha-- dvīti |
dvisahasrasirāśākhāvat pādapadmamūlakam || 98 ||
dvisahasrasirāśākhinaḥ pādapadmālavālaṃ sirāṅkurāṇāmādhārakaṃ pādapadmaprarohāṅkurāṇāmeva amṛtamāharat dhātupoṣakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | dvisahasrasirodbhavāṅkurāṇāṃ saptadhātupoṣakatvena śarīrādhārakatvaṃ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- sapteti |
saptadhātumayaṃ śarīram || 99 ||
ṣaḍrasā ityatra svādvamlalavaṇatiktoṣaṇakaṣāyakāḥ | tataḥ ṣaṭsaṅkhyādravyamāśritya tiṣṭhato guṇavatpoṣakatvasya suprasiddhatvāt | dhāturnāma ‘rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedosthimajjāśuklāni dhātavaḥ' iti | tato dhātumayaṃ śarīramityuktam ||
idānīṃ pratipāditapraśno yogaśāstraṃ bhavati | tanmadhye saptadhātumayaṃ śarīramiti pratipādanaṃ yogābhyāsasyāpi dhātupoṣakatvameva phalamiti dyotanārthaṃ tathā pratipāditam-– āyurbhavatītyarthaḥ | dhātavaḥ rasādipoṣitāḥ śuddharasadravyopajīvakatvāt | tasmāddhātūnāṃ rasādipoṣitatvaṃ suprasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ ||
ityāyurvedasya tṛtīyapraśnasya bhāṣyaṃ yogānandanāthakṛtaṃ
suprasiddhaṃ mahājanasammataṃ pratisūtravyākhyā-
napūrvakaṃ āyurvedabhāṣyaṃ lokopakārakaṃ
saṃpūrṇam.
<4>
āyurvede caturthapraśnaḥ
nanu rasādīnāṃ dhātupoṣakatvaṃ vaktumaśakyam kuṇḍalinīsthitaśvāsochvāsapavanasyaiva amṛtapradatvena pratipāditatvāt | rasādīnāṃ dhātupoṣakatvaṃ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- gateti |
gatāgatāmṛtavaśādāgatasamādhikriyayā yogasānnidhyam || 1 ||
kuṇḍalinyāḥ anilānalacakrātmakatvādeva śvāsapavanasya anilacakrātmakasya prathamaprayāṇagatasya ūṣmarūpatvādeva śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtamāhartuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ | tasmādamṛtavaśāddhātupoṣakatvaṃ samādhihetukamiti kṛtvā tatkriyayā yogānāṃ sānnidhyaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | tasmādyogasya dhātupoṣakatvaṃ paramparayā pratipāditamiti bhāvaḥ |
dhātūnāṃ poṣaṇaṃprati rasādi heturbhavatīti rasādīnāṃ hetutvapratipādanaṃ rasavaddravyādanena bhāvyam, tadannādyadanasya anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ dhātupoṣaṇaṃprati hetutvapratipādanāt | samādhayo dhātupoṣakā iti kathaṃ pratipadyata ityasvarasādāha-- tīvreti |
tīvrasaṃyogākhyāmāsannaḥ samādhiḥ || 2 ||
manasa ātmanididhyāsanasya tīvrasaṃyogatvaṃ nāma itarārthajñānajñāpakasāmagryasamarthitatve sati ātmasamavetatvam | teṣāmātmavi ṣayakaviṣayetarajñānaṃprati hetureva samādhirityarthaḥ | nanu paramātmano vibhutvena manasa ātmanā sākaṃ dhṛḍhatarasaṃskārasya svata eva siddhatvādityasvarasādāha-- saṃvega iti |
saṃvegaḥ kriyāheturdṛḍhatarasaṃskāraḥ || 3 ||
saṃvego nāma kāraṇam | tasya lakṣaṇaṃ kriyāviśeṣamātravailakṣaṇyaṃ tadavacchedakam | sa eva kriyāheturbhavati dṛḍhatarasaṃskāro'pi bhavatītyarthaḥ | taraśabdena sutarāṃ heturiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu etatsamādhānamanupapannaṃ, tathā ca śrutiḥ--
"annādbhūtāni jāyante jātānyannena vardhante | adyate'tti ca bhūtāni tasmādannaṃ taducyata iti | annamamṛtaṃ tamu jīvātumāhuḥ" iti śruteḥ vidyamānatvāt | etatpramāṇapratipāditasamādheradṛṣṭatvādityasvarasādāha-- gateti |
gatāgatopāyabhedavatāṃ samādhiphalaṃ cāsannam ||
atra gatāgataviṣayakajñānamātraṃ na vivakṣitam | annādanābhāve sati dhātupoṣakaśarīrapuṣṭiryasyāsti tasyedaṃ vivakṣitam | te tu paramayogino bhavanti | teṣāmavacchedakaṃ gatāgatopāyabhedajñānavattvam | gatāgatarūpaśvāsochvāsābhyāṃ śarīradārḍhyopāyaṃ, bhidyata iti bhedaḥ annādanājjātajanakayogajanyabheda ityarthaḥ | samādheḥ phalaṃ śarīradārḍhyakaraṇam | śarīreṇa sarvakarmādhikārī bhavati | śarīraṃ satkarmādimokṣahetukaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
nanu śvāsochvāsānilayorgatāgatatvasya śarīriṇāṃ svata eva siddhatvāt svatassiddhakarmaṇaḥ viśeṣavidhiṃ vinā kathaṃ śarīradārḍhyakārakatvaṃ kathaṃ vā mokṣopayogikatvaṃ bhavatītyāśaṅkyāha--mṛdviti |
mṛdumadhyātimātratvāttato viśeṣaḥ || 5 ||
śvāsānilaḥ iḍāmārgaṃ gatvā mandaṃmandaṃ sañcaran śarīraṃ nīrogaṃ karoti | sa eva mokṣahetuko bhavati | tadanantaraṃ madhyamārgaṃ gatvā nābhipradeśaparyantaṃ mandaṃmandaṃ sañcaran śarīradārḍhyakārako mokṣahetukaśca bhavati | atimātragamanena suṣumnāmārgaṃ gatvā atyantamudaramāpūryate cet tasya śarīrapiṅgalāmārgaṃ gatvā śarīrasya niratiśayamokṣahetuko bhavati | tebhyo viśeṣādabhyāsavaśāt prayatnābhāve'pi sarvadā yaḥ kuṃbhakaṃ karoti saḥ arogī bhūtvā rājayogamārgasampādanena sarvakarmaparityāgavān bhavati | tenaiva mokṣaphalamaśnute | tatra śrutiḥ--
"saṃnyāsayogādyatayaḥ śuddhasatvāḥ te brahmaloke tu parāntakāle parāmṛtātparimucyanti sarve" iti śrutipramāṇena asaṃśayajñānagocaravānbhūtvā śarīradārḍhyakarmakaraṇadvāreṇa sarvaviṣayabhogānupabhujya tadbhogaviṣayakapadārthānnaśvarāniti vijñāya sarvadā ātmaviṣayakajñānaṃ ihaloke'pi sarvadā paramānandānubhavahetukam | sa eva tato viśeṣa ityarthaḥ | rājayogo'pi bhavati | tasya upāyebhyo mṛdvādibhedabhinnasya upāyavatāṃ viśeṣo bhavatītyāha-- mṛdviti |
mṛdumadhyātimātrā ityupāyabhedāḥ || 6 ||
pūrvoktarītyā mṛdumadhyātimātragamanaṃ roganivartakadravyānivṛttaroganivṛttyartham | ayamevopāyaḥ śarīradārḍhyakarmakaraṇadvārā mokṣaphalaprāpako bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu yattu trayāṇāṃ mārgāṇāṃ śarīradārḍhyakarmakaraṇaparyantamapi upāyo bhavatītyuktaṃ tato'dhikamārgaparyantagamanamapi upāyo bhavatītyāha-- mṛdviti |
mṛdusaṃyāgo madhyamasaṃyogastīvrasaṃyogaśca || 7 ||
iḍāmārgagamanamandānilenāpi kuṇḍalyudbodhakaraṇaṃ upāyo bhavati | evaṃ suṣumnāmārgagamanapavanena kuṇḍalīsaṃsargajātakuṇḍalyudbodhakarmaṇā ca mākṣopāyo bhavati | taccharīradārḍhyasampādanasāmagrī upāyo bhavatītyarthaḥ | tattriguṇahetukānilānāṃ śarīradārḍhyakaraṇamātrasyāprayojakatvāt tadguṇopāyavyatiriktakarmaṇā yogāgnisandhukṣaṇena bahujanmārjitapāpasaṅghātaṃ bhasmīkṛtya mokṣaheturbhavatītyāha-- tridheti |
tridhā bhedanena vāyornavayogino bhavanti || 8 ||
pavanasya trividhavikāratvaphalaṃ kriyate | mṛdupavanasya saṃyogaḥ mṛdupavanakuṇḍalīsaṃyogaḥ | madhyamavegapavanasya kuṇḍalīsaṃyogaḥ | tīvragatimārgānilasya kuṇḍalīsaṃyogaḥ tīvrasaṃyogaḥ | madhyamānilasya saṃyogaḥ nābherūrdhvapradeśasthaṃ hṛtkamalaparyantaṃ āgatya tatrasthasya niruddhapavanasya antaḥpadamārgamavalambya gacchataḥ ātmani samavasthānam | sa eva samādhiḥ | ātmajñānasambhavāllabhyate | sa eva madhyamasaṃyogaḥ | tīvrasaṃyogaḥ tadupari śiraḥkamalaparyantam | tadupari suṣumnāmārgādāgatapavanagatihetukaṃ sirāmārgapūraṇaṃ sakalaśarīrāṇāmāyuṣkārakam , tatkarmaṇaḥ sakalaśarīropakārakatvāt | śarīrārogyakaraṇārthaṃ navaprakāro bhūtvā śarīraṃ saṃrakṣatītyarthaḥ |
nanu bahiḥpavanaṃ iḍāpiṅgalāsuṣumnāmārgamāvṛtya udaramāpūrya śarīrārogyamātropakārakaḥ samādhiḥ tasya calanasya pradhānāṅgopakārakatvābhāvāt | calanātmakapavanasya sirāpūraṇamātraprayojakatvena "ete jaṅgamāḥ" iti kathaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata ityasvarasādāha-- pañceti |
pañcadaśakamalaṃ jaṅghādeśagataṃ sahasrasirādhārakaṃ pañcāśanmarmagatam || 9 ||
gatāgatasaṃskāropakārakapavanayogassamādhiḥ | kuṇḍalyādhārabhūtapañcadaśakamalaṃ jaṅghāpradeśasthaṃ sahasrasirāvṛtaṃ sat marmasthānānusandhānakārakaṃ jaṅghābhyāṃ calanopakārakamiti vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi | tasmādete jaṅgamā iti pradarśitāḥ | jaṅghāpadmapradeśasthapañcadaśadalapadmavaccharīrāṅgaṃ calanātmakakarmopakārakaṃ, jaṅgamaśarīrāṅgakatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā sthāṇuḥ | tatpañcadaśapadmakaṃ pādasahasrāvalambakasahasrasirāvṛtaṃ dviśatapañcāśatsirāvṛtaikāṅgatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā sthāṇuśarīrāṅgam | pañcāśanmarmagataṃ pañcāśadvarṇātmakaṃ tattadvarṇabodhakābhinayacalanātmakāṅgādhārabhūtatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ ||
gatāgatasaṃskāropakārakapavanayogassamādhiḥ ||
sāmadhiriti-- samādhiścalanakarmaviśiṣṭakarmā, pavanāśritajñānecchāprayatnajanyahetujñāpakadravyāśritatvāt, yathā ghaṭa iti |
tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārajātaśabdānāha-- hasteti | hastavinyāsānusṛtājñābodhakaḥ tacchabdajñāpakābhinayakarmaviśiṣṭaviṣayakajñānāśritatvāt | evamanumānapramāṇena āyurvedasūtrārthavyākhyānaṃ kṛtam ||
nanu caitanyādhiṣṭhitaṃ śarīraṃ ceṣṭāśrayaṃ ceṣṭāśrayajanyaphalānubhavānandātmakatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ caitanyarahitaśarīravat | jīvaccharīraṃ sātmakaṃ, prāṇādimattvāt | yajjīvaccharīraṃ na bhavati tatprāṇādimanna bhavati, śavaśarīravat | īśvarecchādhīnā ceṣṭā īśvarapreritakāryādhīnā bhavatītyata āha-- īśvareti |
īśvarapreritaceṣṭāśrayaṃ hitāhitakāryoddeśyaviṣayapravartakaceṣṭāśrayaṃ śarīram || 11 ||
īśvarapreritaceṣṭāśrayaṃ kīdṛśaṃ caitanyaṃ ? cakṣurvadrūpagrāhakatvābhāvāt, śrotrendriyavacchabdagrāhakatvābhāvāt | tasmāccaitanyasvarūpaṃ gṛhītumaśakyamityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha- īśvareti |
janyaśabdajñānaṃ cetanasadbhāve pramāṇam | tathā hi-- tattadaṅgādhiṣṭhitavarṇāḥ tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārādinā vargātmakāśśabdāḥ īśvarapreritaśabdāḥ śarīrādyajanyatve sati janyavyāpārahetukatvāt | aṅkurādivat |
aṅkurādikaṃ sakartṛkaṃ kāryatvāt ghaṭavat | iti īśvarasadbhāve'numānaṃ pramāṇamiti tena hetunopalabhyate | bījabhūmijīvanasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ kāryamutpadyata iti na tenāpīśvarasiddhiḥ | anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ aṅkurādyutpādakadṛṣṭasāmagrīsattvāt ghaṭavat | aṅkurādivaddṛṣṭāntasadbhāve pramāṇam | śarīrāvacchedakaṃ ceṣṭāśrayam | ceṣṭāśrayayogyāṅgāni bahūni santi | sarvadā calanaprasaṅgasyaivopasthitatvāt | tathā sati atiprasaṅga eva syādityasvarasādāha-- īśvareti |
ceṣṭāsvarūpaṃ hitāhitaprāptiparihārārthaspandanam | na tu spandanamātram | tatsarvaṃ īśvarapreritaceṣṭāśrayam, tathā sati nātiprasaṅgaḥ | yasya yāvaktarmānuguṇabhāgyaṃ yadadhiṣṭhānādhīnaṃ tatsarvaṃ anubhavayogyaṃ kartuṃ pratibhūrbhavatītyarthaḥ | prayojakatvāt ceṣṭārūpeṇa īśvarasadbhāvasya preritatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu śarīrāvacchedakaṃ ceṣṭāśrayamityuktam | tathā sati sarvadā ceṣṭākaraṇaprasaṅgāt, īśvarasya sarvaśarīrāṅgavyāpakatvena siddhatvāt tasya ceṣṭākaraṇameva prayojakaṃ bhavatītyasvarasādāha-- hitāhiteti |
hitaprāptyarthaṃ ahitaparihārārthameva spandanakaraṇaṃ īśvarapreritam | ātmā manasā saṃyujyate, mana indriyeṇa, indriyamarthena, indriyāṇāṃ prāpyakāritvaniyamatvāt | īśvaraḥ yadarthakarmakaraṇamicchati tāvadarthānubhavajñānena anandānubhavavān bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu pariśuddharasādanaṃ yasyāsti tasyendriyādayaḥ suprasannā bhavanti | rasavaddravyādanaṃ ceṣṭā'śrayahetukaṃ śarīrāṅgasya rasādanapoṣitatvāt | tasmācchuddharasavaddravyādanasya calanātmakaśarīrasya ca hetuhetumadbhāvayoreva vaktuṃ śakyatvāt ityasvarasādāha-- śudveti |
śuddharasāhārajanyadhātupreritasirāgatapavanavaśāccalanātmakam || 12 ||
tatra vacanaṃ--
āhāraṃ pacati śikhī doṣānāhāravarjitaḥ pacati |
doṣakṣaye ca dhātūnpacati ca dhātukṣaye'pi ca prāṇān ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa śarīradārḍhyakaraṇaṃ aṅgapracalanaṃ ca rasādanādeva bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
nanu śuddharasādanaṃ dhātupoṣaṇamātraṃ karoti | tena rogābhāvāt pañcendriyāṇyapi svaviṣayaviṣayakaviṣayajñānasya śuddharasavaddravyādanena lābhāt tadvati tatprakārakatvamapi gṛhṇanti | tena calanātmakakarmopakārakatvaṃ na syādityasvarasādāha-- jaṅgheti |
jaṅghāpadmapoṣakāmṛtapravāhapariṇāmayogavaśāccalati || 13 ||
jaṅghāpraveśasthitapañcadaśadalapadmaṃ yena vaśādāgatāmṛtapravāhāt tatpadmaṃ poṣayat taccalanātmakakarmopakārakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu īśvarasadbhāve kiṃ mānaṃ ? svahitaphalaprāpakaviṣaye calanātmakakarmapravṛttiḥ | ahitaphalanivṛttiviṣaye calanātmakakarmapravṛttiśca | tadubhayamapi śuddharasāhārajanyaṃ dhātudārḍhyakaraṇaprayojakam | sakalakarmaviṣayakacalanātmakakriyā pavanena sirāgatāmṛtapravāhapreraṇāvaśāt calati | pūrvoktarītyā īśvareṇa vināpi śarīrāṅgacalanakriyāṃ kartuṃ karmakaraṇenaiva siddhatvāt--
"kamaṇaiva hi saṃsiddhimāsthitā janakādayaḥ" iti | tasmādīśvaro na siddhyatītyasvarasādāha-- lavaṇeti |
lavaṇarasajanyapavanayogarasapravartakakleśakarmavipākāśayāparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa īśvaraḥ || 14 ||
lavaṇarasajanyaḥ tāmasaguṇaviśiṣṭa īśvara ekaḥ śarīre sthitaḥ | sa īśvaraḥ nityo'pi tāmasaguṇasya anityatvāt tadguṇaviśiṣṭatvāt tajjanya ityuktaḥ | pavanajanakasvādurasavaddravyādanajanyasāttvikaguṇaviśiṣṭa īśvaraḥ tadguṇaviśiṣṭe śarīre sthitaḥ ajaḥ | kleśaguṇavipākāśaya ajaḥ | tasmādaparāmṛṣṭaḥ āmayarahitaḥ īśvaraḥ puruṣaviśeṣaḥ | tāvubhāvapi śarīrasthitau | tayorekaḥ ajarassarvasvatantropi sarvaśarīraṃ vyāpya sarvakarmopakārakaḥ | svāna ndānubhavahetukapadārthajñānena icchā jāyate | taccalanātmakaṃ karma pravṛttiḥ | tatsvatvajñāpitāttaditara īśvaraḥ māyāvyatiriktaḥ niṣkalaṅkaḥ nirguṇaḥ niravayavaḥ nityānandātmakaḥ yogināmapyagocaraḥ | tāvubhau patatriṇau śarīravṛkṣaṃ parityajya sthitāvityarthaḥ | kleśakarmavipākāśayo jīvaḥ | kliśyanta iti kleśāḥ avidyādayaḥ | vakṣyamāṇāni avihitāni niṣiddhadravyāṇi naśvararūpāṇi karmāṇi | vakṣyamāṇāni-- vipacyanta iti vipākāḥ karmaphalāni jātyāyussukhaduḥkhabhogātmakāni phalāni | tāni tāmasaguṇahetukalavaṇakaṭurasadravyādanahetukajātāni | teṣāmāśayo nidānasthānātmakaḥ māyāśabalito jīvaḥ | sāśayasthitajaṅghāpadmasthitasahasrasirābhiḥ ātmecchāyāḥ prayatnajñānāt gatāgataprerakapavanavaśāt
jaṅghābhyāṃ padbhyāṃ dharmo'smi | viśi rājā pratiṣṭhitaḥ | pratikṣatre pratitiṣṭhāmi rāṣṭre | pratyaśveṣu pratitiṣṭhāmi goṣu | pratyaṅgeṣu pratitiṣṭhāmyātman | pratiprāṇeṣu pratitiṣṭhāmi pṛṣṭhe | pratidyāvāpṛthivyoḥ pratitiṣṭhāmi yajñe"
iti śrutipratipāditaśarīreṣu viśan karma karomītyarthaḥ ||
nanu śarīrāṅgagataceṣṭādikaṃ ātmādhīnaṃ, tena paramātmā siddhyati | paramātmanaḥ icchāviṣayakajñānakāryaṃ ātmecchāviṣayakajñānapūrvakaṃ, śuddhacaitanyapreritakarmakāryahetubhūtatvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | ityanumānavidhayā paramātmasiddhiḥ |
srotomārgasthitasirāpreritasarvavarṇajñāpakādhāradeśasthitakaraprabhāvāṣṭādaśadalapadmāvalambakatālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārādikaṃ sakartṛkaṃ kāryatvāt ghaṭavat ityanumānena ātmasiddhiḥ | tadarthaṃ śarīrāṅgaceṣṭāṃ vyācaṣṭe-- srota iti |
srotomārgasirātriśatadharāṣṭādaśadalapadmaṃ pratītigamanāgamanaceṣṭāśrayaṃ bhavati || 15 ||
tāssirā marmaśaḥ srotomārgasañcaritāstriśatasaṅkhyākāḥ | etādṛśaceṣṭāśrayasya śarīrasya ātmādhiṣṭhitatvāt tāssirāḥ srotomārgapravāhasya velārūpā bhavanti | kaṇṭhapradeśasthitāṣṭadalapadmaṃ sarvavarṇoccāraṇahetubhūtapavanapravartakaṃ tatprāṇadevatādhāraṃ tasminneva kāryahetubhūtajñāne sati calanātmakakarmakaraṇāt ātmā jñātuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ |
nanu calanātmakaṃ karma īśvarapreraṇājanyam | etatkarma sakartṛkaṃ kāryatvāt ityanumānena paramātmā antassthitassan karma kurute | atra īśvaraprayatnajanyabhāvaḥ calanātmakakarmabhāvaḥ | tathā sati anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvasya tayoreva dṛṣṭatvāt | ityanumānena īśvarasiddhirityarthaḥ | guṇatrayajanakasvādvamlalavaṇarasādanena pavanaprakopo nivartyate | tadannādanena śarīramavikāraṃ sat calanātmakaṃ karma kurute | tattadvikāre sati tattatkarma kurute | karmapravartakaṃ ca guṇatrayam | pratipādakasvādvamlarasavirasādanena suṣṭha karma kartuṃ na śakyate | tatra iḍāmārgeṇa bahiḥpavanastatra bheṣajaṃ bhavati, sādhyarogāṇāmapi tannivartakatvāt |
svādvamlalavaṇarasavirasādanena udarāmayo jāyate | tasyāpi idameva bheṣajaṃ bhavati | pavanadoṣe aprakope sati śarīrāṅgakarmakaraṇaṃ sūcyate | tatprakope sati śarīrajanyakāryābhāvo dṛśyate | śarīrādivikārahetubhūtānnādanaṃ tattatkarmahetukam | tasmādvyādhiprāgabhāvaparipālanameva phalībhūtaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ |
īśvarastu karacaraṇādyavayavī bhūtvā prapañcotpādakaṃ karmakurute | tathāca śrutiḥ--
"brāhmaṇo'sya mukhamāsīt bāhū rājanyaḥ kṛtaḥ | ūrū tadasya yadvaiśyaḥ padbhayāṃ śūdro'jāyata | candramā manaso jātaḥ | cakṣossūryo'jāyata | mukhādindraścāgniśca" iti śarīrāntarātmavyatirikteśvarasadbhāve pramāṇam |
antassthitassan karma kurute | tasmātpratyahaṃ pathyādanena bhāvyam | anyathā rogāssambhavanti | tena śarīriṇaḥ karaṇasāmarthyābhāvāt asādhya udarāmayaḥ | sirāmārgeṣu bahiḥpavanapūraṇaṃ udararoganivartakaṃ tadupayogyanivartakānivṛtte sati pavanapūraṇayogasya karaṇasya nivartakatvāt | ātmamanassaṃyogo yogaḥ yogārjakatvāt | sa eva jaṭharānalaprado bhavati | udarāmayaviṣaye agniprajvalanadravyaṃ nivartakam, yogāgnijanakasāmagyrāstatra bheṣajatvāt | ajīrṇājjātodarāmayanidānaṃ vyācaṣṭe-- svādviti |
svādvamlalavaṇarasajanyānilasirayā pūrayan jaghanapadmaviṣayaṃ pracalati|| 16 ||
svādurasavirasadravyādanādajīrṇādāmāmbuvṛddhirudaraṃ jāyate || 17 ||
svādurasavirasadravyādanaṃ udarāmayahetukam, tadanalenājīrṇe jāte sati tena ambubhūtādhikyapravṛtterjātatvāt tena mandānalo bhavati | adhikānaladoṣo bhavati | tatra nidānavacanam--
"rogāssarve'pi mande'gnau sutarāmudarāṇi tu |
ajīrṇānmalinaiścānnairjāyante malasañcayāt ||
ūrdhvādho dhātavo ruddhvā vāhinīrambuvāhinīḥ |
prāṇāgnyapānānsandūṣya kuryustvaṅmāṃsasandhigāḥ ||
āṣmāpya kukṣimudaramaṣṭadhā tacca bhidyate|
rasavirasādanajātājīrṇena jātarogaḥ tannivartakābhāvasāmagryā āmādivṛddhiṃ janayati | tena mandānilo'pi bhavati | śarīrasya pañcabhūtātmakatvena svādurasādanātpavanaprakopo nivartate | tadvirasadravyādanātpavanaprakopo dṛśyate | tasmādudbhūtābhivṛddhitayā sarandhrakābhyantaradharasirāṇāmapi paripīḍanāt pavanasañcārābhāvatvena āpassravanti ā kukṣiparipūraṇāt | rasāsṛṅmāṃsasadhātuṣu sirā dṛśyate | tasmātkukṣijalaṃ tattu dhātuvat na jīryate | sa udarāmayo bhavati | sa evāṣṭadhā bhidyate ityarthaḥ |
nanu udbhūtarasavirasadravyaṃ pavanaprakopahetukaṃ udbhūtagrahaṇasvādurasavirasadravyatvāt | kathaṃ tarhi phalarasavirasadravyajanyarogasya svādurasavaddravyaṃ na bheṣajānivṛttanivartakaṃ pavanaprakopāgravartakadravyatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ| āmanivṛttidvārā udarāmayo'pi na jāyate | tatprāgabhāvaparipālanakāryasya tadupayogikatvāt ityāha--svādviti |
svādurasavadvirecanadravyaṃ tatra bheṣajam || 18 ||
udarāmayanivartakadravyāṇi kvāthakalkāvalehabheṣajadravyāṇītyarthaḥ | tatra vātakṛtodarāmaye te padārthāḥ | sarveṣāmapi sambhāvitatvāt iti tannivartakasya recanopayogidravyatvena tattaddvyayogyakālaṃ vijñāya cikitsā kāryeti vyapadiśati-- śanairiti |
śanaiśśanaireva recayet || 19 ||
sarvajantūnāmapyudaraṃ jalādhārakamiti, tejaśca balaṃ ca āyuścetyetānyanalādhārakāṇīti matvā śanaiśśanaireva recayet | śarīrāntassthitasirājātābkāryamudarāmayaṃ, tannivṛttikārye antassthitajalavirecanakāryaṃ kurvīta | tatra nidānavacanam--
"sarveṣu tandrā sadanaṃ maṭhasaṅgo'lpavahnitā |
dāhaḥ śśvayathurādhmānamante salilasambhavaḥ ||
sarve tvatoyamaruṇamaśophaṃ nātibhārikam |
gavākṣitaṃ sirājālaiḥ sadā guḍaguḍāyate |
nābhimantraṃ ca viṣṭhabhya vegaṃ kṛtvā praṇaśyati ||"
etadvacanānusāreṇa sirāvikārajanyajalanivartakaṃ yattadvirecanakāryaṃ, tenodarāmayo nivartate sirāvikārajanyodarāmayahetukajaladravyatvāt, tadvirecanayogyārthaḥ nivartaka ityarthaḥ |
nanu gajāśvādīnāmapi udarāmayaḥ prācuryeṇa dyotate | svādurasavaddravyaṃ tatra bheṣajamiti te padārthāstatra nopayuktāḥ teṣāmapi yogyadravyāṇāṃ vivakṣitatvāt | tadudarāmayānāṃ ādhmānanidānasya sarvajanasādhāraṇatvāt | yāvadvyādhyupayuktabhogayogyadravyāṇi pavanaprakopanivartakāni | tattajjātivihitapadārthānāṃ svādurasavaddravyatvaṃ yāvatpadārtheṣu bhāsate | tatrāyaṃ niyamaḥ-- svādurasavaddravyatvasya sarveṣāmapi sambhāvitatvāt ādānanidānādeḥ sarvajantūnāṃ samānadravyatvāt svadurasavaddravyaṃ tatra bheṣajamiti āyurvedapratipāditārthaḥ || sarvajantūnāṃ dhātupoṣakatvaṃ ca sa mānamiti ghaṇṭāghoṣeṇa narāṇāmudarāmayānāṃ svādurasadravyāṇi cāvirasadravyāṇi ca ārogyakārakāṇīti pratipāditānītyarthaḥ ||
mūlādhāro balaṃ puṃsāṃ śuklaṃ yattaddhi jīvitam |
iti vacanānusāreṇāpi niyāmako jñāyate | sarvajantūnāṃ śanaiśśanaireva recayaditi sutrātsamyakpratipāditamityarthaḥ |
nanu ekaikarasadravyeṇa virecanakāryakaraṇaṃ kartavyaṃ vā, uta alpamalpaṃ vā dātavyaṃ vā sarvamelanaṃ vā āmānivṛttikaraṇaṃ kiṃcitkiṃcitkaraṇaṃ vā ? nādyaḥ-- ekaikadravyeṇa virecanaṃ kāryamiti cet uktavirecanapadārthānuddiśya yāvatkaraṇavidhiraprayojakaṃ syāt | na dvitīyaḥ--alpālpakaraṇena kevalamāmanivṛtterevābhāvāt | śuddhāmanivṛttikaraṇaṃ udarāmayanivṛttikaraṇaṃ, udarāmayanivṛttikarmanivartakatvasya caritārthatvāt | na tṛtīyaḥ—sarvapadārthānāṃ samīkṛtya yogakaraṇamiti cet ativirecanaṃ syāt--dhātavaḥ srāvayanti | tatsrāvaṇena atiriktatayā doṣaprakopassyāt | tasmādetāvanmātraṃ vyarthaṃ syāt ityasvarasādāha-- payaneti |
pavanādyaprakopādagnerbalaṃ poṣayankriyākramaḥ || 20
pavanādyaprakopāditi | ādiśabdena vātapittakaphadoṣāḥ pratipāditāḥ | teṣāmaprakopakaraṇaṃ phalībhūtārthassyāt | tasya agnibaladūpakatvāt yena kena prakāreṇa jaṭharāgnipravardhakasāmagrīkāryasya phalībhūtārthatvāt sarvodarāmayānāṃ jaṭharāgnipravṛddhikaraṇaṃ kriyākramaḥ ||
pavanādīnāṃ prakoparūpābhāvakāryasya prāyogikatvena śaddhadoṣādivijñānaṃ śuddharasādanajanyamiti tayorvyāptiṃ gṛhītvā bhūyo darśanena kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ gṛhītuṃ śakyate | tadvirasadravyādanaṃ udarāmayahetukamiti kāryakāraṇabhāvo grāhyaḥ | tasmātpavanādyaprakopakāryasampādanārthaṃ tatraiva kriyākramajñānaṃ vivakṣitam | tathā hi--udarāmayahetukāmanivṛttikaraṇadravyaṃ na kevalaṃ prayojakaṃ bhavati | vātāprakopakāmayayogaḥ kāryaḥ | sa eva kriyākramaḥ | tasmātseyamavipattiriti doṣatrayeṇa avipatkāryaṃ jñāpyate | etadvirecanakaraṇaṃ jaṭharāgnivardhakaṃ mandānalahetukadravyajātāmayanivartakatve sati analapravardhakadravyatvāt | doṣanivartakavyāpatprakṛte udarāmayacikitsāyāṃ tadeva pratipāditam--
vyāghrāśvagandhā ca punarnavā syāt piṇyākaśigrūṇi rasonapitsu |
savajravallīkarikarṇakuṇḍalaṃ rujāpahaṃ sūraṇatiktapatram ||
asminyoge kevalavirecanadravyāṇi svādurasavadvirecanadravyāṇi pratipāditāni | pavanādyaprakopakārakavirecanaguṇābhāvakāryadravyāṇi pratipāditāni | na kevalavirecanakaraṇasya pavanaprakopakārakatvamiti pūrvasūtre pratipāditam | taccintyam | tena pittaprakopo bhavati | tannidānabhūtaliṅgāni bhramamūrcchāvidāhāratyarucyādīni jñātavyāni | pittodrekajātodarāmayasya lakṣaṇānītyarthaḥ | kaṣāyatiktasvādurasadravyādanāt prakopahetukapavanaḥ sirāmārgasthitapavanavigatyā rasāsṛgdhātusāraṃ srāvayati | tatsāra eva pittamiti vijñeyam | ayaṃ pittodarāmayajñāpaka iti vaktavyam | sarandhrakābhyantaradharaistu antarālamārgeṣu biruddhapittaṃ pūryate | anilavigatyā rasāsṛgdhātusāreṇa udarābhyantarapūraṇāt dhātupacanavat | jaṭharānalasya ajīrṇadravyatvāt kukṣau vipakṣādvarddhate sa eva pittodarāmaya ityāha--viraseti |
virasadravyādanājīrṇajanyasirāmārgasthitapavanavigatyā sirārasātiprasravaṇādajīrṇādāmāmbuvṛddherudaraṃ jāyate || 21 ||
virasadravyādanajanyapittodarasya hetavo vaktavyāḥ | kaṣāyarasavirasadravyādanena vā tiktarasavirasadravyādanena vā pittaprakopo bhavati | analājīrṇadravyatvāt | tannivartanaṃ laṅghanādeva bhāvyam | yatra kaṣāyatiktasvādurasavirasadravyādanamupalabhyate tatra pittodarāmayotpatti | tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
kaṣāyatiktamadhurāḥ pittamanyeṣu kupyate | iti ||
anyeṣviti kaṣāyatiktasvādurasavat pittaprakopanivartakam | tadvirasāḥ pittaprakopakārakāḥ | etattrayāṇāṃ madhye ekarasadravyādanajanyapittaprakopasya nivartakaṃ tadvirasānyarasādanam | na tadvyatiriktaśuddharasāḥ udarāmayahetukāḥ pittaprakopanivartakarasavirasā iti "pittamanyeṣu kupyate" ityetadvacanasya tātparyam |
kaṣāyatiktavyatiriktasvādurasavadvyatiriktarasavirasadravyādanaṃ udarāmayahetukaṃ jaṭharānalājīrṇadravyādanatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | ekarasavirasadravyādanaṃ nodarāmayahetukaṃ jaṭharānalajīrṇadravyādanatvāt ityanumānapramāṇena kaṣāyatiktasvādurasavirasadravyādanamelanasya udarāmayahetukatvaṃ suprasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu aṣṭavidhodarāmayānāṃ kāraṇībhūtadravyāṇi ṣaḍrasavirasadravyāṇi | loke etadvyatiriktānāmabhāvena itararogāṇāṃ kāraṇībhūtadravyābhāvāt sarveṣāmapyudarāmayaprasaṅga eva syādityasvarasādāha--tāvaditi |
tāvatsirāgatapradeśabhedāt śvayathubodhakam || 22 ||
vātapradeśāvalambakasirāvikāre sati uktarītyā vātodarāmayo jāyate | tathā pittapradeśāvalambakasirāvikāre sati pittodaraṃ jāyate | kaphapradeśāvalambakasirāvikāre sati kaphodaraṃ jāyate | tattaddoṣahetukasirāṇāṃ kāryaṃ eva dvandvadoṣa iti doṣadvayajanyāstrayaḥ --pavanapittadoṣajanyodarāmaya ekaḥ | kaphavacanadoṣajanyodarāmaya ekaḥ | pittakaphajanyodarāmaya ekaḥ | tridoṣajanyodarāmaya ekaḥ | sarvaṃ militvā'ṣṭavidharogā jātāḥ |
tāvatsirāgateti--tattadviruddharasavirasajātasirāmārgagatapavanagatitirodhānādanale mande sati tenājīrṇatvaṃ tadavacchedakam | tattatpradeśabhedadoṣatrayāśrayādudararogasya hetubodhakatvameva tattadavacchedakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | pittapradeśabhedasthitasirāvikāraḥ kathaṃ jñātuṃ śakyate pittodarāmayajñāne sati tasya nivartakena nivartayituṃ śakyatvādityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha--pavaneti |
pavanaprakopahetukarūkṣātiśītalalaghvativedanārucivivarṇavirasāsyatandrāmūrchādāhabhramatṛṣṇājvarātisārāṅgapītatvaṃ pittaprakopodarāmayahetukam || 23 ||
rūkṣaguṇatvaṃ--sirāmārgarandhreṣu pavanātigatyā tattadaṅgeṣu uṣṇopalabdhirutpadyate | tatra tasya pavanavikārasya rūkṣatvamavacchedakam | yatra pavanasya alpagatyā sirāmārgarandhreṣu pavanagatirodhanaṃ dṛśyate tatrātivedanā jāyate | tadvadeva virasāsyatvaṃ vivarṇatvaṃ ca lakṣaṇaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | sarvaśarīravyāpyapavanasya vikāragrastatvāt | sarvaśarīrāṅgavedanābhāvāt kathamekadeśavedanā ityākāṃkṣāyāṃ yāvadvirasādanajanyasaraḥ yāvatsirāpradeśeṣūpalabhyate tatrāyaṃ niyamaḥ | śaṅkāsamādhānaṃ ca kṛtaṃ-- sarvaśarīrasañcāritapavanasya vikārasya sarvaśarīravedanābhāvajñānameva sirāpradeśasañcārāt tattadaṅge nīrogatvaṃ pratīyate | cikitsākāryaṃ tattadaṅgeṣveva tatpratīkāradravyādanena tadviruddharasādanajanyadoṣanivṛtteḥ na doṣaprakopaṃ nirākṛtya sukhaṃ bhūyādityarthaḥ |
kecidviruddharasajanyādbhiḥ sirābhyantararandhramārgeṣu pūrṇapradeśatvāt vivarṇitvaṃ virasāmyatvaṃ ca jñāpyate | tena pavanaprakopanivartakarasavirasādanajanyamudarāmayamiti jñātuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ | pittodarāmayahetukānidānadravyajanyalakṣaṇāni nirūpyante--
mūrchādāhabhramatṛṣṇārucijvarāṅgapītatvaṃ pittaprakopodarāmayahetukam | prakopanivartakakaṣāyatiktasvādurasavirasadravyādanaṃ pittapradeśavyāpyasirārandhramārgagatapavanagativikārakamāṃsadhātuśoṣakaṃ, pavanadoṣaprakopajanakarasavirasātiriktamāṃsadhātuśoṣakavirasadravyādanajanyatvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | ityanumānavidhayā māṃsadhātuśoṣakajanyamāṃsasāraḥ pittodarāmayahetukaḥ jaṭharānalājīrṇadhātusāradravyatvāt | tena murcchā'pi jāyate | taddhātuśoṣavaśāt dāho bhavati | dhātusārasya itarapadārthasparśanādbhramo bhavati | tena jaṭharānalasthalanirasanāt bahiḥ prajvalanāt jvaro bhavati | tatsārātipradarśanāt tena rāgo bhavati | māṃsadhātośca nirgatarasatvāt paittyaṃ pratīyate | etādṛśalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ pittodarāmayo'yamiti vyākhyātam |
itaḥparaṃ aṣṭavidhodarāmayasya saṅkhyā--
śvāsakhāsabhramavidāhatimiratvakpalitatanuretodhātusirājātarasaḥ kaphaḥ kaphaṃ karoti || 24 ||
māṃsadhātośca medodhātujanakatvāt tadvikāre sati śvāsakhāsabhramavidāhatvaktanutatsirājātasārakaphaḥ kaphaṃ karoti | kaphadoṣaprakopanivartakaṃ tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasavirasadravyādanam | kaphodarāmayahetukaṃ kaphapradeśasthānavyāpyasirārandhramārgagatapavanagativikāramedodhātuśoṣakaṃ, pavanapittaprakopajanakarasavirasātiriktamedodhātujanakaśoṣakarasavirasadravyādanajanyatvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ |
nanu kaṣāyatiktamadhurarasāḥ pittaprakopanivartakāḥ | tadvirasāḥ pittaprakopakārakāḥ | svādvamlalavaṇarasāḥ pavanaprakopanivartakāḥ | tadvirasāḥ pavanaprakopakārakāḥ | vātapittayoḥ svādurasavirasadravyaṃ vātapittaprakopakārakam | rasavirasajanyapavanaprakope sati tadviṣayaroge dvandvaprakopo bhavati | svādurasavirasasya vātapittaprakopahetukatvāt | dvitrividharasajanyarogaprasaktiryatra dṛśyate tatra svādurasavirasadravyādanaṃ dvandvarūpakotpādakamityāha--pavaneti |
pavanapittaprakopajanakadvitrirasajātānusarita uktobhayalakṣaṇayastavātapittodarāmayo jñeyaḥ || 25
asyārthaḥ---
pavanapittaprakopakārakadravyaṃ tadrasavirasādanajanyaṃ tadanya rasājanyatve sati tadrasajanyatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | pavanarogastu svādvamlavirasajanyaḥ tadanyarogakāryāhetukatve sati tadrogakāryajanakadravyatvāt iti vyatirekānumānena nirṇetuṃ śakyatvāt | pavanaprakopanivartakaḥ svādvamlalavaṇarasaḥ | tadvirasajanya udarāmayaḥ | pittaprakopanivartakaḥ kaṣāyatiktasvādurasaḥ | tadvirasaḥ pittaprakopakārakaḥ | tasmādubhayadoṣaprakopajanakasya svādurasavirasadravyādanatvāt sa eva dvidoṣaprakopako bhavati | dvitrividharasajātarogau pavanapittaprakopajātodarāmayau | tannidānaṃ tattallakṣaṇalakṣitādjñātavyamityarthaḥ |
nanu svādvamlalavaṇarasādīnāṃ virasatvaṃ sūtre pratīyate | sa virasaḥ pavanapittaprakopakāraka ityuktam | evaṃ sati kaṣāyatiktasvādurasānāṃ virasatvaṃ asminsutre pratīyate | sa virasaḥ pittaprakopakārako bhavatītyuktam | idaṃ nopapadyate | virasatvaṃ nāma doṣaprakopakaviruddharasavaddravyaṃ bhavati | tadrasadravyādanaṃ kathaṃ doṣatrayopakārakaṃ kālavaśājjātatadvyatiriktarasasya guṇātiriktaguṇavatparipūrṇadravyeṣveva tathā pratītatvāt | tatra sūtravacanaṃ--
madhuraṃ śleṣmalaṃ prāyaḥ jīrṇācchāliyavādṛte |
mudgādgodhūmataḥ kṣaudrātsitāyā jāṅgalāmiṣāt ||
prāyo'mlaṃ pittajananaṃ dāḍimāmalakādṛte |
apathyaṃ lavaṇaṃ prāyaḥ cakṣuṣo'nyatra saindhavāt ||
tiktaṃ kaṭu ca bhūyiṣṭhamavṛṣyaṃ vātakopanam |
ṛte'bhṛtāpaṭīlībhyāṃ śuṇṭhīkṛṣṇārasonataḥ |
kaṣāyaṃ prāyaśaśśītaṃ stambhanaṃ cābhayāmṛte ||"
evaṃ prakāreṇa ṣaḍrasānāṃ rogaprakopahetukatvaṃ pratipāditam | na te rogahārakāḥ | tathā sati tadvirasadravyādīnāṃ rogapratipādakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi na vācyaṃ, madhurarasasya bahuguṇavaddravyatvena tacchaleṣmalamiti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | "madhuraṃ śleṣmalaṃ prāyaḥ" iti vacanaṃ nāśaṅkanīyam | tadvirasadravyaṃ sakaladoṣakārakaṃ ṣaḍrasānāmapi tathā vaktuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ | evaṃ pavanakaphodarāmayasya sūtrapratipāditārthaḥ pūrvoktaprakāreṇa yojanīyaḥ | evaṃ pittakaphodarāmayasyāpi etāvadartho yojanīyaḥ | evamākāreṇa saptasaṅkhyodarāmayā jātāḥ | tadupari sutratrayaṃ vyācaṣṭe--kapheti |
kaphapittaprakopajanakadvitrirasajātānusaritatattallakṣaṇalakṣitakaphapittodarāmayo jñeyaḥ | kaphapittodarāmayaṃ dvitrirasajātānusaritobhayalakṣaṇajñānajanyakaphapittāmayaṃ vidyāt | rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedovikārajanakadvitrirasānusaritatattallakṣaṇajñānagocarapavanapittakaphodarāmayo dussādhyaḥ || 26 ||
nanu doṣatrayajanakarasavirasādanahetukodarāmayasadbhāve kiṃ mānaṃ? tajjanakahetorevābhāvāt | tatraikadoṣajanyāstrayaḥ | dvandvajātāstrayaḥ | tridoṣajanya ekaḥ | evaṃ saptavidha udarāmayaḥ | itaḥparamaṣṭasaṅkhyāpūraṇārthe udarāmaye tadbhinnabhūtadoṣasyaivābhāvāt vātādīnāṃ prayojakatvasya tadbhedopādhivaśāt saptavidhaprakārasya pūrvameva pratipāditatvāt taditarodarāmayasya hetvabhāvena jñātumaśakyatvāt kāraṇābhāve kāryānudayādityāśaṅkya samādhatte klimīti |
klimijanakaplīhodarāmayastathā || 27 ||
asyārthaḥ-- klimayastu virasajātāmayajanyāḥ | tebhyo jātaplīhodarāmayāssambhavanti, klimessārajanyamāṃsagranthirūpatvāt | tasya jaṭharānalakāryasya prayojakatvāt tadvadeva doṣaprayukto bhavati | tasmādaṣṭavidhodarāmayassaṅgacchata ityarthaḥ |
pāṇḍuśophavisarparogāṇāṃ pratipādanasyāvasaratvāt udarāmayajanakasirāmārgagatapavanagatyā udarāmayakāryadhātutrayadoṣaikajanyatvasya eteṣāmapi samānatvāt udarāmayanirūpaṇānantarametannirūpaṇasya prastutatvādāha--kaṭviti |
kaṭvamlalavaṇarasavaddravyādanajātamala mārgāvarodhanāt tatsirārandhramārgagatordhvahṛtparipūraṇapavanaprakopanāt pāṇḍuśophavisarparukprado bhavati || 28
kaṭvamlalavaṇarasāḥ tadvirasāśca pittaprakopakārakāḥ | tadvirasāḥ tajjanyapāṇḍuśophavisarpakārakāḥ | tadvirasādanajanyāmadoṣaḥ pāṇḍuśobhavisarparogakārakaḥ | tadvikāraḥ āmāśayaṃ prāpya sirārandhrādūrdhvamārgamavagamya hṛdgataḥ pavanagativikārakārakatvāt tadvyāpyasirāvikārakārakatvāt tadgataraktapradūṣaṇāt tadvyāpya sthita ssan pāṇḍurūpaṃ labhate | raktavikāre sati śobho bhavati | māṃsavikārakārakatvāt visarpo bhavati | tadgatāmameva pittaṃ bhavati | tadrogasthānasthitatvāt vikāraprado bhavati | tasya viṣarūpatvāt dhātavaḥ pradūṣyante | tajjanyalakṣaṇāni tatratatra prakāśyante | tadāmarūpatvātpittaṃ hṛdayasthitāmapittasāraṃ sirārandhrabhāgamāpūrya pakvāśaye srāvitatvāt tatpakvasārasyaiva mūtratvāt hṛtkamalaṃ praviśya tatraiva sthitaṃ hṛdi spandanaṃ karoti | dvisirāvṛtapadmādhārajihvāpraveśanātpittasya tatsirārandhrapraveśanāt jihvāyāmarucirdṛśyate | hṛdayasthitāmapittasāraṃ sirārandhrabhāgamāpūrya pakvāśaye srāvitatvāt tatpakvasārasyaiva mūtratvāt pācakapittābhāvo bhāsate | taddhātupraveśanamapi tadātmatvena tatpūrvavarṇe vihāya pītavarṇaṃ bhāsate| tatpañcāśatsirārandhradhamanīṃ sampūrya akṣipadmaṃ praviśati | tatra pītatvaṃ pratīyate | śatasirārandhradhamanīṣu pittasārapraveśanāt tacchākhāṅgulīṣu kṛṣṇāruṇakanakapītadatvena tatpūrvavarṇaṃ vihāya pītavarṇaṃ bhāsate | tatrastharaktaṃ bahuvarṇaṃ bhāsate | nābhyāvṛttacakrādhārakuṇḍalyādhārabhūtānīlātmakanābhyāvṛttapadmahṛdgatāmapittasāraḥ nābhyāvṛttacakrādhāratriṃśatsirābhyantaramārgeṣu praviśan tāvadvikārān janayatītyāha--bhrameti |
bhramavamipipāsācharditṛṣṇāmūrchāntarvidāhāṅga spandanārucipāṇḍutā pittapāṇḍuśobhalakṣaṇam | pādajānujaṅghrorukaṭipṛṣṭhabījapārśvātivyathātisārarūkṣatātiśītasarvāṅgaspandanaṃ pavanaśobhapāṇḍulakṣaṇam || 29 ||
vātapāṇḍulakṣaṇānyucyante-- pādeti|
avarṇādibhūtapādapadmasya catustriṃśatsirāvṛtālavālaṃ tadāmapittātisāraṃ śiraḥkamalādāgatāmṛtanirodhanādāmapittātisārapradarśanāt śobhapāṇḍuvisarpāmayacihnajñāpakaṃ bhavati | virasādanavikāravitpavano rasāsṛgdhātugataścet pāṇḍurūpavikāraṃ bhajate | sa eva pavano māṃsadhātugataścet śvayathurbhavati | sa eva dhātugataścet visarpāmayo jāyate | yasya pāde vikāro jāyate tatpradeśasthāvarṇoccāraṇaṃ spaṣṭoccāraṇavadaśrāvyam | tatpradeśe śvayathudarśanaṃ vā pāṇḍuśobhavisarpāmayā vā jñātavyāḥ |
avarṇasthānabhūtajānupadmādhārakaṃ catustriṃśatsirāvṛtajānupadmālavālaṃ śvāsocchāsābhyāṃ śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtopaharaṇamārgaṃ rasavirasajanyāmapittasārasaṃskāraṃ tatsirāmārgagatapavanavivṛtyā pūrvavatpāṇḍuśobhavisarpāmayāḥ | rasāsṛgdhātudūṣaṇātpāṇḍutvam | māṃsadhātudūṣaṇācchvyathudarśanam | medodhātudūṣaṇādvisarparogāḥ prādurbhavantītyarthaḥ |
ye tadbarṇādhāratattaddeśīyapadmāvṛtasirāmārgagatapavanapracāraṇābhāvakāryajanakapāṇḍuśobhavisarpāmayāḥ pūrvavaddhātūndūṣayanti acāṃ varṇānāmuccāraṇasakalasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ aśrāvyatvaṃ, te pavanaprakopajātā iti jñātuṃ śakyatvādeva pittapradeśakamalādhāra śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtapravāhavaśāt tadviruddharasajanyāmapittasārasaṃyogakāryaṃ sirāṇāmeva, tadrogalakṣaṇajñānajñāpakatvāt | yāvatkālaṃ yāvatsirāsaṃsparśanaṃ tattallakṣaṇalakṣitarogābhāvasya tattannidānajñāpakatvāt | pṛṣṭhadeśamārabhya bījapārśvameḍhrādadhaḥpradeśapadmamārabhya kacaṭatapavargāṇāṃ tattadbījajanakapavanādeva etadadhiṣṭhitapadmāni pañcadaśasaṅkhyākāni pittaprakopavikārabhāvakāryakārakapittādhārakapadmāni | pittaprakopasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ tattadvarṇavikārajñāpakalakṣaṇalakṣitāni tattannidānajñāpakāni | āoṣṭhapradeśaparyantaṃ pañcadaśādhārabhūtapadmasthānādhiṣṭhitapittaṃ tattallakṣaṇāni tatraivāvirbhūtānītyarthaḥ |
itaḥparaṃ kaphapāṇḍuśobhavisarpāmayacinhāni prakāśyante-- śvāseti |
śvāsakhāsanāsākṣipakṣmakarṇakapoladantātipīnasaśirastodanaṃ pāṇḍuśobhavisarpāmayalakṣaṇam || 30 ||
kaphapradeśe hṛdi amapittātisārasya triṃśatsirāvṛtahṛtkaramalasthitatvāt tatsirāgatapavanavigatyā śvāsakhāsau bhavetām | akṣipakṣmakarṇakapoladantānāṃ malādhikyaṃ vedanātipratītatvāt | etallakṣaṇāṇi kaphaprakopajanyāmayarūpapāṇḍuśobhavisarpāmayalakṣaṇāni tattannidānena jñāpyante |
nanu pavanapittakaphaprakopena pavanapittakapharogā iti pratipādyate | taccintyam | rasavirasānnādanaṃ vā śuddharasavaddravyādhi kyānnādanaṃ vā tadāśayasthapavanaveganirodhanaṃ vā rogahetukamiti vaktavyam | tathā sati sarvaśarīrasaṃcaritapavanaprakopāḥ virasaviṣasaṃparkadoṣajāḥ tatpavanasya śarīravyāpyasaṃcārakarmaguṇavaddravyatvāt | tadvirasaviṣasaṃparkavaśāt pavanaprakopo bhavatu | kaphapittayo paṅgutvādeva tayorvirasaviṣasaṃsargasya vaktumaśakyatvāt | tadvat ayaṃ kapharogaḥ, ayaṃ pittarogaḥ iti kathaṃ jñāyata ityasvarasādāha--bāhyeti |
bāhyaviṣagrastavaddhātudūṣakāḥ || 31 ||
sarpādinā daṣṭasya viṣagrastasya dṛṣṭatvāt | tayoḥ paṅgutvādeva tadviṣasaṃsargābhāvepi sarvaśarīravyāpyapavanavaccharīrasya pittakaphayoḥ śarīrāntaḥpātitvena viṣasaṃspṛṣṭapavanasaṃsargavaśāt doṣatrayaprakopo bhaviṣyati | tadvadatrāpi pavanavyāpyaviṣarasadaṣṭapavanasaṃyogasya tayorvidyamānatvāt ayaṃ pittadoṣaḥ ayaṃ kaphadoṣa iti vaktuṃ śakyate | tarhi rogamātrasya pavanaprakopajanyatvāt sarvarogāṇāṃ pavanaprakopādhīnatvāt sarve rogāḥ pacanajanyā eva syuḥ | tasmātkaphapittayoḥ pṛthaktayā'vacchedakatāsaṃbhavāt tathā sūtrasthāne pratipāditaṃ--
vibhutvādāśukāritvādbalitvādanyakopanāt |
svātantryapāratantryābhyāṃ vyādheḥ prādhānyamādiśet ||
etadvacanānusāreṇa pavanasyaiva sarvarogaprādhānyaṃ pratītamiti cet na | tatsarvaśarīravyāpyatve'pi dhātupoṣakaṃ na bhavati | mandānilasandhukṣaṇakarmaguṇavaddravyatvāt | analamandatvanivartakasandhukṣaṇaguṇavaddravyasya dhātupoṣakatvābhāvāt | pittakalāyā eva jaṭharānalatvāt pittaprakope sati analo mando bhavati | tena kaphaprakopa eva syāt | pittakaphaprakopo yāvaccharīranāśakaḥ sirāmārgagatadhātupoṣakapavanamārganirodhanadravyatvāt | vyādhyavacchedakaṃ śarīravināśakatvaṃ, pittakaphavyatirekeṇa śarīranāśakatvābhāvāt | vāyostu tatsaṃsargadvārā rogahetukatvapratipādanam | tasmānmukhyatvena prādhānyaṃ pittasyāpi saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ | pavanasya vyādheḥ prādhānyapratipādanaṃ sarvaśarīravyāpyapavanamantareṇa rogakāryahetukatvaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyata eveti tātparyam |
nanu pāṇḍuśophavisarparogāḥ tattadbhinnijñānaviṣayakasāmagrījanyāḥ | natvekapadārthajñānagrāhakasāmagrī bahupadārthagrāhikā bhavati | bahupadārthaviṣayakajñānaṃ naikapadārthaviṣayakajñānagrāhakasāmagrījanyaṃ, ekapadārthaviṣayakajñānagrāhakasāmagrījanyatvāt | tathāhi ghaṭendriyaviṣayakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ ghaṭaviṣayakajñānaṃ jāyate paṭapadārthaviṣayakajñānaṃ nodeti | tadvadevātrāpi | ekarogajanakasāmagrī kathaṃ rogakrayapratipādakakāryajanakasāmagrī, bahukāryajñānagrāhikāyā adṛṣṭatvāt | anyathā ekapadārthagrāhakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ sarvapadārthaviṣayakajñānoptattireva syādityasvarasādāha--vāteti |
vātapittarasavirasadravyasaṃsargajātavātagatirodhanādrasāsṛgdhātuvidūṣaṇāttvakpālityaṃ pāṇḍurogahetukam || 32 ||
asyārthaḥ--vātapittaprakoparogāḥ rasavirasadravyasaṃskārajātāḥ, tena pavanagatirodhanadvārā rasāsṛgdhātuṃ vidūṣya tvakpālityaṃ yatra dṛśyate tatra pāṇḍurogo'stīti nidānaṃ jñātavyam | sa eva pāṇḍuroga ityarthaḥ | nanu vātapittaprakopasāmagryā vātaprakopo jā yate | pittaprakopasāmagryā pittarogo jāyate | paṇḍurāgajñāpakatvakpālityaṃ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha--hṛditi |
hṛdi sthitāmapittaviṣasāraṃ pādapadmaśoṣakaṃ avarṇabodhakacatusriṃśatsirāsaṃsargavaśādamṛtapravāharodhanātpavanajanyāmayāḥ pravardhante || 33 ||
rasavirasadravyādanajātāmāśayavyāpakapavanavigatyā āmapittaviṣasāraḥ avarṇodbodhakatatpādapadmapoṣakasirārandhramārgagatāmṛtapravāhaṃ nirudhya vātavikārajanyapāṇḍurogaḥ hṛdisthitāmapittasāraḥ paraṃparāheturbhavatītyarthaḥ | sa eva vātodarasyāpi heturbhavati | vātapittapaṇḍurogasyāpi paraṃparayā heturbhavati | anilajanyavātāmayasyāpi heturbhavati | ata eva udarāmayanirūpaṇānantaraṃ paṇḍuśophavisarpāmayānāmavasara iti pratipāditam | etatpratipāditāmayānāṃ janakībhūtāmapittarasaviṣadravyasyaikatvāt | ekakāraṇajanyasya nānākāryapratītiḥ upādhibhedādbhavati | suvarṇasyaikatve'pi tajjanyakāryaṃ tattadrūpabhedena bahuvidhaṃ pratīyate, tatra kāraṇabāhulyasya dṛṣṭatvāt |
nanu ayaṃ vātapāṇḍurogaḥ, ayaṃ pittapāṇḍurogaḥ, ayaṃ kaphapāṇḍurogaḥ iti tādṛśapratītervidyamānatvāt tatkāryahetubhūtārthāḥ doṣā eva bhaveyuḥ | virasajanyāmapittaviṣarūpadravyasya virasajanakatvāt tatpāṇḍuśophavisarpāmayānāṃ tattajjanakībhūtā doṣā eva hetavo bhavantītyasvarasādāha--amṛteti |
amṛtapravāhālavālopajīvyāvarṇodbodhakasarandhnakābhyantaradharaṣaṭsirāmapittaviṣasāragrasanaṃ pavanaprakopahetukam || 34 ||
śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtāharaṇayogyaśvāsocchvāsānilābhyāmāhṛta tadvarṇabodhakārikāryotpādakatatpadmānyeva ālavālāni teṣvamṛtapūraṇaṃ kartuṃ sarandhrakābhyantaradharaṣaṭsirā eva hetubhūtā bhavantītyarthaḥ |
nanu sirāmārgagatāmṛtapravāhapratibandhakajanyāmayakāraṇaṃ āmapittaviṣasāraṃ bhavitumarhati tatsirāsaṃsargasaṃskārajātarogahetukatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ| ityanumānapramāṇena āmapittarasasyaiva hetutvaprasaṃgādityasvarasādāha--sire iti |
sirāmārgagatapavanamāṃsadhātvanusṛtajānujaṅghorubījapārśvapadmagata i, u, ṛ, e, o, ai varṇodbodhakasarandhrakābhyantaradharasirāmārgagatapavanagatinirodhānādanilaprakopo bhavati || 35 ||
āmapittarasasāraṃ jānujaṅghorubījapārśvasthitapadmādhārakasirāmārgasaṃsparśanānmāṃsadhātudūṣaṇāt ivarṇabodhakasirāsparśanaṃ pavanagatirodhanaṃ jānupadmadvyahetukaṃ uvarṇabodhakasirāsaṃsparśanaṃ pavanagatirodhakārakaṃ jaṅghorupadmadvayahetukaṃ ṛvarṇabodhakasirāsaṃsparśanaṃ pavanagatinirodhanavatsirābhya utpannaṃ jñānaṃ sirāmārgagatapavanagatijanyavisvarodbodhakahetuko bhavati | yāvadrogajanakasāmagrī tattadroganidānahetubhūtavisvarodbodhakakāryakāriṇī bhavati | udarapāṇḍuśophavisarpakuṣṭharaktavātapavanakāryāṇāṃ ekasāmagrījanyatvāt tatsirāgatapavanakāryabhedādeva tadrogabhedaṃ janayatītyarthaḥ | tattadroganidānaṃ tattatsthasvaraviśeṣāvarṇātmakapadoccāraṇaṃ tattadvyādhivijñānahetukaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu pavanavikārā bahavassanti | tathāhi netravāyuḥ, karṇavāyuḥ, śirovāyuḥ, pakṣāghātavāyuḥ, dhanurvāyuḥ, arditavāyuḥ, gulmavāyuḥ, plīhavāyuḥ, kaṭivāyuḥ, iti | tattallakṣaṇalakṣitāni tattadjñāpakānyapi bahūni santi | sirāsaṃcaritapavanagativikārabhedāt tattadaṅgasthitāni tattadvikārabhūtāni | eka eva pavanassan tattadaṅgagatopādhibhedāt tattannāmābhidheyāḥ tattadaṅgopadravakārakāḥ praṇaghātukāśca bhavantītyasvarasādāha--taditi |
tatsirāmārgagatapavanaprakopabhedādhiṣṭhānopādhibhedātpavanavikārabhedāḥ |  āmapittaviṣarasavirodhidravyaṃ bheṣajam || 36 ||
āmapittarasavirasajātarogāstacchabdārthaḥ | tatsirārandhramārgeṣu anilavikāro bhūtvā tatsirāsaṃsargapradeśeṣu yāvatkāraṇānugatapavanagativaicitryāt uvarṇabodhakasirāsaṃsparśanaṃ pavanagatirodhakārakam | kaṭipradeśapadmaśvayathuhetukam | evarṇabodhakasirāmārgasaṃsparśanaṃ pavanagatirodhakārakam | bījapadmaśvayathuhetukam | aivarṇodbodhakasirāmārgagatapavanagatirodhanaṃ tatpārśvaśvayathuhetukam | āmapittarasasāraṃ bījapārśvaśvayathuhetukam, tadvarṇabodhakasirāmārgagatapavanagatikārakatvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | ityanumānena acsvarādhiṣṭhānahetubhūtatattatpadmādhārakasirārandhrabhāgagatapavanagatiḥ susvaraviśiṣṭavarṇātmakapadajñānapratipādikā, jñānecchāprayatnādinā tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārātmakabāhyapavanāhṛtasaṃskārodbo dhakahetubhūtatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | tattadvyādhinidānajñānaṃ tattatsirādhīnavarṇātmakapadajñānahetukaṃ, avikārahetukasāmagrījanyatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ | bāhyapavanākarṣaṇajātatālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasaṃskārodbodhakavarṇādhārapadmaṃ virasadravyādanajanyapavanaprakopahetukasvarabodhakārakaṃ, pavanaprakopalakṣaṇajñānabodhakadravyatvāt āmapittasirāsāraṃ yādṛśasirāsaṃsparśanāt tattadaṅgajanyarogā iti | sakalapavanarogajanakasāmagrī amapittasāraḥ, tattadaṅgāvalagnasirāsaṃsparśanasāmagrīsvarūpatvāt | nānārūpaghaṭānāṃ ekamṛtpiṇḍameva kāraṇam | tathā ākāśādivadaṅgajanyopādhibhedāt karṇapavanarogaḥ netrapavanarogaḥ śiraḥpavanarogaḥ iti vyapadiśyate |
nanu āmapittasāraḥ udarapāṇḍuśophavisarpakuṣṭhapavanarogāṇāṃ ekameva kāraṇamiti vaktuṃ na śakyate, tattannāmabhedena kāryabhedasya dṛṣṭatvāt | tatra kāraṇabhedo'stīti avaśyaṃ jñātavyam | ghaṭaḥ kalaśa iva bhedo bhavatīti na vaktavyam | taddhi sarvaṃ pṛthubudhnodarākāratvena ayaṃ ghaṭa iti ghaṭatvavati ghaṭatva prakārakatvaṃ jñātuṃ śakyatvāt | na tadvadevātrāpi bhaviṣyati, ayaṃ pāṇḍurogaḥ ayaṃ śopharoga iti rogatvavati rogatvaviśiṣṭaprakārakatvena jñātuṃ śakyatvāditi vācyam, teṣāṃ mṛdvikāravyaktīnāṃ jalānayanakāryopakārakatvaṃ pṛthubudhnodarākāravyaktīnāṃ vyaktibhedajñāne satyapi pṛthubudhnodarākārasya ekaprakārakatvasya dṛṣṭatvāt | dṛṣṭāntenāpi abhedajñānamātramiti nānubhūyate | tadvadatrāpi phalabhedarūpabhedacikitsābhedajñānānubhavasya dṛṣṭatvāt, iti cet nna | tadvadatrāpi kāryabhedābhāvo'sti | tathāhi--āmapittasāraḥ uktarogeṣu eka eva heturbhavati | tattaccikitsābhedena tattannāmabhedānubhavaḥ kathamapalāpayituṃ śakyate ? atra brūmaḥ-- āmapitta rasasārastu udarapāṇḍuśophavisarpakuṣṭhavātaraktavātavikārāṇāmeka eva heturbhavati | sa eka eva kāryajanakaḥ | sirāmārgagatapavanagatinirodhanarūpakāryakārakadravyatvāt | tatpavanagatinirodhanaṃ etadāmayahetukam | tattu kvacidaṅgabhedeṣu rasāsṛgdūṣaṇadvārā tvakpālityaṃ karoti | sa pāṇḍuroga iti vyavahāramātrabheda upalabhyate | tadeva yadā tatsirāsaṃsargabhedavaśāt tatpavanagatinirodhanaṃ māṃsadhātuśoṣakaṃ bhavati itaramāṃsadhātuḥ pṛthurbhavati | tacchobharoga iti vyavahriyate | tadgatinirodhanaṃ yadā medodhātudūṣakaṃ bhavati sa medassāraḥ bahiḥsphoṭarūpeṇa sphuraṇāt pavanavisarparoga iti jñānaṃ jāyate | sa āmasāraḥ medodhātūn sandūṣya tadbahirbhūtamāṃsadhātumāvṛtya tvagdhātuṃ praviśya bahissarpati cet tatkuṣṭhaṃ bhavati | yāvatpavanādhārakasirā āmapittarasasāragrastāssantaḥ yāvatpadmavikārakāryakāritattatsirāvarṇavikāraṃ kurvantīti kuṣṭhāḥ, kutsitāṅgavikaraṃ kurvantīti kuṣṭhāḥ | pādapadmādhārakasirāhṛdgatāmapittarasasārasaṃskārayuktasirā tadviṣasāraṃ sravati | tatpadmamāpūrya pādāṅgulibhyaḥ sravati | tacchākhāni śīrṇāni bhavantītyarthaḥ |
nanu rasavirasajanyājīrṇajanyāmayahetukatve sati medosthimajjādhātugatasirādhārakapadmāśritāmapittarasaviṣasārajanyāmayahetukatvāditi vyatirekyanumānapramāṇena pratipāditāmayānāṃ ajīrṇajanyāmanivartakasāmagryatiriktasāmagrīnivartakadravyatvāt iti | tasmāllaṅghanatadupayogadravyāṇi nivartakānīti āyurvedasūtracayaṃ vyācaṣṭe | pavanaprakopahetukavirasājjātaṃ rasāsṛṅmāṃsacayapradeśagataṃ bhavati | svādvamlalavaṇarasādhikyādanena ajīrṇe jāte sati pavanaprakopo bhavati | acāṃ varṇānāṃ ādhārabhūtapadmādhārakasirāsaṃsa rgavaśāt pavanāmayāḥ pravardhante | tajjanakāmaśoṣe sati tannivartate | tāvadvirasādanādajīrṇādāmapravṛddhau satyāṃ rasāsṛṅmāṃsagatasirāsaṃsparśanādyatra pavanavikāro jāyate sa āmapittasāraḥ āmāśayaṃ praviśya hṛtkamalapraveśanāt viṣasaṃsargavat yāvadrogahetukaṃ tāvaddhātvantargatasirāsaṃsparśanāt taddhātūnsandūṣya rasāsṛṅmāṃsabhedodhātavaḥ dūṣitāssantaḥ kramātpāṇḍuśophavisarpāmayāḥ pravardhante | sutarāṃ medodhātuvikāre sati kuṣṭhaṃ bhavati | sa āmapittasāraḥ rasāsṛṅmāṃsadhātūnsandūṣya medodhātusthitatvāt tāvaddhātusārasya bahiḥpradarśanameva kuṣṭharogakāraṇaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu trisārādhikyādanajātarogāstu ajīrṇarogajanyāmayā bahavassanti | tatkāryabhedasya dṛṣṭaṃtvāt kāraṇabhedo vaktavyaḥ| āmapittasārasya ekarūpatvāt śophapāṇḍuvisarpamayānāṃ dhātudūṣakakāryasya ekatvāt tatra vaktuṃ śakyate | tatkāryavyatiriktakuṣṭharogakāryasya bhinnatvādityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya kuṣṭharogāgamahetuṃ varṇayati--pūrvasmāditi |
pūrvasmāddviguṇaṃ pṛthutayā bhāti || 37||
pūrvadhātubhyaḥ sthūlatvaṃ dviguṇaṃpratīyate | na ca tantubaddhānusārasrāvitarogasvarūpatvāt sirāśauṇḍilyatayā pṛthutvaṃ bhāsate | āmapittasārasya yāvatsirāsaṃsparśanaṃ tattadrogāṇāṃ tattatsirāsaṃsparśanaṃ kāraṇaṃ bhavati | tathā sati kāraṇavaicitryāt kāryavaicitryaṃ pratīyate | tantugatarūpānugatarūpānuvicitrarūpavattvaṃ yāvattantu gatarūpānusārivaicitryaṃ tajjanyapaṭavaicitryaṃ karoti | kāraṇaguṇā hi kāryaguṇānārabhante | yāvatsirāsaṃsparśanaṃ tāvatsirādhārakapadmagatavikārakārakaṃ, tadanyarogakāryahetukatve sati taddhetukatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivamiti | pāṇḍuśobhavisarpakuṣṭharaktavātāmayānāṃ āmapittarūpaviṣasaṃsargaviśiṣṭasirāṇāṃ nānārupakāryajanakatvaṃ sūcyate |
āmapittasārasya ekatve'pi tajjanakadravyādhikyaṃ kāraṇatāvacchedakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu sarvaśarīravyāpyapavanaprakope sati tatsaṃcārayogyadhātuśoṣo jāyate | śoṣaṇaikakāryakārakarūkṣaguṇadravyatvāt anantāvacchedakaṃ rūkṣaguṇavattvadhātuśoṣakakāryakārakatvaṃ prathamata eva prati pratipāditam |
tatra rūkṣo laghuśśītaḥ kharassūkṣmaścalo'nilaḥ || iti.
tasmātpavanavikāramātreṇa āmapittarasasārasya ekadeśasthitatvāt sarvadhātuśoṣaṇaṃ kartuṃ na śakyata ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya pavanavikāraṃ pratipādayati--trisahasreti |
trisahasrasirāhetukaṃ madhurībhūtaṃ jvalayati || 38 ||
dhātupradeśavātapadmādhiṣṭhitāvarṇoccāraṇasāmagrī visvarapratipādikā yadi bhavettadā pavanaprakopakārakamadhurībhūtadravyādanajātapavanavikārakāryahetutvāt svādvamlalavaṇarasavaddravyadhātupradeśavātapadmādhiṣṭhitāvarṇoccāraṇasāmagrī visvarapratipādikā yadi bhavettadā pavanaprakopakārakamadhurībhūtadravyādanaṃ pavanarogaprāgabhāvaparipālanakārakāstāssirāstrisahasrasaṃkhyākāḥ pādapadmādhārakāḥ tā vatsirāvaccharīraṃbhavati pavanavigatyādhārakasirāṇāṃ tattadgatitirodhānakārakadravyaṃ kiṃ vaktavyam ? tatpavanaprakopakārakaṃ bhavati | na tāvaddhātuśoṣakaṃ, kiṃtu vedanā rogakāryaṃ bhavātītyarthaḥ |
nanu sarvadravyeṣu madhurarasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartako bhavati | sthāvarajātisthitamadhurarasaḥ pavanaprakopakārakaḥ | jaṅgamādisthitamadhurarasavaddravyaṃ pavanaprakopahārakam | tathā'pi ubhayamapi paramadhātupoṣakam | sthāvarāṇāṃ svādurasaḥ rasāsṛgdhātuvikārakaḥ | jaṅgamānāṃ svādurasaḥ sarvadhātupoṣako bhavatītyāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya prajājananahetuṃ pratipādayati--prajeti |
prajotpodakahatubhūtaṃ bhavati || 39 ||
jaṅgamānāṃ māṃsasāraḥ payovikāro bhavati | tatsajātīyānyasāro'pi sarvaśarīrāṇāṃ dhāturasassvādureva bhavati | tatsvādurasaḥ prajājananakārakaḥ retodhātuvikārahārakadravyatvāt | khecarāṇāmaṇḍajasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopahārakaḥ | bhūcaraśarīrajātakṣīravikārajātāḥ pavanaharaśukladhātupoṣakā ityarthaḥ | sthāvaradravyaniṣṭhamadhurarasānāmikṣukāṇḍādijanyānāṃ sarvadhātupoṣakatvaṃ vaktumaśakyatvāt, sthāvararasātmakatvāt tanniṣṭhasvāduraso gururbhavatītyasvarasādāha--madhureti |
madhurarasādanādasṛgdhāturbhavati || 40 ||
sthāvaraniṣṭhamadhurarasādanaṃ jaṅgamaśarīrāṇāṃ rasāsṛgdhātuprava rdhakaṃ ikṣukāṇḍādidravyajanyarasavaddravyatvāt | rasāsṛgdhātvabhivardhakassthāvaraniṣṭhasvāduraso bhavatītyarthaḥ |
sthāvaraniṣṭhāmlarasasya prayojanamāha--āmleti |
āmlarasādanaṃ māṃsadhātupradaṃ bhavati || 41 ||
sthāvarāṇāṃ lavaṇarasasyaprayojanamāha--lavaṇeti |
lavaṇaraso medodhātupradaḥ || 42 ||
sthāvarāṇāṃ lavaṇarasaḥ jaṅgamaśarīrāṇāṃ medodhātupravardhako bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu sthāvarāṇāṃ svādurasaḥ pavanaprakopakārakaḥ | jaṅgamaśarīrāṇāṃ svādurasaḥ pavanaprakopahāraka iti bhedakathanaṃ kathaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate | tadvadeva ṣaḍrasānāṃ guṇapradānaṃ bhedadravyasyaiva vaktuṃ śakyatvāt sthāvarāṇāṃ guṇapradānaṃ ṣaḍrasānāṃ tattajjātyavacchedena bheda eva syādityasvarasādāha--ādyeti |
ādyadvidhātusāraṃ jaṅghāpadmahetukam || 43 ||
tiktarasābhivardhitaṃ medodhātvantassthitadaśadalapadmaṃ kaṇṭhapradeśagatam | sahasrasirādāgatāmṛtaṃ tasya poṣakam | ādya iti--svādurasavaddravyam | dve iti-- kṣmāmbhasorekībhūtādhikajātadravyaguṇasāraṃ svādurasavaddravyaṃ tadeva nivartakaṃ bhavatītiyat tatkathaṃ jñātuṃ śakyate ? śukladhātupoṣakasvādurasaṃ ādyadvidhāturasāsṛgdhātudūṣyaṃ bhavati | jaṅgamadravyaniṣṭhasvādurasaṃ sarvadhātupoṣakam |
tathā gavyaṃ payaḥ gavyaṃ ghṛtaṃ rasāsṛgdhātuvikārānivartyataddhātupoṣyaṃ bhavati | tatsvādurasasya sarvaśarīrapuṣṭikaratvāt anila nivartakasvādurasavaddravyatvāt pādapadmādhārakajaṅghāpadmādhiṣṭhitājvarṇānāṃ ādhāro bhūtvā sa eva sugatikārakassan tattatpadmāni tattadadhiṣṭhitavarṇāni ca sahasrārapadmagatāmṛtaṃ sahasrasirābhya āhṛtya tattadvarṇādhārakapadmamāplāvya tattadvarṇānubhavajñānadvārā aikārajñāpakapavanaḥ tasya poṣako bhavati | tikterasābhivardhitapittadoṣaḥ nābheradhassthitadaśadalapadmaṃ svādhāraṃ kṛtvā pavanahṛtāmṛtaṃ svādhiṣṭhānabhūtaṃ daśadalapadmamāplāvya halādivarṇānāṃ śrotrapradeśaṃ prāpayan tiktarasaḥ māṃsamedodhātupoṣako bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu acassapta svarāḥ santaḥ sarandhrakābhyantaradharasirāmārgebhyaḥ śrotrapradeśaṃ prasarantaḥ svarāśśrūyante | pādapadmamārabhya bījapārśvapadmaparyantaṃ pavanasthānamiti | ata eva calanātmakaṃ karma kurute | jaṅghābhyāṃ padbhyāṃ dharmo'smīti śarīrāpagamāgamau bhavataḥ | tadevaṃ calanātmakagatāgatabhedaṃ pavana eva kurute | tenaiva sa pracarati | bījapārśvapadmamārabhya hastapadmaparyantaṃ kacaṭatapavargapañcakasya tattatpadmādhiṣṭhitatvāt | sarandhrakābhyantaragatapavanastu svābhimānapadmeṣu sthitvā acāṃ varṇānāṃ tattadvarṇasandhyuccāraṇārthaṃ supadaprayogoccāraṇārthaṃ tatpadasamūhavākyoccāraṇārthaṃ svābhipretārthabodhanārthaṃ padavākyaprayogavyatirekeṇa svasyāparasya bodhayitumaśakyatvāt | tasmādajbhiḥ sākaṃ halādayaḥ svābhipretārthaṃ pareṣāṃ vibodhayituṃ śaknuvanti | ata eva śabdaśāstre vaiyākaraṇaiḥ prathamata eva pratipāditaṃ--
"vṛddhirādaic, adeṅ guṇaḥ, iko guṇavṛddhī" etatsūtratrayasyārthe mahābhāṣye sarvaṃ prapañcitam | tatra yatpratipāditārthaṃ ajbhiḥ sākaṃ halādayaḥ kāryakārakā bhavanti | pittadoṣābhimānakukṣisthitatattatpadmasthitavargapañcakavarṇānāṃ tattatsirāgatapavanaḥ svābhimānabhūtājyavarṇān yathāvidhi śāstrapratipāditārthān bodhayituṃ svarasahitahalādīn sirāsyadoṣagatijātaśabdaḥ karṇayoḥ prapadyata iti | pittapradeśapadmasthitavarṇān pavana eva prāpayatītyāha--tikteti |
tiktarasābhivardhitaṃ medodhātusthitadaśadalapadmaṃ kaṭipradeśagatam | sahasrasirādāgatāmṛtaṃ tasya tatpoṣakam | tiktarasādhikakaphapradeśāmṛtaṃ tatra doṣajanyam | piṅgalāmārgādāgatapavananirodhanaṃ subheṣajaṃ supoṣakam || 44 ||
tiktarasadravyādanasaṃskārajanyaguṇāḥ tattadviruddhādanarasajanyarogavaśāt svasthoccāraṇādhīnavarṇātmakapadasamūhavākyaśravaṇaṃ tattatpadmādhiṣṭhitarogavijñānahetukaṃ bhavati | tālvoṣṭhavyāpārādhīnavarṇoccāraṇahetubhūtatattatpadmasthitarogajanakasāmagryā yāvaddhīnavarṇoccāraṇajanyavarṇākāraṃ vijñāya kaphavikārahetukasvādvamlarasavaddravyādhikyādanajanyakapharogasya tannivartakāmṛtaṃ piṅgalāmārgādāgatapavananirodhanajanyāmṛtaṃ subheṣajaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
hīnavarṇoccāraṇahetukatadrogasya tannivartakadravyābhāve'pi bahiḥ pavanarecanaṃ tatpūraṇaṃ ca nivartakaṃ bhavatītyāha--bahiriti |
bahiḥ pavanaṃ recayet tameva pūrayet || 45 ||
asya phalamāha | tasmāditi--
tasmāccirāyurbhavati || 46 ||
nanu etatsarvaṃ na saṃsṛjya ghaṭate | talvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ vāñchitārthaḥ śrotrapradeśagataśabdajanitavākyaśravaṇena tatpadārthaḥ vākyaviṣayo bhavati | na tatpadmādhiṣṭhitavarṇasirābhireva jñāpyate, gauravāt | "yaddhi manasā dhyāyati tadvācā vadati, tatkarmaṇā karoti" iti śrutirevātra pramājñāpikā | kiṃca ‘akuhavisarjanīyānāṃ kaṇṭhaḥ |icuyaśānāṃ tālu | upūpadhmānīyānāmoṣṭhau | ṛṭuraṣāṇāṃ mūrdhā | lṛtulasānāṃ dantāḥ" ityādivārtikavacanānāṃ vidyamānatvāt tattatpadmādhiṣṭhitavarṇān śrotrapradeśaṃ prāpayituṃ tattatsirāṇāṃ tatsāmāgryabhāvādityasvarasādāha--candreti |
candrakalāyatamarutpracoditapadmaṃ mukulībhavati | sūryakalāgatapavanādvikasati || 47 ||
candrakalā tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ candrasvarādāgatapavanena mukulībhavati | etadvarṇotpādakatālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasāmagrījanyapavanātsarandhrakābhyantaradharasirāmārgaṃ praviśya tattatpadmādhiṣṭhitavarṇāngṛhītuṃ tatpadmamukulībhāva eva kāraṇaṃ bhavatīti tatsarvaṃ manasi nidhāya tattadvyādhinidānaṃ pūrvasvarādvikṛtasvarāḥ, tatpadmādhiṣṭhitarogavijñānahetutvāt ityatra pramāṇaṃ varṇoccāraṇaśravaṇajñānānubhava eva | tadjñānaṃ kīdṛśamityukte anubhavaṃ pṛcchet na tadbodhayituṃ śakyam | kliṣṭoccāraṇaṃ yadā kriyate tasya bījapārśvasthānatvāt tadvyāpārajanakapavanastatsirāmārge sañcaran kava rṇaṃ gṛhītvā śrotrapradeśaṃ gamayati | tadā'tra 'kaḥ' śabdo jāyate | tatkarṇavivaraṃ gatvā āvirbhavati | teṣāṃ varṇānāṃ kaṇṭha eva sthānaṃ bhavati | icuyaśādivarṇā api tālupradeśaṃ gatvā āvirbhavanti | lṛtulasā api dantadeśaṃ gatvā āvirbhavantīti paraṃparayā tattatsajātīyā apyāvirbhūtā ityarthaḥ | evaṃ sūryakalā sahāyaṃ kṛtvā tattatpadmaṃ vikāsayantī varṇān janayati | evaṃ candrakalā ca sūryakalā ca tattatpadmānāṃ nimīlanonmīlanaṃ ca karoti | yāvadarthaṃ jānāti tadarthamicchati tāvadarthavaduccāritaśabdo viṣayo bhavatītyevaṃ manasi nidhāya tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpāraṃ karoti | tena vāyurutpannassan tajjātīyasirāgatapavanaṃ samprāpya tadvāñchitārthapratipādakavarṇasamūhaṃ padamuddiśya tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasāmagryā ca śabda utpadyata evetyarthaḥ |
nanu bherīdaṇḍasaṃyogena yāvatpavanānugataśabdaśśrūyate | tatra vāyossirādivyāpāraṃ vinā sa vāyuśśabdabodhako bhavati | tadvadevātrāpi syāditi cet na | tasyāpi śarīramasti carmapuṭamāvṛtya ākāśābhyantarassan bherīśarīramiti vyapadiśyate | tatra sirāgatapavanabyāpāraṃ vinā śabdotpattiḥ kathaṃ syāt | tadvadeva varṇātmakapadaṃ śrūyate | tatra bherīśabdo jāta iti kathamanubhūyata iti tādṛśārthadhvanyātmakaśabdamātrameva śrūyate | sa varṇātmakaḥ caitanyādhiṣṭhitasirādivyāpāraṃ vinā tasya asaṃbhāvitatvāt | dhvanyātmakaśabdatvāt asyāssāmagryā na tatra tātparyam |
nanu varṇānāṃ caitanyādhiṣṭhitaśrutiravatvāt tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasya samānatvāt varṇātmakaśabdaḥ kathaṃ sa syāditi cet manuṣyāṇāmiva paśvādīnāṃ aviralakaṇṭhābhāvatvena varṇātmakaśabdo na śrūyate nārthabodho'pyasti, hitārthamiyaṃ pravṛttirasyāstīti | aviralakaṇṭhatvamevātra prayojakam | tasmāttṛṇādikamāhāro bhavati |
nanu aviralakaṇṭhatvameva varṇātmakaśabdaniṣpattau prayojakam, tarhi aviralakaṇṭhatvaṃ khecarāṇāmapyasti tatra varṇātmakaśabdaniṣpattiḥ kathaṃ na syāt ? manuṣyāṇāmiva aviralakaṇṭhatvasya khecaraṇāmapi samānadharmatvāt | varṇātmakaśabdaḥ kathaṃ nopalabhyate teṣāṃ ? yatkiṃcicchabdāḥ śrūyante | manuṣyāṇāṃ tadviṣayakajñānābhāve'pi tattajjātīyānāṃ taduccāritaśabdaviṣayakajñānamasti | anyathā teṣāṃ pravṛttireva na syāt | tadanyathānupapattyā śabdaviṣayakajñānaṃ kalpyate | aviralakaṇṭhavajjātimajjantūccāritaśabdā varṇātmakāḥ, tattajjātiviṣayakajantūccāritaśabdajñānapravṛtterupalabhyamānatvāt maduccāritaśabdaviṣayakajñānānusaritapravṛttiviṣayavat | tasmādaviralakaṇṭhavajjātiviśiṣṭajantūnāṃ tattajjātividhivihitaviṣayakajñānānubhavānusaritapravṛtterdṛṣṭatvāt ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha-- iḍeti |
iḍāpiṅgalābhyāṃ pravahati || 48 ||
iḍāpiṅgalāpavanavajjātiviśiṣṭajantūccāritaśabdāḥ varṇātmakāḥ, candrasūryakalāgrastatattatpadmādhiṣṭhitavarṇānāṃ nimīlanonmīlanasaṃskārajātaśabdātmakatvāt | paśvādīnāṃ na tathā, ekadā ubhayasvarātmakatvāt | ata eva tṛṇādidravyamapācitamannamatti paśuḥ, ubhayānilānalātmakatvāt | khecarāṇāmapi tathaiva | jalacarāṇāmapi apācitānnādanaṃ iḍāpiṅgalāgatānilābhyāṃ pācakapittaṃ pravahati | manuṣyānyajātīnāṃ yugabhedena candrasūryakābhyāṃ tattadvarṇātmakādhiṣṭhitāni saṃprāpya khecarapaśvādyuccāritaśabdā varṇā tmakāḥ, purāṇādau tathā pratipāditatvāt | anyayugādau prāṇādipañcavāyūnāṃ asthigatatvāt tatkāle śabdā varṇātmakā bhavanti | sarvaśarīrāṇāṃ sātmakatvādeva tathā vaktuṃ śakyate | jīvaccharīraṃ sātmakaṃ prāṇādimatvāt ityanumānena pramāṇena yāni yāni śarīrāṇi sātmakāni tāni sarvāṇi prāṇātmakānyeva | kaliyugakālagrastaśarīrādigataprāṇādivāyavaḥ rasāsṛgdhātugatāḥ | tadanyayugakālagrastaśarīrādhiṣṭhitaprāṇādivāyavaḥ asthigatā bhavanti | etadyugakālagrastāni śarīrāṇi adharmātmakānītyatra adharmatvamupādhiḥ |
nanu śarīratvāvacchedena sarvaśarīrāṇāṃ prāṇādimattvādeva śarīratvāvacchedakasya sthāvarāṇāmapi sattvāt teṣāmapi śabdoccāraṇatvaprasaṃga ityasvarasādāha--ṣaḍiti |
ṣaṭkamalānāmādibhūtaṃ mūlādhārakam || 49 ||
ṣaṭkamalādhiṣṭhitaśarīratvaṃ yeṣāmasti teṣāṃ śabdoccāraṇe'pi sāmarthyamasti | tathā na sthāvarāṇāṃ pādapadmāvacchedakaṃ bhavati | jaṅgamaśarīrāṇāṃ śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtasāravatpoṣakatvasya teṣāmavacchedakatvāt | tatsahasrārapadmādhiṣṭhiśīrṣavattvāt śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtavattvaṃ śarīrapoṣakam | tatsthāvaraśarīrāḥ pādapā iti | tasmātteṣāṃ mūlādhārapadmaṃ ṣaṭkamalātmakaṃ na bhavati | sarveṣāṃ śarīratvāvacchedakatvasya sādhāraṇatve'pi yeṣāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ mūlādhārapadmaṃ ṣaṭkamalātmakaṃ teṣāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ śabdoccāraṇatvasya yogyatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu sthāvarāṇāmapi mūlādhārapadmaṃ daśadalātmakaṃ tiktoṣaṇarasātmakaṃ tadeva | tadeva śiraḥkamalasthaṃ sanmāṃsamedodhātvātmakam | tatra manuṣyāṇāṃ sahasrārapadmaṃ tattacchirasi pratibhāti | sthāvarā ṇāmapi agraśākhaṃ tadeva | śiraśśikhāmūlātmakatvāt śākhāgrāṇi pravartante | ṣaṭkamalānāmādibhūtamūlādhārakatvalakṣaṇasya sthāvarāṇāmapi sattvādityasvarasādāha--tikteti |
tiktoṣaṇarasapradānajanyamedomajjādhārakadaśadalapadmaṃ sahasrasirādāgatāmṛtaṃ tatra siñcati || 50 ||
śākhāṅkurādayaḥ tiktoṣaṇarasaprādhānyena jāyante | aṅkuraprādurbhāvasamaye tiktaraso vā ūṣaṇaraso vā yāvaducitasāraḥ pratibhāti, tattadaṅkurasya medomajjādhārakatvāt aṅkurādikameva daśadalapadmaṃ, mūlādhārakaṃ ca tadeva bhavati, ubhayorapi kadācidbījāvāpakatvasya dṛśyatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu manuṣyāṇāmiva kuṇḍalinīśakterabhāvāt iḍāpiṅgalānusaritatvāt śvāsocchvāsābhāvena tadvaśādeva amṛtāvasecanaṃ śākhiśarīrasya ayogyamityasvarasādāha-- iḍeti |
iḍāpiṅgalāgatāmṛtaṃ siñcati || 51 ||
