tatpatracalanameva śākhināṃ śvāsocchvāsau bhavataḥ | śākhāgraṃ daśadalapadmātmakaṃ surūpatvāt | sulāvaṇyātmakatvāt | cakṣurindriyaviṣayasukhajñānānubhavarūpaviṣayakatvāt | tacchākhāgravartidaśadalapadmaṃ cakṣurindriyaviṣayakaṃ sukhānubhavaviṣayasvarūpatvāt | śākhināṃ tatpadmameva poṣakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu śākhāgrāṇi surūpāṇi sulāvaṇyāni sukumārāṇi, cakṣuḥpratītiviṣayasukhānubhavaviṣayahetubhūtatvāt iti yaduktaṃ taccintyaṃ, sarvapadārthānāṃ cākṣuṣapratītiviṣayatvādeva sarvapadārthānāmapi sukhānubhavaviṣayatvasya vaktumaśakyatvādityasvarasādāha--sarvārthānāmiti |
sarvārthānāṃ hetubhūtānāṃ sarvasukhasādhakam || 52 ||
sarvārthānāmiti--
"samidho yajati | vasantamevartūnāmavarundhe | tanūnapātaṃ yajati |" ityanuvākasya svargasādhakayāgānuṣṭhānahetubhūtatvāt dharmaviṣayapratipādakatvamityuktam | phalitārthasya ādirūpeṇa phalaviṣayakārthapratipādakatvāt padārtharūpapuruṣārthaprāpakadravyatvāt "puruṣārtho'yamiti phalitapuṣpaphalānāṃ hetubhūtatvāt kāmanāviṣayakatvādeva tṛtīyapuruṣārthasya hetubhūtatvāt sarvaśāstrābhijñasya dharmajñasya hastakauśalyādijñānavataḥ sarvadā paropakāraśīlasya paṇḍitasya vipravarṇabheṣajasya āyurvedārthajñānadvārā cikitsakasya mokṣaprāptihetubhūtatvāt cikitsitaṃ mokṣaheturbhavati | tasya śrutireva pramāṇapratipādikā | tathā hi--
"aśvatthe vo niṣadanaṃ parṇe vo vasatiṣkṛtā |
gobhāja itkilāsatha yatsanavatha pūruṣam ||
yadimā vājayannahamoṣadhīrhasta ādadhe |
ātmā yakṣmasya naśyati purā jīvagṛbho yathā ||
yatrauṣadhīssamagmata rājānassamitāmiva |
viprassa ucyate bhiṣagrakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ" ||
ṛgvedaḥ 10, 97,sū.
iti śrutervidyamānatvāt cikitsitasya viprasyaiva mokṣaprāptihetubhūtārthatvāt tacchākhāgrāṇāṃ caturvidhapuruṣārthaprāpakatvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ | "yajñāsya śiro'cchidyata | te devāvaśvinābruvan bhiṣajauvai stha imaṃ yajñasya śira pratidhattam" ityanuvākasya tātparyaṃ śastradhāraṇaṃ brāhmaṇānāmayogyaṃ, iyaṃ śastracikitsā yāvadbrāhmaṇānāmayogyeti cikitsābhede niṣedhitam | tasmāt śastra cikitsā na kāryā | śūdraireva kārayitavyā | tatrāpi tattacchāstrapratipāditasirādibhedaṃ vijñāya śūdraireva kārayitavyā ityasya ya upadeṣṭā sa eva kartā | asyāpi saiva śrutiḥ pratipādikā--"tayostredhā bhaiṣajyaṃ vinyadadhuragnau tṛtīyaṃ apsu tṛtīyaṃ brāhmaṇe tṛtīyam | tasmādudapātramupanidhāya brāhmaṇaṃ dakṣiṇato niṣādya bheṣajaṃ kuryāt" iti vidheḥ vidyamānatvāt | evamāgamaśāstre pratipāditam--
dīrghaṃ tīvrāmayānāṃ bhiṣagiva kauśalaṃ no dhātumāyātu śauriḥ |" āpadgrastānāṃ trātuṃ cikitsā kāryetyarthaḥ | ata eva sarvasukhasādhakamityuktam |
nanu yaduddiśya pravṛttiḥ īśvaraprītyarthamupakārikā bhavati sā sarvaphalapradāyiketi vaktuṃ śakyate | atra tu cikitsākāryapratipādakopakārakadravyatvādeva eteṣāṃ śākhināṃ caturvidhapuruṣārthaphalapradānapratipādakatvaṃ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha --prakṛtīti |
prakṛtipuruṣayoraikyaṃ bhavati || 53 ||
sarvadā prakṛtipuruṣau phalopakārakau | sarveṣāṃ prāṇadau bhavataḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ--"annaṃ brahmeti vyajānāt | annāddhyeva khalvimāni bhūtāni jāyante | annena jātāni jīvanti | annaṃ prayantyabhisaṃviśantīti | tadvijñāya |" iti śrutirevātra pramāṇam | tasmādyaduddiśya pravṛttiḥ īśvaraprītyarthaṃ puruṣārthopakārikā jagadrakṣaṇārthaṃ tāvubhau sthāvaraśarīrāṇyabhūtām | jaṅgamaśarīrābhivṛddhiṃ kurutaḥ| tāvubhau abhivardhete | sthāvaramūlasāramūlatvaksārakandasārasāratvaksārakṣīrasārapuṣpasāraphalasāraniryasasārakṣīrasārakāḥ asthisāramajjāsārakāḥ | kāṇḍasārakāḥ | sarve te pañcadaśasārāḥ sthāvaraśarīrāḥ | tattadvyādhīnāṃ tattatsārabhedā nivartakā bhaveyurityarthaḥ |
nanu sarvaśarīrāṇi pāñcabhautikāni | śabdasparśarūparasagandhānāṃ ākārādiguṇatvāt tadguṇavyatirekeṇa teṣāmavasthānasya vaktumaśakyatvāt tadguṇinamantareṇa tadguṇābhāvasya sattvāt etatsārāṇāṃ vyādhinivartakatvaṃ kathaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha --pārthiveti |
pārthivāvayavādhikyopalabdhiryatra tatsārakaṃ pārthivaguṇam || 54 ||
āmlarasassugandhaścobhayamapi pṛthivīguṇo bhavati | gandhavattvaṃ pṛthivyā avacchedakatvam | āmlarasavattvaṃ gandhavattvaṃ ca yugapadevopalabhyate | tattadaṅgameva tattadvyādhinivartakaṃ bhavati | tathā hi--puṣpeṣu adhikagandhopalabdhiḥ | tatpuṣpasāradravyamiti vijñeyam | nanu arkavṛkṣasya kṣīrasāradravyatvāt tāvanmātrameva prayojanaṃ na bhavati | tatpatrasyāpavananivartakatvam | tanmūlatvaco'pi pe pavanadoṣaprakopānivartakatvam | tatpuṣpāṇi krimiroganivartakāni | ityasya vṛkṣasya sāratrayamupalabhyate | tatra mūlasārapatrasārayossāravattvenopalabhyamānatvāt tatsārau anubhavaikavedyau bhavataḥ| tatkṣīraṃ yāvadaṅgenopalabhyate tatprayojanakāryasya kṣīra dravyatvādeva tatpatrasaro mūlasāra iti vyapadeśamātram | tatprādhānyena vaktumaśakyatvāt ityasvarasādāha eketi |
ekadoṣanivartakaṃ yāvattadekasārakam || 55 ||
tattaṅgeṣu bhinnagandhābhāvatvāt bhinnarasābhāvatvācca ayamarkavṛkṣaḥ pavanaprakopanivartakaḥ tiktarasavaddravyatvāt | ityanumānapramāṇaṃ vaktavyam | tadvadeva vyāptirvaktavyā | tathā hi--yāvattiktarasavaddravyaṃ tatpavanaprakopanivartakam, pittanivartakasvādurasavaddravyasya pavanaprakopanivartakadravyatvāt | evamākārā vyāptiḥ | tayorvyāptau gṛhītāyāṃ satyāṃ viṣamavyāptireva syāditi nāśaṅkanīyam | yāvatpittanivartakasya pavanaprakopanivartakatvena yāvatpittanivartakadravyaṃ tāvatpavanaprakopanivartakam | pittanivartakasvādurasavaddravyasya pavanaprakopanivartakadravyatvāt | evamākāreṇa vyāptiḥ gṛhītuṃ śakyate | na tu tiktarasavaddravyamātraṃ pittanivartakam | tadyathā--viṣataruphalasāraṃ pittaprakopakārakaṃ sat pavanaprakopanivartakadravyaṃ tiktarasavaddravyatvāt ityevamākāreṇa vyāptigrahasya vaktuṃ sukaratvāt | na tatra bhogayogyādanadravyatvamupādhiḥ | bhogayogyādanayogyātiktarasavaddravyaṃ pittaprakopanivartakaṃ, na tiktarasamātram | viṣataruphalasāravaddravyaṃ tiktarasavaddravyamapi bhogayogyādanadravyaṃ na bhavatīti tatra nāvyāptiḥ | bhogayogyādanadravyaṃ yanna bhavati tatpittaprakopanivartakamapi na bhavati, yathā ghaṭaḥ | tiktarasavaddravyaṃ pavanaprakopanivartakaṃ na bhavati | ekarasavaddravyatvaṃ yatra bhāsate tatra anekasārasatve'pi etatsāramekadoṣaprakopanivartakaṃ bhavati | iti nāśaṅkanīyaṃ, bhogayogyātiriktarasavaddravyātiriktatiktarasavaddravyatvāt | tasmāt yasmindravye eka gandhavattvaṃ ekarasavattvaṃ ca bhāsate tatra anekasārasatve'pi tatsarvamekadoṣaprakopanivartakaṃ bhavati | tattadaṅgeṣu pratibhātasyaikasāratvāt tatsāra eva sarvāṅgavyāpaka iti tadekasāravaddravyamiti jñātavyamityarthaḥ ||
nanu abdravyādhikadravyopalabdhiryatra tatraiva madhurarasapratisvādyāvayavapradeśeṣu svādurasaḥ pratīyate | tatrābdravyāvayavādhikyamapi pratīyate | ekasārasya avayavaviśeṣasthānasatvābhāvādityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha--abdravyeti |
abdravyāvayavādhikyajanyasvādurasopalabdhiryadyattattatsārakam || 56 ||
abdravyāvayavādhikyaṃ yatra yatra pratīyate tatraiva svādurasādhikopalabdhiriti svādurasavattvaṃ jaladravyāvacchedakamityuktaṃ, taccintyam |pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti tanmadhye abdravyādhikyaśarīrabhūruhakumāryādi | tatra jalādhikyadravyamupalabhyate | tasyāvacchedakatvaṃ tiktarasavattvam | tasmāttatpavanaprakopakāri bhavati | tiktarasavadviṣataruphalasāraṃ pavanaprakopanivartakam | yatra jalavaddravyādhikyaṃ tatpavanaprakopanivartakaṃ svādurasam | tasyāvacchedakaṃ tiktarasam, jalavaddravyasyāvacchedakatvābhāvāt | jaladravyāvacchedakasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopakārako bhavati | jalavaddravyādhikarūpekṣukāṇḍasvāduraso'pi pavanaprakopanivartako bhavati | iti tātparye guṇābhāvāt, kiṃ tu pittaprakopanivartakaḥ | tasmājjaladravyaniṣṭhasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartako bhavati ityāśayavān | samādhatte-- ya iti | ye ye śarīrāssamadravyātmakāḥ tatra tatra kiṃciducitasvādurasaḥ || 57 ||
pavanaprakopanivartako bhavatīti tātparyam | jaladravyasya atiśītalaguṇavattvena pavananivartakadravyatvaṃ na bhavati | tarhi svādvamlalavaṇarasāḥ pavananivartakā iti pratipāditaśāstrasya kā gatiriti nāśaṅkanīyam | ghṛtaniṣṭhasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartakaḥ | kṣīrāṇi ca tathā | tailaguṇaśca | eraṇḍatailaguṇaśca | jaladravyāvayādhikyābhāvajanyaśarīrajātasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartako bhavatīti na tātparyam | ikṣukāṇḍaniṣṭhasvādurasasya adhikajalāvayavāvṛtaśarīratvena atiśītaladravyatvena tanniṣṭhasvādurasasya madhuratvāt tatpavanaprakopanivartakaṃ na bhavatīti tādṛśasvādurasaḥ pittaprakopanivartaka iti tātparyam ||
nanu yatrābdravyāvayavādhikyaṃ taccharīraniṣṭhasvādurasasya atimādhuryāt tatrādhikasvādurasavaddravyasya jalatvasya avacchedakatvāt tadvacchītasparśavattvamapi tasyāvacchedakaṃ bhavati | yatra śītasparśavattvaṃ tatra jalatvaṃ, iti vyāptergṛhītuṃ śakyatvāt | tacchītasparśaviśiṣṭajalaniṣṭhasvādurasaḥ kathaṃ pavanaprakopanivartako bhavet ? anubhavavirodhāt | tathāhi--śītasparśaviśiṣṭasvādurasavadravyaṃ pavanaprakopakārakam | tanniṣṭhapavanaprakopanivṛttyarthaṃ analasaṃyogajanyakāryasaṃskāraviśiṣṭajalaṃ pavanaprakopanivartakamiti tajjalānalasaṃparkavaśāt pavanajanakaśītalaguṇaṃ vihāya tasya analasaṃyogavaśāt tajjalaṃ pavananivartakaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ? tasmādyatrayatrābdravyāvayavādhikyaṃ tatratatra tathā atiśītalasparśavattva mapi saṃbhavati | tasmāttanniṣṭhasvādurasasya pavanaprakopanivartakatvaṃ kathaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- taditi |
tatpavananivartakaṃ subheṣajam || 58||
jaṅgamaśarīrajanyasvādurasa eva tacchabdārthaḥ | tajjanyasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartakaḥ | sarvaśarīrāṇi pāñcabhautikāni | yatra pārthivadravyādhikyagandhopalabdhirdṛśyate tatraiva tajjanyasvādurasasya pavanaprakopanivartakatvāt | jalamātraniṣṭhasvādurasasya na brūmaḥ, jalasya jaḍarūpadravyatvāt tasya śarīrābhāvāt | nanu uṣaḥpānavidhau śuddhajalasevanaṃ sakaladoṣanivartakamiti śāstre pratipāditam, anyathā tatpratipādakaśāstravirodhaḥ syāt | sarvaṃ jagat jalena vinā jīvituṃ na bhavati samartham | tasya svabhāvena "jīvanam" iti naighaṇṭukaiḥ nāma pratipāditam | tasmājjalaniṣṭhasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartako bhavatīti yaduktaṃ tanna rocata iti cet na, sthāvarāṇāṃ ca jīvanopakārakatvasya jaladravyasyocitatvāt jaladravyaṃ jīvanaṃ bhavet | jaṅgamānāṃ tu na tathā | teṣāmapi analasaṃyogajanyasaṃskārāt jātajalaniṣṭhasvādurasasya pavanaprakopanivartakatvamityuktatvāt | tatra uṣaḥpānavidhāvapi haṃsodakakarmakaraṇaviśiṣṭajalaṃ uṣaḥpānāvidhau praśastamityuktatvāt | itaratrāpi analasaṃyogajasaṃskārābiśaṣṭasya jalasya pavananivartakatve analasaṃyotvamupādhiḥ | yatrayatra analasaṃyogajasaṃskāraviśiṣṭajalatvaṃ bhāsate, tatratatra pavananivartakatvamapi saṃbhavati | śuddhaśītalaguṇaviśiṣṭajalasya pavanaprakopakārakatvaniyamāt | śaityaguṇaviśiṣṭajalaniṣṭhasvādurasaḥ na pavanaprakopanivartakaḥ | tatra śrutivacanaṃ pramāṇaṃ-- agnirhimasya bheṣajam" iti | tannivartakavidhau analasaṃyoga evopādhirityarthaḥ
nanu eva lavaṇarasaḥ abdravyajanyaḥ abvikāradravyatvāt, lavaṇasamudrasyaiva sāgaratvāt | lavaṇaṃ tajjanyaṃ tatpākajanyaṃ sumanoharaṃ sugandhaviśiṣṭāmlarasavaddravyaṃ pavanaprakopanivartakaṃ, apakvaṃ tu na tathā | sugandhaviśiṣṭaguṇābhāvadravyatvāt lavaṇarasasya śoṣakadravyasya kathaṃ pavananivartakatvaṃ kaphanivartakadravyatvāt kaṭurasavat, lavaṇarasasya kaṭurasāntaḥpātitvāt | tatkālaviśiṣṭalavaṇarasaḥ pavanavikāro na bhavati kaṭurasasya analadravyaguṇavattvāt | svādvamlalavaṇaviśiṣṭadravyāṇi pavanaprakopanivartakāni abdravyaniṣṭhaguṇavaddravyatvāt jambīraphalavat | lavaṇarasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartakaḥ abdravyaguṇavattvāt | na sarvajalaṃ svādurasavattvādeva, svādurasavaddravyebhyaḥ lavaṇadravyotpatterabhāvāt | lavaṇasamudrasya pārthivaśarīrasya saptadhātvātmakatvāt maccharīravat ||
nanu kecittaravo'nekasāravanto bhavanti | ekajātīyataruṣu anekabhūtāvayavādhikyasya vaktavyatvāt | tadyathā--śigrujātīyataruṣu bījasāravattvaṃ, patrasāravattvaṃ puṣpasāravattvaṃ mūlatvaksāravattvaṃ mūlasāravattvaṃ, etādṛśasāravantaḥ ete śigrutaravaḥ | yaddravyādhikyāpekṣayā ekasya anekasāravattvaṃ bhāsata ityāha--analeti |
analadravyāvayavādhikyajātādhikarūpopalambhakayāvadrasavaddravyaṃ tatsārakam || 59 ||
analabhūtāvayavādhikyadravyatvāt tadvattvaṃ yatra pratīyate tadvastu analādhikyadravyamiti | yatrayatrādhikarūpopalambhakayāvadyogadravyaṃ yāvadrūpavattvena nayanānandahetukaṃ bhavati tādṛśacākṣuṣadravyaṃ tattsārakam | kecinmanuṣyāṇāmayogyatve'pi tadyogyādanavattvāt jātā ye bahavassanti teṣāmapyupakārāya bhavanti, analabhūtāvayavādhikyadravyādhikyavanta iti tatrānekasāravattvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvāt tatsarvasāraṃ pavanaprakopanivartakaṃ bhavati | analadravyabhūtārthasya sarvebhyo bhūtebhyaḥ ādhikyāt tādṛśasārataravaḥ sarvabhūtānāmupakārāya bhavantītyarthaḥ ||
nanu yāvadbhūruhaḥ tattadāmayanivartakāḥ bhūruhadravyatvāt sampratipannavat deśasārauḍrala ? dravyabhedena tattajjantūnāṃ tattaddoṣavināśakāḥ | kecittaravaḥ jagajjīvanopakārakāḥ | tasya ekadā sarvapadārthajñānena bhavitavyam | tatsarvapadārthajñānābhāvena nirṇetumaśakyatvādityata āha--gaganeti |
gaganadravyādhikaśabdaguṇopalambhakatvaṃ pramāṇam ||
atra gaganadravyādhikaśabdatvaṃ nāma deśadehakāladravyakarmaṇāṃ yathāyathaṃ dravyayogakaraṇasya śāstrajñānābhāve'pi āptodīritaśabdassarvatrāpi pramāṇam | yasmindeśe yatra yatra yatpravartakaṃ tacchāstramiti vijñāya yadvyādhinivartakaṃ yaddravyaṃ tadeva tadbheṣajamiti tatra gaganādikaśabdajātakāryajñānaṃ pramāṇamiti | yadyapi yasya kasyacitpadārthasya kasyacidāmayasya pratīkāratvaṃ bahuvādisammatāptodīritaśabda eva yatra bhāsate tatra gaganādiguṇopalabdhireva pramāṇamityarthaḥ | evaṃ pañcabhūtodbhavasāravaddravyaṃ tattaddeśāvacchedakabhedavaśāt tattaddehāvacchedakabhedavaśāt tattatkālāvacche dakabhedavaśāt idaṃ sarvamālocya tatsarvamavalokya tatsāradravyaṃ vijñāya bheṣajaṃ kāryamiti | āyurvedapratipāditārthatattvajñānameva nirṇītamiti phalitārthaḥ || nanu sthāvarasthasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartakopayogyabheṣajadravyaṃ na bhavati, saptadhātvātmakaśarīrajanyatvābhāvāt ikṣukāṇḍaśarīravat | yassaptadhātumayaśarīrajanyasvāduraso na bhavati sa pavanaprakopanivartakopayogyabheṣajadravyaṃ na bhavati, saptadhātvātmakaśarīrajanyatvābhāvāt ikṣukāṇḍaśarīravat | yassaptadhātumayaśarīrajanyasvāduraso na bhavati sa pavanaprakopanivartakasvāduraso'pi na bhavati, yathā tālaphalajanyasvādurasaḥ | ikṣukāṇḍasthitasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopakārakaḥ kālapākajanyasvādurasavatvādeva analapākajanyatvābhāvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭa iti | evaṃ āmlarasaḥ pavanaprākepanivartako na bhavati | saptadhātumayaśarīrajanyasvādurasadravyatvābhāvāt | evaṃ lavaṇarasavaddravyaṃ pavanaprakopanivartakaṃ na bhavati, saptadhātvātmakaśarīrajanyatvābhāvāt kālapākajanyasvādurasavaddrabyatvācca sa pavanaprakopakārako bhavatītyarthaḥ | lavaṇarasavadbhūruhadravyaṃ nānilanivartakaṃ ūṣarabhujanyabhūruhadravyatvāt saṃpratipannavat | ityanumānapramāṇenāpi yāvatsaptadhātvātmakāni śarīrāṇi tadīyasvādurasavaddravyaṃ pavananivartakaṃ bhavatītyasvarasādāha-- tikteti |
tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasāḥ rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedobalapradāḥ
sarve ca bhūruhaḥ rasādicaturdhātvātmakāḥ tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasaguṇapradhānabalādhikadravyatvāt | śuklamajjāsthidhātvabhāvadravyatvena tatpoṣakasvādvamlalavaṇarasaguṇapradhānadravyatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu svādvamlalavaṇarasāḥ sthāvarasthāḥ | tanniṣṭhasvādurasānāṃ kā gatiḥ? rasapradhānatvena dravyaguṇān vaktuṃ yogyatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu sthāvarajaṅgamaśarīrāḥ prakṛtipuruṣātmakāḥ | ata eva saptadhātvāvṛtaṃ śarīramiti śarīrāvacchedakaṃ saptadhātvāvṛtatvamiti | tadbadeva ṣaḍrasātmakaṃ śarīramiti vaktuṃ sukaratvāt sthāvaraśarīrāścaturdhātvātmakā ityasya vinigamakābhāvāt śarīratvāvacchedakāvacchinnasya sarvatrāpi samānatvāt ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha-- recaketi |
recakapūrakadhārakebhyo vikasati || 62 ||
recakapūrakapavanadhāraṇāt tattadvarṇādhiṣṭhitapadmānāṃ nimīlanomīlanena tattadvarṇabodho jāyate | tasmādidaṃ śarīraṃ saptadhātvāvṛtaṃ varṇoccāraṇahetubhūtadravyatvāt | te tu na tathā | tāvadayogyatvāt caturdhātvātmakena ardhaśarīraravattvācca tatkorakapuṣpapatralatādayaḥ nimīlanonmīlanaṃ ca kurvanti bahiḥ pavanasaṃparkavaśāt | recakadhārakātvamātraṃ vyapadiśanti | tasmāccalanātmakaṃ karma kurvate |
nanu jaṅgamaśarīraṃ śarīrāntarajanakaṃ sthāvarotpādakahetubhūtadravyatvāt | evaṃ vṛkṣaḥ vṛkṣāntarajanakaḥ sthāvarotpādakahetubhūtātmakatvasya ca ubhayorapi samānatvāt | ata eva sthāvaraśarīrāṇāmapi saptadhātvātmakatvaṃ kathaṃ niṣidhyata ityasvarasādāha-- liṅgeti |
liṅgayonisaṃyogācca prajayā paśubhiḥ prajananaṃ prajāyate || 63 ||
liṅgasya prajājananahetubhūtatvāt sthāvarāṇāṃ ca prajājananakāle vṛkṣāntarasaṃyogakāryasya adṛṣṭatvāt, sthāvaraśarīratvāt jaṅgamaśarīravat sthāvarāṇāṃ vaktumayogyatvāt | ata evoktaṃ rasādicaturdhātvāvṛtaṃ śarīramiti | nanu "idaṃ strīvacanaṃ, idaṃ puruṣavacanaṃ" iti tayorbhedapratītiḥ | tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārāghaṭanāt jātapavanaḥ smerāsyadoṣagatijātaśabdaḥ strīṇāṃ yonivivaraṃ praviśya strījātijñāpakavacanaṃ ca | jaṅghāmeḍhrapradeśapadmagatasarandhrabhāgasya alpadvāratvāt puruṣaliṅgasthitapadmavivaramabhyupetya varṇātmakaśabdaḥ tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārāghaṭanājjātapavanabhedasya sarveṣāmapi samānatvāt tatsāmagrīviśiṣṭastrījanoccāritaśabdānāṃ ajādhikyasvarātmakatvāt śabdā upalabhyante | tadvadeva puruṣajanyatālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpāradikasya samānatve'pi strījanoccāritaśabdaḥ puruṣajanoccāritaśabdādbhinna iti kathamupalabhyate ? śabdajñānasāmagryāḥ samānatvādityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha--pṛṣṭheti |
pṛṣṭhajaṅghāśiśnopasthadeśabhedātsvarādayaḥ prabhavanti
jaṅghāśiśnapadmapradeśaṃ samprāpya śabdaḥ prabhavati | ityetattu vivicya jñāpyate--jaṅghāśiśnapradeśagatisarandhrabhāgamabhyupetya bahirnissaran san halādivarṇādhikyabodhakatayā varṇātmakaśabdaśśrūyate | ayaṃ puruṣaśabda iti jñātuṃ śakyate | evaṃ strījanānāṃ tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārasāmagryāṃ smerāsyadoṣagatijātaśabdaḥ karṇapradeśavivarabhāgamabhyupetya pṛṣṭhopasthapadmagatasirārandhrabhāgamupetya tasyā yonivivarādhārāt bahirnirgatassan acsvarādivarṇādhikatayā śabdaśśrūyate | sa śabdasstrījanoccāritaśabda iti bhedaḥ pratīyate| tattajjātiviśiṣṭaśarīrā bahavassanti | tattaccharīrāvacchedakabhedāt varṇabhedaḥ | tattatpṛṣṭhayonipadmapradeśagatasarandhradeśā eva tattadvarṇabhedajñāpakā iti vaktavyam | evaṃ puruṣajanānāmapi bhedo jñātavyaḥ|
nanu śvāsocchvāsānilātmakakuṇḍalyādhārabhūtakaṭipradeśagatapadmaṃ sarveprāṇādhārakaṃ śirassthitāmṛtapravāhālavālopajīvyarūpatvāt amṛtapravāhālavālaparipūrṇakāryahetubhūtatvāt | tasmādyogadhāraṇavaśāt dhātupoṣaṇaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-kaṭīti |
kaṭipradeśagataṃ kāyādhārakam || 65 ||
kaṭipradeśagataṃ padmaṃ catussirāvṛtaṃ ovarṇārṇotpādakabhūtapāṇipādābhyāṃ calanātmakakāryahetubhūtatvena gatāgatopāyakāryakārakayāthātmyakatvāt tadeva śarīradhārakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | na tu prāṇādhārahetukapavanamātrādhārakatvāt sarvavarṇādhārakapadmavat |
nanu āyurvedārthajñānaṃ caturvidhapuruṣārthasādhakayogadhāraṇakāryahetukaṃ īśvarasākṣātkārānubhavasya hetubhūtatvāt | īśvarassuprīto bhūtvā tasya manovāñchādikaṃ phalameva dadāti | tasmādyogasādhanaṃ vihāya tattadvirasadravyādanena taddhetubhūtāmayānsampādya tannivartakakāryakāraṇena kimadhikaphalaṃ labhyata ityāha-- nābheriti |
nābheradhassthitaṃ kuṇḍalyādibhūtaṃ pañcasahasrasirāvṛtaṃ śatadalapadmaṃ prajāyate || 66 ||
nābheradhassthitaṃ jaṭharānalapadmaṃ tayormadhyagataṃ bhavati | tatpañcasahasrasirāvṛtam | sarvavarṇādhārakapadmānāmādibhūtam | saptadhātvādhārātmakam | pañcabhūtātmakaṃ ca | tacchatadalapadmaṃ aviruddharasadravyādanena avikāraṃ bhūtvā tattatsāradravyaiḥ śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtaṃ muhurmuhuḥ prajanayat vartate | vikārābhāvavatpavanagatyā tacchatadalapadmasantarpaṇena saptadhātavaḥ puṣṇanti | rasāyanādanena vā, rogadhāraṇādvā, pathyānnādanena vā , śarīramavikāraṃ kurvat bahukālajīvanaṃ labhyata ityarthaḥ | ataśśarīrasaṃrakṣaṇameva sarvapuruṣārthasādha kam | nīrogaśarīraṃ sarvānandahetukam | ānandajñānahetukam | śabdasparśarūparasagandhāḥ ātmaviṣayajñāpakāḥ indriyādhārabhūtatvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭa iti | idaṃ nīrogaśarīraṃ yogadhāraṇasādhanam, tattvajñānamanoviṣayakātmādhiṣṭhitaśarīratvāt caitanyādhiṣṭhitaśarīravat | sarvapadārthatattvajñānaṃ mokṣahetukaṃ, heyārthopāyavyatiriktopādeyajñānahetukatvāt | idaṃ nīrogaśarīraṃ sarvapadārthajñānānubhavapūrvakaṃ roganivartakadravyādanasādhyajñānapūrvakatvāt | āyurvedacatuḥpraśnapratipāditārthaḥ etadanumānacatuṣṭayādhīnaḥ uktaśāstrapratipāditārthajñānapūrvakatvāt ||
ityāyurvedasya caturthapraśnasya bhāṣyaṃ yogānandanātha-
kṛtaṃ suprasiddhaṃ mahājanasammataṃ lokopakārakaṃ
pratisūtravyākhyānapūrvakaṃ ayurvedabhāṣyaṃ
sampūrṇam.
<5>
āyurvede pañcamapraśnaḥ.
guhāntaranivāsinīṃ gurukṛpākaṭākṣodayaiḥ
sahāyamanucintayansarasijeṣu ṣaṭsu kramāt |
sahāsamabhinartayansatatamaṣṭavāgvādinīṃ
mahātripurasundarīmiha muhurmuhurbhāvaye ||
nanu nābheradhassthitaśatadalapadmaṃ prāṇādhārakaṃ sarvendriyādhārabhūtaṃ ca | tatpañcasahasrasirāvṛtapadmaṃ śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtamāhṛtya pavanena pūritatvāt āyurabhivṛddhikārakamityuktam | atastrayo doṣā avikṛtāścet tatpadmameva sakalaśreyasāṃ hetubhūtamiti vaktuṃ śakyate | śuddharasavaddravyādanaṃ doṣāṇāṃ samyaggatikārakaṃ bhavati | virasadravyādanaṃ vikārakārakaṃ bhavati, tattatpradeśasthitadoṣādivijñānaṃ vinā tadjñānaviṣayakajñānagrāhakatvasyāsambhavāt | śuddharasavaddravyaguṇāḥ śuddhadhātuvardhakāḥ | viruddhadravyaguṇāvikārakārakāḥ | ataeva doṣasthānaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ ca vyācaṣṭe--nābheriti |
nābheradhastādūrdhvaṃ hṛdi sthitavātapittakaphadoṣāssamastā asamastā asamagrā anyonyaṃ te āmayotpādakāḥ || 1 ||
nābheradhassthitaḥ pavanaḥ | tadūrdhvapradeśaḥ nābherūrdhvasthānam | tatpittadoṣasyākaro bhavati | tadupari hṛdayapradeśaḥ kaphadoṣasya sthānaṃ bhavati | etatsarvaṃ sarvapadārthaguṇāguṇajñānaṃ ca doṣasvarūpajñānahe tubhūtam | ato doṣādeḥ jñānamāvaśyakamiti | doṣādivijñānaṃ vinā tattadāmayānāṃ nirṇetumaśakyatvāt | tathāhi-- sa kīdṛśo vāyuḥ ? kuṇḍalinīsthitaśvāsocchavāsarūpamupapannassan dvividhaprakārako bhavati | sa eva vāyuḥ prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānabhedaḥ upādhibhedātpañcavidha upahūyate | sa eva āyussarvasirābhyantaragatassan tattatpadmādhiṣṭhitasarvavarṇānāhṛtya tālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārāghaṭṭanāt jātapavanasya upādhibhedavaśāt tattatpavanagatiśaktyā sarvavarṇān tattatpadmamavagamya tattatpadmasthitavarṇasamūhātmakaśabdaṃ śrotrapradeśaṃ gamayan tattacchabdārthaviṣayakajñānajanyajñānaṃ karoti | yatra yatra virasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati anilātmakaśvāsocchavāsagatiḥ sirārandhramārgamupagatasya pavanasya vikāragatiṃ karoti | yatra vikāragatijñānaṃ tatra tadativedanāviṣayakam | tattatsirāgatapavanaḥ tattatpadmaparyantaṃ viruddhagatyā varṇagrāhakaścet tadvarṇaṃ śrotravivaramupetaṃ paragrāhyaṃ yadā bhavati tadā vivarṇaśabdātmakapadaṃ śrūyate | yāvadvarṇagrāhakapavanamabhyupetya doṣamārgamupagamya pavanaścarati, tadā samastadoṣā asamarthā bhavanti | sa eva vāyuḥ virasādanādajīrṇe jāte sati alpamalpaṃ carati | so'samagragatiriti | teṣāṃ pavanānāmanyeṣāṃ saṃkaragatiryatra dṛśyate sa eva rogo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu doṣatrayanirūpaṇe dhātupoṣakapratipādakaśāstraṃ na pratīyate | te duṣṭāssantaḥ dhātudūṣakā bhavanti | te tu aduṣṭāścet yathāpūrvamanatikramya sthityupakārakā bhavanti | ato doṣanivāraṇena kiṃ phalaṃ labhyata ityasvarasādāha-- yatreti |
yatrasthā ye rasāstattadudbhūtajātāste dhātupoṣakāḥ ||
yatra yāvadbhūtādhikyajātayāvaddravyādanaṃ tattadbhūtādhīnatattannivartakadhātupoṣakam | yāvadudbhūtabhūruharasāḥ yāvadudbhūtabhūtajātadhā tuśoṣakapoṣakāḥ yāvadudbhūtabhūruhavirasādanajātaroganivartakatatsajātīyarasavaddravyatvāt | yāvadudbhūtabhūruhajātarasāt taditaraśarīrabhūtādhīnadhātuśoṣakaruṅnivartakaṃ bheṣajaṃ, ityanumānaracanā kāryahetukavyāptigrahavaśāt tannivartakanivartyayoḥ vyāptigrahe sati sarvatrāpyuttaratrāpi tathā vaktuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu doṣaprakopanivṛttidvārā rasādīnāṃ dhātupoṣakatvaṃ vaktavyam | yāvadbhūtaniṣṭharasāḥ yāvadbhūtāvayavajanyaroganivartakā iti pūrvasūtre pratipāditam | taccintyam | kecidrasāḥ dhātupoṣakāḥ, kecidrasāḥ dhātudūṣakā iti vaktuṃ śakyate | viruddharasādajīrṇe jāte sati tattadvidhicoditarogā āvirbhavanti | te tu doṣānugatāssantaḥ dhātugatāścet teṣu tadvikārā āvirbhavanti | yadā aviruddharasādanena doṣā aduṣṭāssantaḥ dhātūnāmavikāraṃ kurvanti, śuddhadoṣāśca dhātūn prāṇāṃśca saṃrakṣanti | te tu duṣṭāścet dhātuprāṇendriyaghātakā bhavanti | rasādīnaṃ doṣaprakopakārakatvaṃ doṣaprakopanivartakatvaṃ ca vaktumucitam, ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya ṣaḍrasānāṃ ca tattadguṇān varṇayati-- dvīti |
dvirasārtharasahīnārthahīnarasavānanilanivārakaḥ ||
dvirasārtha iti svādvamlalavaṇaviśiṣṭadravyaṃ pavanaprakopanivartakam | svādurasavaddravyaṃ pavananivartakaṃ na bhavati, ikṣurasakadalīphalanārikelatālaphaleṣu ca vyabhicārāt | svādurasavattvaṃ āmlarasavattvaṃ ca yasmindrabye yadā pratīyate tatra tadrasavadviśiṣṭadravyaṃ pavananivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | kevalalavaṇarasavaddravyaṃ pavananivartakaṃ na bhavati, ūṣarabhūmijanyalavaṇarasasya pava nanivartakatvāsambhavāt | sāmudrajaṃ tu na tathā | svādurasatvaṃ jaladravyasyāvacchedakam | tathā sati sāmudrajalavaṇaṃ tu jalasya svabhāvaniṣṭhasvādurasasya tatraivāntarbhāvāt lavaṇarasaviśiṣṭabhūdravyeṇa jalaniṣṭhasvādurasasyābhipūritatvāt | tarhi tathā sati atsti cedupalabhyeta | anupalabhyamānatvāt tannāstīti na vaktavyam | lavaṇaviśiṣṭayogyadravyaṃ vyañjanādidravye rucitayā pratīyate | svāduruciretatsvādutayā pratīteti vyavahartuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ | kāścidbhuvaḥ lavaṇarasaviśiṣṭā bhavanti | kāścidbhuvaḥ svādurasaviśiṣṭā bhavanti, tattadbhūmijalakūpādau tathā dṛṣṭatvāt | yatrayatra lavaṇarasopalabdhiḥ tatratatra tāddaśabhūmijanyatvaṃ tatropādhiḥ| svādujalaviśiṣṭakūpajanyajalaguṇasvādurasasya pratītatvāt tatra lavaṇarasajanakabhūmitvābhāvaviśiṣṭabhūjanyalavaṇatvasya tatra vidyamānatvāt | tatra svādurasapratīteḥ vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | saindhavadeśajanyalavaṇarasaviśiṣṭadravyasya śuddhabhūjanyatyāt āmlarasavattvaṃ bhūmeravacchedakam | tatratatra dravyajanyatvādeva pavananivartakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ | tatra dvirasārthabhūtadvayadravyaguṇaviśiṣṭatvāt tatra dvirasārthapravṛttiḥ pavananivartiketyarthaḥ |
rasahīnārtha iti rasahīnadravyaṃ pāradādidravyam | tatra auṣadhādirasānāṃ sarvadā sarvakāle'pratītatvāt | tatra pavananivartakatvaṃ sarvajanasiddhaṃ sarvaśāstramatasiddham pārada ityupalakṣaṇam | raso'varalohādisādhāraṇamahārasa iti pāradaśabdena sarvaṃ saṃgṛhyate | asminpāradadravye rasādaya āvirbhūtā bhavanti | hīnarasavattvaṃ tvanyadravyasya virekakāritvaṃ suprasiddham | tasya guṇasya pavananivartakatvasya suprasiddhatvāditi bahavaḥ ||
sūtrasthānavacanasyāpīdameva tātparyam | tathāhi avyabhicaritatvaṃ vyāpteravacchedakam | yatra svādurasavattvaṃ tatra pavananivartakatvaṃ ityevamākāreṇa vyāptigrahānantaraṃ anumitau pravṛttiḥ | ikṣukāṇḍasthitasvādurasaḥ pavanaprakopanivartakaḥ, jalabhūmyātmakāvayavagrastatvāt urvārukaphalarasavat | etadanumānenāpi ekasvādurasasya pavananivartakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ |
nanu āmlarasavaddravye kālapākavaśāt mādhurye jāte sati sa āmlarasa eva bhavati | virasadravyādanaṃ vikārakārakam | tadvadeva dvirasavaddravyamiti vaktuṃ śakyate | sa pavananivartakarasaḥ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha-- madhureti |
madhurātimadhuraṃ kramādvātapittaghnau kaphaṃ kurutaḥ ||
madhuratvaṃ nāma madhurarasavaddravyātiriktasvādurasavaddravyaṃ pavananivartakam tatkālavaśāt madhurībhūtaṃ bhavati | tatsvādurasavaddravyaṃ pittaprakopanivartakaṃ, tadvirasavaddravyaṃ na bhavati | ekarasajātiviśiṣṭadravyatvāt ekarasadravyameveti | āmlarasavaddravyatvajātiviśiṣṭadravyaṃ kālapākajopādhikaṃ madhurarasavaddravyaṃ bhavatīti yadā pratīyate tadā tadviruddharasavaddravyaṃ na bhavati, tadyogyakālātītadravyabhāvadravyatvāt | tatra virasavaddravyātītakālatvamupādhiritimadhurarasavaddravyaṃ tasmādevātimadhurarasavaddravyaṃ pittaprakopanivartakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | āmlarasavaddravyajātiviśiṣṭaikarasavaddravyasya pavananivartakatvamātre caritārthatvāt pavanavyatiriktadoṣanivartakadravyābhāvadravyatvāt ityata āha--āmleti |
āmlātyāmlau ca tathā || 5 ||
āmlarasajātiviśiṣṭadravyasya pākakālavaśāt atyāmlarase jāte sati tasya anyadoṣanivartakatvaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate |
ekarasajātīyadravyatvāt | tasmātpavananivartakatvameva suprasiddhamiti tātparyam |
lavaṇarasavaddravyasyāpi tathātvādityata āha--lavaṇeti |
lavaṇaṃ pavanaṃ hanti | kaphapittakaraṇam || 6 ||
tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasapratīterevābhāvāt kṣāravaddravyatvācca pittaprakopakārakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu lavaṇarasasya pavanaprakopakārakatvaṃ vaktumucitam | kaphaprakopakārakamiti vaktuṃ nopayujyate | kṣāradravyatvācca pavananivartakaṃ bhavedityasvarasādāha-- hīneti |
hīnādhikalavaṇarasaḥ kaphapavanaṃ hanti pittaṃ kurute || 7 ||
svādurasaguṇavaddravyajanyarasaḥ lavaṇarasaḥ hīnalavaṇaraso bhavati | tato'dhikalavaṇarasaḥ sindhudeśodbhavalavaṇarasaḥ amlarasādhikyadravyajanyatvāt | baḍavānalasaṃyogavajjalajanyalavaṇarasadravyatvātkaphanivartakatvaṃ brūmaḥ |
nabha ādityabhūtaśarīrajanyatiktarasasya kaphadoṣaprakopanivartakatvaṃ vaktuṃ nabho'niladravyajanyatvādeva tiktarasasya pavanaprakopakārakatvaṃ kaphapittaroganivartakatvaṃ ca vyācaṣṭe-- tikta iti |
tiktaḥ kaphapittaṃ hanti mārutaṃ kurute || 8 ||
tikta iti nabho'nilabhūjanyatiktarasaḥ ākāśasya niravayavadravyatvāt, vāyośca niravayavadravyatvāt ubhābhyāṃ jātaśarīraṃ tiktarasavadbhavati | tiktarasadravyādanaṃ kaphaprakopanivṛttiṃ karoti | yatpratipāditaṃ tatsarvaṃ samyageva pratipāditam |
pittaṃ hantītyasya anilabhūtādhikyagaganabhūtasaṃyogajanyatvāt rasagrahaṇīkalāsandhukṣaṇapavanabhūtagaganabhūtasaṃyogājjātatiktarasaḥ ubhayorapi sarvāgamavyāpyadravyatvāt pittaprakopanivartakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ |
nanu tasya tiktarasasya pavanaprakopakārakatvaṃ na saṃbhavati svajanyatvāt | svajanakaśarīracyutanimbakuṣṭhabhūnimbaśṛṅgiśārkakarkāṭakakuṭajapaṭolakumāribhūtalatatalapoṭukarkandhūdevaguggulādyāḥ tiktarasapadārthāḥ pavanaprakopakāryanivartakadravyatvāt bhogayogyadravyatvācca jaṭharāgniprajvalanakāryāṇāṃ hetubhūtadravyatvācca | sarve te pavanaprakopanivartakā iti vijñātatvātkathaṃ "mārutaṃ kurute" ityuktamiti nāśaṅkanīyam | tiktarasavirasavaddravyādanaṃ pavanaprakopaṃ kuruta iti tātparyāt | virasadravyādanaṃ nāma viruddharasadravyādanam | tadeva viṣavaddravyādanaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
analānilabhūtāvayavādhikyajātakaṭurasaḥ kaphaprakopanivartaka ityāha--kaṭviti |
 kaṭu kaphaṃ hanti | vātapittahetukam || 9 ||
kaṭurasasya kaphanivartakatvaṃ suprasiddhameva | pittaprakopakārakatvamapi suprasiddhameva | kaṭukāḥ śuddharasāḥ kapharoganivṛttikārakāḥ pittaprakopaṃ ca kurvate | ityetaddvayaṃ suprasiddham | analānilabhūtajanyakaṭurasadravyatvena kaphaprakopaṃ nivartayati, pittaprakopaṃ ca kurute | pavanaprakopanivṛttiṃ ca kurute |
śuṇṭhī rāsnā karṇagranthiḥ cavyaṃ mūlakaṃ tamālapatraṃ ardhakaṃ laśunaṃ hiñgu etāni kaṭurasadravyāṇi bhavanti | pavanaprakopa kārakāṇīti sūtrasiddhamapi etadvirasādanaṃ pavanaprakopaṃ karotīti tātparyam | ata eva sūtrāntaraṃ vyācaṣṭe--kaṭuketi |
kaṭukātikaṭukaḥ kaphānilaṃ hanti | pittaṃ kurute || 10
śuddhakaṭurasaḥ pittaprakopaṃ kuruta ityarthaḥ |
kaṣāyaḥ kaphapittaṃ hanti | mārutaṃ kurute | hīnādhikakaṣāyaḥ kaphamārutaṃ hanti | pittaṃ kurute || 11
kaṣāyaḥ kaphapittaṃ hanti | mārutaṃ kurute' ityasya sutrasya tadvadeva vyākhyānaṃ yojanīyam |
nanu ṣaḍrasadravyāṇāṃ madhye ekarasadravyasya tattadroganivṛttikāryaṃ tattatkarma tattatphalaṃ ca pratipāditam | idānīṃ bahurasadravyāṇāṃ yogakāryasya tattadroganivṛttireva phalaṃ yatra dṛśyate tatra anekarasavaddravyāṇāṃ samūhakāryakārakatvaṃ bhavatīti matvā ekaroganivṛrttyarthamanekarasadravyāṇāmanekatvayaṃtropalabhyate, tatra bahurasavaddravyāṇāṃ ekaroganivartakatvasyāyogyatvāt, tattadvyādhīnāṃ nivartakatvamanekadravyāṇāmiti tattadvyādhinivartakatvāvacchedakatvasya vaktumāyogyatvāt, ekaikadravyasya ekaroganivṛrtakaguṇatvāvacchedakasya bhinnatvāt "bahukalpaṃ bahuguṇam" iti nyāyaśāstramavalambya bahudravyayogakāryakārakatvamuktaṃ cet atiprasaṅgaḥ syāt | ekadravyamekaroganivartakamityarthasya bahuśodṛṣṭatvāt | malanirodhanahetujanyaroge ekasyaiva recakadravyasya tannivartakasāmarthyasya bahudravyeṣu bahuśo dṛṣṭatvāt | tatrāpi bahudravyakalpanāgauravāt ekadravyasyaiva ekaroganivartakasāmarthyasya bahuśo dṛṣṭatvāt ityasvarasādāha-- doṣa iti |
doṣe tridoṣahetvanyahetukavikāraviruddhayāvadrasaikabhāvātmakaikarasādiḥ nivartakaḥ || 12 ||
tridoṣahetvanyahetuketyatra tridoṣāṇāṃ vikārābhāvaprāgbhāvaparipālanārthaṃ aviruddharasavaddravyādanasya phalaṃ aniṣṭaparihāra eva bhavati | doṣāṇāṃ svabhāvagatikārakarasavaddravyaṃ doṣāhetukam | doṣapravaṇakāryavadrasavirasadravyādanaṃ vikārakārakam | tadviruddhakāryahetubhūtayāvadrasavaddravyādanam tadyāvadrasavaddravyaikabhāvātmakabahurasavaddravyādanayogyayogakaraṇaṃ ekavyādhinivartakam |
kvāthyadravyayogyayogajanyarasavaddravyādanaṃ tattadroganivartakam | bahudravyayogyayogajanyacūrṇīkṛtadravyarasavaddravyādanaṃ tattadroganivartakam ||
tailaghṛtalehyarasāyanādyaiḥ anekaikadravyajanyayogyayogakṛtijanyasaṃskārayuktānekarasadravyādanaṃ yāvadrasaikabhāvātmakaṃ yattadekarasavaddravyaṃ bhavati | tadanekadravyāṇāṃ ekaguṇaphalaṃ prayacchati | kaliyuge saṅghe śaktirbhavatīti, na tu ekasya dravyasya roganivartakaśaktiḥ, kaliyuge abhibhūtatvāt saṅghe śaktirbhavatīti pratipāditam | brāhmaṇāśīrvādavat | ekabrāhmaṇavadanoccāritavākye śakterabhibhūtatvāt teṣāṃ saṅghe śaktirbhavatītivat | tattadrasavadbhūruhāṇāṃ ekasyaiva dṛḍhataraśakterabhāvāt saṅghe śaktirbhaviṣyatīti bahudravyamelanaṃ tattannivartakakāryaphalam | bahuyogajanyasaṃskāra eva tatkratuphalaṃ prayacchati | asminkaliyuge adharmabāhulyāt vyādhayo bahavassaṃbhavanti | tataśśarīrāṇāmalpatvāt | tannivartakāni kvacitphalaṃ prayacchanti | kvacitsakalasāmagryāmasatyāmapi tat tatphalaṃ prayacchati | ata evoktaṃ--
tacchāntirauṣadhairdānaiḥ japahomārcanādibhiḥ | iti |
asminyuge bahusāmagrībhiravaśyaṃ bhāvyamityarthaḥ ||
nanu idaṃ dravyaṃ etaddravyādanajātaroganivartakamiti, ayaṃ rogaḥ tannivartakakaidravyasādhyaḥ tannivartakaikadravyavattvāt ityanumānavidhayā prāmāṇye sthite sati tatra prajāyate | sā pramā samarthapravṛttisādhiketi tayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaṃ niścitya tatra pravartate | tatra vyādhinivartakadravyasandehe sati tannivṛttikāryaviṣaye pravṛtterabhāvāt, tayorvyāptigrahasya sutarāmaśakyatvāt, ayaṃ roganivartaka iti niścayajñāne sati tatra pravṛttiḥ, niścitārthe saṃśayābhāvāt, tanniścayasya taddhetutvāt | ekapadārthasya tannivartakatvasandehe sati padārthāntarasaṃgrahapravṛttau atiprasaṃgassyādityasvarasādāha--rasādīti |
rasādidravyasārajāssakalāmayanivartakāḥ || 13
rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedomajjāśuklādidhātūnāṃ vikāre jāte sati taddravyaṃ tannivartakamiti niścitya tatra virecanavidhiḥ kārya iti jñāne jāte sati harītakīprayogavidhiḥ kriyate | tādṛśaharītakyadanena tadāgatamalanissaraṇaṃ jāyate | tatra tanniścaye spaṣṭe tvaritavyāptigrahasya sukaratvāt | tatra harītakītyupalakṣaṇam | tadrasaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyajātimātrameva vivakṣitam | vikāragrastadoṣāṇāṃ yāvaddhātusaṃcāritvaṃ yatra pratīyate tāvaddhātugataroganivṛttyarthaṃ anekadravyāṇāṃ yogyayogakaraṇaṃ vidhiriti tatra vidhīyate | tannivartakayāvaddravyeṇa rasavaddravyādanaṃ vivakṣitam | tannivartakadravyamātrasya tannivartakatvāvacchedakatvamiti tayorvyāptigrahaṇānantaraṃ samarthapravṛ tterapi niścitatvāt | tatsārajā rasāḥ sakalāmayanivartakā iti sūtrārthassamyakpratipādita ityarthaḥ |
nanu yāvannivartakānekayogyadravyāṇi bahūni santi | tānyeva santu bheṣajāni | tattatsthitānekarasavaddravyasamūhātmakayāvaddravyaṃ mahārasaṃ, tādṛśarasavaddravyaṃ mahārasavaddravyaṃ bhavati, anekarasavaddravyasamūhatvāt bahūdīritaśabdasamūhavat ityanumānena bahūnāṃ śabdānāmekīkaraṇavidhau yathā mahāghoṣo jāyate tadvadeva bahudravyasajātīyamelanavidhau bahuguṇaṃ vidhatte | kvāthacūrṇatailaghṛtarasāyanādau yogyadravyāṇāṃ ekīkaraṇapākajavidhau sakalāmayanivṛttiretadjñānapūrvikā tattadroganivartakarasavaddravyajñānapūrvikā anekarasavadviśiṣṭadravyasamūhatvāt | anyathā tattadbhūruhāṇāṃ pratyekaguṇapratipādakaśāstrasya vyarthatāpatteḥ | tathātve bahudravyāṇāṃ ekarasavattvāsaṃbhavāt rasānāmeva guṇapradhānatvasya suprasiddhatvāt | tadekaprakāratvena tadavacchedakamekameveti niścetumaśakyatvādityasvarasādāha--adoṣeti |
adoṣadhātupoṣakamaneka sārakam || 14 ||
tattaddravyaniṣṭhadoṣanivṛttyarthaṃ tattadvidhicoditasaṃskāravaśāt adoṣadravyaṃ bahusāravadbahurasaguṇavatsaṃbhavati yattadeva adoṣadravyamiti tadrasavattvaṃ tattaddhātupoṣakayāvaddravyasyāvacchedakaṃ bhavati | taddravyamekamapi anekarasavatsat tattadbahuguṇaṃ prayacchatītyarthaḥ |
nanu ṣaḍrasavaddravyāṇāṃ dhātupoṣakatvaṃ yadā sambhavati, tadā teṣāṃ rasānāṃ pavanādiprakopakārakatvasya tathā teṣāṃ dhātūnāmapi poṣakatvasya vaktumaśakyatvāt te rasāstadā aprakopakārakā bha veyuḥ, teṣāṃ dhātupoṣakatvasya vaktuṃ sukaratvāt ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya yadrasebhyo vyādhayassambhavanti taireva te nivartanta iti tadubhayamapi vyācaṣṭe-- pakveti|
pakvāmāśayagāḥ pavanādyāḥ svādukaṣāyakaṭukaistiktāmlalavaṇaiḥ kālapākajayogyaiḥ dhāraṇarecakātmakadravyairvividhaiḥ dvividhāmayāḥ pravardhante || 15 ||
pakvāmāśayagāḥ pavanādyā iti pakvāśayamalāśayagatā doṣāḥ lavaṇaiḥ svādukaṣāyakaṭukaiḥ kevalaṃ svādurasadravyādanaṃ kaphaprakopahetukaṃ bhavati, svādurasajanyaḥ pavanaprakopo jāyate, tatpavanaprakopanivṛttyarthaṃ lavaṇāmlarasadravyādanaṃ kuryāt | kevalaṃ kaṭurasadravyādanaṃ pittaprakopajanakam | pittaprakopanivṛttyarthaṃ svādurasadravyādanaṃ kuryāt tiktarasadravyādanaṃ ca | ubhayaṃ pittanivartakaṃ bhavatītyartha ityevaṃprakāreṇa āyurvedapraṇetṛparabrahmatātparyaṃ varṇitamityarthaḥ | tathāhi--atikaṭurasadravyādanena pavanaprakopahetukarūkṣaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyādanāt pavanaprakopo jāyate | kaṭurasadravyādanāt rūkṣaprakopataijasaguṇajanyagrahaṇīkalāprakopasya dṛṣṭatvāt | tena pavanaprakope sati grahaṇīkalāyāḥ sandhukṣaṇakārakasvabhāvavattvena pavanaḥ atiprajvalanahetubhūto bhavati | athavā atimāndhyakārako vā bhavati | tasmātkaṭurasadravyādanaṃ pittaprakopakārakaṃ rūkṣaguṇaviśiṣṭapavanaprakopahetubhūtakaṭurasavaddravyatvāt | tena grahaṇīkalāyā eva pittadravyatvāt tayā anilasamagatyā grahaṇīkalā śuddhā satī prajvalitā bhavati | tadviṣamagatyā tadvikāro bhūtvā analaṃ mandaṃ karoti |tenājīrṇarogāścāvirbhavantīti parabrahmaṇa āśayaśca varṇita ityarthaḥ ||
kālapākadravyajarogastatsarvarecanaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ | sā kalā āmanivartakāntaraguṇaviśiṣṭā | āmakārakadravyaṃ sakalāmayahetukam | etadguṇaviśiṣṭadravyādanādāmayāḥ pravardhante |
nanu vātapittakaphadoṣaireva tadrogotpādakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ tattadrogā āvirbhavantītyuktam | taccintyaṃ, tathāhi--
pañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti śarīrasaṃrakṣaṇaṃ śarīrasyaiva sarvarogālayatvāccharīrameva yāvadudbhūtātmakayāvaddravyāntarbhūtāvayavādhikyahitakāryajātaguṇakārakaṃ yāvadbhūtāvayavābhivardhakatatsajātīyarasadravyādanajanyatvāditi tattadbhūtaniṣṭharogāṇāṃ tattadbhūtāvayavādhikyajātakāryahetukatvam |
bhūguṇau gurumandau | snigdhahimāvāpaḥ | ślakṣṇasāndratarā'gneḥ | mṛdusthitā gurumārutaḥ | sūkṣmaviśadā viyataḥ | viruddhaviṣayakāḥ pratikārakāḥ | deśadehakāladravyakarmaṇāṃ yathāyogo yāvadrogaghātakaḥ || 16 ||
bhvavayavādhikyaguṇopalambhakadehagauravaguṇopalabdhiḥ mandaguṇaśca jāyate | ubhāvapi bhūguṇau bhavataḥ | tat pārthivāvayavādhikyopalambhakapañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti taddravyāvayavādhikyopalambhakapārthivadravyāvacchedakagandhavattvajñānānubhavātmakatvaṃ tatpārthivāvayavādhikyapañcabhūtātmakamiti tādṛśarasavaddravyādhīnaṃ tadvikāraviśiṣṭadravyādhikyapañcabhūtātmakaṃ śarīramiti |
evamanaladravyāvayavādhikyopalambhakavahnitvavaddravyācchedakataijasaguṇavattvaṃ vahnibhūtādhikyavahnidravyādhikyajñāpakataijasarūpopala mbhakajñānānubhavātmakatvaṃ tattaijasāvayavādhikyapañcabhūtātmakamiti tāddaśaguṇaviśiṣṭapañcabhūtātmakadravyādhikyādanaṃ vahnibhūtāvayavavikārahetukam |
evaṃ sarvadravyeṣu yojanīyam | tathāhi--pārthivadravyāvayavādhikyādanaṃ pārthivadravyāvayavavikārahetukam | jalādhikyaguṇopalambhakarasādhikyadravyādanaṃ jaladravyāvayavavikārahetukam | vahnidravyāvayavavikārakārakaṃ vahnyādhikyaguṇopalambhakarasādhikyadravyādanam |
iti yathāyogaṃ yāvadrogaghātātmakaṣaḍrasadravyāṇāṃ nānāroganivartakatvaṃ pratipāditam | taccintyam, tathāhi--
etatprayojanaṃ doṣatrayaprakopakārakatvaṃ doṣatrayaprakopanivartakatvaṃ ca | deśadehakālabhedena tattaddoṣanivartakamiti pratipāditam | tanna kṣodakṣamamityasvarasādāha-- svastheti |
svasthāsvasthāssāmajāmayāssadoṣagā vidoṣagāḥ || 17 ||
yāvatpārthivadravyādanena doṣāḥ svasthā bhaveyuḥ, yāvadvirasadravyādanena ajīrṇe jāte sati anyonyasthānasthitatvāt asvasthā bhaveyuśca | nirdiṣṭakāryavyatiriktakāryakārakatvaṃ teṣāmevāvacchedakam | tattadravyādanam doṣāṇāṃ svasthatvakārakam | viruddhagatikāryahetubhūtadravyādanajanyā ye ta eva sāmajāmayāḥ | teṣāṃ nivartakadravyādanena nīrogatvam |
sadoṣeti-- tatra doṣāṇāṃ ye vikṛtāste vikāragrastāḥ | dhātuśoṣakaśarīramārgaṃ gacchantīti te doṣagāḥ tadvikārābhāvakāryahetukarasadravyādanena vidoṣagāḥ vikārarahitā bhavantītyarthaḥ |
iyaṃ doṣavikāragatiḥ, iyaṃ doṣavikārābhāvagatiriti viruddhāviruddharasavaddravyādanajanyā iti pratipāditāḥ, viruddhāviruddharasavaddravyādanamātrajñānahetukatvāt ityanumānaracanena pratipāditā satī vartate | balavadvahnyādikā ye etatpratipāditārthāvacchedakatvānubhavāsta eva śarīraṃ tathā rakṣanti tadbhakṣitānnaṃ tenānalena jīryate, etatpratipāditārthasya saṃbhāvitatvādityasvarasādāha--vihiteti |
vihitapākaheturasā vipākāntassthitaviruddharasā viṣamadoṣadhātumārgagā viṣamadoṣāḥ || 18 ||
viruddhāviruddharasadravyādanajñānaṃ vihitarasavipākāntasstharasavaddravyādanajñānapūrvakaṃ tadvikārakāryahetubhūtarasavaddravyādanajñānajanyanivartakajñānapūrvakatvāt | te'ntassthitarasādanaviruddharasāḥ vikārakārakāḥ tadvikāragrastadoṣā viṣamadoṣāḥ, dhātumārgaṃ gacchantīti dhātumārgagāḥ | ta eva viṣamadoṣā ityarthaḥ | tadvirasavaddravyādanajanyavikārajñānajñāpakaṃ svahastatalameva ghrāṇendriyaviṣayakajñānagrāhakaṃ tatpratyahaṃ tatraiva yāvaddrasābhāvadarśanaṃ ārogyahetukamiti pratyahaṃ vijñāya tattu bhūyobhūyo darśanena svahaste vā antassthitāmayānāṃ hetubhūtarasāḥ dakṣiṇahastatale āvirbhavanti | ārogyajñānaṃ yāvadvirasādanajanyāmayahetukarasavirasābhāvajñānaviṣayakajñānaṃ bhavitumarhati | tathāhi--ghrāṇendriyaviṣayahastagatarasajñānaṃ vipākāntassthitāmayaheturasavirasāvirbhāvajñānaviṣayakajñānahetukarasavirasādanajanyāmayajñānahetūpalambhakaṃ tadrasavaddravyādhikyādanam | anila dravyāvayavavikārahetukaṃ aniladravyādanaguṇopalambhakatadrasādhikyadravyādanam | ākāśadravyāvayavavikārahetukameva | evaṃ viruddhaviṣayasya pratiyogitvena hetutvāt |
nanu tattaddravyaguṇaviśiṣṭarasānuguṇayāvaddrasavaddravyaṃ yāvadrogaghātakamiti vartate | tathāhi--gaganabhūtādhikyajñāpakakaṣāyarasānuguṇaṃ sat śrotravivarapradeśagataśabdajñānapratibandhakaśrotrākāśavivaragatanābhasakaṣāyarasādhikyadravyaṃ tannivartakaṃ syāt | tasmāttadgaganaguṇādhikyamiti vijñeyam | tathaiva pavanaguṇādhikyadravyamapi vijñeyam | pavanaguṇādhikyadravyajñāpakamṛdusthiragurudravadravyajñānaṃ viruddhaviṣayakapavanabhūtāvayavādhikyajñāpakaṃ pāñcabhautikaviruddhaviṣayakadravyamiti | evamanilādhikyaguṇajñāpakaślakṣṇasāndratādiguṇādhikyataijasaguṇābhivardhakadravyaṃ analabhūtādhikyajñāpakaṃ pāñcabhautikam | evaṃ jalabhūtādhikyajñāpakasnigdhahaimaguṇaviśiṣṭajaladravyaṃ viruddhaviṣayakam |
evaṃ pṛthivītvajñāpake gurutvamandatve | āmlarasadravyatvāt | pārthivadravyāvayavādhikyasvādurasajñāpakaṃ yat tatpañcabhūtātmakaṃ viruddhaviṣayakam | etatsarvaṃ vijñāya cikitsā kāryā |
nanu cikitsājñānaṃ viruddhaviṣayakajñānapūrvakam | viruddhaviṣayakatvaṃ nāma viruddhadravyajñānaviṣayakatvam | tatra viruddhadravyajñānasya pratiyogitvena hetutvāt | tathā sati śarīrasya lakṣaṇaṃ pañcabhūtātmakatvam | tallakṣaṇaṃ tadjñānapūrvakam | tatra śrutiḥ--
"ākāśādvāyuḥ | vāyoragniḥ | agnerāpaḥ | adbhyaḥ pṛthivī | pṛthivyā oṣadhayaḥ | oṣadhībhyo'nnam | annātpāñcabhautikaṃ śarīramiti |"
cikitsākāle paṇḍitānāṃ dravyajñānamāvaśyakamiti kālayogaviṣayadravyajñānaṃ deśayogaviṣayadravyajñānaṃ dehayogaviṣayakadravyajñānam— etatsarvajñānaṃ vinā pañcabhūtātmakarasavaddravyajñānaṃ kimāsīdityāha-- deheti |
dehadeśakāladravyakarmaṇāṃ yathāyogakaraṇaṃ yāvadrogaghātakam || 19 ||
deśabhedena dravyabhedaṃ vijñāya cikitsā kāryā | kālabhedena dravyabhedaṃ vijñāya cikitsākaraṇaṃ vidhiḥ | eteṣāṃ karmakaraṇaṃ yathā--yogakaraṇaṃ tattaddeśīyānāṃ tattaddravyabhedaguṇaṃ vidhatte | tattatkālaviśiṣṭadehānāṃ tattaddravyabhedaguṇaṃ vidhatte | evaṃ dehadeśakālabhedena rasavaddravyāṇāṃ yogaḥ bahuvidhaguṇān prayacchatītyarthaḥ | tattaddravyakarmaṇāṃ yathāyogo yāvadrogaghātakaḥ |
nanu tattaddravyabhedaguṇaṃ deśakālabhedajñānena tattannivartakarasavaddravyakarmaṇāṃ kṛtiḥ anekadhātusañcalitajanitadoṣasyāpi ekadhātukatvāt ayaṃ dvidoṣaḥ tridoṣo'yamiti pratītiḥ tajjarasāmayabhāvādeva tadjñānaṃ kathaṃ syādityasvarasādāha--viraseti |
virasaviṣamapacanajātadvidoṣaikavidhadhātumārgagā dvidoṣagāḥ || 20 ||
virasaviṣamapacanaṃ nāma viruddharasavaddravyasaṃyogajayāvaddravyaṃ pakvāśayaṃ praviśya taddravyānuguṇavikāraṃ kurute | tatpācakānalo 'pi vikāraṃ bhajati | viṣamarasapacanamapi karoti | tatsaṃskārajanyapavanagatiḥ visvaravarṇakaśabdajñāpakatālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpāraṃ karoti | tatpadādhārapadmaṃ mukulīkaroti | tadanantaramapi pittaprakopakārakarasavaddravyāntarāpādanena tattatsaṃskārajātaguṇānvayameva bibharti | yadā tadyāvadvikāraṃ karoti tadā pittaviśiṣṭapavanagatyaiva jñāpyate | rasaviṣamadoṣeṇa saṃskārajātapittavikārayuktassan tattaddhātumārgaṃ saṃprāpya sañcaran tattaddhātūn sandūṣya tattadvikārajñāpanaṃ kurute | tadā pittaprakopadoṣo'yamiti jñātuṃ śakyata evetyarthaḥ | evaṃ kaphaprakopakārakadravyāntarādanaṃ saṃbhavati | tadā kaphaprakopajñāpakaguṇānvidhatte | kaphapittāmayagato devadattaḥ iti tattadravyaguṇaviśiṣṭapavanasya tattaddoṣaguṇajñāpakagatiḥ pareṣāmapi jñāpyata iti | ayaṃ kapharogayuktaḥ pavanavikāraḥ dvandvayuktapavanavikārakaphajñāpakagatiṃ karoti | tadā viṣamarasādanajātasaṃskāraguṇaviśeṣapavanadoṣa eva anekadhātumārgagatikārakaśaktyā bhavati | tattaddhātupoṣaṇaṃ ca karotītyarthaḥ |
nanu pittaprakoparasavaddravyādanena pittaprakopakārakaguṇān gacchati | sa eva pittapavanaprakopagatyā jñātavyaḥ | evaṃ kaphaprakopakārakarasavirasadravyādanena kaphaprakopaguṇānvidhatte | tadā kaphaprakopadvandvagatiriti vyavahriyate | evamayaṃ tridoṣaroga iti evamākārajñānena vyavahartuṃ śakyate | tattajjanakāṅgānāmadṛṣṭatvāt 'ayaṃ tridoṣarogaḥ' iti jñānaṃ cātra kathaṃ syādityāha--carameti |
caramarasapacanarogastridoṣagaḥ || 21 ||
caramarasapacanadhāturmajjādhātuśśukladhāturiti tāvubhau caramadhātū ityupalabhyate | pavanapittakapharogāṇāmasthidhātuparyantaṃ gatirbhavati | tadā taddhātuśoṣaṇamātraṃ kurute | prāṇaghātaṃ na karoti | yāvadviruddharasādanasāmagrījātarogasya yadā majjādhātupraveśo bhavati tadā prāṇānvimucya nirvāṇaṃ gacchati | tadā tridoṣagatiriti vijñeyā |
nanu tattadviruddharasadravyādanena tattaddoṣāḥ prakupitā bhavanti | sarve rogāḥ doṣaprakopajanyāḥ | tattaddroganivṛttyarthaṃ doṣapacanaṃ kāryamityāha-- yāvaditi |
yāvadādoṣapacanaṃ nivartakaṃ || 22 ||
yāvanto rogāḥ yāvadrasadoṣajanyā iti doṣaprakopatvabhūyobhūyodarśanahetubhūtavyāptigrahajanyānumānaṃ tatra pramāṇamityarthaḥ | pakvāśaye virasapradhānāste doṣāḥ vikāraṃ bhajanti | dhātumārge sañcaranti ca | analādyāmarasāste prādurbhavanto ghrāṇendriyaviṣayā bhavantītyuktam | tadviruddhadoṣagamanapratibandhakāryaṃ tatpācanavidhiprayatanaṃ laṅghanakaraṇena prekṣaṇīyam | sa eva phalitārtha ityāha--yāvaditi |
yāvaddhātuprasādādhīno doṣapacanakālaḥ || 23 ||
viruddharasajātarasāsṛgdhātuvikārakārakasyāmarasavaddravyādanajanyājīrṇajanyakapharogaḥ | viruddharasajātataditarapavanapittavikārahetukarasavirasadravyādanābhāva kāryāvalambilaṅghanakaraṇameva bheṣajaṃ bhavati | yāvaddhātvadhirohaṇaparyantaṃ laṅghanakaraṇaṃ bheṣajaṃ bhavati | taditaradoṣābhāvakāryakaraṇaṃ subheṣajaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ | sa eva yāvaddhātugatasāmayaikadhātugatāmaya iti jñeyam |
pakvāśayena sāndrasāndratānalena doṣavikārapācanavidhi prayatanaṃ laṅghanakaraṇameva phalitārthaḥ |
nanu doṣaprakopābhāvajñānaṃ tattadrogakāryahetubhūtavirasādanaviṣayakajñānavyatiriktajñānajanyajñānaviṣayakaṃ, samarthapravṛttijanakajñānaviṣayakatvāt ityanumānaprayogaprakāreṇa roganivṛttijñāne sati rogābhāvajñānaṃ jātam | tena doṣaprakopanaṃ vinā tadabhāvajñānaṃ kathaṃ labhyata ityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāya viruddhetisūtrasya asvarasāntaramāha--viruddheti |
viruddharasajātayāvaddhātvadhirohaṇādekadhātukaḥ || 24 ||
asyārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe--
ekaviruddharasavaddravyādanajanyājīrṇajanyasaṃskāraviśeṣadoṣadhātugatiḥ ekavirasadravyādanajanyā sā gatiḥ virasadravyādanajanyājīrṇajātā satī anekadhātusaṃcārakārye dṛṣṭe sati ekarasavirasadravyādanajātājīrṇavattvaṃ anekavirasādanajanyatvābhāvena ayaṃ ekadhātuvikārakaroga iti jñānaṃ kathaṃ pravartate ? tasmādvirasādanajātarogaḥ yāvaddhātvārohaṇāt ayaṃ ekadhātugatijātarogaḥ, ayaṃ dvidoṣagatijātarogaḥ iti jñātuṃ nivartakābhāvaheturogābhāvasya taddhetutvajñāpakatvāt tadabhāvādyahetutvāt ajīrṇajanyarogābhāvakāryaṃ taddhetunivartakalaṅghanenaiva bhāvyaṃ, iti anumānapramāṇena yatra ajīrṇābhāvaḥ tatra rogābhāva iti tayoḥ vyāptau satyāṃ doṣapacanaṃ nivartakaṃ syāditi na vācyam | doṣāḥ duṣṭāssantaḥ kṣubhitā bha vanti | taddhātukṣobha eva prāṇaghātaṃ karoti | tasmāddoṣāṇāṃ avikāragatijñānaparyantaṃ laṅghanakaraṇaṃ āvaśyakamiti dhyeyam | tena doṣaprakopanivṛttirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu viruddharasavaddravyādanena ajīrṇe jāte sati tadajīrṇajīrṇatāparyantaṃ laṅghanakaraṇaṃ vidhiḥ doṣaprakopanivṛttiparyantaṃ yogyatvāt ajārṇajanyapavanasya laṅghanakaraṇenājīrṇanivṛttau satyāṃ pavanaprakopanivṛtteḥ dṛṣṭatvāt | tatra tailādikaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavati | pittadoṣapravartakarasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati tatprakopo bhavati | laṅghanakaraṇādeḥ pittanivartakatvāt kvāthena cūrṇena vā tannivṛttiḥ | evaṃ pacanavikārakārakadravyādanena ajīrṇe jāte sati kaphavikāro jāyate | kaṣāyakalkacūrṇādayastatpratikārakā bhavanti |
evaṃ tattadrogāṇāṃ bahūni nivartakāni | doṣā ajīrṇena prakupitāssantaḥ yāvatkālaṃ dhātukṣobhaṃ kurvanti | tatrāyaṃ laṅghanakaraṇavidhiḥ |
ajīrṇajanyasaṃskāre dhātuśoṣaikakārye vikāre dṛṣṭe sati tattajjvaro jāyate | tatra āmadoṣapacanarasya niyāmakatvāt | tadajīrṇajanyamantassthitaṃ sat tadvirasasarvarasāntaraṃ bhajati | tatsaṃskārajanyadoṣaḥ doṣāntaraṃ bhajati | sa doṣaḥ dhātumārgaṃ gatvā dhātukṣobhakāryakaraṇaṃ karotīti yattasya svahastatale virasagandhajñānabhedaṃ yadādāvupalabhate tadaiva tattaddoṣāvirbhāvakāryasya tattatprāgabhāvajñānābhāvajñānavicchedanaparyantaṃ doṣaprakopajanyarogāntarakāryasya hetubhūtatvāt tādṛśajvarasya nidānaṃ svahastatale tattadgandhajñānameva niyāmakaṃ labhyate | tāvatparyantaṃ laṅghanakaraṇaṃ bheṣajaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu asthigatājīrṇajanyarasavirasajanyabhedaviśiṣṭajrabhedajñāne jāte sati tāvatkālaparyantaṃ laṅghanavidhirbhavatu | agāmijanyadoṣāgatijanyakāryaṃ viṣamajvarakāryam | tasya liṅgajñāne jāte sati tayorvyāptiṃ bhūyodarśanena tatsahacarajñānaṃ saṃgṛhya bhaṣajaṃ kartuṃ śakyate | āgāmirogasya liṅgajñānābhāvāt kathaṃ laiṅgikajñānāvadhāraṇaṃ bhavet ? ityata āha-- raseti |
rasājīrṇavirasādhikaviruddharasajadoṣādhikaikavaśābhibhūtassvasthānacyuto bahirujjvalayan jvarassāmajātaḥ || 25 ||
nanu doṣāṇāṃ pacanamiti vigrahaḥ | atra ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ | tatpācanameva nivartakaṃ karmeti pratibhāti | doṣāṇāṃ śarīrasthitadhātugatyavikāraikaphalasya saṃbhāvitatvāt laṅghanena doṣapacanaṃ avaśyakaṃ kāryamityukte sati bandhaḥ syāt | dhātupoṣakapuruṣārthopakārakakarmaṇi taddoṣapacanārthaṃ laṅghanakarmakaraṇasya doṣasya balahīnakāryakarmaṇassamarthanaikasvabhāvatvāt | kāraṇabalādhikyakārye kartavye sati taddhetubalahīnakāryakarmakaraṇasya tathātvāt | dhātupoṣakapuruṣārthopakārakakarmaṇi taddoṣapacanārthaṃ laṅghanakarmakaraṇasya tathātvāt | teṣāmantyapavanadoṣahīnapacanakāryaṃ sūcyate | tasmāt virasājjātājīrṇanivṛttiparyantaṃ laṅghanakaraṇaṃ vidhiriti | anyathā rasājīrṇena rasā virasā bhavanti | tadvirasabāhulyena doṣā api balahainyavanto bhavanti | hīnakāraṇena hīnakāryaṃ bhavati | yatra viruddhā rasā āvirbhavanti tatra doṣā api duṣṭā bhavanti | taddo ṣavikārādhikyavaśāt virasajanyājīrṇe vṛtte sati doṣavikāragatyā agnisandhukṣaṇakaraṇamapi na kriyate | jaṭharānalaḥ svasthānaṃ vihāya bahiḥ prajvalan sa tu sāmajvaramāvirbhāvayatītyarthaḥ |
nanu laukikasāmagrīmātraṃ na kāraṇaṃ sukarmakaraṇavidhavidyādayo'pi saṃbhavanti,
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vyādhirūpeṇa bādhate |
avaśyamanubhoktavyaṃ kṛtaṃ karma śubhāśubham ||
iti vārtikavacanānusāreṇa jñātavyam | rasavirasavaddravyādane jāte sati keṣāṃcidvyādhayassabhavanti | iti sarvatra niyamena vaktumaśakyatvāt "sarvaṃ balavataḥ pathyaṃ" iti nyāyavacanānusāreṇa balavadviṣaye rasājīrṇavirasādhikaviruddharase jāte satyapi tatra rogo na dṛṣṭaḥ | laukikasāmagrīmātrameva kvacinna kāraṇam | āmādyātmakakāryāṇāṃ saṃbhāvitatvāt prārabdhakarmaṇāṃ balitvāt sarvatrāpi rasājīrṇajanyarogāssambhavāntītyetatpratipādanaṃ vyarthaṃ syāditi | tena yatra rogotpādakāmavṛddhirbhavati tatra tādṛgrogakāryamāvirbhavati | tathāhi--rasājīrṇavirasadhikaviruddharasajakāryavaicitryāt tattadrogajanakakāraṇavaicitryaṃ punarvaktuṃ śakyate | yāvadajīrṇajanyāmayasya laṅghanameva nivartakaṃ roganivartakasāmagryatiriktasāmagrīnivartyatvāt, laṅghanādisāmagrīkāryaṃ vinā anirvartyatvācca | tasmādvirasadravyādanasāmagrī yādṛśī bhavati tadrogakāryavaicitryaṃ tādṛśaṃ saṃbhavati | tathāhi-- sarve rogāssaṃjātāssvajanakādhikyavaśāttrayodaśarūpajvarāssannipātavikārāssaṃbhavanti | svādurasavirasatvajātayāvatkāryaṃ śiraḥkamalasthāmṛtaṃ sarvasirāndhrabhāgamupagamya virasaviṣamadoṣasaṃparkavaśāt yadā vikāro jāyate tadā āmarasaḥ svādurasajātatāndrikadoṣanāmakarogākṛtijvaraṃ jvalayati | rasājīrṇavirasādhikaviruddharasājīrṇajātatvāt | taddhi svādurasaḥ rasājīrṇarogaḥ tāndrikadoṣaḥ | sa tadadhīnarasavirasaḥ saṃgrahajātarogatvāt pavanagativaśāt āmavirasasaṃgrahasaṃsargājjāyate | tatra amṛtasaṃparkaḥ poṣako bhavati | pavanagatyādhārakājīrṇajanakasarandhrakābhyantaradharasirāmārgagatapavanasya sagandhisandhidoṣaprayuktasāmajvarajñāpakapādapadmasthāvarṇopadhagabījapārśvameḍhrapṛṣṭhapradeśagatakavargapañcakavarṇasamūhapadavyañjaketaravarṇoccāraṇapadagatisājātyābhāvajñānaviṣayakaṃ bhavitumarhati | rasājīrṇavirasādhikaviruddharasajanyadoṣādhikyavattvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivamiti | sandhigatadoṣaprayuktasāmajvarapādabījapārśvameḍhapṛṣṭhapradeśakramāt avarṇopagatakavargapañcakātmakapadajñāpakacatustriṃśatṣatriṃśatsirāsaṃsparśavaśāt nābhiśūlapādaśobhāṅgavedanābalopadravajñāpakakaphapraṇavatāpāntaradoṣaprayuktasāmajvarajñāpakajānupadmasthāyipavarṇopadhapṛṣṭhapradeśajaṭharanābhipadmagatacavargapañcakavarṇapadābhivyañjaketaravarṇasājātyābhijñānaviṣayakaṃ rasājīrṇavirasādhikyaviruddharasajanyadoṣādhikyavattvāt ayamāntaradoṣayuktasāmajvaraḥ jānupṛṣṭhapradeśanābhipadmagatajānupadmagatevarṇabodhakacavargapañcakacatustriṃśatsirāsaṃsparśavaśāt dāhavidāhavividhamohayātanātiśiraḥkampakarātisārahikkātiśvāsādiviguṇaviśiṣṭāntaradoṣaprayuktasāmajvaraḥ jvalayati | sarvadhātumārgaṃ daśadinaparyantaṃ dhātuṣu sañcārya prāṇānviyojayati | tasmādāntaradoṣo'yamiti vyavahriyate |
ayamāntaradoṣaḥ jānupadmagatevarṇabodhakapṛṣṭhapārśvagatanābhipradeśasthevarṇopadhacavargavarṇapañcakātmakapadārthāvabodhaka varṇasamūhapadakadambakavākye cavargocitavarṇetarebhyo bhinnaghoṣātmakavākyaṃ śrāvayati | tadvarṇabhedamavaśyaṃ vijñāya tadā tadvirasārthajanyāmapittasaṃsparśāt tatsirāmārgagatāmṛtaṃ tatpadmādhiṣṭhitadhātuśoṣaṇaṃ karoti | tatpratīkārakamapi bhavati | so'yamasādhyaroga iti vijñeyam |
viruddhāhāradoṣaprayuktasāmajvarajñāpakajaṅghāpadmasthāvarṇabodhakatriṃśatsirāvṛtanābhyāvṛtacakrapadmacatussirāvṛtaromarājihṛtpadmacatustriṃśatsirāvṛtahṛdayapadmaṃ ca ṭavargapañcakavarṇātmakapadābhivyañjaketaravarṇasājātyābhāvajñānaviṣayakaṃ rasājīrṇavirasādhikyaviruddharasajanyadoṣādhikyavattvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ, ityanumityā viruddhāmaviśiṣṭasāmajvaraṃ jaṅghāpadmanābhyāvṛtapadmaromarājipārśvapadmahṛdayapadmāni jaṅghāpadmasthavarṇaprayuktakavargapañcakajñāpakovarṇaipadhagavarṇātmakapadasamūhavākyajanyajñānaṃ jaṅghāpadmāvalambakaviṃśatisirāvabodhakanābhyāvṛtapadmāvalambakatriṃśatsirāvabodhakaromarājipārśvādhārakadvidvisirāvṛtavarṇabodhakahṛdayapadmasthāvalambakacatustriṃśatsirāvṛtavarṇabodhakasakalasirāsaṃsparśavaśāt vedanāsahitarūkṣopasamarthaṃ dahyate | nābhyāvṛtacakrapadmasya vṛttāvṛtapradeśatvāt | tatra jvaraḥ jvalayati | kukṣau ca tadvadeva pratibhāti | hṛdayapadmamapi jvalayati | dāhaṃ karoti | bhramo'pi bhavati | tasmādetallakṣaṇalakṣitatvāt sa rogī jīvati | ayaṃ sāmajvaraḥ pittadoṣaprakopajanyaḥ, pittadoṣaprakopakāryakatvāt | tattatpadmāvalambakasirārandhragatāmṛtasya rasājīrṇavirasādhikaviruddharasajadoṣādhikyasaṃsparśavaśāt jaṅghāpadmapradeśaṃ jvalayati | nābhyāvṛtapadmapradeśaśca tadvadeva dahyate | hṛdayapadmapradeśe ca tathaiva duḥkhaṃ karoti | tatpadmagatavarṇavikārajñāpakapadārthātmakaviṣamarasasaṃskāravyāptavarṇāmṛtapoṣakadravyatvāddāhaṃ karoti | mūrcchāmāpādayati | sa eva doṣajvaraḥ vibhramadoṣa iti vyavahriyate | ṛvarṇajanakamūrupadmaṃ ca tavargapañcakapañcāśatsirāvṛtastanadvayapadmaṃ ca triṃśatsirāvṛtakaṇṭhadeśapadmaṃ ca ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtagrīvāntassthitapadmaṃ ca pavanagatidhārakasirārandhramārgaṃ rasājīrṇavirasādhikaviruddharasajadoṣādhikyasirāsaṃsparśavaśāt jātadoṣaḥ sirāṅgadoṣayuktassāmajvaraḥ pañcadaśadivasaparyantaṃ dhātumārgeṣu saṃcaran tatpradeśasthitapadmapratāpakāmayatvāt sadoṣasirāgatasarvāṅgagatajvaranivṛttyā rasadhāturasājīrṇavirasasaṃsparśavaśāt sa rasaḥ dhātuvikāro bhūtvā śītāṅgaṃ karoti | ṛ ṣavarṇajanakaśroṇipradeśapadmaṃ triṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ paphavarṇabodhakaṣoḍaśasirāvṛtapadmaṃ ca babhavarṇāvalambakapañcāśatsirāvṛtapadmaṃ ca varṇodvacanapralāpanakaram | sāmajvaraḥ ayaṃ pralāpadoṣa iti vyavahriyate | caturdaśadinaparyantaṃ jvarāvṛttiḥ, doṣaprakopakālāvadhiḥ | evarṇajanakakaṭipradeśagatacatussirāvṛtaṃ sat yavaralavarṇānāmupadhābhūtaṃ bhavaddvisirāvṛtayavarṇādhiṣṭhitarasabandhanapadmaṃ ca ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtarephavarṇajñāpakoṣṭhapadmapradeśaṃ ca lavarṇabodhakadvisirāvṛtagandhavāhapadmaṃ ca rasājīrṇavirasādhikaviruddharasajanyadoṣādhikyavaśāt dvisirāvṛtayavarṇajñāpakarasabandhanapadmasya ṣoḍaśasirāvṛtarephavarṇajñāpakoṣṭhapradeśapadmasya vikāraṃ kurvan karṇakubjadoṣavikārātmakaḥ bahireva jvalayan jvarassāmajātaḥ |
ekakāraṇajanyasāmagrījātajvaraḥ tattadvarṇādhiṣṭhitatattatpadmāvalambakasirāsaṃsparśavaśāt tattadvarṇajñānabhedaviṣayakajñānaṃ tattaddoṣayuktasāmajvarabhedaviṣayakapūrvakaṃ tattadvarṇādhiṣṭhitapadmāvalambakasāmaviṣayajanyasirāsaṃsparśabhedajanyajñānaviṣayakatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭa ityanumānapramāṇena varṇabhedajñānavijñānānubhavasya doṣajanyajvarajñānaviṣayajñānānubhavavattvādityarthaḥ |
nanu sarve rogāḥ āmarasavirasajātāmayājīrṇavirasaviṣamāt jātarogakāryāḥ ekasāmagrījanyasāmajvarasamayabhedāt nānārūpā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tasmāt ekakāraṇajātakāryasyāpi samarthopadhabādhāchinnatve'pi kāraṇavaicitryasaṃbhāvitatvāt janyamapyekameva kāraṇaṃ sarvasyaṃ janyatvāviśeṣāt | ajīrṇajanyakāryāvacchedakaṃ ajīrṇamātranivartakameva nivartakam | ata evājīrṇajanyāmayāśca ajīrṇajanyavirasaviṣamarasajātarogāśca tattatkaraṇabhedabhinnā bhavanti | ajīrṇajanyāmayebhyo'jīrṇavirasaviṣamādanajātarogāṇāṃ bhinnatvāt tattannivartakā api bhinnā bhavantītyarthaḥ | tathā'pi ajīrṇamātrajanyāmayānāṃ laṅghanakaraṇamātrameva kevalaṃ nivartakam | tatra nivartakāntarasāmagrīmapi nāpekṣate alpakāraṇasāmagrījanyatvāt | tadviṣamarasāmayajanyājīrṇavyatiriktatadvirasaviṣamarasādanāt nānārogā bhavantītyāha--viṣamaraseti |
viṣamarasapacanarasavirasādiyāvaddhātuprasādādhīno doṣapacanakālaḥ || 26 ||
viṣamarasādanājjātarogāṇāṃ yāvannivartakakaraṇaṃ viṣamarasadoṣajanyasaṃskārakāryasyaiva puruṣārthatvāt | tadviṣanivṛttikāryasya viṣamarasavaddravyādanajātakāryajanyasaṃskāraviśeṣadhna eva nivartakaḥ | tasmādajīrṇajanyāmayā api bhinnāḥ ajirṇajanyavirasaviṣamajanyadoṣajanyatvāt | ajīrṇasya ubhayatrāpyekatve'pi tajjanakībhūtarasānāṃ bhinnatvakāraṇavaicitryāt kāryamapi bhidyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu sāmajvarassamayabhedāt bhinnaḥ bahurūpavadupāyavaicitryāt | kācijjvarotpādakasāmagrī ajīrṇarūpā | kācitsāmagrī ajīrṇajanyavirasaviṣamarasasirāsaṃsparśajanyadoṣaviṣamagatikārakasāmagrī | tannivartakaṃ tu tadvirasānekarasa ddravyādanasāmagrī | yāva ddoṣajanyadhātuvikārābhāvaparyantaṃ doṣavikāragatinivṛttiparyantaṃ tattannivartakena tattatpacanaṃ kuryāditi vaktavyam | tathā sati asthimajjāśukladhātugatā satī yāvaddoṣagatiḥ prāṇahāniṃ kurute | teṣāṃ nivartakānāmaprayojakatvāt śreyānprasādādhīno doṣapacanakāla iti tāvaddoṣapacanamārogyakārakaṃ na bhavatītyasvarasādāha--evamiti |
evamekadhātugatāssusādhyāḥ || 27 ||
evamuktarītyā sarvadoṣa ekadhātugatijanyavikāraḥ yatra pradṛśyate tatrāyaṃ niyamaḥ | na dvitridoṣeṣu niyamaḥ | evaṃprakāreṇa ekadhātugatavyādhīnāṃ susādhyatvamityarthaḥ |
nanu doṣaprāṇāgatiḥ kenopāyena jñātavyā ? pavanadoṣagatiḥ svābhāvikavikāravedyā | paṅgukaphaḥ paṅguriti tayorgamanasya vaktumaśakyatvāt |
nābhipradeśaparyantaṃ pādapadmādārabhya bījapadmaparyantaṃ pavanapradeśaḥ | bījapārśvapadmamārabhya hṛdayapadmaparyantaṃ pittadeśaḥ | tadūrdhvadeśaḥ śiraḥpradeśaparyantaṃ kaphapradeśaḥ | evaṃ deśabhedaṃ vijñāya doṣāṇāṃ gamanamīdṛṅniścitya ayaṃ sādhyarogaḥ ayamasādhyaroga iti vyavahartuṃ śakyate | doṣasya doṣāntareṇa saṃsargo dvandva iti | kvacittrayāṇāṃ doṣāṇāṃ samāhāratvaṃ tridoṣatvam | sarvaṃ kenākāreṇa jñātavyamityasvarasādāha--dviraseti |
dvirasajadvidoṣapracāramāṃsamedonugatarasapradhānaikadoṣapacanadvidoṣajā dussādhyāḥ || 28 ||
dvayorvātāpittayoḥ prakopakāryahetubhūtau yau rasau, alpasvādurasavirasaḥ pavanaprakopahetukaḥ | adhikasvādurasavirasaḥ pittaprakopahetukaḥ | tāveva virasau kāryabhedakārakau | tābhyāmutpanno vikāraḥ dvirasajaḥ | dvidoṣapracāreti | caratīti caraḥ | prakṛṣṭaṃ caratīti pracaraḥ | rasāsṛṅmāṃsadhātuvidhā ye te māṃsamedonugatā iti | śiraḥ ṣaṭkamalātmakaṃ, māṃsādhārakaṃ śiraḥ | medodhārakaṃ bhinnam | taddhātuśoṣakarasau bhinnau | tatra svānugatakāryamuddiśya pravartakau yau rasau tāveva pradhānabhūtakarmakārakau | evaṃ rasapradhānaikadoṣapacanaṃ yatropalabhyate tatreyaṃ sūtrapravṛttiḥ | prakārāntaramapi dyotyate | rasavirasajanyadoṣaprakopasya pacanalaṅghanauṣadhaiḥ prakoparāhityakaraṇameva pacanamiti kecidvyākhyānaṃ vyācakṣate | rasapradhānaikadoṣapacanaṃ rasoparasalohādīnāṃ mahārasānāṃ yogakarma sarvaroganivartakamiti vivakṣitam | taddravyaniṣṭharasāḥ taijasā iti vyavahriyante | tatsajātīyānalasaṃyogena tadraseṣu tattadguṇā āvirbhavanti | etatsarvaṃ taijasadravyaṃ āmavirasaviṣajanyaprakopajanyarogāṇāṃ nivartakam | rasavaddravyaniṣṭhaṣaḍrasādīnāṃ analasaṃyogajanyarasaviśiṣṭadravyamadhikarasāvirbhāvaprabhāvahetukaṃ, adhikakālarasaguṇapradhānarūpakākāradravyatvāt tailaghṛtalehyādivat |
analasaṃyogajanyamahārasavaddravyaniṣṭhaṣaḍrasādirasaguṇaḥ kālapānayogakaraṇajātarasavirasādivibhāvādeva rasaguṇaviśiṣṭaguṇapradhānaśīlakaḥ analasaṃyogajanyaguṇāvirbhāvaguṇaviśiṣṭakālādhikyadravyatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā kālādiḥ ityanumānapramāṇajñānasya tadadhikaraṇajñāpakatvaṃ vivakṣitam | evaṃ dvandvarogāṇāṃ sarvapadārtheṣu nivartakajñānamanivartakajñānamubhayajñānaviṣayakajñānaṃ roganivartakakāryahetukam | nivartakaikanivṛttadravyatvāt | itararasoparasa lohasādhāraṇamahārasānāmanyadravyasaṃyogajanyayogakaraṇaṃ guṇaṃ prayacchati | tadvirasāntaraprāpakasāmagrīsānnidhyābhāvadravyatvāt |
nanu yāvaddoṣaprakopahetukavirasadravyājīrṇajanyajvarāḥ tattaddoṣanivartakāḥ, doṣaprakopajanakasvādurasavirasadravyajātājīrṇajanyajvarāḥ tattaddoṣanivartakāḥ doṣaprakopajanakasvādurasavirasadravyajātājīrṇajanyatvāt | ajīrṇajanyavirasarasāḥ jvaropadravakāryaṃ kurvanti, tadupadravaviśiṣṭajvaranimittakadravyatvāt |
jvaraprakopajñānajanakadaśopadravarogāṇāmutpattiṃ darśayituṃ vyācaṣṭe--evamiti |
evamanyonyadoṣajātāśca || 29 ||
anyonyanimittakajvaropadravāḥ daśāvirbhavanti | śvāsamūrchābhramacharditṛṣṇātisārahikkākāsāṅgavedhanamūḍhātirogāḥ | ete jvaropadravā daśa | tadupadravāvirbhāvajanakasāmagrī jvaraprakopakāryahetubhūtāmarasavirasaviṣakṛmisaṃsparśanaṃ yāvadvarṇādhārakapadmāvalambakasirāsaṃsparśajātapavanagativikārajanyatattadrogakāryasirāgatapavanagatijanyarogātmakānyonyasaṃsargahetubhūtatvāt | evamanyonyadoṣajātarogāḥ jvaraprakopakāryahetukā ityarthaḥ |
nanu virasādhikyajātajvaraḥ māṃsamedādoṣagata ityuktatrividharasādhikyajātajvaraścet asthimajjādhātuparyantaṃ jvaro'nudhāvati | doṣatrayaprakopajanakavirasajātajvarasya asthimajjādhātvadhirohakatvāt | ayaṃ tridoṣajvara iti vyavahartuṃ śakyata ityata āha--dviraseti |
dvirasādhikaikajātatridoṣarasarūpānuguṇarogā asādhyāḥ || 30 ||
vātapittaprakopajanakā dvirasāḥ āmarasasthitakālajātajvaraprakopakārakā bhavanti | tadā āmarasavirasavattvaṃ viruddhakāryajanakaṃ dvirasādivirasāntarajanakakālahetukadravyatvāt | ityeṣa jvaraḥ saptadhātugatassan prāṇaghātako bhavati | tādṛśarogo'sādhyo bhavati | tathāhi--
tattajjvarotpādakāmarasavirasasāmagrīkāryaṃ jvarotpādakakāryamātraṃ saptadhātūnviśoṣya prāṇānvimocayati iti yaduktaṃ taccintyam | āmo jvaramātrotpādakasāmagrī jvaraṃ janayitvā svayaṃ tatkṣaṇajanyasaṃskāraṃ tattatkārye vidhāya svarūpānna dṛśyate | teṣu poptadasāmagrījanyeṣuvat sarvajyarāṇāmajīrṇajanyatvāt |
jvarotpādakasāmagrīmātrajanyajvaraḥ prāṇānvimocayatīti yattat tathācedatiprasaṃgassyādityasvarasādāha--evamiti |
evamekadhātugatāścāsādhyāḥ || 31 ||
evamuktarītyā tadupadravarogāśca prāṇaghātakāḥ | tadvirasajanyadoṣānusaritajvarahetukaprāṇāpakārakadravyatvāt | viṣādanajātarogavaccharīravat | varṇapadhagapadmānusārikavargapañcakajñāpakapadmādhārakasirāḥ dhātuśoṣakapoṣakāḥ | tadādhārakarasavadāśritaśarīra tvāt | tadvirasānusaritasaṃskārodbodhakasaṃsargasirādhigatadoṣagativikārakāryakārakopayogirasadhātutvāt | adhautadhātuṃ saṃprāpya pavanavikārakārakagatiḥ tadanyavikārakārakatvāt śvāsaṃ ca mūrchāṃ ca (karoti) | tadubhayorapi rasadhātujatvena dṛśyatvāt | evaṃ rasadhātuṃ saṃśoṣya anyadhātuśoṣaṃ kartuṃ sa eva jvaraḥ taddhātuṃ saṃprāpyojjṛmbhate | bhramacchardī bhavataḥ | evaṃ māṃsadhātuṃ saṃprāpya hikkādiśokau bhavataḥ | evamevānyadhātuṃ saṃprāpya bhramahikkārogau jāyete | sa eva jvaraḥ kāsamanvahaṃ janayan vartate | asthi saṃprāpya śophaṃ karoti | sa eva jvaraḥ vātapittakaphānusaritassanmajjādhātumavalambya vijṛmbhate | tadā sa daśavidharogākāro bhavati | evamanyadoṣajāśceti sūtravyākhyānaṃ kṛtam |
nanu rasavirasajanyavirasādhikaviruddharasadoṣāḥ sarvarogahetukāḥ | ajīrṇaguṇāvirbhāvajñānābhāvajanyavirasajanyadoṣavikārābhāvajñāpakaśuddharasajñānadoṣāḥ vikārābhāvakāryakārakāḥ | evaṃ doṣāḥ sarvarogahetukāḥ |
evaṃ doṣāḥ duṣṭāssantastrayo'pi vikārakārakā bhaveyuḥ doṣadhātvātmaśarīriṇaḥ | sukarmaparipākavaśāt doṣā aduṣṭāssantaḥ vikārapratibandhakā bhaveyuḥ | sarve rogāḥ virasajātā iti yatpratipāditaṃ tanna, kṣodakṣamamityasvarasādāha--adoṣā iti |
adoṣāssadoṣāssamadoṣāssarujā rujaḥ || 32 ||
prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānāḥ pañca vāyavaḥ śarīrakāryopakārakāḥ | śarīrādhārakasya adoṣatvaṃ kathaṃ samyaktayā bhāsate ? pittasyāpi doṣatvādeva sadoṣapañcarūpopapannaṃ saṃgṛhṇāti pacati, vide śayati, muñcati--ityeteṣāṃ pañcavidhakarmaṇāṃ śarīrādhārakatvācca kaphadoṣasyāpi śarīradārḍhyakārakatvāt doṣatrayaṃ śarīraṃ rakṣati | tasmādadoṣatvaṃ kathaṃ śarīropakārakamiti nāśaṅkanīyam |
doṣāṇāṃ vikārakārakatvābhāvaḥ adoṣaśabdārthaḥ | sadoṣāḥ vikārakāryāpādakagatiguṇaviśiṣṭadoṣāḥ | sadoṣāssaptadhātuśoṣakārakāḥ | tasmāccharīrasya sadoṣatvaṃ duṣkarmānubhavavaśāllabhyate | tena dhātavaḥ pīḍyante | duḥkhaṃ cānubhūyate | samadoṣatvaṃ nāma tadubhayābhāvagativiśiṣṭadoṣāpravivardhakatvam | tena dhātavaḥ puṣṇanti | nīrogatvaṃ prāpnuvanti | tasmātsamadoṣaṃ yathā bhavati tathā śarīraṃ saraṃkṣaṇīyamityarthaḥ | sarvadā śarīrasthadoṣāṇāṃ ekarasahetubhūtakāryasya kiṃcinnimittaṃ vaktavyam | tadetadrasajanyavirasapariṇāmajātarogakāryamevamiti jñātavyam ||
nanu viṣamagativiśiṣṭo yo doṣaḥ tasya vaiṣamye rogaḥ | doṣasya samā gatirārogyamiti vyapadeśaḥ kṛtaḥ | tathā sati ṣaḍrasāḥ saptadhātvātmakāḥ | virasaṣaṭkasyādanaṃ dhātuśoṣakam, tadrasātmakadoṣadhātuvirasadravyatvāt ityanumānena dhātuśoṣakakāryasya rasādihetutvam | tasmāddoṣavaiṣamyaṃ rogaḥ | doṣasāmyamaroga iti | yatpratipāditaṃ tadviruddhamityata āha--ta iti |
te trayo doṣahetubhūtāssaptadhātava iti || 33 ||
saptadhātava iti vyapadeśamātraṃ bhavati | ṣaḍrasā eva niścitam | teṣāṃ saptasaṃkhyākatvaṃ kathaṃ syāditi na vaktavyam | rasa evāsṛk iti tayoḥ pārthakyābhāvavivakṣayā satyaṃ ṣaḍrasāssaptadhātava iti vyavahartuṃ śakyate | tathāhi--
svādurasavaddravyatvaguṇavat rasadravyamityanubhūyate udbhūtarasavyatiriktarasavaddravyābhāvāt | tasmādrasādayo guṇā guṇinamantareṇa guṇā nāvatiṣṭhanta iti dravyamāvaśyakam, dravyāśrayā guṇā iti | saptadhātukaśarīradravyatvāt rasādīnāṃ guṇatvāt guṇaguṇinorbhedasya avasitatvāt rasādayo dravyāśrayā bhavanti | tasmātsaptadhāttvātmakaṣṣaḍrasavyatirikta iti na vācyam, dravyarasātmakatvāt | śarīrāntassthitarasāḥ dhāturūpeṇa pariṇamanti | tāvaddhātūnāṃ dravyaniṣṭhatvāt dhātūnāṃ dravyaguṇatvāt rūpādivat bhedo na dṛśyate | rasavaddravyaṃ rasanendriyaviṣayakam | rūpavaddravyaṃ cakṣurindriyaviṣayakam | rasadravyaṃ rūparasavaddravyaṃ sādṛśyāt ! ṣaḍrasavikāra eva dhāturiti rasasapta ... nendriyeṇa gṛhyate | tatsajātīyaṃ saptadhātusajātīyaraseṣu labhyate | te'ntassthitāssantaḥ dhātumeva puṣṇanti | tadyathā– ikṣukāṇḍasthitasvādurasaḥ yatrayatra bhedavaśāt vartate tanniṣṭhasvādurasaṃ saṃgṛhya tatsaṃsargajātidravyaṃ vibhajan rasaṃ karoti | tadanantaraṃ tatsvādurasaṃ vihāya tatsaṃyogidravyaṃ pṛthaktayā tadbhaktatayā bhajate rūpādivat | asigābhavanaṃ budrābhāvāt ?| nīlo ghaṭa iti ghaṭaniṣṭhanīlarūpaṃ vihāya anyadravyāvayavatayā sa bhāsate | atra rasavyatiriktadravyasyābhāvāt tadvat eṣu ca dhātutvapṛthagākārakatattadguṇaviśiṣṭatayā cakṣuḥpratītiḥ jāyate | tadvat lavaṇoṣaṇakaṣāyā ityāhvayante, āśrayādivat | śarīrāṅgasthitāni bhavanti | tena rasādaya ityāhūyante | na vācyam, śarīraṃ saptadhātvātmakam | dhātavaśca rasātmakāḥ śarīraṃ rasātmakadravyaṃ saptadhātvāvṛtatvāt | tadarthaṃ pṛthubudhnodarākāratvaikabhāsaṃ tat | svādurasaśśukraśleṣmarūpavattvena pariṇamati | āmlarasaḥ majjārūpākṛtiḥ pariṇamati | lavaṇarasaḥ asthirūpākṛtiḥ pariṇamati | tiktarasaḥ medovikārākṛtissan pariṇamati | ūṣaṇarasaḥ majjādhātvākāratvena pariṇamati | kaṣāyarasaḥ rasāsṛgdhātvākṛtissan bhāsate | tasmātpṛthaktayā abhidheyatve'pi āśrayādivat rasādivyañjakaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate | bahiḥpradeśasthitāḥ śarīrāntaḥ praviṣṭāssantaḥ tattatsajātīyadhātuṣu vilīyante tattadrasāḥ | tattaddhātūnāṃ poṣaṇaṃ ca kurvanti | saṃśayaṃ vinā śuddhaguṇaviśiṣṭabahurasavaddravyaṃ śarīradārḍhyakaraṇaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanu rasavaddravyādanena prakupitapavanadravyasya rasāsṛgdhātuśoṣaṃ kartumicchataḥ pavanabhūtasya prakopanivartakaṃ kaṣāyarasavadekaṃ bhavati | kaṣāyarasasya rasāsṛgdhātūnāṃ pavanaprakopanivartakatvaṃ vaktumayogyamiti pūrvasūtravyākhyānaṃ na kṣodakṣamamityasvarasādāha--seti |
sānilānanilādhikānilarūkṣalaghubhāvito rasāsṛggataḥ || 34 ||
asyārthaḥ dravyasaṃyogaviśiṣṭajanyakaṣāyatvasajātīyarasā na dhātuvikārakārakapratibandhakā iti vaktuṃ śakyate, tatsajātīyavirodhipratibandhakadravyatvāt | tadrasāsṛgdhātupravartakoṣṇasvabhāvaguṇavattvaṃ laghutvasvabhāvaguṇavattvaṃ bhajat rasāsṛgdhātuśoṣaṇaṃ karoti | ataḥ bahiḥpradeśasthitapavanadravyādane tatsaṃjātaviśiṣṭarasaguṇaviśiṣṭassan dhātuśoṣaṇaṃ karoti | tatsarvametadvākyena pratīyate | na pūrvasūtravirodhaśca | tatra śrutireva pramāṇam |
ṣaḍrasāssaptadhātujanakāḥ rasavyāptatvāt | rasānāṃ dhātūnāṃ ca upavyañjakatvabhāvasaṃbandhāt | rasāsṛgdhātujātarasaprakārajatvena bhedasyāpratīyamānatvāt | āmlaśarīrā eva dhātava ityevaṃ ta ttaddhātubhedena tattaccharīrabhedāt rasabhedajanyasya tadadhīnajñānajanyatvāt | dravyabhedo'pi tathā pratīyate yatra rasādhikyapratītiḥ tatraiva tattadravyādhikyaṃ tattadbhūruhāṇāṃ pratīyate | svādurasaviśiṣṭo'yaṃ vṛkṣaḥ | lavaṇarasaviśiṣṭo'yaṃ vṛkṣaḥ | kaṣāyarasaviśiṣṭo'yaṃ vṛkṣa iti tattatsārabhedena pratīyate | tadetaccharīravanta iti bahujanasiddhatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu pittadoṣapradeśāśritamāṃsamedodhātussvādurasāśritaḥ | tasmātsvādurasasya pittaprakopanivartakatvaṃ vaktumaśakyam | virodhassyāt | rasānāṃ dhātvātmakatvāt | dhātūnāṃ rasāśritamāṃsāśritattvāt | taddoṣāśritarasātmakadhātūnāṃ doṣaprakopanivartakatvaṃ vaktumayogyamityasvarasādāha--pitteti |
pittāśrayāttadadhīnapittamāṃsamedonusarannasthimṛdukārakaḥ || 35 ||
pittāśritatvāt māṃsamedodhātussvādutiktakaṣāyattvāt tadāśritapittavirodhakatvaṃ śuddhasvādurasasya śuddhatiktarasasya śuddhakaṣāyarasasya ca vaktuṃ na śakyate, tadāśritarasātmakatvāt | kiṃtu pittaprakopāya virodhitvasya vaktumucitatvāt pittāśritatvāditi tadadhīnapittamiti pittaṃ rasādhīnam | pittaprakope sati tadrasavaddravyasyaiva tannivartakatvāt svādurasādhīnaṃ pittamiti māṃsamedodhātugataṃ sat vartate | mṛdusthaulyaguṇaṃ vidhatte |
nanu doṣatrayaniyuktarogā asādhyāḥ | taddoṣaprakopakāryatvaṃ rasavirasādhīnajanyavikārahetukaṃ doṣavṛddhikṣayotpādanakāryanivartakadravyatvāt | ataḥ kaṣāyaprakopayuktakāryopayogiketaradoṣāśrayakāryaṃ vyapadiśati--mandeti |
mandoṣṇādisamadoṣādhikākṣīṇavṛddhiguṇakāsthimajjāmedo'dhiṣṭhitāḥ || 36 ||
mandaguṇavattvāvacchedakapavanattvaṃ uṣṇaguṇavattvāvacchedakapittatvaṃ taditaradoṣakaphāvacchedakaṃ tatkṣīṇavṛddhiguṇāśrayatvam, tathā sati majjādhātuparyantagatasakalasāmagrīsānnidhyaṃ sampādayan rogābhivṛddhiṃ kurvan tadrogiṇamantyasannihitaṃ karotītyarthaḥ |
nanu doṣāṇāṃ majjādhātuparyantaṃ rogāvarohaṇena sakaladhātudoṣagasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ pañcadhātugataprāṇodbhavānāṃ ca vāyūnāṃ saṃñcārābhāvatvena taccharīraṃ vihāya bahiḥpraveśagatatvāt sa jīvaḥ śarīrāvatarito bhavati | doṣāṇāṃ kāryakāritvasya tāvanmātreṇaiva caritārthatvāt śukladhātupraveśajakāryakaraṇasya prayojakatvābhāvāt | tasmātsaptadhātugaṇanasya prāyojakatvābhāvena vyarthaṃ syādityasvarasādāha--adoṣeti |
adoṣadhāturasānusārānusarāsthirasapacanacaramadhātupracārakāḥ || 37 ||
etatkāryakārakasya pacanatvaṃ doṣāṇāṃ caramadhātupracāraṇatvam | pracarantīti pracārakāḥ | śukladhātupoṣakakāryaṃ śuddhasvādurasādanahetukam | na śoṣakakāryam | adoṣatvaṃ nāma adoṣadhāturasānusārānusaradoṣaprakopakāryābhāvavṛddhikārakamityarthaḥ | uktamarthamupasaṃharati--bhūteti |
bhūtapūrvapadārthajātadhātuhetubhūtānalasamarasapacanaviruddhārtharasapākayogādviparyayaḥ || 38 ||
bhūtapūrvapadārthajāteti | pañca bhūtāḥ pūrve yasya sa tathā | bhūtapūrvaścāsau padārthaśca | śarīraṃ pañcabhūtātmakamiti sarvapadārtho vivakṣitaḥ | tajjātā dhātavaḥ | teṣāṃ hetubhūto yo'nalaḥ tenānalena samarasapacanaṃ sārakiṭṭatayā vibhajya pacatīti samarasapacanam | tasmācchuddharasavaddravyādanajātassaṃskāraḥ | tasmāddhātavaḥ | puṣṇanti | śarīramabhivardhate | nīrogatvaṃ bhajatāṃ puṃstvamabhivardhata iti tātparyam | indriyavān bhavati | viruddhārtharasapākayogaḥ virasādanapākajāto yo rogaḥ sa dhātuviruddakāryako bhavati | sa evaṃ bhaviṣyan viparyayo bhavati |
nanu dhātūnāmākārajñānaṃ vikārajñānapūrvakaṃ tadvikārābhāvakāryābhāvajñāne sati sṛṣṭisaṃhārakramajñānāt | virasādikāryābhāvajñānaṃ vikārābhāvasādhyakāryahetukaṃ tadvikārakāryābhāvahetubhūtadravyādhīnatvāt yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yadhā ghaṭaḥ iti nyāyanayena byatirekānumānasiddhau uktarītyā upasaṃhārajñānaṃ vyarthaṃ syādityasvarasādāha--ṣaḍiti |
ṣaṭkamalānāmādibhūtaṃ mūlādhārakam || 39 ||
asya sūtrasyāyamarthassampannaḥ--ādhārapadmādhiṣṭhita āmlarasaḥ | svādurasakamambutattvam | ūṣaṇarasāhitalavaṇaguṇaviśiṣṭamanalatattvam | tiktarasaguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ vāyutattvam | kaṣāyarasātmakamākāśattattvaṃ ca | etāni padmāni rasāsṛṅmāṃsamedomajjāśukladhātvātmakāni pañcabhūtātmakāni | prakṛtipuruṣādhārasahasrārapadmaṃ ākāratattvapadmajanakam | tadvāyutattvapadmajanakam | tattejastatvapadmajanakam | tadambutattvapadmajanakam | tatpṛthivītattvapadmajanakam | tatsajātīyāmlarasavirasavadvikāro bhūtvā pṛthivītattvamambutatve vilīyate | svādurasātmakāmbutattvaṃ svādurasavirasavadvikāro bhūtvā tejastattve vilīyate | kaṭurasāntarhitalavaṇarasaguṇaviśiṣṭatejastattvaṃ lavaṇarasavirasavikāro bhūtvā vāyutattve vilīyate | tiktarasaguṇaviśiṣṭavāyutattvaṃ tiktarasavirasādanajanyarogagrastaṃ sat ākāśatattve vilīyate | kaṣāyarasātmakākāśatattvaṃ kaṣāyarasavirasādanajātāmayagrastaṃ sat svayameva vilīyate | ayaṃ saṃhārakramaḥ | tattatpadmaniṣṭharasāḥ tattatsajātīyavirasadravyādanajātarogāḥ saṃhārakramopakārakāḥ | etādṛśārthaṃ manasi nidhāya ṣaṭkamalānāmādharabhūtapadmaṃ tattatpadmavināśapūrvakaṃ tattaccharīrāntaśśarīranāśaṃ kuruta iyarthaḥ |
nanu pañcabhūtavikārāḥ pañcendriyāṇi | tadvikārāḥ ṣaḍrasāḥ | sapta dhātavaḥ | iti tatra rasāsṛjī ekīkṛtya ṣaḍrasātmako'yamiti upayuktatvena rasāsṛjī ekīkṛtya tathaiva pratipāditam | rasā eva dhātavaḥ | dhātūnāṃ ca rasānāṃ ca bhedābhāvāt | ataḥ pañcamahābhūtānāṃ indriyāṇāṃ viṣayādivat rasādīnāmapi tadvadeva bhūtavikāratvaṃ vaktavyam | tasmādrasānāṃ ṣaṭsaṃkhyāgaṇanaṃ vyarthaṃ syāditi ūṣaṇarasāntarhitalavaṇarasasya asthidhātujanakatvāt | etāvatparyantaṃ varṇabodhakasirāṇāmeva ....... dhātumadhyagatassan ubhayapārśvadhārakatvena daśadalapadmaṃ sahasrasirātmakapadmādhārakaṣaṭkoṇacakrasya ....... tvāt | lavaṇoṣaṇarasayorekatvena daśadalapadmātmakāsthidhātuṃ sampadya edhate | ityanunayet | sakalavarṇodbodhakasirādhāracakramadhye adṛṣṭatvāt sahasrasirādhāraka ṣaṭkoṇacakrasya amṛtopajanya.......tvāt | etāvatparyantaṃ varṇabodhakasirāṇāmeva tatpoṣakatvaṃ nānyeṣāmiti dhātumadhye'ntargatatvāt .......sattvena tattadvarṇabodhakapadmānāṃ sahasrasirādhārakatvāt | teṣāmamṛtasirāsaṃsargāt | lavaṇaika.......pradhānajanyānalabhūtātmakāsthidhātujanakaṣaṭkoṇacakrasya amṛtopaharaṇaśīlābhāvatvāt ityasvarasādāha--iḍeti |
iḍāpiṅgalāpūritānilatatsirāgatāmṛtaṃ siñcati || 40 ||
iḍāpiṅgalapūritānilātmakaṣaṭkoṇacakrabodhakakumbhakānilapūraṇakāryahetukaḥ saḥ iḍāpiṅgalapūritānilaśiraḥkamalādamṛtamāvṛtya tāvatsirāssaṃpūrya ṣaṭkoṇānilacakrasya unmīlinadravyatvāt analāniladravyayossahasaṃcārayoḥ dravya.......piṅgalapūritānilānalaprajvalanakāryasya vyañjanānilatvāt | tasmātpavanapūraṇacakrapratibhāsaheturityarthaḥ |
nanu recakapūrakakumbhakānilena udarakumbhamāpūrya amṛtaṃ siñcatīti yat taccintyam, ṣaṭkoṇacakrasya kuṇḍalyadhassthitatvāt | kuṇḍalyuparigatapadmānāmeva tatpūraṇakāryakaraṇapratipādanāt | kuṇḍalīḍāpiṅgalapavanena amṛtasecanakavarṇassyādityasvarasādāha-- pṛṣṭheti |
pṛṣṭhorūdarajaṅghāśiśnopasthadeśādyairabhivardhate ||41 ||
lavaṇoṣaṇajanyāsthidhātvākārabhūtaṣaṭkoṇacakraṃ kuṇḍalīpradeśādadhassthitatvāt pṛṣṭhacakravat tadūrdhvodaravat pṛṣṭhadeśavat pākakarmaṇi sahasrasirābhirabhivardhate | teṣāmapi śvāsocchāsapavanagatisaṃyogadvārā abhivardhitatvena śvāsocchāsayoretatpoṣakakarmahetukatvapratipādanāt pṛṣṭhodarapadmavadabhivardhata ityarthaḥ |
asthidhātvantarbhāvitamajjādhātoramṛtāgamanasaṃsargaprasaṃgābhāvena amṛtopajīvyatvaṃ nopapadyata ityasvarasādāha--nābheriti |
nābheradhassthitaṃ kuṇḍalyādibhūtaṃ śatadalapadmaṃ pañcasahasrasirāvṛtaṃ saroruhamajāyata || 42 ||
kuṇḍalyadhassthitarasāsṛṅmāṃsamedodhātūnāṃ śvāsocchāsapavanayoḥ sparśayogyatvābhāvāt upajīvyopajīvakasaṃbandhasyānavakāśāt tatpoṣakakarmaṇaḥ āvaśyakatvena pañcasahastrasirāvṛtaśatadalapadmaṃ ajāyata tāvaddhātupadmapoṣaṇārtham | etadvinā poṣakatvābhāvena tadādhārabhūtasaroruhamajāyatetyarthaḥ | majjādhātvantarbhūtasvādurasopajīvyaśukladhātvabhivardhanakāryaṃ, kathaṃ saṃgacchata ityāha--jaṭhareti |
jaṭharāgnerālavālakatayā bhāti || 43 ||
jaṭharāgnerāvirbhūtapācakapittakalāpakvādikasya ādhārabhūtatvena jaṭharāgnirūpapittakalāprajvalanārthaṃ ālavālādhārabhāvatvena jvalanakriyāyogyatvāt śukladhātorāpādatalamastakaparyantaṃ sarvadhātvādhārakatayā ālavālakatayā bhāsata evetyarthaḥ ||
saptadhātūnāṃ ṣaḍrasātmakatvaṃ svādvamlavaṇatiktoṣaṇakaṣāyarasānāmamṛtapoṣakatvaṃ kathaṃ syādityata āha--madhureti |
madhurarasaṃ pracālayan śukladhātusthāne svatejasā bhāti || 44 ||
madhurarasavaddravyam, sa ca pittena pacyamānassan tatpācakapittapākajanyakriyāsaṃskāravaśāt śukladhātutvena vardhate | kaṣāyarasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati pavanaprakopo bhavati | pavanadoṣaprakopaḥ śukladhātugatassan śuklaśoṣaṇakāryaṃ karoti | tadvirasadravyaṃ pācakapittena pacyamānaṃ sat rasāsṛgdhātuvikāraṃ karoti | śukladhātusthānaṃ gaganabhūtapradeśaḥ | gaganabhūtaguṇādhikyadravyasiddhaṃ tejaḥ | pittakalāpacanajātasaṃskārayuktatejastvāt śukladhāturūpaguṇo bhātītyarthaḥ | tatsvādurasavirasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati so'sṛgdhātuvikāraṃ karoti | tadanivartane jalamalātisārarogādayassaṃbhavantītyarthaḥ | śukladhātuvṛddhirūpalavaṇarasādhikyadravyatvāt tejasā bhāti | rasāsṛgdhātuvikāravṛddhiśca etadvirasādhikyasvatejasā bhātīyarthaḥ ||
nanu yāvadrasavadannādanaṃ kaphapradeśahṛdgatamātraṃ sat bhuktaṃ madhurībhūtavikāraṃ bhajate | tataḥ pakvāśayaṃ praviśya āmlarasavikāraṃ bhajan pācakapittena pacyamānaṃ sat svabhāvāmlarasavadravyamajjādhātumedhate ||
dhātugatāmayānavahṛtya kālapākajītīyajātaupādhikarasapradhānaguṇān yacchati | tadāmlarasadravyaṃ māṃsadhātuvikāraṃ karoti | tena kaphaprakopanakāsaśobhapāṇḍuvisarpādayassaṃbhavanti | taddoṣajanyāmayaprakopanivṛtyarthaṃ kaṭurasavaddravyādanam | tena taddoṣajanyāmayaprakopanivṛttiṃ kurvat rasapācakapittena pacyamānaṃ sat śukladhāturūpaṃ gataṃ padaṃ naḥ tena bhramaṇamūrchāratirogādayaḥ prāpnuvantītyarthaḥ ||
nanu yāvadrasavadannādanaṃ kaphapradeśahṛdgatamātraṃ sat tadbhūktānnaṃ madhurībhūtaṃ bhavati | tatpakvāśayaṃ praviśya āmlībhūtaṃ sat pācakapittena pacyamānaṃ sat tattaddravyabhedena pākaṃ kartuṃ na śaknoti | madhurarasakāryamāmlarasavikārakāryaṃ ca kaṇṭhamārgapraveśānantaraṃ pūrvānnānadanajātamadhurarasaṃ prāpya kaphapradeśahṛdgataṃ sat madhurarasaṃ bhajati | tadanantaraṃ āmlarasavikāraṃ kurvan pakvāśayaṃ praviśya pākāntaramadhigamya tadrasāntaraṃ bhajati | atimadhurarasadravyaṃ kathaṃ śukladhātupravardhakamityasvarasādāha--āmleti |
āmlarasaṃ vipācayan majjādhātusthāne svatejasā bhāti || 45 ||
