āmlarasadravyaṃ pācayan yāvadannasya bhuktasya prathamadravyaniṣṭhatattadvidhivihitaguṇānprayacchati | kālapākajanyopādhivaśāt tadrasāntaraṃ bhajannapi aupādhikarasaguṇānna prayacchati | sarvarasasaṃsargayuktānnādanatve'pi tatpācakapittaṃ tattadrasavaddravyaṃ ekadā vibhajya pacati | sa eva rasaḥ svetararasaguṇādhīnaphalaṃ prayacchati | tādṛśāmlarasadravyaṃ majjādhātutvena pravardhate | taddhātu gatapavanavikāraṃ hanti | tannaivāmlarasadravyaṃ māṃsadhātuvikāraṃ karoti | tadvirasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati tattaddhātuvidhivihitāmayāḥ prajāyante | anilādhikāmlarasadravyaṃ majjādhātupoṣakam anilabhūtādirūpopalambhakāmlarasadravyatvāt | anilabhūtādirūpopalambhakāmlarasadravyaṃ māṃsadhātuvikārakārakamāṃsadhātuśoṣaṇopakārakadravyatvāt | ityanumānapramāṇena āmlarasavadaniladravyaṃ majjādhātubalakārakaṃ māṃsadhātuvikārakārakaṃ cetyubhayaphalaṃ prayacchati tattajjātīyadhātupoṣakatvaṃ pratīyate | māṃsadhātuviruddharasadravyasya māṃsaśoṣakatvaṃ ca pratīyate | evaṃ sakalabhūtotpattikramaḥ | evameva sakalabhūtānāṃ saṃhārakramaśca boddhavyaḥ | anyonyaṃ yāvaddhātupoṣakatvaṃ tadanyonyayoreva dhātunāśakatvaṃ ca brūmaḥ | rasānāṃ dhātupoṣakatvaṃ yatra pratīyate sa eva sṛṣṭikrama iti jñātavyaḥ | etadrasānāmeva dhātuśoṣaṇaikakāryaṃ yatra pratīyate, sa eva saṃhārakrama iti boddhavyaḥ | tadvaddoṣāśca malāścālayāśca tadanyonyakāryaṃ viśiṣṭasṛṣṭiṃ sṛjati | tadeva sṛṣṭināśaṃ karoti | ata evātmānamātmanā sṛjati | ātmakṛtanāśatvamātmanyeva | yata ubhayorapi dhātavaḥ rasā eva jñātavyā ityarthaḥ ||
nanu svādurasadravyāṇi ikṣukāṇḍādīni bahūni santi | tadvadāmlarasavadbhūruho bahavassanti | atha svādurasasya śukladhātupoṣakatvaṃ, āmlarasasya majjādhātupoṣakatvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate | tadvat lavaṇarasavadbhūruho'yaṃ bheṣajayogya iti adṛṣṭatvāt kathaṃ lavaṇarasavaddravyaṃ dhātupoṣakamiti vyavahartuṃ śakyata ityasvarasādāha--lavaṇeti |
lavaṇarasaṃ vipācayan asthidhātusthāne svatejasā bhāti || 46 ||
idaṃ lavaṇarasavaddravyaṃ pathyavargeṣu auṣadhayogakāryeṣu lavaṇarasavadbhūruha iti vyavahārābhāvepi sakalarasānāṃ dhātupoṣakatvaṃ lavaṇarasena vinā pākaṃ kartuṃ aśakyamiti lavaṇarasatvaṃ lavaṇadravye prasiddhamiti bhojyayogyadravyajanakadravyādiṣu lavaṇarasapākasya kartuṃ yogyatvāt | ata eva asthidhātorlavaṇadravyātmakatvena lavaṇaṃ samudrajalavikārabaḍavānalakāryāntaḥpraviṣṭatvena tadbaḍavānalātmakamiti suprasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ ||
lavaṇaṃ medorogahetukam | medodhātuśoṣakadravyatvāt | lavaṇarasaḥ asthidhātupoṣakaḥ analasajātīyadravyatvāt, sarvarasopakārakatvāt | ata eva pācakapittaṃ lavaṇarasaṃ vipācayat, asthidhātusvarūpaṃ bhajata sakalabhāraṃ vahati | lavaṇarasayogadravyādanaṃ asthidhātubalapradam | sakalarasayogavattvena hi sakaladhātupoṣakatvam | ataeva lavaṇarasasya ṣaḍrasānāmapi balapradāyakatvam | śuddharasavaddravyādane'pi lavaṇarasaṃ vinā pācyābhāvāt | śuciśabdasya lavaṇatvābhidhāne tu lavaṇaraso medodhātuvikārakārakaḥ, lavaṇāmbujanyalavaṇarasadravyatvāt | tadvirasadravyaṃ asthidhātuśoṣakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
nanu kaṣāyarasavaddravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati śukladhātuśoṣakakārye pavanavikāro jāyate | tatpavanaprakopanivṛttyarthaṃ svādurasadravyādanaṃ vidhiḥ | tasmācchukladhātuvikāre jāte sati svādurasavaddravyādanaṃ vidhīyate | kaṭurasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte majjādhātuvikārakārakapavanaprakopo jāyate | tādṛśapacanaprakopanivṛtyarthaṃ āmlarasadravyādanaṃ vidhīyate | (lavaṇarasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati), tiktarasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati ca pavanaprakopo jāyate | tatprakopanivṛttyarthaṃ lavaṇarasadravyādanaṃ vidhīyate | kaṭurasadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati pavanaprakopo jāyate | pittavikāraśukladhātupoṣaṇaṃ karoti | tatra mūrchābhramatandrārucisarvāṅgatodāratayo jāyante | etadguṇāvirbhāvakapavanapittaprakopahetukarasavirasādanādajīrṇe jāte sati tatra dvandvadoṣaguṇāvirbhāvo bhavati | tatra svādurasadravyādanaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavati | tiktarasadravyādanakāryaṃ aprayojakaṃ syādityasvarasādāha--tikteti |
tiktarasaṃ vipācayan medodhātusthāne svatejasā bhāti || 47 ||
pācakapittaṃ tiktarasaṃ vipācayat medodhātugataṃ pittavikāraṃ harat tatsthāne svatejasā pācakapittajanyatejasā svayameva bhāti | tiktarasādanādajīrṇe jāte sati tajjanyarogo'sthidhātuśoṣaṃ karoti | tatpittaviruddhalavaṇarasena pittaprakopo bhavatītyarthaḥ ||
nanu āmlarasavaddravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati kaphaprakopo dṛśyate | tatprakopanivṛttyarthaṃ kaṭurasadravyādanaṃ vidhiḥ | āmlarasaviśiṣṭagandhavaddravyatvaṃ pṛthivītvāvacchedakam | pṛthivī abdravyajanyā sārdradravyatvāt | tajjanyatvena śītatvaṃ gurutvam | e tallakṣaṇalakṣitakaphaprakopasya pṛthivīdravyasaṃyogajanyatvāt | tadviruddhadravyādanamapi kaphaprakopahetukam | kaṭurasadravyādanenaiva kaphaprakopanivṛttirityāśayaṃ manasi nidhāyāha--ūṣaṇeti |
ūṣaṇarasaṃ vipācayan māṃsadhātusthāne svatejasā bhāti || 48 ||
sarvatra sattvāt pavanaprakopakārakakaphaprakopanivartakatvasya bahuśo dṛṣṭatvāt pavanaprakopakārakamiti vaktumaśakyatvācca | tathā hi--sarandhrakābhyantaradharasirāmārgagatapavanaprakopasya hataḥ kaphaprakopo bhavati | kaṭurasādhikadravye pakvāśayagate sati tatkaphaprakopaṃ haratīti vaktavyam ||
ācāryassarvaceṣṭāsu loka eva hi dhīmatām |
eṣa śirastodo yatra prakāśyate tatra kaṭurasavaddravyavilepanaṃ śirastodanivartakaṃ bhavati | kiṃcānalamandādajīrṇe jāte sati kukṣau vedanā jāyate | tatra kaṭurasadravyādanaṃ nivartakaṃ bhavati | śītodakasnānena rasājīrṇe sati sarvāṅgeṣu pavanaprakopo jāyate | tatra analasaṃyogādinā svedanaṃ āmajvarādīnāṃ nivartakaṃ bhavati | tatra pavanaprakopasya nivṛttiḥ yadā bhavet tatsirārandhramārgāgatapavanagatitirodhānaṃ sarvatrāpi māṃsadhātoḥ kaphagrastatvāt kaphaprakope sati pavanagatitirodhānaṃ kurute | tatra vedanā'pi jāyate | tatra pavanaprakopakāryanivartakadravyaṃ vā bheṣajaṃ tajjanakakaphaprakopanivartakadravyaṃ vā bheṣajam | tatra pavanaprakopanivartakarasavaddravyādanamantareṇa tatpavanaprakopanivṛttiṃ kartṛmaśakyatvāt | tasmātkaṭurasavaddravyaṃ pavanaprakopakāryanivartake vidheyamiti ceṣṭā svācāryaḥ pareṣām | rogajanakasamūhāhetutvāt | kaṭurasavaddravyaṃ pavanaprakopanivartakamiti vyapadeśamātreritārthaḥ | tajjanakībhūtarasavirasādanādajīrṇajātāmapittaviṣakriminivartanadvārā kaṭurasasya hetutvamityarthaḥ | kaṭurasasya krimidoṣanivartanamātre caritārthatvāt | māṃsadhātuprakopakārakakaphodrekakāryaṃ pavanodrekakāryahetukaṃ tatkāraṇanivartakamantareṇa tatkāryanivṛtterasaṃbhavāt | tatpavanaprakopakāryanivartakaṃ kaphaprakopanivartakasāmagrīsaṃpādanameva vidheyaṃ bhavati | tatsāmagrīsampādanaṃ kaṭurasadravyādanamevetyarthaḥ |
nanu madhurarasaguṇādhikyadravyaṃ abdravyatādṛśādhikyadravyādanādajīrṇe jāte sati rasāsṛgdhātuvikāraṃ karoti | tadādānanidānabhūtāḥ rogāḥ jalātisāramahāmalātisāragrahaṇyatisāraraktapittarogāśca raktavāyuśca saṃbhavanti | tannivartakaṃ kaṣāyarasavaddravyādanamiti manasi nidhāyāha-- kaṣāyeti |
kaṣāyarasaṃ vipācayan rasāsṛgdhātusthāne svatejasā bhāti || 49 ||
pācakapittena kaṣāyarasadravyādane pakvāśayagate sati tatpittakalāyāḥ pākaḥ kriyate | tatpākajanyasaṃskāreṇa rasāsṛgdhātuvikāraṃ harati | tannivartakakaṣāyarasadravyādānaṃ tatra heturiti rasāsṛgdhātuvikāranivartakasāmagrīrūpabalavattejasā samartho'smīti pratibhātīti atra svatejasā bhātīti pratipāditam ||
kaṣāyarasavirasādanādajīrṇe jati sati tacchukladhātugativikāraṃ kurvat tacchuklagatapavanaprakope sati taddhātugativihita rogāssambhavanti | mūrcchārogaśca hikkārogaśca bhramavikāraḥ raktavāyuśca śobhāśca aṅgakampaścāpasmāraśca pādasphuraṇaṃ ca sambhavantītyarthaḥ |
nanu sṛṣṭisaṃhārakramadvayamapi pratipāditam | pañcabhūtānāmutpattikramaḥ tatsaṃhārakramaśca jñāpitaḥ | tathā sati dṛṣṭaṃ jagaccharīrātmakaṃ jīvaccharīrātmakaṃ prāṇādimattvāt itīdaṃ jagatsarvaṃ śarīrātmakaṃ prapañcātmasvarūpatvāt ityanumānapramāṇena svāṅgaśarīrotpattiḥ vaktavyā | tatra śarīrotpādakasāmagryāṃ satyāṃ nityaṃ śarīrakāryaṃ jāyate | śarīrotpādakasāmagryāmasatyāṃ tatkāryotpatteraśakyatvāt ityasvarasādāha-- sakaleti |
sakalarasādhārādibhūtamaṅguṣṭhadalaṃ nābhipadmādhiṣṭhitaṃ trikoṇaṃ nāma saroruhamajāyata || 50 ||
sakalarasādhārādibhūtaṃ nāma aṅguṣṭhadalaṃ ṣaḍrasātmakaṃ saptadhātvātmakaṃ avarṇopadhagakavargapañcavarṇādhārabhūtaṃ pañcabhūtātmakaśarīrotpatteḥ ādhārabhūtatvena sakalarasādhārabhūtamiti | aṅguṣṭhadalamātraviśiṣṭanābhyadhassthitapadmaṃ trikoṇapadmam--
aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo'ṅgaṣṭhaṃ ca samāśritaḥ |
iti śrutyanusāreṇa prakṛtipuruṣayorādhārabhūtamiti tāveva prajājananaṃ kurutaḥ | tayoradhiṣṭhānatvena tatra sthitaḥ asṛgeva rasa iti | ata eva śarīrādhārabhūtamiti samyakpratipāditam | "raso vai saḥ rasam̐hyevāyaṃ labdhvā''nandī bhavati" | iti śrutervidyamānatvāt | tatraiva trikoṇapadmaṃ puruṣasyāpyasti | bīje' dhaḥkoṇau, taduparikoṇamekamādhārabhūtaṃ kāmagiryātmakaṃ rudrātmakaśaktiviśiṣṭaṃ kāmeśvaradevatātmakam | etādṛśaguṇaviśiṣṭamūrdhvakoṇaṃ adhaḥkoṇadvayasya dakṣiṇapārśvakoṇasya sūryacakrātmakajālaṃdharapīṭhe uḍḍiyāṇadevatātmakataditaravāmakoṇaṃ somacakātmakapūrṇagirigahvarapīṭhaṃ parabrahmātmakaśaktiviśiṣṭaṃ trikoṇapadmaṃ prakṛtipuruṣayoraikyakarmaṇā sarvadā ānandānubhavasukhamanubhūyate | strī prakṛtiḥ | puruṣastu īśvaraḥ | tayoraikyasukhaṃ sarvadā anubhūyate | ata eva sarvarasādhārabhūtamiti | sarvajantūnāṃ trikoṇapadmaṃ sarvarasāśrayaṃ prajājananahetubhūtasukhaikāśrayatvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivaṃ yathā ghaṭa itīdaṃ sarvaṃ rasāśrayamiti sarvapramāṇasiddham ||
prajāṅkurāvirbhāvavadbhūmipradeśaḥ rasādīnāmāmlarasavāniti vyapadiśyate | ata eva sarvarasātmakatvaṃ brūmaḥ | prajājananahetubhūtapradeśatvāt puṃsāmapi meḍhrapradeśaḥ trikoṇavadbhāsate | bījadvayasthitatrikoṇadvayavikārabhūtabījadvayādhiṣṭhitaṃ tadupari meḍhrapradeśamekakoṇam | tasmātsarvapuṃsāṃ trikoṇapadmaṃ pratītamiti yattadayuktam | aṇḍajātapuṃvaccharīrāṇāṃ meḍhrātmakabījadvayapadmasya abhāvāt teṣāṃ trikoṇapadmasyābhāvāditi nāśaṅkanīyam | bījādutpannatvasya ubhayorapi samānatvāt | te aṇḍajā iti sarvaśāstrasiddhatvāt | pratyakṣeṇa trikoṇavikārameḍhrabījābhāve'pi bījajanyaṃ piṇḍamityubhayorapi samānam | ata eva aṇḍajānāṃ antassthitapeśīti vyapadiśyate iti kecit | aṇḍajānāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ aṇḍajatvaṃ samānamityarthaḥ |
nanu sarvaśarīrasthitapadmānāṃ sarvavarṇādhāratvāt, teṣāṃ prayojanamapi varṇajñāpakatvamātrameveti pratibhāti | tatra trikoṇapadmasya agaṇitatvāt kathaṃ prajājananahetubhūtapadmaṃ na pratibhāsata iti | tathā sati sarvakāle'pi trikoṇapadmasya yāvaccharīraparyantaṃ satvāt prajotpattissyāt | strīpuruṣasaṃyogavyatirekeṇa prajotpatteradṛṣṭatvāt iti nāśaṅkanīyam | saptadhātumayaśarīraṃ śarīrāntarajanakam | tasmāttrikoṇapadmāntaraṃ jāyata ityarthaḥ | indriyaṃ prajājananahetubhūtaṃ na varṇotpādakaṃ bāhyakarmakṛtyādhārabhūtatvāt | tasmātprajājanmapratipādanakathanaṃ sarvakāle'pi saṃbhāvitamiti yattadayuktamiti tātparyam | mahāśarīrapipīlikāśarīrāṇāṃ idaṃ trikoṇapadmaṃ sarvasukhasādhanamiti niścitya pravṛtterdṛṣṭatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu kevalaṃ trikoṇapadmaṃ na prajājananahetukaṃ śukladhātoreva prajājananakāryahetutvāt | tasya ādhārabhūtamātratvenaiva caritārthatvāt | aṅkurotpādakabījāvāpaṃ vinā prajājananakāryasyāyogyatvādityasvarasādāha--tasmāditi |
tasmāttanurajāyata | tasmācchākhāśśarīre bhānti || 51 ||
tasmātpūrvoktarītyā strīṇāṃ puṃyoge sati prajājananakāryaṃ śakyate | anyathā tatsarvaṃ śākhāśarīrasya meḍhrasya prajājananahetubhūtatve'pi (na saṃbhavati) tanmeḍhrasyāpi śākhāśarīratvāt | tasmādityupasaṃhārasūtradvayapradeśāt trikoṇasthānamapi prajājananahetubhūtam | tadvanmeḍhrasyāpi prajājananahetutvamiti tanmeḍhrasyāpi śākhāśarīratvāt | tasmādityupasaṃhārasūtradvayapradeśāt trikoṇasthānamapi prajājananahetubhūtam | tadvanmeḍhrasyāpi prajājanana hetukatvamiti kṛtvā tasmādityupasaṃhārapadaṃ sūtradvaye'pi paṭhitamiti ||
ityāyurvedasya pañcamapraśnasya bhāṣyaṃ yogānandanāthakṛtaṃ
suprasiddhaṃ mahājanasammataṃ pratisūtra
vyākhyānaṃ lokopakārakṛtaṃ
samāptam.
mahādevastvenaṃ prakaṭayati tadvaibhavapadaṃ
gadadviṭsāmrājyaṃ satatamabhavattadvilasitaiḥ |
sa dakṣo vāmākṣyā'pyadhikadhanavidyāvitaraṇe
kaṭākṣaiḥ kāmākṣī kalayatu śubhaṃ naḥ pratidinam ||
janakaśśaṃkarassākṣāt jananī sarvamaṅgalā |
rakṣaṇe'vahitau tau me ko vicāraḥ kuto bhayam ||
sarveśvarapadāntike samarpitam.

atha ṣaṣṭhaḥ praśnaḥ.

ekaśarīravaddravyamekaikabheṣajam |1
tiktarasavattadāmayajanyarogahārakaḥ ||2

yaddravyocitasāro yadrasānusaritaguṇapradhānakarasaḥ tajjātīyo'nya rasapratibandhakaguṇadāyakaḥ |5
yāvaddhātugatadravyocitasārā stajjātīyānyadravyasthāstadrasānuguṇakāmayotpādakāḥ |6

kṣmāmbhorasavadbhūtāmburasakṣāraguṇānusara svādurasavadbhūruhāḥ rasāsṛkpittapavanāpahāḥ |13

vāmanī kaṭurasā vātaśleṣmaharā hṛtkaṇṭhakṛmīn hanti |79
krimighnaḥ kaṭurasaḥ vātapittajit kriminetrāmayānhanti |80
lavaṇo lavaṇarasa ucyate | sakalaśūlapavanāpahaḥ |81
paṭujaḥ kaṭurasaḥ raktātisāratridoṣaghnaḥ |82
sāmakaṃ kaṭurasaṃ gulmādanāvacanam ? |83
gudācca dvyaṅgulādūrdhvaṃ meḍhrāśca dvyaṅgule tathā |
caturaṅgulimāyāmaṃ vistāraṃ kukkuṭāṇḍavat ||84
omātmahitadaṇḍesmi'n cakramadhye mate'niśam |
aṣṭaprakṛtirūpā'sti kuṇḍalī suptanāgavat ||85
ityāyurvedasya daśamaḥ praśnaḥ samāptaḥ
<11>
atha ekādaśaḥ praśnaḥ.
[kleśatanūkaraṇārthaḥ] dehānilagatikaraṇārthaśca |1
avidyāsmitārāgadveṣābhiniveśāḥ pañca kleśāḥ |2
avidyākṣetramuttareṣāṃ prasuptatanuvicchinnodārāṇām |3
anityāśuciduḥkhānātmasunityaśucisukhātmakhyātiravidyā |4
dṛgdarśanaśaktyorekātmatevāsmitā |5
sukhānuśāyī rāgaḥ |6
duḥkhānuśāyī dveṣaḥ |7
svarasavāhī viduṣo'pi tathārūḍho'bhiniveśaḥ |8
te pratiprasavaheyāḥ sūkṣmāḥ |9
dhyānaheyāḥ tadvṛttayaḥ |10
kleśamūlaḥ karmāśayo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ |11
satimūle tadvipāko jātyāyurbhogāḥ |12
te hlādaparitāpaphalāḥ puṇyāpuṇyahetutvāt |13
pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhaiḥ guṇavṛttivirodhācca duḥkhameva sarvaṃ vivekinaḥ |14
heyaṃ duḥkhamanāgatam |15
draṣṭṛdṛśyayoḥ saṃyogo heyahetuḥ |16
prakāśakriyāsthitiśīlaṃ bhūtendriyātmakaṃ bhogāpavargārthaṃ dṛśyam |17
viśeṣāviśeṣaliṅgamātrāliṅgāni guṇaparvāṇi |18
draṣṭā dṛśimātraḥ śuddhopi pratyayānupaśyaḥ |19
tadartha eva dṛśyasyātmā |20
kṛtārthaṃ prati naṣṭamapyanaṣṭaṃ tadanyasādhāraṇatvāt |21
svasvāmiśaktyoḥ svarūpopalabdhihetuḥ saṃyogaḥ |22
tasya heturavidyā |23
tadabhāvāt saṃyogābhāvo hānaṃ taddṛśeḥ kaivalyam |24
vivekakhyātiraviplavā hānopāyaḥ |25
tasya saptadhā prāntabhūmiḥ prajñā |26
yogāṅgānuṣṭhānāt aśuddhikṣaye jñānadīptirāvivekakhyāteḥ |27
yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhayo'ṣṭāvaṅgāni |28
ahiṃsāsatyāsteyabrahmacaryāparigrahā yamāḥ |29
ete jātideśakālasamayānavacchinnāḥ sārvabhaumā mahāvrataṃ |30
śaucasaṃtoṣatapassvādhyāyeśvarapraṇidhānāni niyamāḥ |31
vitarkabādhane pratipakṣabhāvanam |32
vitarkāhiṃsādayaḥ kṛtakāritānumoditā lobhakrodhamo
hapūrvakā mṛdumadhyādhimātrā duḥkhājñānānantaphalā iti pratipakṣabhāvanam |33
ahiṃsāpratiṣṭhāyāṃ tatsannidhau vairatyāgaḥ |34
satyapratiṣṭhāyāṃ kriyāphalāśrayatvam |35
asteyapratiṣṭhāyāṃ sarvaratnopasthānam |36
brahmacaryapratiṣṭhāyāṃ vīryalābhaḥ |37
aparigrahasthairye janmakathantāsambodhaḥ |38
śaucāt svāṅge jugupsā parairasaṃsargaḥ |39
satvaśuddhisaumanasyaikāgryendriyajayātmadarśanayogyatvānica |40
santoṣādanuttamasukhalābhaḥ |41
kāyendriyasiddhiraśuddhikṣayāttapasaḥ |42
svādhyāyadiṣṭadevatāsamprayogaḥ |43
samādhisiddhirīśvarapraṇidhānāt |44
sthirasukhamāsanam |45
prayatnaśaithilyānantasamāpattibhyām |46
tatodvandvanabhighātaḥ |47
tasmin sati śvāsapraśvāsayoḥ gativicchedaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ |48
satu bāhyābhyantarastambhavṛttiḥ deśakālasaṅkhyābhiḥ paridṛṣṭo dīrghasūkṣmaḥ |49
bāhyābhyantaraviṣayākṣepī caturthaḥ |50
tataḥ kṣīyate prakāśāvaraṇam |51
dhāraṇāsu ca yogyatā manasaḥ |52
svaviṣayāsaṃprayoge cittasya svarūpānukāra iva indriyāṇāṃ pratyāhāraḥ |53
tataḥ parama vaśyatendriyāṇām |54
ityāyurvedasya ekādaśaḥ praśnassamāptaḥ
<12>
atha dvādaśaḥ praśnaḥ.
deśabandhaḥ cittasya dhāraṇā |1
tatra pratyayaikatānatā dhyānam |2
tadevārthamātranirbhāsaṃ svarūpaśūnyamiva samādhiḥ |3
trayamekatra saṃyamaḥ |4
tajjayātprajñālokaḥ |5
tasya bhūmiṣu viniyogaḥ |6
trayamantaraṅgaṃ pūrvebhyaḥ |7
tadapi bahiraṅgaṃ nirbījasya |8
vyutthānanirodhasaṃskārayorabhibhavaprādurbhāvau nirodhakṣaṇacittānvayo nirodhapariṇāmaḥ |9
tasya praśāntavāhitā saṃskārāt |10
sarvārthataikāgratayoḥ kṣayodayau cittasya samādhipariṇāmaḥ |11
śāntoditau tulyapratyayau cittasyaikāgryatāpariṇāmaḥ |12
etena bhūtendriyeṣu dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmā vyākhyātāḥ |13
śāntoditāvyapadeśadharmānupātī dharmī |14
kramānyatvaṃ pariṇāmānyatve hetuḥ |15
pariṇāmātrayasaṃyamādatītānāgatajñānaṃ |16
śabdārthapratyayānāmitaretarādhyāsāt saṅkarastatpravibhāgasaṃyamāt sarvabhūtarutajñānam |17
saṃskārasākṣātkaraṇāt pūrvajātijñānam |18
pratyayasya paracittajñānam |19
na tatsālambanaṃ tasyāviṣayībhūtatvāt |20
kāyarūpasaṃyamāt tadgrāhyaśaktistambhe cakṣuḥprakāśāsamprayoge'ntardhānam |21
sopakramaṃ nirupakramaṃ ca karma tatsaṃyamādaparāntajñānamariṣṭebhyo vā |22
maitryādiṣu balāni |23
baleṣu hastibalāni |24
pravṛttyālokanyāsāt sūkṣmavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭajñānam |25
bhuvanajñānaṃ sūryasaṃyamāt |26
candre tārāvyūhajñānam |27
dhruve tadgatijñānam |28
nābhicakre kāyavyūhajñānam |29
kaṇṭhakūpe kṣutpipāsānivṛttiḥ |30
kūrmanāḍyāṃ sthairyam |31
mūrdhajyotiṣi siddhadarśanam |32
prātibhādvā sarvam |33
hṛdaye cittasaṃvit |34
satvapuruṣayoḥ atyantāsaṃkīrṇayoḥ pratyayāviśeṣo bhogaḥ padārthāt svārthasaṃyamāt puruṣajñānam |35
tataḥ prātibhāśrāvaṇavedanādarśanāsvādavārtā jāyante |36
te samādhāvupasargā vyutthāne siddhayaḥ |37
bandhakāraṇaśaithilyāt pracārasaṃvedanāt cittasya paraśarīrāveśaḥ |38
udānajayāt jalapaṅkakaṇṭakādiṣu asaṅga utkrāntiśca |39
samānajayāt jvalanam |40
śrotrākāśayoḥ sambandhasaṃyamāt divyaṃ śrotram |41
kāyākāśayoḥ sambandhasaṃyamāt laghutūlasamāpatteścākāśagamanam |42
bahirakalpitā vṛttiḥ mahāvidehā tataḥ prakāśāvaraṇakṣayaḥ |43
sthūlasvarūpasūkṣmānvayārthavattvasaṃyamādbhūtajayaḥ |44
tato'ṇimādiprādurbhāvaḥ kāyasampattaddharmānabhighātaśca |45
rūpalāvaṇyabalavajrasaṃhananatvāni kāyasampat |46
grahaṇasvarūpāsmitānvayārthavattvasaṃyamādindriyajayaḥ |47
tato manojavitvaṃ vikaraṇabhāvaḥ pradhānajayaśca |48
satvapuruṣānyatākhyātimātrasya sarvabhāvādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sarvajñātṛtvaṃ ca |49
tadvairāgyādapi doṣabījakṣaye kaivalyam |50
sthānyupamantraṇe saṅgasmayākaraṇaṃ punaraniṣṭaprasaṅgāt |51
kṣaṇatatkramayoḥ saṃyamāt vivekajaṃ jñānam |52
jātilakṣaṇadeśaiḥ anyatānavacchedāt tulyayostataḥ pratipattiḥ |53
tārakaṃ sarvaviṣayaṃ sarvathāviṣayamakramaṃceti vivekajaṃ jñānam |54
satvapuruṣayoḥ śuddhisāmye kaivalyam |55
janmauṣadhimantratapassamādhijāḥ siddhayaḥ |56
jātyantarapariṇāmaḥ prakṛtyāpūrāt |57
nimittamaprayojakaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ varaṇabhedastu tataḥ kṣetrikavat58
nirmāṇacittānyasmitāmātrāt |59
pravṛttibhede prayojakaṃ cittamekamanekeṣām |60
tatra dhyānajamanāśayam |61
karmāśuklākṛṣṇaṃ yogitastrividhamitareṣām |62
jātideśakālavyavahitānāmapyānantaryaṃ smṛtisaṃskārayorekarūpatvāt |63
tāsāmanāditvaṃ cāśiṣo nitvatvāt |64
hetuphalāśrayālambanaiḥ saṅgṛhītatvāt teṣamabhāve tadabhāvaḥ |65
atītānāgataṃ svarūpato'styadhvabhedāt dharmāṇām |66
te vyaktasūkṣmā guṇātmānaḥ |67
pariṇāmaikatvāt vastutatvam |68
vastusāmye cittabhedāt tayorviviktaḥ panthāḥ |69
na caikacittatantraṃ vastu tadapramāṇakaṃ tadā kiṃ syāt |70
taduparāgāpekṣitvāt cittasya vastu jñātājñātam |71
sadā jñātāścittavṛttayaḥ tatprabhoḥ puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvāt |72
na tatsvabhāsaṃ dṛśyatvāt |73
ekasamaye cobhayānavadhāraṇam |74
cittāntaradṛśye buddheratiprasaṅgaḥ smṛtisaṅkaraśca |75
citerapratisaṅkramāyāḥ tadākāratāpattau svabuddhisaṃvedanaṃ |76
ityāyurvedasūtre dvādaśaḥ praśnaḥ
<13>
atha trayodaśaḥ praśnaḥ.
dhātuśoṣakakarmānekaviṣayakakāryajanyamandānalaprayukta sirāpathagatadoṣagatapratibandhakayāvaddhātuvikārakārakayāvatkrimigatadoṣavṛddhikṣayaḥ kṣayaḥ |1
tannivartakapoṣakadravyaṃ tatra bheṣajam |2
lakṣaṇānyapi tathā |3
yogyaistavacca nivartayet |4
anekahetukadravyamanekakāryakṛt |5
anāmapālanaṃ kuryāt |6
āmaṃ hi sarvarogāṇām |7
ādibhūtamāha brahmā |8
gandhavadguṇabhūyiṣṭha pārthivadravyāvayavādikarasavirasadravyādanajanyāmapittaviṣayakrimigrastasirāvikārarasāsṛgdhātuvikārā atra dṛśyante |9
rasavirasavaddravyādanājīrṇajanyāmajvare laṅghanaṃ viśoṣakadravyaṃ ca tatra bheṣajam |10
tacchophapāṇḍvāmayātisārārśaṃsi kuṣṭharaktapittamehavikārā dṛśyante |11
anekarogānugato bahurogapurogamaḥ | itihetukaṃ ṣaḍrasavirasadravyaṃ yathāyogaṃ dhātunāśakṛt |12
raso hyasṛk | raso vai saḥ | rasaṃ haivāyaṃ labdhvā''nandī bhavati |13
śukle svādu | majjā''mlam ! asthi lavaṇam | medaḥ tiktam | māṃse ūṣaṇam | kaṣāyarasāḥ rasāsṛgdhātupoṣakāḥ |14
rasāsṛksvādu | māṃsamāmlaṃ | medo lavaṇam | asthi tiktam | majjoṣaṇam | śuklaṃ kaṣāyarasaḥ virasaḥ | virasā rasāsṛgdhātunāśakāḥ |15
ye ye rasāstadbhūtajātāste tattadbhūtapoṣakāḥ |16
rasoparasalohādinādhāramahārasātpañcabhūtātmakāḥ ye ye mahārasāḥ mahāroganivartakāstattatkṛtiviṣayakarasā jāraṇābhāvanātmakāḥ |17
nājāritaṃ rasaṃ mārayet |18
jāritā rasā ārogyabhaktimuktipradāyakāḥ |19
somarasāssapittāssaviṣāḥ pākīkṛtā rasāsṛṅmāṃsagāmapittajvarānugata pavanapittavibhramatandrārugdāhadoṣaprajāgaropanivartakāḥ |20
mṛtalohāmṛtaṃ sapākīkṛtāśśuddharasāḥ medosthigatavikārānusaritapavanapittadvandvahetukarasajīrṇājanyāmapittaviṣakrimikaṇṭhakubjasitāṅgakarṇikaraktajihvātale tandrādāhaśoṣaprakopanivartakāḥ |21
yāvadgandhapavanājīrṇajanyāmavikārakavacanadoṣā yāvadbahirdṛśyante, yāvadgandhetarājīrṇajanyāmavikārapittadoṣā yāvaddehe prabhavanti, yāvadgandhapavanājīrṇajanyāmavikārapittajanakadoṣāḥ yāvadbahiḥ pradṛśyante; tāva dgandhaviśeṣājñānaṃ doṣatrayapūritahetukājīrṇajanyāmapacavanarvavikāradoṣāḥ |22
rasavirasavaddravyādanājīrṇajanyāmaviśeṣaḥ pittadoṣaḥ |23
na taijanajanyaḥ pittadoṣaḥ |24
bahiḥsthitā doṣā dantavikārakārakāḥ |25
pañcāśadvarṇādhiṣṭhitā yathāyogaṃ tathā tattadaṅgābhivardhakāḥ |26
bāhyāgniragnisthāne jvalayati |27
agnirme vāci śritaḥ, vāgghṛdaye, hṛdayaṃ mayi, ahamamṛte amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |28
vākpravartakaḥ sirādhamanīḥ prajvalayati |29
vāyurme prāṇe śritaḥ | prāṇo hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |30
agniṃ vā vādityaḥ sāyaṃ praviśati | tasmādagnirdūrānna dadṛśe | sūryaścakṣurgamayatu | sūryo me cakṣuṣi śritaḥ | cakṣurhṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |31
manovikārākārakamannamadyāt |32
rasavaddravyaṃ śironirāmayakārakam | sarvendriyāhlādakam |33
dussaṃyogavarṇamanodravyaṃ pañcaprāṇādhārakam |34
candramā me manasi śritaḥ | mano hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |35
abdravyaṃ sarvaśarīrapoṣakam |36
reto vā āpaḥ | āpo me retasi śritāḥ | reto hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi | retaso vā tasminneto dadhāti |37
vigandhavigatarasālavālaṃ sarvaśarīrapoṣakam |38
pṛthivī me śarīre śritā | śarīraṃ hṛdayaṃ | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |39
yāvatsarasavaddravyaṃ nirāmayakārakam |40
yāvattanūruhastāvattanūṃṣi bhibhrati |41
oṣadhayaḥ some rājñi praviṣṭhāḥ | pṛthivī tanum | oṣadhivanaspatayo me lomasu śritāḥ | lomāni hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |42
indro me bale śritaḥ | balaṃ hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |43
yāvattanūruhastāvattanūṃṣi vibhūti | parjanyenopadhivanaspatayaṃ prajāyante | parjanyo me mūrdhni śritaḥ | mūrdhā hṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |44
vanaspatiprerito manyurbhavati | īśāno me manyau śritaḥ | manyurhṛdaye | hṛdayaṃ mayi | ahamamṛte | amṛtaṃ brahmaṇi |45
ayurvedaśajñānaṃ nakṣatrajñānapūrvakam |46
ityāyurvedasya trayodaśaḥ praśnaḥ samāptaḥ

atha caturdaśaḥ praśnaḥ.
aśvanīnakṣatrātmakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ avarṇādhārakapādapadmaṃ sarvaśarīrādhārakam |1
bharaṇi-ṛkṣarūpaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāśritaṃ ivarṇajānupadma calanakriyākārakagatāgatakarmopakārakam |2
kṛttikābhādhiṣṭhitaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ uvarṇāśritajaṅghāpadmaṃ jaṅgamaśarīrādhārakam |3
rohiṇī-ṛkṣarūpaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ ṛvarṇāśritameḍhrapadmaṃ
prajājananakārakaṃ | sirāvalambaka-ṛvarṇabodhakorupadmaṃ
jaṅgamaśarīradhārakam |4
mṛgaśironakṣatrarūpaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāśritaṃ lṛvarṇabodhakapradeśagatakamalaṃ jalamalāśayadhārakam |5
ārdrānakṣatrātmakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāśritaṃ evarṇajñāpakapṛṣṭhapradeśakamalaṃ atisukhakārakam |6
punarvasūbhāpratipādakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirādhārakaṃ aivarṇādhārakamalaṃ ratisukhakārakam |7
tiṣyatārārūpaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ ovarṇāśritanābhipadmaṃ śvāsapavanagatikārakam |8
āśleṣānakṣatrātmakaṃ sāptatriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ auvarṇādhārabhūtaṃ kuṇḍalīkṛtanābhyāvṛtapadmaṃ pavanagatikārakam |9
makhānakṣatrātmakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvalambakaṃ ‘am' ityanusārabodhakanābhyāvṛtapadmaṃ pācakapittapratipādakam |10
phalgunīnakṣatrarūpaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvalambakavarṇajñāpakavṛṣaṇadvayapadmaṃ saptadhātvaṅkurālavālapoṣakam |11
uttaraphalgunīnakṣatrātmakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvalambakhavarṇajñāpakaromarājipadmaṃ visarjanarūpasukhahetukam |12
phalgunīnakṣatrātmakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvalambakakhavarṇajñāpakasvādhiṣṭhānapadmaṃ sarvāśayadyotakam |13
hastoḍurūpapratipādakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāśritagavarṇajñāpidakṣiṇapakṣapadmaṃ garbhāśayakārakam |14
citrānakṣatrarūpaṃ saptaviṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ ghavarṇabodhakavāmapakṣagatapadmaṃ paripūrṇagarbhāśayakāraṇam |15
svātītārāpratipādakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirādhārakaṃ ṅavarṇajñāpakaṃ dakṣiṇahastapadmaṃ tattaccharīragatabhinnasvarajñāpakam |16
viśākhānatrarūpaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvalambakacavarṇadyotakavāmahastapadmaṃ strīpuṃsasvarabhedajñāpakam |17
anūrādhānakṣatrātmakaṃ saptatriṃśatsirāvṛtaṃ chavarṇadyotakaṃ dakṣiṇabhujapadmaṃ ūrdhvakāyadhārakam |18

patthyādhātryakṣapunarnavāhiṅguśarapuṅkhavyāghrīdurālabhapūgīphalatamālakṣārāmlajambīrārkasnuhiśigruparvakogragandhakarañjakhadirāgniśrīparṇīśrībhanajayāgneyāḥ cūrṇakvāthaleharūpāḥ ajīrṇāmayanivartakāḥ |20
agnibhūtadravyādhikasvasamānādhikaraṇāmlarasopalambhakadravyādanajātājīrṇajātāmaroganivartakaṃ pārthivaguṇādhikasvādurasavaddravyam |21
viśvadevakusumakaṭukaṇāgnirasonadīpyakakarañja punarnavāyavajīrakahiṅgustavatsakavasukapatthyājigrasuragījātiphalatalapoṭakaṭakabarburakuṣṭhayāvatsarasavaddravyaṃ cūrṇakvāthakalkādivikāraṃ sarvājīrṇāmaroganivartakam |22
ambupavanabhūtasvasamānādhikaraṇarasopalambhakadravyādanājīrṇāmapittajvarajanyamandānilaruṅnivartakapavana
bhūtādhikasandhukṣaṇakarmakārakatiktarasavaddravyaṃ agnimāndyāmanivartakam |23
pāṭhākarañjānalaśarapuṅga punarnavānimbalaśunayavānīcarabandhana dīpyakajīrakabilvātiviṣāvacāsaindhavaviśvacavyanaitalarāsnā mṛtātasī bhṛṅgakuberākṣalodhrakavacāmāṃsi-elāvatālapatrikā kesadāruluka grandhyāgneyāḥ cūrṇakvāthaghṛtatailaleharūpāḥ mandānalāmayanivartakāḥ |24
gaganapavanabhūtasvasamānādhikaraṇoṣaṇarasopalambhakadravyādanajātājīrṇamapittaviṣarūpopādhikagrahaṇīrogahārakagaganabhūtādhikaguṇopalambhakakaṭurasavaddravyam grahaṇīroganivartakam |25
ārdrakavatsakatalapoṭakacūtārjunavaṭāśvatthabilvabadaralaśunaviśvagāṅgerukīnāgabalaciñcājamodarasadāḍimakarañjavāśāśukalodhravṛddhadāruśarapuṅkhamarīcadīpyakajīrakalavaṅgakaruṇābhīrunimbakuṭajādyāḥ cūrṇakvāthalehabhṛtā grahaṇīrujāpahāḥ |26
analānilaguṇabhūyiṣṭhasvasamānādhikaraṇakaṣāyarasopalambhakayāvaddravyādanajātājīrṇapittaviṣakrimigatasirāvikārajātasaptadhāturasakāraka gaganabhūyiṣṭhadravyaṃ viṣūcyāmayanivartakam |27
kaṭvādi daśadravyaṃ cūrṇakvāthabheṣanaṃ śvāsachardyāmayanivārakam |28
candanādisaptapadārthāḥ cūrṇakvāthopayuktāḥ śvāsachardyāmayanivārakāḥ |29
svarjikādidvādaśapadārthāḥ dhūpalepakvāthīkṛtāḥ gudāṅkurāmayahārakāḥ |30
karañjādekādaśārthāḥ dhūpalepakvāthatailopayuktāḥ arśoroganivārakāḥ |31
daśamūlādyekaviṃśadravyaṃ dhūpalepakāthatailaleharūpaṃ sat gudāṅkurāmayatāhārakam |32
bhārṅgyādi dvādaśabheṣajāḥ cūrṇakvāthaghṛtatailalehyarūpāḥ gudāṅkurāmayahārakāḥ |33
ativiṣādipañcadaśapadārthāḥ cūrṇakvāthalehavikārabhūtā atisāravikārahārakāḥ |34
marīcyādyaṣṭādaśadravyaṃ tadvadatisāranivartakam |35
niṣṭhādi daśadravyaṃ tadvadatisāranivartakam |36
pathyāditrayodaśadravyacūrṇakvāthābhyāṃ ajīrṇāmayo nivartate |37
ākranditrayodaśapadārthāḥ cūrṇakvātharūpāḥ ajīrṇāmayahārakāḥ |38
viśvādidaśadravyaṃ cūrṇakvāthaleharūpaṃ sat ajīrṇāmaroganivartakam |39
mustādidvādaśadravyaṃ cūrṇakvāthopayuktaṃ ajīrṇāmayaroganivartakam |40
veṇvādi pañcadaśadravyaṃ cūrṇakvātha ghṛtatailarūpaṃ agnimāndyāmayahārakam |41
saindhavādidvādaśadravyaṃ tadvadbheṣajaṃ mandāgniroganivartakam |42
viśvādi saptapadārthāḥ kvātharūpā mandānalahārakāḥ |43
ārdrakādidaśadravyaṃ kvāthaleharūpaṃ grahaṇīroganivartakam |44
laśunādidvādaśadravyaṃ cūrṇakvāthalehaghṛtarūpaṃ mandānalaroganivartakam |45
elādiṣaṭpadārthā cūrṇakvāthavikārabhūtāḥ mandānalaroganivartakāḥ |46
āyuṣkāmayamānena dharmārthasukhasādhanam |
āyurvedopadeśeṣu vidheyaḥ paramādaraḥ ||
vicārya yatnātsarvasmānnityaṃ dehaṃ nirīkṣayet |
ā daśāhānnirīkṣyainaṃ pathyenaiva tu taṃ haret ||
na pīḍayedindriyāṇi na ca tānyatilālayet |
trivargaśūnyaṃ nārambhaṃ bhajettaṃ cāvirodhayan ||
vṛddhissamānaissarveṣāṃ viparītairviparyayaḥ |
cayaprakopapraśamā vāyorgrīṣmādiṣu triṣu ||
varṣādiṣu ca pittasya śleṣmaṇaśśiśirādiṣu ||
ityāyurvedasya ṣoḍaśaḥ praśnaḥ samāptaḥ.
